**Owner calls:** FE-F21-2 (which work types may be deleted at all), FE-F21-3 (who runs Calendar Times), FE-F21-9 (what System Options shows) — taken at the start of RF1.

**Found along the way, not F21's to fix:**
- **F26 — the React Compiler has not run since 2026-06-01.** Commit `814c5da` moved to Vite 8 + `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6, which dropped the `babel` option; `vite.config.js` still passes `babel: { plugins: ['babel-plugin-react-compiler'] }`, which is now ignored (JS config, so no type error). No app chunk in `dist/` and no dev transform (`ExchangeRatesSettings`, `GeneralSettings`, `Modal`) contains the compiler's memo cache. v6 wants `@rolldown/plugin-babel` + `reactCompilerPreset()`. Four months of code were written under "the compiler memoizes, don't" — turning it back on needs its own verification pass.
- **F23** — the browser-tab titles are this clinic's name on every install: `public/index.html` ("Shwan Orthodontics - Practice Management"), `login.html`, `portal.html` and `PortalLogin.tsx#document.title`. Nothing retitles the staff app from the configured `CLINIC_NAME`.
- **F22** — `EmailService#getConfig` masks the SMTP password as `'********'` rather than `MASKED_SECRET`, and `updateConfig` doesn't skip the mask, so the Email form's save path needs checking for a round-tripped mask.

## F20 · Lab tracking, templates, label printing · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: `components/react/LabelPreviewModal` 982 · `components/templates/**` 1,255 (`GrapesJSEditor` 341, `TemplateDesigner` 249, `CreateTemplateModal` 225, `TemplateManagement` 213, `TemplateCard` 111, `TemplateStats` 66, `DesignerToolbar` 50) · `components/react/lab-tracking/**` 878 (`LabCaseModal` 567, `LabCaseCard` 158, `LabFilters` 92, `LabBoard` 61) · `routes/LabTracking` 75 · `hooks/useLabCases` 116 · `hooks/useLabelModal` 35 · `config/labStages` 61 — 3,402 LOC — plus their CSS modules, `PrintQueueContext` and `PrintQueueIndicator` (the queue the label dialog prints). Cross-checks the surface forced: `lab-case.contract.ts`, `lab-case-service.ts` + `remake-guard.ts` + `lab-case-queries.ts#updateLabCaseMeta`; `template.contract.ts`, `template.routes.ts`, `template-queries.ts`, `receipt-service.ts` (`renderTemplate`, `resolveTemplateFile`); `aligner-label.routes.ts`, `aligner-label-generator.ts`, `pdf-assets.ts#resolveLogoPath`; `branding.routes.ts` + `clinic-branding.ts` (the configured logo); the route table's gates; the `options` rows behind labels (`LatestLabelPos`, `LabelTemplatePath`).

**Runtime: the throwaway install** (as F19/RE2), with two lab cases, aligner sets from RE1's fixtures, and four probe templates. Playwright as `Admin`, API probes as `Admin` and `doc1` (clinical). The template probes wrote into the repo's own `data/templates/` (the dev server's working directory) — every probe file was deleted afterwards and the three tracked templates are untouched. **Live database: read-only SQL.** No screenshots.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F20-1 | [BUG] | 🔴 | `template.routes.ts` (`GET /:id/html`, `POST /:id/save-html`) · `template.contract.ts#updateTemplate` | **Any staff user could read or overwrite any file the server can reach.** `PUT /api/templates/:id` accepted `template_file_path` from every role (`authorize(CLINICAL_ROLES)`), and the designer's read and save joined it to the app directory with no containment (`path.join(process.cwd(), stored)`; only the receipt renderer had the check). Runtime as `doc1` (clinical): pointed a template at `package.json` and `GET …/html` returned it; pointed it outside the repo and `save-html` wrote the file there. In production that is `.env` (database, session and API secrets) for reading, and `dist-server/` for writing, on a service that runs as Administrator. **Fixed on the spot** (the 🔴 rule) — see RE3 | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-2 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `template.routes.ts#save-html` | **Saving one template could overwrite another's design.** The file name was the template name's Latin slug: "Receipt A" and "receipt a" both wrote `receipt-a.html`, and every Arabic-named template slugged to `''` and shared `data/templates/.html`. Runtime: four templates, two files; each save replaced the other template's HTML. Naming templates in Arabic is the normal case for a clinic here. Live: three templates with distinct files, so nothing is damaged yet. **Fix:** one file per template id; a template found sharing a file gets its own on its next save | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `pdf-assets.ts#resolveLogoPath` (`DEFAULT_LOGO_PATH = public/shawan logon.png`) · `aligner-label.routes.ts` · `LabelPreviewModal` "Include Logo" | **Every install prints this clinic's logo on its aligner labels.** The route never passes a logo, so `resolveLogoPath` falls back to the bundled `shawan logon.png` — the logo Settings → General lets each center replace in the header is not used. RE1's hand-down ("the print queue's logo choice"). **Fix:** the label logo is the configured clinic logo (`CLINIC_LOGO`), or none; owner call for this clinic, which has no `CLINIC_LOGO` set | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `LabelPreviewModal.tsx#handleGenerate` | **A blocked PDF window is reported as a successful print, and the queue is cleared.** The PDF opens with `window.open` after the request returns, which is no longer the click's user gesture; a popup blocker returns `null`, and the code doesn't look. Runtime (`window.open` → `null`): the dialog closed and toasted "Generated 8 labels on 1 page(s)"; in queue mode `onQueuePrintSuccess` empties the queue, so the labels are gone. Also: a failure toasts the raw JSON body (runtime: `Failed to generate labels: {"success":false,"error":…}`), and the request has no timeout | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `LabelPreviewModal.tsx` (`startingPosition` seeded to 1) · `aligner-label.routes.ts` (`X-Next-position`) · option `LatestLabelPos` | **The label sheet position is forgotten between prints.** Every print starts at position 1; the server returns `X-Next-position` and the client drops it. The legacy option `LatestLabelPos` (live: 4) held exactly this and nothing reads it now. On a part-used OL291 sheet the user must remember where the last print ended, or the labels print onto spots already peeled off. Live: 82 batches manufactured in the last 90 days. **Fix:** store the next position after a successful print and pre-select it | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `LabCaseModal.tsx:415-437` · `lab-case-service.ts#remakeLabCase` | **One click delivers a lab case from any stage, and a delivered case can't be sent back from the screen.** *Deliver* sits beside *Advance* on every open case with no confirmation (runtime: offered on a Sent-to-Lab case; the API advances `sent_to_lab → delivered` in one step). Delivered cases leave the board, and the modal hides *Send back (remake)* on them although the service documents and accepts the post-delivery remake (runtime: remake from delivered → 200). A misclick has no way back. **Fix:** confirm a delivery from before *Ready to Cement*; offer *Send back* on delivered cases | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-7 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `LabCaseModal.tsx` · `LabCaseCard.tsx#submitQuick` | **Lab case modal leftovers.** (a) The *Send back to* list offers every stage, including the current one and later ones, which the server refuses (runtime: 400 "returnToStatus must be an earlier stage"). (b) A note can't be cleared: an emptied note is sent as `undefined`, which the server reads as "unchanged" (it would clear on `''` — runtime 200). (c) Advance and the new due date are two requests; when the second fails the toast says "Failed to advance case" although the case moved. (d) A double click on *Advance* or the card's *Confirm* sends two requests; the second is refused and toasts an error after the success (runtime: 200 + 400). (e) A failed case read has no *Retry*; due dates print raw | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-8 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `LabFilters.tsx:84-100` · `LabTracking.tsx:199-204` · `LabCaseCard.tsx:236` | **Board leftovers.** (a) Typing in the search and then changing another filter within 300 ms throws the filter change away: the debounce fires with the filters it captured (runtime: stage picked, URL kept only `q=zz`). (b) A bad URL parameter (`?status=bogus`) leaves "Failed to load lab cases" up for good (the server 400s it). (c) Card bodies can't be reached by keyboard (`tabIndex -1`, the lint rule disabled), so a keyboard user can't open a case from the board | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-9 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `TemplateDesigner.tsx:126-130` · `GrapesJSEditor.tsx:221-232` · `receipt-service.ts#renderTemplate` · `CreateTemplateModal.tsx` · `TemplateManagement.tsx:82` | **Template designer leftovers.** (a) Leaving by anything but the designer's own *Back* (a header link, the browser's back) drops unsaved design work silently, while *Back* always asks, changed or not. (b) The palette's first block, *Clinic Header*, inserts `{{clinic.Name}}`, `{{clinic.Location}}`, `{{clinic.Phone1}} \| {{clinic.Phone2}}`, and receipts have no `clinic` data, so it prints an empty header with a stray "\|". (c) The title reads "Receipt Template Designer" for every document type. (d) *Create* has no in-flight guard (a double submit makes two templates). (e) The client sent `created_by: 'user'` / `modified_by: 'user'`, so every template read "Created by: user" (the server now records the session user — RE3) | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-10 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `LabelPreviewModal.tsx` | **Label dialog consistency.** Both modes hand-roll their title bar instead of `<ModalHeader>`, and the icon-only close button has no accessible name (runtime: `aria-label` null). Neither mode guards unsaved edits: Escape throws away an edited queue. The "Dr." prefixing is re-implemented (twice, with the queue context) beside `doctorLabel()`, and with no doctor the field holds "Dr. ", which passes validation | ✅ RE3 |
| FE-F20-11 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `LabelPreviewModal.tsx:36-38,107-114` · `TemplateManagement.tsx:32-35` · `TemplateDesigner.tsx:20-24,50-52` · `GrapesJSEditor.tsx:16-20` · `CreateTemplateModal.tsx:10-38` · `TemplateManagement.module.css` | **Shadowing types, dead props and casts.** `interface Set` in the label dialog shadows the global `Set` the same file constructs; `onGenerate` and `isGenerating` are never passed. The template screens re-declare `Template`, `DocumentType` and `TemplateSubmissionData` by hand and cast the contract-typed reads into them (`as Template[]`, `as Template \| null`). `useLabelModal`'s manual `useCallback`s; three CSS classes with no reader (`.modalDialogLarge`, `.previewContainer`, `.previewLoading`) | ✅ RE3 |

**Owner call:** FE-F20-3 — which logo the labels carry (decided at the start of RE3: the Settings logo, or none).

**Found along the way, not F20's to fix (owner/infra):**
- Template files live in the application folder (`<app>/data/templates`, i.e. `C:\ShwNodApp\data\templates` in production), not on the clinic data volume. A center's designed receipts sit beside the code, and editing a seeded default rewrites a git-tracked file in the deployment. Moving them under `MACHINE_PATH` is a data-location change for the owner to schedule.
- The seeded templates carry this clinic's name (`Shwan Orthodontics Default Receipt`, `shwan-orthodontics-*.html`) — the identity literal the demo-seed work already reported.
- `LAB_CASE_EVENT_TYPES` includes `note`, which nothing writes (a note edit leaves no timeline row). Backend, 🔵.

## F19 · Stand inventory & POS · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: `components/stand/**` (19 files, 3,449 LOC: `ItemFormModal` 714, `POSCheckout` 317, `SaleDetailModal` 213, `CategoryManagerModal` 191, `StockAdjustModal` 180, `ItemTable` 178, `RestockModal` 175, `POSCart` 171, `POSItemSearch` 169, `ItemFilters` 146, `SalesHistoryTable` 146, `SalesTrendChart` 143, `StockMovementsModal` 133, `TopItemsChart` 129, `ExpiringItemsPanel` 123, `StandKPICards` 106, `DeleteItemModal` 85, `BarcodeInput` 68, `LowStockPanel` 66) · `routes/Stand*` 778 (`StandInventory` 235, `StandPOS` 171, `StandSalesHistory` 160, `Stand` 120, `StandReports` 92) · `hooks/useStand` 432 — 4,659 LOC — plus their 22 CSS modules. Cross-checks the surface forced: the whole `stand.contract.ts`; the server behind every read and write (`stand.routes.ts`, `StandService.ts`, `stand-queries.ts`); the stand tables' constraints and indexes in the baseline (`ux_standitems_barcode`/`_sku`, `stand_categories_categoryname_key`); `GET /api/patients/search` and its `q` clause (`patient-search-queries.ts`); `PatientSearchCombobox` + `TransferWorkModal` (the shared patient picker); `roleCaps`; the `qk.stand.*` keys and their factories; the Dashboard's FINANCE card filter.

**Runtime: a throwaway install** (the RE1 PostgreSQL 18 data dir, copied; every `.env` key blanked; sinks, WhatsApp and LocalSend off), with stand fixtures written through the API: three categories, six items (an expired one, one expiring in 10 days, one low, one out, one deactivated), a restock, a handful of sales. Playwright as `Admin` and as `desk1` (front desk), plus API probes. **Live database: read-only SQL** (`BEGIN READ ONLY … ROLLBACK`) for the facts below. No screenshots; the probes live in the session scratchpad.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F19-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `StandReports.tsx:26-30,68-71` | **The Reports page's "Net Profit" counts the stock's cost twice, so it reads as a loss every month the stand restocks.** Gross Profit is already revenue minus the cost of what was sold (`total_profit` = `total_amount − qty × cost_price`). The page then subtracts every restock bought in the range (`purchases.totalPurchases`) again. Live: June 13,405 − 139,670 = **−126,265 IQD**; July 39,355 − 93,275 = **−53,920**; August 58,425 − 114,600 = **−56,175**, while the stand made 13,405 / 39,355 / 58,425 on what it sold. Runtime (throwaway): Revenue 20,000 · Gross Profit 8,000 · Purchases 16,000 · **Net Profit −8,000**. **Fix:** owner call on what the fourth card means; the subtraction as written is not a profit | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-2 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `stand-queries.ts:451,870` · `ExpiringItemsPanel.tsx:44,54` · `POSItemSearch.tsx` · `StandPOS.tsx#addToCart` · `StandService.validateAndCreateSale` | **An item drops out of every expiry warning the day it expires, and the till sells it without a word.** The *Expiring Soon* list and the KPI count only `expiry_date >= current_date`, so the panel's own "Expired Nd ago" state can never render. The POS search shows price and stock but no expiry, the barcode path adds silently, and the sale service doesn't look at the date. Runtime: Gel C (expired 3 days ago, 5 in stock) is missing from the list and the KPI (1 = Paste E only); the POS search offers "Gel C · 6,000 IQD · 4 in stock"; `POST /api/stand/sales` sells it (201). Live: 10 of 17 items carry an expiry, none past it today, so nothing has been sold expired yet. **Fix:** list and count expired-but-active stock (first, red); the POS shows the expiry and stops on an expired item; owner call: refuse or warn | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `POSCheckout.tsx:57-66,244` · `patient-search-queries.ts:117-128` | **"Link to Patient" can't find a patient by name.** The box says "Search patient name…" but sends `/api/patients/search?q=`, and `q` matches phone and ID only. Runtime: «أحمد» and "Ah" → 0 results; "07" (a phone prefix) → 2. Live: **0 of 60 sales** carry a `person_id`. **Fix:** reuse `PatientSearchCombobox` (name, or phone/ID when the term starts with a digit) like `TransferWorkModal` | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `POSCheckout.tsx:34-45,91-103` · `StandPOS.tsx:222-224` | **The next customer's sale is linked to the previous customer and carries their note.** After a sale only the amount resets (it is keyed to the cart total); the linked patient, the note and the payment method stay. Runtime: sale 1 linked to #13 with a note → *New Sale* → sale 2 posted `personId: 13` and the same `customerNote`. Latent only because FE-F19-3 makes linking by name impossible. **Fix:** remount the checkout after each sale | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `StandPOS.tsx:207-228,255-259` · `POSCheckout.tsx:153-161` | **A double click on *Confirm Sale* sells twice.** The button's `disabled` waits for `isPending` to reach React, which lands after the second click. Runtime: one double click → two POSTs → sales 13 and 15, 0.6 ms apart, stock taken twice, revenue doubled. FE-F17-10's family. **Fix:** an in-flight ref in the confirm handler | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `BarcodeInput.tsx:24-28` · `StandPOS.tsx:263-288` · `POSCheckout.tsx#handleAmountChange` | **A scan goes wherever the focus happens to be.** The barcode box takes focus once, on mount. After a sale the dialog hands focus back to the disabled *Confirm* button, so it lands on `<body>` and the next scan is lost (runtime: cart still empty after a blind "2222⏎"). With the cursor in *Amount Paid*, a scan types its digits into the amount, which keeps digits only (runtime: "2,000" → "2,0005555"; the Enter is ignored), so the till shows about 20 million change. The item search swallows a scan the same way. **Fix:** a page-level listener that recognises a scanner burst (fast keystrokes ending in Enter) outside the barcode box, routes it to the lookup and restores the field; refocus the barcode box after an add and after a sale | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-7 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `ItemTable.tsx:382-402` · `StandInventory.tsx:155-160` · `SalesHistoryTable.tsx:282-292` · `SaleDetailModal.tsx:189-198` · `stand.routes.ts` (`ADMIN_ROLES` on delete/adjust/void/categories) | **Front desk is offered four controls the server refuses.** Deleting an item, adjusting stock, the category manager's add/rename/deactivate and voiding a sale are admin-only on the server; the UI shows them to every role that can open the Stand. Runtime as `desk1`: *Delete* ×5, *Adjust* ×5, *Manage Categories*, *Void* ×5 on screen; each write → 403 "Insufficient permissions". **Fix:** `roleCaps().adminWrites` | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-8 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `ItemTable.tsx` · `stand.contract.ts#updateItem` · `stand.routes.ts#handleStandError` · baseline `ux_standitems_barcode`/`ux_standitems_sku`/`stand_categories_categoryname_key` | **A deleted item can't come back, and its barcode can't be reused.** *Delete* only deactivates, the unique barcode/SKU indexes include inactive rows, and nothing can reactivate a row (no field in the form, none in the contract). So re-adding the product fails, and so does any duplicate barcode, SKU or category name: as a 500 "Failed to create stand item" that doesn't say which item has it. Runtime: re-add barcode 6666 (held by the deleted "Old F") → 500; duplicate 1111 → 500; category "gel" (exists) → 500. *Show inactive* lists the deleted row with *Edit / Restock / Adjust / Delete* and no *Reactivate*. Live: one deactivated item holds a barcode (`ZZZ_TEST_ITEM`). **Fix:** 409 naming the item (or category) that owns the value; a *Reactivate* action on inactive rows (`isActive` on `updateItem`); re-adding a deactivated category name reactivates it | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-9 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `RestockModal.tsx:35-45,140-153` · `stand-queries.ts#restockItem` · `StandService.validateAndCreateSale` | **A restock at a new price never reaches the profit figures.** The restock dialog pre-fills *Unit Cost* from the item's cost and records what's typed on the movement only; `cost_price` stays, and every later sale books its cost from `cost_price`. Live: 4 restocks at another cost (TePe Implant Ortho Brush, cost 3,535, restocked twice at 4,000; BEA Interdental Brush, cost 5,750, restocked at 4,800), so TePe's profit is overstated by 465 a unit and BEA's understated by 950. **Fix:** owner call on the cost rule (last cost, weighted average, or unchanged), and the dialog says which | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-10 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `ItemFormModal.tsx:351-359,662-668` · `stand.contract.ts#createItem/updateItem` (`expiryDate: z.string()`) | **A non-ISO expiry date (from the AI scan, or any client) is a 500.** Gemini is asked for YYYY-MM-DD but packaging often prints "05/2027"; the scan writes whatever comes back into state, the date input shows blank (an invalid value), and Save sends it. Runtime: `expiryDate: "05/2027"` → 500, `"2027-05"` → 500. **Fix:** `optionalDateString` in the contract; the scan normalises "YYYY-MM" to the month's last day and drops anything else with a note | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-11 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `StandSalesHistory.tsx:41,112-118` · `SaleDetailModal.tsx:73-77` · `Stand.tsx:18-24` · `StandKPICards.tsx:33-35` | **Failed reads look like loading or like nothing.** A failed sale-detail read keeps "Loading sale details…" up for good (runtime: 500 → still spinning after the retries). A failed KPI read renders no cards and no message (the page toasts only the two panel errors). **Fix:** an error with *Retry* in the dialog; the KPI failure toasts like the panels | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-12 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `ItemTable.tsx:264-278` · `SalesTrendChart.tsx:113-116` · `ExpiringItemsPanel.tsx:233` · `SalesHistoryTable.tsx:181-189` · `SaleDetailModal.tsx:24-32` · `StandReports.tsx:19-23` · `stand.routes.ts:572-616` | **Date handling leftovers.** `ItemTable` and the trend chart parse a `date` column with `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')` (UTC midnight): an item turns "expired" at 03:00 on its last good day in Baghdad, and a day early west of UTC, where the chart's labels also shift a day. `ExpiringItemsPanel` freezes "today" at mount. Two hand-rolled `formatDateTime` copies and one `formatDate` where `utils/formatters` has `formatLocaleDateTime`/`formatLocaleDate`. Clearing a report date shows four "0 IQD" cards instead of asking for a range, and the report endpoints take unvalidated dates (`startDate=abc` → 500). **Fix:** `parseLocalDate`, the shared formatters, a computed "today", a range prompt, and `validate({ query })` with `dateString` on both report routes | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-13 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `StandInventory.tsx:8-23,56-70` · `ItemFormModal.tsx:196-203,389-416` | **The item form comes back by itself, empty.** The page persists `{ isOpen: true }` to `sessionStorage` while the form is open and removes it only when the form closes. Leave the page with the form open (the back button) and the form's own draft is dropped on unmount while the page's flag stays, so an empty *Add New Item* opens on every later visit and reload (runtime). Separately, *Update Item* sends every field from the row the form was seeded with, so it rewrites fields changed elsewhere in the meantime (runtime PUT: all ten fields for a notes-only edit). FE-F17-1's family. **Fix:** clear the flag on unmount (keep the reload restore); send only the changed fields | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-14 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `StandPOS.tsx:149-201` · `POSCart.tsx:359-367,440-449` · `stand.contract.ts#createSale` | **Smaller POS defects.** (a) The cart keeps each item's price and stock from when it was added; a price edited in between is refused as "Underpaid: total is X" on a total the screen never showed. (b) The contract takes a fractional `quantity` (`z.coerce.number()`) into an integer column (runtime: 1.5 → 500). (c) The stock warnings are toasted inside a `setCart` updater, a side effect React runs twice in dev. (d) The quantity box can't be emptied to type a new number (an empty value is ignored, so the old digit stays). **Fix:** take price and stock from the fresh item list at checkout, refuse a server price mismatch with the new total, `int().positive()` in the contract, toasts outside the updater, a draft quantity committed on blur | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-15 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `POSItemSearch.tsx:91-163` · `StandSalesHistory.tsx:120-157` · `SalesTrendChart.tsx`/`TopItemsChart.tsx` · `StockMovementsModal.tsx:219-227` | **Accessibility and consistency.** The item search is a `combobox` without arrow-key movement or `aria-activedescendant`, so a keyboard user tabs through every option. The void dialog hand-rolls its title instead of `<ModalHeader>`. Both charts repeat the panel's heading as a chart title in Chart.js's default grey (low contrast in dark mode). The movement badges style two types that don't exist (`purchase`, `return`) and leave `void` and `waste` grey, and every negative adjustment is labelled "waste" (live: 15 "waste" rows, reasons "mistake" ×5, "MIS" ×3, "change" ×2). **Fix:** arrow keys + active descendant; `ModalHeader`; drop the canvas titles; a badge per real type and "Removed" for a negative adjustment in the UI | ✅ RE2 |
| FE-F19-16 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `useStand.ts:65-99,120-146` · `POSCart.tsx:5-15` · `POSCheckout.tsx:7-10,65` · `POSItemSearch.tsx:37` · `ItemTable.module.css` `.noteCell` · 9 × `React.memo` | **Parallel types, no-op casts and memo wrappers.** `StandItemCreateData` and `SaleCreateData` re-type the contract's `CreateItemBody`/`CreateSaleBody` by hand; `POSCart` declares its own `StandItem`; `PatientSearchResult` + `as` and `data as StandItem[]` cast to what the contract already infers. `useStandItemByBarcode`'s `loading` and `error` are never read. Nine components are wrapped in `React.memo` and the POS pieces carry manual `useCallback`s under the React Compiler. `.noteCell` has no reader. `loadModalState()` reads `sessionStorage` on every render | ✅ RE2 |

**Owner calls:** FE-F19-1 (what the fourth report card shows), FE-F19-2 (refuse or warn on an expired item at the till), FE-F19-9 (which cost a restock leaves on the item).

**Found during RE2 and folded into FE-F19-15:** the Stand page wrapped each panel (which carries its own titled card) in a second card repeating the same heading, so "Low Stock Items" and "Expiring Soon" each appeared twice.

**FE-F19-3 and FE-F19-4 are one remediation.** Fixing the search makes the sticky link live, so both go in together.

## F18 · Aligner lists, Archform, announcements · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: `pages/aligner/ArchformMatcher` 784 · `AllSetsList` 572 · `Announcements` 419 · `components/react/AlignerDoctorsSettings` 313 · `PatientsList` 218 · `SearchPatient` 129 · `DoctorsList` 106 · `layouts/AlignerLayout` 50 · `components/react/AlignerModeToggle` 53 — 2,644 LOC — plus their eight CSS modules (3,110). Cross-checks the surface forced: `aligner.contract.ts` (the archform, doctor, patient-list and all-sets schemas) and the whole `announcement.contract.ts`; the server behind every read and write (`aligner-archform.routes` + `aligner-archform-queries` + `services/archform/archform-db.ts`; `aligner-doctor.routes` + `AlignerDoctorService`; the list handlers in `aligner.routes` and `aligner-patient-queries`; `getAllAlignerSets`; `announcement-queries`); the FKs onto `aligner_doctors`; the option write path (`PUT /api/options/:optionName` → `updateOption`) and `services/setup/install-setup.ts`; the portal's `AnnouncementBanner` (how `link_url` is rendered); `working-files.service#resolveWorkingFile` (the thumbnail route); `qk.aligner.*`/`qk.announcements.*` and their factories; the route table's gates; `docs/archform-matcher.md`; and the hand-downs to F18.

**Runtime: read-only against the live database**, as in F16/F17: a dev server on the live DB with the three CDC sinks, WhatsApp auto-init, LocalSend and the Cloudflare reconcile off by env. Playwright aborted every non-GET; a few writes were answered by the probe with a success body (adding a doctor) so the client's success path could be watched with nothing written; three GETs were rewritten in flight (a manual announcement added to the list; Archform patients mocked, since the Archform file isn't reachable from this box; failures and delays injected). Plus `BEGIN READ ONLY` SQL and a scan of the photo volume's file names. No screenshots; the probes live in the session scratchpad.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F18-1 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `Announcements.tsx:143` · `shared/validation.ts:179` (`timestampString`) | **Every save of an edited announcement moves its expiry one day earlier.** The form seeds the date box with `a.expires_at.slice(0, 10)`, but a timestamp reaches the browser as an ISO **UTC** string (the server's `Date` goes through `toISOString`). An expiry stored as local midnight 10 Oct arrives as `2026-10-09T21:00:00.000Z` in Baghdad. Runtime (a manual row added to the list in flight): the card reads "10/10/2026, 12:00:00 AM", the edit form's date box reads **2026-10-09**, and *Save changes* with nothing touched sends `"expiresAt":"2026-10-09"`. Every edit (to fix a typo, say) takes another day off, so the doctors lose the notice early. Any install east of UTC is affected (all of Iraq and the Gulf). No manual announcement exists on the live DB yet (all 143 rows are auto), so no data is affected. **Fix:** seed with the local date (`toLocalDateString(new Date(a.expires_at))` or `utils/date#toDateOnly`) | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `AllSetsList.tsx:111-114,163-166,314,317,385,427` | **The All Sets "No Next" filter is mostly patients who aren't waiting for anything.** `isWaitingForNextBatch` is "delivered, and no made-but-undelivered batch", which also matches a set whose **final** batch is already delivered (the row's badge shows "—") and a set whose next batch exists but isn't made yet (badge "Pending"). Live: **No Next (47)**, of which 37 are final-delivered, 4 pending manufacture, and **6** really have no next batch, which is what the legend's "Not Created (6)" counts. The code comment says the filter should include only "patients who are waiting for next batch". The legend's *Final* also breaks the comment above it ("Legend counts derive from the same state machine as the badges"): it counts `is_last` flags (39), while the badge's final state needs the last batch delivered (37), so the legend adds up to 69 for 67 rows. **Fix:** filter and count on `getNextBatchState(s) === 'not_created'` (or rename the toggle to what it shows); Final = `stateCounts.final` | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `ArchformMatcher.tsx:116-153,451-456,550-566` · `aligner-archform-queries.ts:64-83` · `PatientSets` (Archform button, F17's file) | **A set linked to an Archform patient that no longer exists can't be unlinked or re-linked from any screen.** The link is a bare integer, `aligner_sets.archform_id`. Only a delete made from this page clears it (`clearArchformIdByPatientId`). A patient deleted or merged inside Archform itself leaves the set linked to a dead id. The matcher builds its rows from the Archform list, so that set never appears as a row (no *Unmatch*), and it shows as a **disabled** option in every row's dropdown. Runtime (Archform list mocked without the linked ids, real matches list): all 16 linked sets were unreachable. A row's dropdown showed "اية هوكر - Set 1 - Dr. Hoger Mustafa" with `aria-disabled=true`, and the stats line read "0 matched · 149 aligner sets", so nothing says 16 links exist. The set card's *Archform* button keeps opening the dead id. The server doesn't check either: `updateArchformId` overwrites the set's link without looking, and nothing stops two sets carrying the same Archform id. So a page loaded before a colleague's match can silently replace that match, or link one Archform patient to two sets (the map keeps the last one, and the other becomes unreachable the same way). How many of the 16 live links point at dead ids is unknown: the Archform file isn't reachable from this box. **Fix:** a "linked to a missing Archform patient" section listing the sets whose id isn't in the Archform list, each with *Unmatch*; server-side, refuse (409) a match onto a set that already has a different id, or an id already linked, or add a partial unique index on `archform_id` on both DBs | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `ArchformMatcher.tsx:420-436` · `archform-db.ts:53-55,77-99` · `options-queries.ts:83-96` · `services/setup/install-setup.ts` · `AlignerModeToggle.tsx:19` | **Archform defaults to this clinic's machine, and a new install can't point it anywhere else.** With no `ARCHFORM_DB_PATH` row, the server opens `\\workPC\Archform\__ARCHFORMDB`. The unavailable screen says "update the path in General Settings (ARCHFORM_DB_PATH)", but General Settings lists only rows that already exist, `PUT /api/options/:optionName` is update-only (404 "Option not found" on a missing row), and `db:setup` doesn't seed the row. So on a fresh install the advice leads nowhere, and only SQL can fix it. The *Archform Match* tab is in the aligner nav on every install, including centers that don't use Archform. Runtime: this box resolves the live row `\\WORK_PC\Archform\__ARCHFORMDB` to `/mnt/work_pc/…`, and the tab opens on "Archform Database Unavailable". FE-F17-7's family (this clinic's identity as a default). **Fix:** `db:setup` seeds an empty row (or the unavailable screen offers a path field that upserts); drop the clinic-specific default; owner call: hide the tab until a path is configured | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-5 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `DoctorsList.tsx:90` · `PatientsList.tsx:165` | **A stray "0" on every doctor card and patient card.** `{x.UnreadDoctorNotes && x.UnreadDoctorNotes > 0 && (…)}` renders the number `0` when the count is 0, and the server always sends a number. Runtime: all 9 doctor cards start with a text node "0", and so do all 35 cards on Dr. Shwan's patient list. Same shape as FE-F17-8's stray "0". **Fix:** `x.UnreadDoctorNotes > 0 ? (…) : null` | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-6 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `PatientsList.tsx:31-52,90-117,141-144` · `SearchPatient.tsx:29-34,116-123` · `DoctorsList.tsx:14-45` | **A failed read looks like an empty result, and a deep link names "Dr. undefined".** Runtime: with `/api/aligner/patients/all` failing, the All Doctors page reads "0 patients · No patients with aligner sets", with no toast and no retry. With the search failing, the page goes back to its "Quick Search / Enter a patient name…" intro with no message. With the doctors read delayed 2.5 s on a direct load of `/aligner/doctor/1`, the header reads **"Dr. undefined's Patients"** until it lands (the loading gate covers only the patients read). DoctorsList on failure shows a toast, then "0 doctors" and the All Doctors card. AllSetsList already handles this right ("a failed load … must not masquerade as an empty clinic"). **Fix:** AllSetsList's error state with *Retry* on the three pages; the header waits for the doctor, or reads "Patients" until it's known | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-7 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `SearchPatient.tsx:71,121` · `aligner-patient-queries.ts:154-200` | **Quick Search promises "name, phone, or patient ID", but the server searches names and phone only.** Runtime: "6305" (person 6305 owns aligner set 58) → "No aligner patients found"; the work id 10049 finds nothing either. The query also has no `LIMIT`, so a two-letter term returns every match (rendered in full), and `%`/`_` in the term act as wildcards. **Fix:** an exact `person_id` branch when the term is all digits, a `LIMIT` with a "showing first N" note, and escaped wildcards; or change the placeholder | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-8 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `PatientsList.tsx:158,165,173-185,198` · `aligner-patient-queries.ts:22-75` · `working-files.service.ts:96-118` | **Leftovers on the doctor's patient list.** (a) Cards are keyed by `person_id`, but the rows are one per **work**, so a patient with two aligner works (a re-treatment) renders two cards with the same key. There are 0 such patients live today. (b) The All Doctors view never shows the unread banner, because `/api/aligner/patients/all` doesn't select `UnreadDoctorNotes` (the by-doctor read does). (c) F13's hand-down, half closed by RD: the thumbnail now goes through the working-files route, but it still guesses the name `${id}00.i13`, in lower case and from the tp-0 session only. Of the 79 aligner patients, 25 have only `…00.I13` on disk, so on the planned case-sensitive Linux volume those 25 lose their photo. 50 have no tp-0 smile at all; each of those cards costs a 400 "Invalid working file" (no tp 0) or a 404, plus a console error. (d) The phone is shown raw here, while Search formats it with `PhoneDisplay`. **Fix:** key by `workid`; add the unread subquery to `patients/all`; take the thumbnail's real name from the server (per CLAUDE.md's photo-URL convention, e.g. the list query returns it) | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-9 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `AlignerDoctorsSettings.tsx:63-82,155-230` · `ArchformMatcher.tsx:364-384,762-779` · `Announcements.tsx:168-178,291-414` · `ConfirmDialog.tsx` | **Double clicks write twice, and two data-entry modals drop typed work.** Runtime: one double click on *Add Doctor* sent two POSTs, so two doctors are created; the email uniqueness check is check-then-insert and is skipped when no email is given. *Delete* on an Archform patient sent two DELETEs (on the server the second one 404s, which shows an error toast after the success one). *Delete* on an announcement sent two DELETEs. The announcement and doctor modals have no `unsavedGuard`: Escape closed a half-typed form of each with no prompt. FE-F17-10's family. **Fix:** in-flight guards; a `busy` prop on `ConfirmDialog` covers all three confirms; `unsavedGuard={{ watchInput: true }}` on both modals | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-10 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `AlignerDoctorsSettings.tsx:38-39,84-98,201-216,270-282` · `query/keys.ts:130-132` · `AlignerDoctorService.ts:148-172` | **Doctor edits leave the other doctor lists stale, and the form promises things it doesn't do.** Writes invalidate only `qk.aligner.doctorsAdmin()` (`['aligner','doctors-admin']`). That leaves stale `qk.aligner.doctors()` (`['aligner','doctors']`: the Browse-by-Doctor cards, PatientsList's header, the Announcements audience) and `allSets` (doctor names). Runtime: after *Add Doctor* (answered 200 by the probe), Back to `/aligner` sent 0 requests, so the old list stayed up. The delete prompt says "This will affect all their aligner cases", but the server refuses a doctor who has sets ("Cannot delete doctor. They have N aligner set(s)…"); what a delete does remove, unannounced, is the announcements targeted at that doctor and their read receipts (FK CASCADE). "Portal Access: Enabled" means only "has an email". *Logo Path* is a free-text server path with this clinic's placeholder `C:\Aligner_Sets\Labels\logo.png` (F17's hand-down); it is never checked, and per F17's note the label PDF never uses it (F20). **Fix:** invalidate the `['aligner']` prefix (FE-F17-6's `invalidateAlignerLists()`); reword the prompt (refused with sets; removes the doctor's announcements); a neutral placeholder, and the logo question goes to F20 | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-11 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `Announcements.tsx:109-112,344-352,365-384` · `announcement.contract.ts:56-69` · portal `AnnouncementBanner.tsx:107-121` | **Gaps in the announcement form.** (a) *Expires* accepts today or a past date. An expiry is stored as local midnight (the contract: it lives "through the prior day"), so picking today, most likely meaning "today only", saves a row that is already expired; after "Announcement published" it disappears from the default list, which hides expired rows. (b) *Audience* lists all 9 doctors, including the 4 with no email, who have no portal access, so an announcement targeted at one of them is never seen. (c) *Link URL* is free text. The portal treats anything not starting with `/` as an external `<a href>`, so `example.com/form` opens as a relative link inside the portal. `javascript:` is not an injection: the portal is on React 19, which blocks it. **Fix:** `min` = tomorrow and a label that says what the date means (or store end of day); mark or disable doctors without portal access; accept only `/…` or `https?://…` | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-12 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `Announcements.tsx:160-161,261,407` · `GET /api/sync/supabase-status` | **"Published" means "saved locally", not "visible to the doctor".** The plan's question, answered: the page reads and writes only the local DB. A doctor sees the row once the failover sink has forwarded it, and *N read* moves only when the reverse sink brings a receipt home. With either sink disabled, stale or backed up, the toast still says "Announcement published", the delete prompt still says "Doctors will no longer see it", and the card's "0 read" looks like "not read yet". The page shows no sync state, though `/api/sync/supabase-status` already reports each sink's flag, backlog and `stale`. On an install with no doctor portal at all, this page, the doctors' *Portal Access* column and the two auto announcements per batch still exist. **Fix:** a banner from the status endpoint when the failover or reverse sink isn't draining; wording that says "saved, shown to doctors once synced"; owner call: hide portal-only screens when no portal is configured | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-13 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `Announcements.tsx:105` · `AllSetsList.tsx:46-47,144-189` · `PatientsList.tsx:42-79,141-156` · `ArchformMatcher.tsx:145-153,550-566,668-681` · `SearchPatient.tsx` | **The plan's ceiling question: the lists download everything and filter in the browser.** (a) Announcements with *Show expired*: no limit, and nothing purges old rows. Two auto rows are written per batch (143 in under three months here, about 490 B each), so a busy center reaches hundreds of thousands. (b) All Sets: every set ever, inactive and finished included (148 rows, 55.7 KB, about 376 B a row, 81 of them hidden by the default filters); the hidden share grows with the years. (c) The All Doctors patient list: every aligner patient ever, each rendered as a card, and `getFilteredPatients()` runs twice per render on every keystroke. (d) The Archform matcher mounts one react-select per unmatched row and does a linear `alignerSets.find` per row. Measured with 2,000 mocked Archform patients and 3,149 sets (dev build): 5.1 s to the first table and 4.4 s to redraw when the filter is cleared; its dropdown offers every set ever made, finished works included. (e) Quick Search has no `LIMIT` (FE-F18-7). The all-sets query itself was already rewritten for this ceiling (one grouped pass), so the server side is fine. **Fix:** server-side default filters (active, unfinished, not expired) with an opt-in for history; paginate announcements (or purge auto rows past expiry); one picker for the Archform row being matched, and a `Map` instead of `find` | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-14 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `ArchformMatcher.tsx:21-49,250-268,473-479,580-615` · `PatientsList.tsx:129-136` · `SearchPatient.tsx:68-75` · `AllSetsList.tsx:518-526` · `AlignerLayout.tsx:21-31` · the "Admin" sites | **Accessibility and consistency leftovers.** The matcher's sortable headers are clickable `<th>`s with no keyboard handler or `aria-sort` (AllSetsList has both). Its inline name edit has placeholder-only inputs, and Enter doesn't save and Escape doesn't cancel. Two icon-only clear buttons have no label (`ArchformMatcher:474`, `PatientsList:130`). The search box has no label. All Sets rows are focusable `<tr>`s that answer Enter but not Space and have no role. The layout marks *Browse by Doctor* `aria-current="page"` on the patient's sets page and on Announcements, even when the user came from Search or All Sets. The matcher's react-select `styles` carry literal colours and `zIndex: 9999`; dark theme still renders right because the global sheet wins (runtime, dark: options `rgb(34, 41, 55)`). The "Dr." prefix with its `=== 'Admin'` exception is written out by hand in six places (`SetFormDrawer`, `AlignerDoctorsSettings`, `DoctorsList`, `PatientsList`, `AllSetsList` ×2) and missing from the matcher's set labels and its auto-rename (`Dr_Admin_1`). **Fix:** copy AllSetsList's header; labels; `aria-current` only on the four list routes; tokens; one `doctorLabel()` helper | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F18-15 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `ArchformMatcher.tsx:68-69,116-153` · `SearchPatient.tsx:30` · `AlignerDoctorsSettings.tsx:19-23,79,95` · `aligner.types.ts:117-120` · `aligner.contract.ts:386` · `docs/archform-matcher.md` | **No-op casts and stale text.** `as ArchformPatient[]`, `as AlignerSetForMatch[]` and `as AlignerPatient[]` cast to the types the contract already infers: harmless today, but they would hide a drift. A manual `useMemo` with an `exhaustive-deps` disable runs under the React Compiler. `getArchformToSetMap` and `getMatchedSetIds` build the same map twice. `AlignerDoctorsSettings` takes an unused `onChangesUpdate` and calls `console.error` twice. `aligner.types.ts` has an empty "ARCHFORM MATCHING TYPES" section. The contract says `/archform/matches` returns "sets carrying archform_id", but it returns every set. `docs/archform-matcher.md` says dev and prod both run on Windows and calls the WSL mount legacy (CLAUDE.md: dev is WSL, and this box resolves the share to `/mnt/work_pc/…`) | ✅ RE1 |

**FE-F18-3 and FE-F18-4 are one remediation** on the Archform matcher: a configured path (seeded row, upserting field, no clinic default), the dangling-link section, and the 409 on a conflicting match.

**FE-F18-10 rides on FE-F17-6.** `invalidateAlignerLists()` (the `['aligner']` prefix) covers the doctor writes too, so RE writes it once.

**FE-F18-9 rides on FE-F17-10.** The `busy` prop on `ConfirmDialog` closes the double deletes on both sessions' screens.

**Owner calls:** FE-F18-4, show the *Archform Match* tab only on installs with a configured path. FE-F18-12, hide the portal-only screens (Announcements, *Portal Access*, the auto announcements) on installs without a doctor portal.

**Found along the way, not F18's to fix:**
- **`docs/archform-matcher.md:54` has the work PC's share credentials in plain text** (`username=Shwan,password=12345`), committed to git. Not frontend. The owner should change that password, if it's still in use, and drop it from the doc.
- `archform-db.ts#updateArchformPatient` reports success for an id that is no longer in Archform (an `UPDATE` of 0 rows). The rename toast says "Patient name updated", and the row then vanishes on refetch. Backend, 🔵.
- `PUT /api/announcements/:id` edits an **auto** announcement too. Only the client hides *Edit* on auto rows (the page header calls them "system-managed"); `updateAnnouncement` has no `auto_event IS NULL` guard. Backend, 🔵.

## F17 · Aligner sets & batches · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: `pages/aligner/PatientSets` 2,264 · `BatchFormDrawer` 854 · `SetFormDrawer` 711 · `pages/aligner/aligner.types` 143 · `hooks/useSetDrawer` 44 + `useBatchDrawer` 52 · `utils/aligner-labels` 58 · `utils/batchLastFlag` 49 (+ its test) · `pages/aligner/AlignerPhotoViewer` 91 — 4,266 LOC — plus `PatientSets.module.css` (288) and `AlignerPhotoViewer.module.css` (134), and the rules in `css/components/aligner-common.css` that the three screens' global classes resolve to. Cross-checks the surface forced: the whole `aligner.contract.ts` (697); every server route the page calls and what it does underneath (`aligner.routes` sets/payments, `aligner-batch.routes`, `aligner-note.routes`, `aligner-file.routes`; `updateAlignerSet`, `deleteSetWithBatches`, `createBatch`/`updateBatch`/`deleteBatch`/`updateBatchStatus`, the note create and toggle-read paths, the batch error map); the route table's role gates; the FKs onto `aligner_sets`/`aligner_batches`/`aligner_notes`; the portal's write paths (`aligner-portal-external/src/lib/api.ts`: `updateBatchDays`, notes, activity flags); `PortalActivityBell`'s navigation; `PrintQueueContext.addToQueue`; `alignerPatientWorkLoader`; F2's invalidation map; and the earlier sessions' hand-downs to F17.

**Runtime: read-only against the live database.** A dev server on the live DB with the three CDC sinks, WhatsApp auto-init, LocalSend and the Cloudflare reconcile off by env. Playwright aborted every non-GET, except a few it answered itself with a success body (manufacture, deliver) so the client's success path could be watched with nothing written; one GET was rewritten in flight (a set's cost set to 0). Plus `BEGIN READ ONLY` SQL. No screenshots; the probes live in the session scratchpad.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F17-1 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `BatchFormDrawer.tsx:62-74,275-292` · `aligner.contract.ts:540-566` · `aligner-batch-queries.ts:323-466` · `PatientSets.tsx:779-807,1057-1085,1109-1141` · `aligner-set-queries.ts:604-652` | **Edits send a stale copy of the row, so a staff save can silently undo the doctor's portal change or a colleague's.** (a) The batch edit is a full replace (the contract says so: an omitted field is written as 0/NULL), seeded from the batch list as it was when the page loaded it. The doctor can change a batch's `days` from the portal (`updateBatchDays`, reverse-synced home). A later edit of that batch from a page loaded before the change (to tick *Last batch*, fix a note) writes the old days back, logs a `DaysChanged` flag ("days changed from 14 to 10"), and forward-syncs the revert to the doctor. FE-F17-2 makes the stale page the likely case. (b) The three inline edits (Set URL, PDF URL, Case Video) PUT the **whole cached row**: runtime, 28 keys, including `is_active`, `set_cost`, `days`, `set_sequence`, both counts, `aligner_dr_id`, `notes` and `type`. The contract strips only the 15 it doesn't know. So a link edit reverts any of those fields changed elsewhere since the page loaded. On a set that was active at load but has since been superseded, `is_active: true` re-activates it, and `updateAlignerSet` deactivates the newer set. **Fix:** the inline edits send only their field (`updateSet` is already partial); the batch edit sends only what changed, which needs `updateBatch` made partial, or an `updated_at` check that answers 409. Same shape as RB4's note on the work edit form | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientSets.tsx:164-192,394-421` · `PortalActivityBell.tsx:113-117` | **The page never re-reads what it has already loaded.** (a) Clicking a Portal-activity item for the patient already on screen navigates to the same URL, and nothing refetches. Runtime: 0 requests after the click. So the doctor's new note, days change or upload that the bell announced doesn't appear, and the note isn't marked read. (b) Batches, notes and photos live in component state keyed by set id for as long as the page stays mounted, and both `loadAlignerSets` and `toggleBatches` skip any set already loaded (`if (!batchesData[setId])`). A → B through the bell, then Back to A, shows A's batch list and notes from the first visit, with no refresh control on the page. **Fix:** the four reads move to React Query, keyed per work and per set, and the bell invalidates the set it opens (see FE-F17-14). The minimum fix is to refetch on every navigation (`useLocation().key`) and drop the skip-if-loaded checks | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientSets.tsx:148-192,351-398` | **Switching patients without a remount: a failed read leaves the previous patient's sets under the new patient's name.** The page isn't keyed by `workId`, and the sets list is replaced only when a read succeeds. Runtime: work 9867 → bell → work 10049 with `/api/aligner/sets/10049` failing. The header shows the new patient (person 6305), but the page lists **9867's five sets** ("5 sets"; 10049 has four), with one toast. *Open Folder* on the first card points at `\\WORK_PC\Aligner_Sets\1\6305\1`: the old set's doctor and sequence, the new patient's id. *Print Labels* and *Add to queue* would label the previous patient's batches with this patient's name and code. The success path also lacks a guard: the old work's load chain lowers `loading` when it ends, whichever work is on screen, and a late `setAlignerSets` would overwrite the new list. FE-F13-12 / FE-F15-8's family, here with a UI path (the bell). **Fix:** `key={workId}` on the two route elements, an ignore flag in the effect, and clear the list on failure | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientSets.tsx:1379-1493,1938-2008` | **Enter or Space on any button in a set's header toggles the set instead of pressing the button.** The header is a `role="button"` div whose `onKeyDown` calls `preventDefault()` on Enter/Space. The eight buttons inside it (Edit Set, Open Folder, View PDF, Case Video, Archform, Add Payment, Delete, View Batches) get the keydown first; it bubbles up, and the parent cancels the button's activation. Runtime: with *Edit Set* focused, Enter collapsed the set and opened no drawer, and Space expanded it again; a mouse click opened the drawer. The photo and scan-file cards have the same shape around their Delete button, so Enter on Delete opens the viewer or the download instead (read, not run). A screen reader also gets nested controls inside one "button". The header handler dates from `0f66d1b` (2026-06-13) and the cards' from `18422a6` (2026-08-10). ESLint is silent: the config has no nested-interactive rule. **Fix:** make the expander a real `<button>` (the *View Batches* chevron already exists) and the header a plain container; give the cards a separate view button beside the delete button | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `BatchFormDrawer.tsx:330-378` · `useBatchDrawer.ts:40-46` | **Applying a manufacture or delivery date closes the batch drawer and throws away the rest of the form.** The date handlers call `onSave()` to "refresh data", but `onSave` is `handleBatchSaved`, which closes the drawer. Runtime (the PATCH answered 200 by the probe): typed a note, *Set* → *Apply* → the drawer closed, no PUT was sent, and no unsaved-work prompt appeared, because the close goes around `<Modal>`'s guard. Count, note and *Last batch* edits made before setting a date are lost, and both dates can't be set in one sitting. **Fix:** a separate `onRefresh` that reloads without closing, with the drawer reseeded from the reloaded row | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientSets.tsx` (every write) · `query/keys.ts:129-144` · F2's map | **No write on this page refreshes the aligner lists.** None of the page's ~15 writes invalidates a `qk.aligner.*` key. That leaves stale: All Sets (`allSets`: lab status, *Needs Mfg*, *In lab*, *No next*, final batch), a doctor's patient list (`patientsByDoctor`/`allPatients`: sets, active, unread notes), and the doctors list (`doctors`: the unread-note badge). Runtime: read All Sets, opened a patient, *Mark Manufactured* (answered 200 by the probe), went forward to All Sets 9 s after the first read: 0 requests, so the cached list, which still shows the batch as needing manufacture, is what appears. It stays until the 30 s `staleTime` has passed and the screen remounts, or someone presses Refresh. The common flow (list → patient → act → back) fits inside 30 s. FE-F7-8's hand-down answered: the Works page's lifecycle writes (status, delete, transfer) don't refresh these lists either. **Fix:** one `invalidateAlignerLists()` (prefix `['aligner']`), called after every aligner write and the work lifecycle writes; auto-marking notes read (FE-F17-12) refreshes `qk.aligner.doctors` too | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-7 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientSets.tsx:453-457,842-852,2138,2234` · `aligner-set-queries.ts:279,303,408` | **The aligner folder path and the lab's name are this clinic's.** The client builds `\\WORK_PC\Aligner_Sets\{dr}\{person}\{set}` from literals, and the folder-access check expects a folder named `Aligner_Sets`. On any other install, *Open Folder*, the PDF picker's start folder and the copied path all point at a machine that doesn't exist. `aligner_sets.folder_path` exists, the server reads and sends it, and it is NULL on 149 of 149 rows: nothing writes it. Lab notes are labelled "Shwan Lab" on every install (F16's hand-down). **Fix:** a clinic option for the share root (like `PatientsFolder`), served to the browser; retire or use `folder_path`; label lab notes with the clinic name (`CLINIC_NAME`) or a neutral "Lab" | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-8 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `SetFormDrawer.tsx:614-628` · `PatientSets.tsx:1462,1541-1559` · `aligner-set-queries.ts:539-652` | **Clearing a set's cost saves 0, not "no cost", and a 0 renders as a stray "0".** The cost box stores `parseInt(digits) \|\| 0`, so emptying it after typing sends `set_cost: 0` (runtime PUT body: `0`); a box left untouched sends `''` → NULL. A 0-cost set reads "Cost: Not set", has `PaymentStatus` 'Unpaid', and React renders the `0` of `set.set_cost && …` as text: runtime, one bare "0" in the header's button row and one in the info grid. The box also drops a decimal point ("525.50" becomes 52,550) though the column is `numeric(10,2)` (visible while typing), and a cost can be set below what has already been paid, which makes the balance negative; neither side refuses it. **Fix:** an empty box sends `null`; test `set.set_cost != null`; allow two decimals or label the field whole USD; refuse a cost below the amount paid | ✅ RE1 |

The set's payment is refused there (*Add Payment* is disabled with its reason), and the money is taken on the Works page as an invoice with no set id, so the set reads Unpaid forever. Live: three sets (122, 123, 173) on works 10192, 10193 and 10768, which are three of RB1's sub-10,000 "IQD" works handed to the owner (totals 690/570/525 look like USD). Recommendation: correct those works' currency first; then refuse `set_cost` server-side on a non-USD work, and disable the field with the same reason. (b) `PUT /api/aligner/sets` is open to `clinical`, and the drawer shows *Set Cost* to every role, while set payments are finance-only (`caps.writeFinance`) and the owner kept `item_cost` finance-only (FE-F7-7). Should doctors and assistants set aligner prices? | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-10 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `ConfirmDialog.tsx:44-54` · `PatientSets.tsx:534-709,1144-1164,2073-2078` | **A double click writes twice.** `ConfirmDialog` stays open, with Confirm enabled, while the async handler runs. Runtime: one double-click on *Delete Set*'s Confirm sent two DELETEs; the second answers "not found", which shows an error toast after the success. Delete Batch, Delete Note, Mark Manufactured/Delivered and the two undos have the same shape (idempotent on the server, so only the toasts double). *Send Note* has no in-flight guard either: runtime, a double click sent two POSTs, and the doctor gets the same message twice in the portal. **Fix:** in-flight flags, and a `busy` prop on `ConfirmDialog` | ✅ RE1 |

Left unsaid: the doctor's own messages go too, because notes are two-way; the batches' announcements disappear from the doctor's portal (FK cascade); the activity flags go; and the doctor's uploaded photos and scans (R2 `sets/<id>/`) and the Drive PDF are left as orphans that no screen can reach. A set with payments is refused with a clear 409, which is fine. Batch: only "cannot be undone", though the delete also removes the doctor's announcements for it and renumbers the later batches (refused, with a clear message, when a later batch is already made). The photo delete uses `window.confirm`, says "photo" for scan files too, and removes the doctor's upload for good. All three confirms show a blue Confirm, not `isDangerous`. **Fix:** wording, plus `useConfirm` with `danger` | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-12 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `PatientSets.tsx:205-229,255-264,1370-1377` · `aligner-set-queries.ts:338-344` · `routes/api/aligner-note.routes.ts:96-124` | **Doctor notes are marked read just by loading, and the banner doesn't follow.** `loadNotes` marks every unread Doctor note read as soon as its set opens, including the active set's on page load. So opening the patient for any reason clears the doctor's unread state (the doctors list badge, the patient list), whether or not anyone read the notes. The set's "N new updates from doctor" banner comes from the sets read, which isn't repeated, so it stays up after the notes were marked read. And `toggle-read` is a toggle: two tabs opening the set at once flip the note back to unread. Dev's StrictMode does it on every load (runtime: every request twice). **Fix:** an idempotent mark-read (set, not toggle). Either mark notes read only when they are actually viewed, or keep the auto-mark and refresh the sets and `qk.aligner.doctors` after it (owner call: is "opened" the same as "read"?) | ✅ RE1 |
| FE-F17-13 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `SetFormDrawer.tsx:74-91,259-280,450-461` · `BatchFormDrawer.tsx:290-299,322-327,640-760` · `aligner-batch-queries.ts:466` | **Leftovers in the two drawers.** A type outside the three options shows as "Select Type": live set 58 is 'Correction'; runtime, the select's value is `''` while the form holds 'Correction'. A duplicate set sequence hits `uq_setsequence_workid` and comes back as a 500 "Failed to update aligner set" (read, not run: the set routes map no 23505), with no client check (though `allSets` is at hand) and no 409. Deleting the PDF inside the set drawer happens at once, not on Save, so *Cancel* leaves the card's *View PDF* on the deleted file until a reload. In the batch drawer, `formatDisplayDate` parses a date-only string as UTC midnight (a day early west of UTC) and formats it en-GB beside the card's en-US. The date rows' classes (`date-display-with-actions`, `date-edit-inline`, `btn-outline`) have no CSS rule (runtime: block layout, the button under the input).
