Runtime: Tab lands on `radio=profile` (`:focus-visible` true), and the chip shows no outline and no shadow. Arrow keys do change the type. (b) The stepper's `aria-label` sits on a plain `<div>`, where it is ignored, and the current step has no `aria-current`. (c) The canvas has no accessible name. (d) The page's document-level Escape handler also runs while the share sheet is open. Runtime: Escape closed the sheet **and** cleared the Image 1 target | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F13-15 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `CompareComponent.tsx:69-73,203-207` · `ComparisonEngine.ts:245-255` · `ControlsPanel.tsx:267` | **Error paths.** (a) The load toast shows the internal URL: "Failed to load image: /DolImgs/101.I10" names neither the session nor the view. (b) `toggleLogoColor` flips the flag before loading, and the button `void`s the promise. If the logo fails to load, the button shows the black state over the white logo, and the rejection reaches the unhandled-rejection reporter with no toast. (c) A failed session-list read toasts once and leaves an empty panel with no retry | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F13-16 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `CompareOverlay.tsx:31,76-78,106` · `ComparisonEngine.ts:23,121` · `CompareComponent.tsx:99,132-149` | **Leftovers.** `dragRef` is written and never read, and the same goes for `DragState.abort` (the listeners use the closure's controller). `EngineSnapshot.version` has no reader (`useSyncExternalStore` compares by identity). `result.data as string[]` casts what the contract already types (`z.array(z.string())`). `isPhotoTypeAvailable` and `availablePhotoTypes` implement the same predicate twice, and the first one's "nothing selected → true" branch is unreachable, because `SelectionPanel` checks `pairChosen` first. Five exports are used only in their own file (`ImageInfo`, `CanvasSizeOption`, `ToolOption`, `dataURItoBlob`, `UseComparisonEngineResult`) | ✅ fixed (RD) |

**FE-F13-1, -5 and -8 have one cause.** Compare is the only photo reader that rebuilds file names itself, from a DB list, instead of taking them from the gallery endpoint. The grid, the editor and the slideshow all get `name` + `mtime` from that endpoint, which checks the disk in both cases. Moving Compare onto it clears all three, and it is the natural first RD slice. FE-F13-4 and FE-F13-12 then want the engine's `clear()`.

**Found along the way, not F13's to fix:**
- **Other `/DolImgs` readers without a version token** (FE-F13-1's shape): `WorkComponent.tsx:514` (the patient's *Initial* smile, `{id}00.i13`; F7, fix in RD), `routes/ChairDisplay.tsx:304` (F11, fix in RD), `slideshow/configResolver.ts:120` (**F15**, filed as FE-F15-4(b)) and `pages/aligner/PatientsList.tsx:173` (**F18**). The first and last also hard-code lower case, so on a case-sensitive volume they 404 for Dolphin-era patients (FE-F13-5's shape).
- **Session and editor writes still don't refresh `qk.patient.timepointImages`.** This is FE-F12-3(c) seen from the reader's side. With the 30 s staleTime, a view rendered in the editor and opened in Compare within 30 s stays disabled until a refetch. It goes with FE-F12-3's fix, or with F14's editor writes.

## F12 · Timepoint grid + file explorer · 2026-10-01

Scope read in full: `GridComponent` 1,165 · `files/**` 1,156 (`FileExplorer` 600 · `FileEntryTile` 176 · `FilePreviewModal` 160 · `WorkingFilesView` 112 · `fileHelpers` 108) · `PhotoSessionDialog` 425 · `TimepointActionsMenu` 145 · `DeleteTimepointModal` 116 · `EditTimepointModal` 99 · `hooks/useImportFolder` 139 — 3,245 LOC — plus their seven CSS modules (1,695 lines: `GridComponent`, `TimePointsSelector`, `TimepointActionsMenu`, `TimepointModals`, `PhotoSessionDialog`, `FileExplorer`, `WorkingFilesView`). Cross-checks the surface forced: the two other `PhotoSessionDialog` call sites (`ViewPatientInfo`, `Navigation`), `ContentRenderer`/`PatientShell`/`routes.config.tsx` (how the three screens mount and where their errors land), the six F12 `queryOptions` factories and `query/client.ts`'s defaults, `Modal`'s `closeOnBackdropClick` default, `brandingQuery`; the contracts (`patient.contract` timepoints / gallery / folder / visibility / update / delete, `photo-editor.contract#photoDates`, `file-explorer.contract`); and the server halves the client depends on — `patient-timepoint.routes.ts` (list, folder check, PUT, DELETE and its three scopes, gallery), `imaging/index.ts#getImageSizes`, `photo-cleanup.service.ts`, `native-timepoint-queries.ts` (the `MAX+1` allocator), `file-explorer.routes.ts` (the multer upload) and `file-explorer.service.ts` (`sanitizeName`, `resolveSafe`, `finalizeUpload`, `softDelete` vs `hardDelete`), `patient-portal-admin.routes.ts` (the two visibility endpoints), and the route-table gates for every endpoint above.

**Runtime-verified on the owner's WSL box against a throwaway install, never the clinic's data.** PostgreSQL 18 was unpacked into the session scratchpad (port 55432, `Asia/Baghdad`) and installed with plain `npm run db:migrate`, then `npm run db:setup -- --yes` with the clinic name **"Bright Smile Orthodontics"** and `npm run db:seed:demo` (patient #1 is the photographed case: four sessions, eight views each). `MACHINE_PATH` pointed at an empty scratch folder, and a wrapper blanked **every** `.env` key before setting the throwaway `PG_*`, so no sink, WhatsApp, Supabase, Cloudflare or real-volume setting reached the run (see [[temp-db-real-photo-volume-collision]] in memory: on this box `C:` is the real `clinic1`). Express `:3101` + Vite `:5273`. **Real writes, all to the throwaway DB and the scratch volume:** five folders, two uploads, three renames, a cropped delete, an entry delete, an all delete, visibility toggles, one new session; a `clinical` user was created for the role checks. A Windows browser was simulated by defining `navigator.share`/`canShare` in an init script. The probe scripts lived in the scratchpad.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F12-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `routes/api/file-explorer.routes.ts:344,393` · `file-explorer.service.ts#finalizeUpload` · `FileExplorer.tsx:174-195` | **A file uploaded with an Arabic (or any non-ASCII) name is saved under a garbled one.** multer 2.2 decodes the multipart `filename` as **latin1** unless it is given `defParamCharset: 'utf8'`, and Chrome sends the name as raw UTF-8. Nothing decodes it again, so `originalname` reaches `finalizeUpload` already broken. Runtime: `صورة المريض.txt` and `صورة الابتسامة.jpg`, uploaded into a folder named `صور قديمة`, are on disk and in the listing as **`ØµÙØ±Ø© Ø§ÙÙØ±ÙØ¶.txt`** and **`ØµÙØ±Ø© Ø§ÙØ§Ø¨ØªØ³Ø§ÙØ©.jpg`**. Folder names are unaffected, because they travel in a JSON body. The bytes survive (`Buffer.from(name, 'latin1').toString('utf8')` gives the name back), so every file uploaded under a non-Latin name since the explorer shipped is still on the clinic volume, under a name no one can read. **Fix:** `defParamCharset: 'utf8'`. Put it in one shared multer factory, because three more routes use `originalname` (*Found along the way*), and add a one-off rename for the names that decode cleanly | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `GridComponent.tsx:521-600,667-678` | **On a Windows desktop the lightbox has no *Send Message* button, and has two buttons called "Share".** The toolbar treats "has `navigator.share` and `navigator.canShare`" as "mobile". Chrome (89+) and Edge on Windows have both, so a clinic PC gets the phone toolbar: the OS share sheet instead of *Send Message* (the clinic-WhatsApp page, `/send-message?file=`). Commit `aeee357` (2025-12-05), which added the branch, says "Desktop still shows send button". Runtime, with the two properties defined the way Chrome on Windows exposes them: `Hide from patient · Download Image · Share · Share · Zoom…`, against `… Download Image · Send Message · Share …` on Linux Chrome. (The native share and LocalSend buttons carry the same title.) The phone-only prefetch then downloads the full-resolution image again on each slide change: 9 `/DolImgs` GETs against 6, over four slides. *Send Message* itself works; its popup opened `/send-message?file=<volume>/working/101.i20`. Not yet seen on a clinic PC; to confirm, open a photo there and look for the paper-plane. **Fix:** decide by `matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)')`, or always show *Send Message*, and give the LocalSend button its own title ("Send to device") | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `GridComponent.tsx:112-116,820-871,1150-1158` · `ViewPatientInfo.tsx:657-662` · `Navigation.tsx:488-493` | **After a session write, the other screens still show what was deleted or renamed** (the plan's invalidation question). (a) *Delete → Cropped photos only* on a tab you are not viewing refreshes nothing for that tab, because `reloadGallery()` runs only when `removed.tpCode === tpCode`. Runtime: while viewing *Progress*, *Initial*'s cropped photos were deleted, and the eight files left `working/`. Clicking *Initial* then showed **all 8 deleted photos**: the cached gallery, drawn from the browser's 7-day thumbnail cache. No gallery request went out and no empty state appeared. This lasts until the 30 s staleTime runs out and something remounts. (b) An edit that renames the originals folder, and *Delete → Everything*, which removes it, invalidate only `qk.patient.timepoints`, never `qk.patient.filesAll`. Runtime: after *Progress* → *Progress B*, the Files root (cached 10 s earlier) still listed `Progress_30-03-2025` and not `Progress B_30-03-2025`, and nothing refetched it. Clicking the old name gave "Not found". (c) No session write touches `qk.patient.timepointImages` (Compare) or `qk.patient.workingFiles`. (d) The other two `PhotoSessionDialog` call sites (patient info, sidebar) don't invalidate `qk.patient.timepoints` after *prepare*, as the grid's does, so backing out of the editor can leave the new session off the tabs for up to 30 s. A *conflict* override rewrites `works.i_photo_date`/`f_photo_date` with no `qk.work` refresh, and the work and visit forms show those fields. Whether a save then writes the old value back was not runtime-checked. **Fix:** one `invalidatePatientPhotos(personId)` covering the photo keys (timepoints, every gallery, visibility, timepointImages, workingFiles, filesAll), called by every session write | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `GridComponent.tsx:132-154,343-366` · `patient-portal-admin.routes.ts:150-153` | **The grid's "hidden from patient" marks can show the opposite of what is stored.** The hidden set is seeded from `photoVisibilityQuery` whenever the tab changes, a toggle updates only the local set, and nothing invalidates the query while the grid stays mounted. (The comment "a visibility invalidation re-seeds it from the server" refers to `reloadGallery`, which runs only after a cropped delete or a background render.) Runtime: hide *Rest* on *Progress* → badge shown, eye reads "Make visible to patient" → switch to *Initial* and back → **badge gone and the eye reads "Hide from patient"**, while `private_photos` holds `(1, 101.i12)`. The screen always falls back to the state from when the grid mounted, so a staff member who has just made a photo visible can see it marked hidden again (and the reverse). **For `clinical` it is always wrong:** `GET /photos/visibility` is admin/front-desk only, so the read 403s and every photo looks visible. Runtime: a hidden photo showed no badge, and its eye read "Hide from patient"; clicking it → 403 "Insufficient permissions". **Fix:** `setQueryData` on the visibility query from the toggle (or invalidate it), and decide FE-F12-6 for the clinical half | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `patient-timepoint.routes.ts:211-232,298-312` · `GridComponent.tsx:820-834` · `DeleteTimepointModal.tsx:50-58` | **Renaming a session onto an existing folder name leaves the session pointing at someone else's photos, and *Delete everything* then destroys those.** The edit renames `{name}_{DD-MM-YYYY}` best-effort. When the new name's folder already exists (409) or the rename fails for any other reason (on Windows, e.g. while that folder is open in Explorer on another PC), it logs a `warn` and still returns 200, and the grid shows "Time point updated". The session's derived folder name has now moved, but its photos have not. Runtime: session *Final* (2026-09-01) deleted with *Cropped + session*, which **keeps** `Final_01-09-2026` by design. *Progress* (2026-01-03) was then renamed to *Final* / 2026-09-01: 200, and "Open original folder" now resolved to the kept `Final_01-09-2026`. **Delete → Everything** on it (the dialog says "Original photos will be permanently deleted") removed `Final_01-09-2026`, the photos the user had chosen to keep, through `hardDelete` (no trash). Its own 8 originals stayed in `Progress_03-01-2026`, now linked to nothing. The failed-rename variant is milder: "Open original folder" is disabled ("No folder"), and *Delete everything* leaves the originals it promised to delete. **Fix:** refuse the edit (409, "a folder with that name already exists") when the target exists or the rename fails, instead of warning and carrying on; and send a session's originals to `.trash` (`softDelete`) the way the explorer's own delete does, since today the more destructive action is the one that can't be undone | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | route table `PUT/DELETE /api/patients/:personId/timepoints/:tpCode` · `POST /photos/visibility` · `POST …/files/{upload,folder,rename,delete-batch}` · `DELETE …/files` (all `admin\|front_desk`) vs `POST /api/photo-editor/:personId/{prepare,render}` (`\|clinical`) · `GridComponent.tsx:909-918` · `FileExplorer.tsx:415-468` · `FileEntryTile.tsx:148-169` | **A doctor or assistant is offered every photo-session and file write, and the server refuses each one** (FE-F6-6 / FE-F7-7's shape). The same role *can* open a new session and render it in the editor, but cannot rename or delete that session, hide a photo, or add a folder or upload. Runtime as `clinical`: the eye toggle → 403 "Insufficient permissions"; *Edit name & date* → 403, toasted as "HTTP Error: 403 Forbidden" (FE-F12-12); *New folder* → 403. *New folder*, *Upload*, *Select* and nine rename and nine delete buttons were all enabled. **Owner decision:** should `clinical` manage photo sessions and patient files? Either open these routes to `CLINICAL_ROLES` (as RB1 did for treatment items), or hide them for that role | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-7 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `GridComponent.tsx:231,374,995-1016` · `public/images/logo.png` | **The photo grid's centre cell shows this clinic's logo on every install, with this clinic's name as its alt text** (CLAUDE.md's identity rule; FE-F11-6's shape). `LOGO_SRC = '/images/logo.png'` is a file in the repository, and the cell's `alt` is the literal `'Shwan Orthodontics'`. The cell is also a lightbox slide, and it stays in the full-screen presentation mode staff use to show patients their photos. Runtime, on the Bright Smile install: the centre `<img alt="Shwan Orthodontics" src="/images/logo.png">`. The configurable logo (`brandingQuery().logo`, `CLINIC_LOGO`) and `clinicName` already exist, and RC1 moved the kiosk and Videos onto them. This leak was reported to the owner during the 2026-09-29 demo-seed work and left for a decision. **Fix:** the branding logo when set, a neutral mark when not; alt from `clinicName`. Siblings in F13/F14 files: `photo-editor/SlotGrid.tsx:108` (same alt), `photo-editor/SlotCanvas.tsx:151`, `compare/types.ts:128` | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-8 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `GridComponent.tsx:745-759` · `routes.config.tsx:413` | **Loading and error states replace the whole grid, session tabs included.** (a) Every switch to a tab that isn't cached swaps the page for "Loading gallery..."; runtime, with that gallery delayed 1.5 s: **0 tabs** on screen during the load. (b) A gallery read that fails, after its two retries, leaves "Error: Failed to load gallery images" alone on the page: no tabs, no *New session*, no retry (runtime, a mocked 500 → 3 attempts). The way out is the sidebar's *Photos* link, which always goes to tp0. **Fix:** render the tab strip outside the loading and error branches, give the error a Retry, and use `placeholderData` for the grid. (c) **FE-F4-17's concrete case, measured:** a failed Photos chunk (the `GridComponent` import aborted) takes the patient sidebar with it: 0 sidebar links. The boundary also names the wrong product: "An error occurred while loading the **Patient Portal** page". `routeName="Patient Portal"` labels the whole staff patient shell, while *Patient Portal* is the patient-facing app | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-9 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `FileExplorer.tsx:197-220,559-561` | **Enter in the rename and new-folder prompt submits twice when pressed twice.** The button is disabled on `busy`; the input's `onKeyDown` is not, and both presses run the same closure. Runtime: Enter, Enter → **2** `POST /files/rename` with the same body. The first succeeded; the second failed with `ENOENT`, which the server logs at `error` ("[Files] rename failed"), so each one adds a line to the production `error.log`. New folder: the second gets a 409. A drop onto the list is likewise accepted while an upload is still running | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-10 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `PhotoSessionDialog.tsx:81-93,103-107,174-182,355-391` | **The new-session dialog's date shortcuts.** (a) `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')` parses as UTC midnight (FE-F10-16's shape). West of UTC, each button shows and **selects** the day before. Runtime in `America/New_York`: the 1 Sep 2026 appointment reads "31 Aug 2026" and sets the date to `2026-08-31`, so the session is created a day early. (At +3 it is right.) (b) "Recent Appointments" is the newest five, **future ones included**: the first button is the appointment on 20 Oct 2026, nearly three weeks ahead (the run was on 1 Oct). (c) *Open Editor* before the patient read has settled toasts "Patient name is required"; the button waits for the dates read only | ✅ fixed (RD) |

Runtime: *Final* (tp 3) with `103.i10` hidden → deleted (entry) → a new *Retention* session got **tp 3**, and `(3, 103.i10)` was still there, so its Profile photo would be hidden from the patient as soon as it was rendered, without anyone choosing that. It is the safe direction, but the grid shows a hidden mark nobody set. **Fix:** delete that session's `private_photos` rows in the same delete | ✅ fixed (RD) |

Runtime: renaming to an existing name and date toasts **"HTTP Error: 409 Conflict"**, not "Another time point already has that name and date". A name containing `/` toasts "HTTP Error: 400 Bad Request", not "Name cannot contain path characters such as / \ :". The edit also both invalidates and refetches `qk.patient.timepoints`: 2 GETs, the first cancelled. That is F0's `TODO(phase3)` at `:334` | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-13 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `FileEntryTile.tsx:93-171` · `TimepointActionsMenu.tsx:42-65` · `FilePreviewModal.tsx:34-41` | **Keyboard and screen-reader gaps.** (a) Each explorer tile is a `role="button"` that contains its own Download, Share, Rename and Delete buttons (nested interactive controls). Its accessible name runs them together: Playwright resolved "Rename a#b" to the tile, "a#b Oct 1, 2026 Rename a#b", as well as to the real button. (b) The session kebab popover is `role="menu"`, but it gets no focus when it opens, has no arrow-key movement, and doesn't return focus when it closes. | FE-F12-14 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `FilePreviewModal.tsx:114-140` | **The preview keeps downloading after it has what it needs.** A text file is fetched whole and then cut to 200,000 characters. Runtime: a 40 MB `.log` → **83,886,080 bytes** received to show 200,000 characters (40 MB in production). Phones over the tunnel are the explorer's main users (the docblock says so). A closed video or audio preview is unmounted but never emptied, so its download continues (FE-F11-13's mechanism, measured on Videos; RC2 fixed only that screen). **Fix:** `Range: bytes=0-199999` plus an `AbortController`, and `pause()` + `removeAttribute('src')` + `load()` on close | ✅ fixed (RD) |

If the server ever sends `tp` as a number, `r.tp === tpCode` would silently drop every badge. `PhotoSessionDialog` casts the typed `photoDates` payload to hand-written `Appointment[]`/`Visit[]` (`description` isn't in the schema). The `FileListing`/`FileEntry[]` casts are no-ops, since `api.types` re-exports the contract's own types. The grid also keeps a hand-written `Timepoint` and a `LegacyTimepoint` adapter for its two modals | ✅ fixed (RD) |
| FE-F12-16 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `PhotoSessionDialog.module.css:113-137` · `PhotoSessionDialog.tsx:143` · `fileHelpers.ts:103-108` · `GridComponent.tsx:277,334` | **Leftovers.** `.checkboxGroup`/`.checkboxLabel` (four rules) style a checkbox the dialog no longer has. There is a `console.error` in the prepare `catch`, which the toast already covers and production silences. `fileHelpers.errorMessage` is a re-export alias whose own comment says new code should import `httpErrorMessage` directly (two users). **FE-F0-2 is confirmed:** the `no-restricted-syntax` disable at `:277` has its reason on the two lines above, not after `--`. **F0's `TODO(phase3)`** at `:334` is the double fetch in FE-F12-12 | ✅ fixed (RD) |

**FE-F12-1: how it went unnoticed.** Folder names come in a JSON body, and the demo data's file names are Latin, so nothing but a real upload exercises it. Only a real upload from a phone or scanner with an Arabic name hits it. The same default hits every multer instance in the tree. `video.routes.ts` hides it by replacing each non-`[a-zA-Z0-9_-]` character with `_` (an Arabic title becomes underscores, a separate, deliberate choice). `tv-display.routes.ts:203`, `media.routes.ts:205` (the name sent to WebCeph) and `AlignerPdfService.ts:207` keep the garbled name. For the owner, read-only, to size it on the clinic volume (PowerShell on the server): `Get-ChildItem C:\clinic1 -Recurse -File | Where-Object Name -match '[ØÙ]' | Measure-Object`.

**FE-F12-5: why the two deletes differ.** The explorer's *Delete* moves an entry to `clinic1/.trash/{personId}/{stamp}/`, and the modal says "Move to trash". The session's *Delete everything* goes through `hardDelete`, `fs.rm(recursive)`. The kept-originals scope (*Cropped + session*) is there so a mistaken session can be dropped without losing photos, and it is the step that sets up the collision. A new session with the same name and date **reuses** the kept folder by design (the photo editor's `folderName` convention), which is correct. Only the *edit* path can land on it while leaving its own folder behind.

**Found along the way, not F12's to fix:**
- **Upload-name siblings of FE-F12-1** (their sessions are closed or later): `tv-display.routes.ts:203` (TV playlist names, F11 / RC), `media.routes.ts:205` (the WebCeph image name, F9 / RB), `AlignerPdfService.ts:207` (aligner PDFs, **F17**). One shared multer factory fixes all of them; do it with FE-F12-1.
- **Photo editor and Compare carry the same identity literals as FE-F12-7:** `SlotGrid.tsx:108` alt "Shwan Orthodontics", `SlotCanvas.tsx:151`, `compare/types.ts:128` `LOGO_BLACK_URL`. File them with **F13** and **F14**. (F13 filed its sibling as **FE-F13-7**.)
- **`clinic1/.trash` is write-only**: no screen lists or restores from it, and nothing purges it. "Move to trash" is recoverable only by someone with the server's disk. For **F22** (system settings) or a deployment pass.

## F11 · Daily appointments (SSE), chair display, videos · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: `components/react/appointments/**` (`DailyAppointments` 326 · `AppointmentCard` 322 · `AppointmentsHeader` 193 · `DoctorFilterSelect` 146 · `StatsCards` 145 · `AppointmentsList` 119 · `ConnectionStatus` 65 · `MobileViewToggle` 44) · `routes/DailyAppointments` 20 · `routes/ChairDisplay` 262 · `TvDisplaySettings` 1,005 · `routes/Videos` 727 — 3,374 LOC — plus their eleven CSS modules (2,980 lines). Cross-checks the surface forced: `useAppointments`, `useAppointmentsSync`, `services/sse-appointments.ts` and `constants/sse-liveness.ts` (F3 read them; re-read here only for the plan's questions), `dailyAppointmentsLoader`, the two "restore the last date" entry points (`UniversalHeader`, `Dashboard`), `appointment.contract.ts` (daily / state / undo / quick check-in), `tv-display.contract.ts`, `video.contract.ts`, `chair-display.contract.ts`; the server halves the client depends on — `appointment.routes.ts` (daily read, state, undo), `appointment-queries.ts` (`updatePresent`, `undoAppointmentState`, `getDailyAppointmentsOptimized`), `AppointmentService.ts` (`quickCheckIn`, `getDailyAppointments`), `utils/date.ts#formatTime12/formatClock12`, `sse-broadcaster.ts` (both channels), `chair-payload-builder.ts`, `visit-queries.ts#getLatestVisitsSum/buildVisitSummary`, `chair-display.routes.ts`, `tv-display.routes.ts` + `tv-display-store.ts`, `video.routes.ts` + `utils/stream-file.ts` + `imaging/qrcode.ts`, `middleware/timeout.ts`; `SettingsComponent` (what the TV tab's unsaved flag drives), the `vite.config.js` proxy table and the `Caddyfile`; and the route-table gates for every endpoint above.

**Runtime-verified in a cloud container, not on the owner's dev box**, on the same recipe as F9/F10/RB3: a **throwaway** PostgreSQL 16 database installed with plain `npm run db:migrate`, then `npm run db:setup -- --yes …` with the clinic name set to **"Bright Smile Orthodontics"** (so an identity literal cannot hide behind this clinic's own name) and `npm run db:seed:demo`. `MACHINE_PATH`, `TV_DISPLAY_MEDIA_DIR` and `TV_DISPLAY_SETTINGS_FILE` pointed at scratch folders; the three CDC sinks, WhatsApp auto-init and the drift check were off; the database and the server ran on `Asia/Baghdad`. Express (`:3001`) and Vite (`:5173`) ran as separate processes so Express could be killed and restarted under an open page. **Fixtures added by SQL:** two unchecked appointments tonight (20:00, 20:30) and one checked in at 19:52 but not seated. **Real writes to the throwaway DB**, where the write was the point: check-ins and undos driven from a second logged-in context ("another desk"), one quick check-in, the future-day check-in (undone after), TV-display uploads, one probe video (deleted after). **Aborted on purpose:** one check-in POST (`route.abort`). **A frozen transport** was built with a small TCP proxy in front of Vite whose existing sockets could be paused without FIN or RST. The probes (`scripts/e2e/.f11-probe*.tmp.mjs`) launched the preinstalled Chromium through `executablePath`; they, the temporary `.env`, the scratch folders and the recorded video were deleted, the database and role were dropped, and PostgreSQL was stopped. No screenshots were taken.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F11-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `appointment-queries.ts:63,186-188` · `AppointmentCard.tsx:74-88,122-124,186,206,230` | **Every check-in, seat and dismiss time after noon is shown as AM.** The server turns `present`/`seated`/`dismissed` into 12-hour `hh:mm` with no marker (`formatTime12` without `withMeridiem`), and the card then re-derives the marker from that 12-hour hour. Runtime: 13:08 → "**1:08 AM**", 17:38 → "5:38 AM", 19:52 → "7:52 AM" (Arabic "7:52 ص"), in the chip and in its tooltip ("Completed: 1:35 AM - Click to undo"). The same card reads "07:30 PM" for its booking. On a 10:00–20:30 clinic day that is most stamps. It has been this way since the first commit, and the contract does not declare these fields, so nothing catches it | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `useAppointments.ts:104,166,175,234-236` · `DailyAppointments.tsx:251-261` | **One failed check-in blanks the board for good.** A state change that fails for any reason other than the 400 conflict sets `actionError`, which the hook folds into `error`, and the page's error branch replaces the header and both lists with "Failed to load appointments: …". Only the next state change clears it, and there is nothing left to click. Runtime: one aborted check-in → 0 cards, no header, "Failed to load appointments: Failed to fetch"; a second desk's check-in then made the board refetch (1 GET) and the screen stayed blank. Recovery is navigating away and back. On the Arabic board the message is English | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `services/sse-appointments.ts:147-158` (and `sse-whatsapp.ts`, the same code: FE-F1-7) | **After a restart behind a proxy, the live board never reconnects.** EventSource treats any non-200 as final (CLOSED, no retry). Behind Caddy, the cloudflared tunnel or Vite, the reconnect that lands mid-restart gets a 502. The singleton only emits `error`. Runtime: Express killed → reconnect at +3 s → **502** → "Connection Error"; Express back at +11 s; 20 s later still "Connection Error", with no further stream request; a second desk's check-in never reached the board (0 GETs, the row stayed under *All Appointments*). *Jump to today and refresh* does not reconnect; only changing the date away and back did. The kiosk has a reload for exactly this; the staff singleton has nothing, so every open board drops to the 5-minute safety net after each deploy | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `DailyAppointments.tsx:177-210,293-319` · `appointment.routes.ts:173-237` · `appointment-queries.ts#updatePresent` | **Check-in, seat and dismiss are offered on every date, and accepted.** Neither side compares the appointment's day with today, and the stamp is the current clock. Runtime: on Thursday 2026-10-01's list, the first card's check-in → `POST {state: "present", time: "18:26:17"}` → **200**. The row moved to *Checked-in* with `present_time` "06:26", which FE-F11-1 then shows as 6:26 AM. The indicator read "Static", and nothing on screen looked unusual (undone after). On the day, that patient shows as checked in before arriving; attendance and no-show figures are wrong either way. Owner decision: refuse future days; for past days, either refuse or ask for the real time | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `TvDisplaySettings.tsx:186-189` · route table `POST /api/tv-display/media [uploadMediaFiles]` · `tv-display.routes.ts:64-85` | **TV uploads get 30 seconds, for files the code expects to be hundreds of MB** (FE-F1-2's sibling, missed by RA1). The client uses `postFormData`'s default 30 s, and the route has no `timeout(...)`, so the global 30 s applies; multer allows 1 GB × 20. Runtime (upload throttled to 160 KB/s, a 6 MB clip ≈ 38 s): at +30 s the toast read "Request timed out after 30000ms". The file never reached the folder, the staging folder was left empty, and the playlist was unchanged. It fails cleanly, but it always fails. Over the tunnel, or clinic Wi-Fi with a real clip, the normal case fails | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

The operatory kiosk says "Welcome to **Shwan Orthodontics**", and the printed video QR handout's footer says "Shwan Orthodontics". The public TV page's `<title>` and its empty-playlist placeholder are the same literal, while the Settings tab tells staff that "the screen shows the clinic name". The TV tab also prints `C:\Users\Administrator\lgtv-scheduler\tv_daemon.py` ("this deployment's install location") and five repository source paths as product UI. Runtime, on the Bright Smile install: the idle kiosk reads "Welcome to Shwan Orthodontics", and the printed QR footer reads "Shwan Orthodontics". The QR itself encodes **`https://remote.shwan-orthodontics.com/v/1`**, because `config.urls.publicUrl` falls back to this clinic's domain (`config/config.ts:149`). An install without `PUBLIC_URL` hands its patients a link to another clinic's server, and nothing on the modal says the address is a default | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-7 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `sse-appointments.ts:5-11,61-64` · `ConnectionStatus.tsx:14-19,25-29` · `ChairDisplay.tsx:78-80,136-148` · `sse-broadcaster.ts:110-115` | **"Live" and "● Connected" can sit over a dead stream.** The server's 25 s keep-alive is an SSE comment, which EventSource never surfaces, and freshness is just `readyState === OPEN`, so no client can notice a transport that went quiet without closing. `ConnectionStatus`'s docblock promises "heartbeat received recently"; nothing checks one. Runtime: the stream socket frozen (no FIN/RST), then a second desk checked a patient in → **still "Live" at +10 s and +70 s**, 0 GETs, row unchanged. The board's 5-minute safety net bounds the damage. The kiosk has none, and its recovery is `visibilitychange`, which a wall kiosk never fires, so a kiosk in that state keeps showing the last patient's name and photos to whoever sits down next. This answers the plan's laptop-sleep question: the indicator is right only if the tab was hidden for more than 2 minutes or Chrome itself tore the socket down on resume | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

(b) The "restore last date" in `sessionStorage` cannot tell "was looking at today" from "picked a date", so the next morning the header and the Dashboard tile open **yesterday's** list in a tab left open overnight. (c) `?dr=` naming a doctor who is not in the list (flag turned off, or a stale bookmark) filters by that id under an "All doctors" label | ✅ fixed (RC2) |

| FE-F11-12 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `useAppointments.ts:233` · `AppointmentsList.tsx:50-63` · `AppointmentsList.module.css:10-15` · `sse-broadcaster.ts:56-60` · `DailyAppointments.tsx:116-119,168-174` | **How the board refetches.** Every refetch (another desk's write, the 5-minute net) sets `loading`, which dims a non-empty list and sets `pointer-events: none` on it, and swaps an empty list for skeleton cards. Runtime: 8 writes from a second desk → **8** board GETs (one per frame, nothing coalesced) and 6 skeleton frames over an empty filtered list. That answers the plan's storm question: a burst costs one day-query per frame per open board. React Query cancels a superseded refetch, so a client never has two in flight, but the server coalesces nothing, despite the hook's comment. That is fine at the CLAUDE.md ceiling; a short per-day coalesce in `onAppointmentsUpdated` would still cut bursts. Also: *Refresh* from another date fetches today twice (key change, then invalidate), and the "updated" flash's 1 s timer is never cleared, so overlapping flashes cut each other short | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F11-13 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `Videos.tsx:288-355,486-511,514-628,631-670` | **Videos: reuse and lifecycle.** The player modal hand-rolls its title bar (no `ModalHeader`, and the ✕ has no accessible name). Delete is a bespoke confirm modal where `useConfirm` exists, with no in-flight guard. The upload form has no `unsavedGuard`, and closing it mid-upload does not abort: the upload finishes, the list refreshes, and "Video uploaded successfully" appears for a form the user dismissed. **Closing the player does not stop the download** (the plan's long-session question). Runtime: the first request is `Range: bytes=0-` → 206, and seeking is served by `streamFile`, which is fine. With the transfer throttled, though, 21 KB more arrived between 10 s and 20 s after the modal closed, with no `<video>` left in the DOM. The element is unmounted but never emptied (`pause()`, `removeAttribute('src')`, `load()`), so each video opened and closed keeps downloading until it ends or is garbage-collected | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F11-14 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `TvDisplaySettings.tsx:143-145,266-268,297-298,597-601` | **The TV tab drops a pending playlist edit.** Upload and delete both run `setPlaylistDraft(null)`, so a reorder not yet saved silently reverts. The tab's unsaved flag reports only the settings draft (`onChangesUpdate(dirty)`), never `playlistDirty`. Runtime: after a reorder the list read `b-promo, a-logo` under "Unsaved playlist changes"; uploading `c-new.jpg` then produced `a-logo, b-promo, c-new` and the banner disappeared. Separately, clearing *Seconds per picture* snaps it to 1 mid-edit | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F11-15 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `ChairDisplay.tsx:112-116,189-195,251` · `sse-broadcaster.ts:170-176` | **Chair-kiosk edges.** (a) `new Date(visit_date)` on a date-only string (FE-F10-16's shape). Runtime: the same visit reads "Latest Visit · Sep 22, 2026" on a browser set to Baghdad time and "**Sep 21, 2026**" on one set to New York time. (b) Two kiosks with the same chair number evict each other. The server `end()`s the previous stream, the evicted EventSource reconnects in ~3 s and evicts the other one. Runtime: two pages on `chair=2` for 20 s → 3 and 4 stream opens; at the sample, one read "○ Reconnecting…" while the other read "● Connected". (c) The idle clock's size is read from `window` during render, so it does not follow a resize or rotation. (d) The CLOSED-stream reload fires once. If the server is still down after 15 s, it lands on the proxy's error page, which has no script to try again | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F11-16 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `video.routes.ts:29-38` · `video-queries.ts:111-121` · `services/setup/` · `GeneralSettings.tsx:648` · `Videos.tsx:325,333` | **On a fresh install, Videos cannot upload anything.** `VideosPath` is an `options` row that neither the migrations nor `db:setup` create. No Settings screen can add it either: General settings edits only rows that already exist. The upload handler throws "VideosPath not configured in tbloptions", which becomes a 500, and the page toasts the generic "An unexpected error occurred. Please try again or contact support." Runtime: exactly that on the Bright Smile install; it worked once the row was inserted by SQL. Same deployment class as F9's unseeded `work_statuses` / `patient_types`. **Fix:** default it in `db:setup` (a folder under `MACHINE_PATH`, like the other clinic paths) or derive it from `MACHINE_PATH` when unset, and give it a Settings field | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F11-17 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `useAppointments.ts:106-126` · `loaders.ts:388-436` · `PatientManagement.tsx:485-490` · `WorkComponent.tsx:472` | **A walk-in checked in from the patient screens can be missing from the board you return to.** The board's only live refresh is SSE, and its listener exists only while the page is mounted. The two quick check-ins invalidate `qk.patient` and `qk.calendar`, not `qk.appointments.daily`. On return, `dailyAppointmentsLoader` fetches the day again, but that result is only `initialData`, which React Query ignores when the key is already cached; a cache under 30 s old is fresh, so nothing refetches. Runtime (twice): board → *Search* → quick check-in → *Appointments* about 5 s later → the only GET was the loader's, and it **contained** the walk-in, but the board did not show it. It was **still missing at +34 s, +64 s and +124 s** with no further GET; nothing would fetch until the 5-minute safety net or another desk's write. (An earlier, noisier run did show the walk-in after an extra refetch whose trigger I did not identify; the two clean runs both reproduced.) The booking forms have the same gap for a same-day booking: create, edit and delete invalidate `qk.patient` and `qk.calendar` only. F2's map lists `appointments.daily` as "never invalidated, and that is correct"; that holds for other desks, not for the desk that made the write. **Fix:** invalidate `qk.appointments.daily(today)` in both quick check-ins, and have the loader `setQueryData` (or go through `loaderQuery`) so its fetch is not thrown away | ✅ fixed (RC1) |

**FE-F11-1 — the times are converted twice.** `getDailyAppointmentsOptimized` stores `presentTime: fmtTimeStr(r.present)`, and `fmtTimeStr` is `formatTime12(t)` with `withMeridiem` left `false`, so `13:08:00` leaves the server as `"01:08"`. The query's own comment on the sort shows the author knew that string loses the half of the day ("13:45→'01:45' which would collate PM check-ins before AM"). `AppointmentCard.formatTime` then parses `"01:08"`, sees hour 1, and appends `t('card.am')`. Hours 12:xx come out right (12 → PM) and 00:xx–11:xx come out right, so only the afternoon is wrong, which in this clinic is most of the day. Why 🟠: it is on every checked-in card and every undo tooltip, all afternoon, and staff use these stamps to see how long a patient has waited. **Fix:** send the raw `HH:MM:SS` for the three state times (the card already formats it with a localized marker, and the server's sort already uses the raw value), and declare them in `appointmentRowSchema` so the next change is caught. Do it together with FE-F11-11(c): the board should format `apptime` the same way instead of receiving "07:30 PM".

**FE-F11-2 — an action error is not a load error.** `runStateChange` keeps the right half of the contract: a 400 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION` is recovered silently with a reload. Every other failure (a dropped connection, a 408, a 500 such as `updatePresent`'s "Appointment not found" for a row another desk just deleted) goes to `setActionError(httpErrorMessage(err, failMessage))`. The hook returns `error: actionError ?? (query error)`, and `DailyAppointments` renders its whole-page `errorMessage` for any `error`. The next successful `runStateChange` is the only thing that resets `actionError`. The SSE refetch doesn't, and neither does a date change, and neither does the 5-minute net. **Fix:** surface action failures as a toast (the conflict path already uses `window.toast`), keep `error` for the read alone, and give the read-error state the header plus a Retry, so a failed read of *another date* is not a dead end either.

**FE-F11-3 — CLOSED is final, and only the kiosk knows.** From the EventSource spec: a network error means "reconnect", but a response that is not `200 text/event-stream` means "fail the connection". Express itself refuses connections while it boots (it listens only after `mountRoutes`), so a direct client reconnects. Every proxy in front of it answers **502** instead: Caddy on the LAN, the tunnel remotely, and Vite in dev. `ChairDisplay` handles this with a delayed reload (`CLOSED_STREAM_RELOAD_DELAY_MS`); `sse-appointments` emits `error` and waits for a `visibilitychange` longer than 2 minutes, a `pageshow`, or the refcount dropping to zero. The same code is in `sse-whatsapp.ts`. **Fix:** in the channel (the FE-F1-7 extraction RA4 deferred to "F11 or F16"), treat CLOSED as "retry with backoff while refcount > 0". Let a 401 take the auth path: the funnel's next read will redirect. Do it with FE-F11-7's watchdog, since both belong in the same place.

**Note for FE-F3-8's remediation (the casts).** The card's real readers are `appointment_id`, `person_id`, `dr_id`, `patient_name`, `patient_type`, `patient_type_id`, `patient_type_name_ar`, `apptime`, `app_detail`, `present_time`, `seated_time`, `dismissed_time`, `hasActiveAlert`, `is_ortho_visit` and `has_visit` (a boolean from the server, typed `boolean | number | null` on the client). **Do not** widen the schema with `present` / `seated` / `dismissed`: delete their readers (FE-F11-9). FE-F11-1 changes the three time fields' format, so declare them after that fix.

**Found along the way, not F11's to fix:**
- **`Caddyfile`** still carries two `@websocket` matchers and `reverse_proxy @websocket` blocks, and the server's boot banner prints `local.shwan-orthodontics.com`. WebSockets are retired (CLAUDE.md); both are this deployment's config in the product. File with **F26** (or a deployment pass).
- **`chair-payload-builder.ts`** returns `null` when the payload build fails, after the epoch is bumped, so the kiosk is neither updated nor cleared. It is safe today only because `PatientShell` sends *cleared* before *loaded* (FE-F4-10 is about that order). Backend; note it with FE-F4-10.
- **The quick check-in's timestamp** (F10's hand-down). On the board it is right: a walk-in's "time" is the minute it arrived, and it sorts correctly. Its calendar consequence is FE-F10-2. Nothing more to file.

## F10 · Calendar + booking workflow · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: `AppointmentCalendar` 791 · `CalendarGrid` 631 · `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` 527 · `EditAppointmentForm` 448 · `AppointmentForm` 402 · `CalendarHeader` 217 · `HolidayQuickModal` 213 · `PatientAppointments` 211 · `MonthlyCalendarGrid` 208 · `AnalogClock` 204 · `CalendarDayContextMenu` 201 · `calendar.types` 115 · `CalendarContextMenu` 105 · `doctorColors` 89 · `DoctorFilter` 75 · `CalendarLegend` 41 · `routes/Calendar` 12 — 4,490 LOC — plus the five CSS modules (2,022 lines) and the global `css/components/appointment-calendar.css` (1,289). `PeriodNavigator` (106) is in the plan's list but belongs to Statistics: F5 read it and FE-F5-8 covers it, so it is not re-derived here. Cross-checks the surface forced: `calendar.contract.ts`, `appointment.contract.ts`, `holiday.contract.ts`; the five calendar factories and `qk.calendar.*`; `ContentRenderer`'s three appointment branches; the server halves the client depends on — `calendar.routes.ts` (all seven handlers), `CalendarViewService.ts`, `calendar-queries.ts` (`getWeeklyCalendarSlots`, `getCalendarStats`, `fillCalendar`), `AppointmentService.ts` (create / update / quick check-in rules), `appointment.routes.ts` (PUT / DELETE / broadcast), `appointment-queries.ts#APPOINTMENT_SUMMARY_SELECT`, `lookup-admin-queries.ts#tblHolidays`, `employee-queries.ts` (the three doctor lists), `staff.routes.ts#/doctors`; the route-table gates for every endpoint above; `services/setup/` (what a fresh install seeds for the calendar) and `1789460500000_seed-product-constants.sql` (the `Clinic` employee); the i18n ratchet for the four booking files (`eslint.config.js`, `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS`, the `index.html` FOUC check — all in sync).

**Runtime-verified in a cloud container, not on the owner's dev box.** Like F9 and RB3: a **throwaway** PostgreSQL 16, installed with plain `npm run db:migrate` on an empty database, then `npm run db:setup -- --yes …` and `npm run db:seed:demo` (20 fictional patients). `MACHINE_PATH` was an empty scratch folder; the three CDC sinks, WhatsApp auto-init and the drift check were off by env; dev ran on Vite `:5173` + Express `:3001`. The throwaway database was first left on UTC (the clock check flagged it) and was set to `Asia/Baghdad` before the time-sensitive checks. **Fixtures added by SQL:** a walk-in-shaped appointment at 15:07, one on the seeded demo holiday, six filling 12:00–14:30 on one day, one Implant booking, one at 20:30 with the late slots configured as `20:00,20:30`, and `get_appointments = true` on the demo Assistant. **Real writes to the throwaway DB**, where the write was the point: one appointment Update (unchanged values), one calendar delete, the Assistant booking (refused, 400), and four API bookings into one slot. **Aborted:** the drag-reschedule PUT, and every `/api/wa/send-appointment`. **Clock:** the month-arithmetic check fixed the page clock to Sat 2026-10-31 (`page.clock.setFixedTime`); the server clock was real. The probes (`scripts/e2e/.f10-probe*.tmp.mjs`) launched Chromium through `executablePath`; they, the temporary `.env`, the session file and the scratch folder were deleted, the database and role were dropped, and PostgreSQL was stopped. No screenshots. **No live-data facts are recorded for F10**; the queries the owner can run are in the F10 clean list.

About 180 lines duplicated between the two forms. `qk.calendar.availability(0, 0, range)` / `slots(date, 'all')` put placeholders into parameters named year/month/doctorId. Four `console.error` calls | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F10-19 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `AppointmentCalendar.tsx:102-122,131,159,375-397` · `CalendarGrid.tsx:35-71,93` · `MonthlyCalendarGrid.tsx:25,33` · `calendar.routes.ts:50-120` · CSS | **selection mode and friends.** `AppointmentCalendar` has one caller with no props, so `initialDate`/`initialViewMode`/`mode`/`onSlotSelect`/`selectedSlot`, `internalSelectedSlot` (set, never rendered: the grid ignores `selectedSlot`), the selection branch, `CalendarMode` and the month grid's `mode` are all dead. The slot "＋" hover mark and `role="button"` promise booking, but a click does nothing. `TINT_MODE` is a one-branch flag (`V4_TIME_TINT` colourful mode). `GET /api/calendar/week` + `calendar.contract#week` have no caller (removing them diffs `route-table.txt`). CSS: `.header-loading` / `.loading-text` / `.loading-spinner-small` (+ its reduced-motion rule) and `.dayTooltip` are dead, while `.calendar-loading`, `.calendar-error`, `.error-actions` and `.delete-modal` are used but unstyled | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F10-20 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `AppointmentCalendar.tsx:161-174,540-555,567-574,694-786` · `PatientAppointments.tsx:51-68,181-206` · `AnalogClock.tsx:112-120` | reuse and altitude. Three bespoke delete confirms with hand-rolled `<h3>` titles, where `useConfirm` / `<ModalHeader>` exist. Neither delete toasts success (the reschedule does), and the calendar's Delete has no in-flight guard (a double click sends two DELETEs; harmless, since the server 200s a missing row). Narrowing the window below 768 px forces a 1-day view **and persists** `cal-day-count = 1`, so the desktop zoom is lost on widening. `AnalogClock` (the chair display's clock) memoizes behind an `exhaustive-deps` disable, which makes React Compiler skip the component (harmless at one render a second) | ✅ fixed (RC2) |

**FE-F10-1 — the calendar hands the edit form a row it cannot read.** `CalendarContextMenu.handleEdit` navigates to `/patient/:id/edit-appointment/:apptId` with `state: { appointment }`, where `appointment` is the grid's `CalendarAppointment`: `{appointment_id, appDetail, drID, patientName, personID, slotStatus, slotDateTime, app_date, person_id}`. `EditAppointmentForm` prefers router state over its own fetch (`enabled: !existingAppointment`) and seeds `AppDetail: appt.app_detail || ''`, `DrID: String(appt.dr_id || '')`. Both keys are absent, so both selects open on their placeholder. Someone already noticed half of this: `CalendarViewService` adds `app_date` and `person_id` as "compatibility" aliases for exactly this form, and `calendar.contract.ts` documents them, but not the other two. The patient list's router state is the DB row (`APPOINTMENT_SUMMARY_SELECT`: `app_detail`, `dr_id`), so that path seeds correctly. That is why this looks intermittent. Runtime: Implant with Karwan Omar opened from the calendar → `#doctor` `""`, `#details` `""`, *Selected time* "Sun, Oct 4, 5:00 PM"; the stored row is `{app_detail: "Implant", dr_id: 5}`. Validation then refuses Save until both are re-picked, so the damage is a **wrong** pick, not a silent blank: the appointment moves to another doctor's filter, legend colour and reminder. Why 🟠: it is the calendar's only edit path, it fails every time, and it invites a data change. **Fix:** always fetch `appointmentByIdQuery` (router state at most as `placeholderData`), which also closes the stale-state half: a Back/Forward into an old edit entry, or a stale list row (FE-F10-10), re-seeding pre-edit values into a whole-row PUT. Or at least map `appDetail`/`drID`.

**FE-F10-2 — the grid shows the slot table, not the appointments.** `getWeeklyCalendarSlots` is `FROM calendar LEFT JOIN appointments ON ta.app_date = tc.app_date`, so an appointment exists on the calendar only if a `calendar` row has **exactly** its timestamp, and a day exists only if it has `calendar` rows. `transformToCalendarStructure` builds days from those rows; `/range` then keeps only the configured `times` minus the hidden early/late lists, and `CalendarGrid` draws only those rows (the header's "N appts" sums the same rows). Four ways a real appointment falls out, three confirmed at runtime:
- **Past days.** `fillCalendar()` opens with `DELETE FROM calendar WHERE app_date < CURRENT_DATE`, and a fresh install's first fill starts today. So last week renders "Loading calendar data..." forever, and this week shows only today onward under a "6 days" title. Whether the live clinic has history depends on when *Regenerate* was last pressed; each press erases it.
- **Walk-ins.** `quickCheckIn` inserts `app_date = now` (e.g. 15:07:23), which is never a slot, so no walk-in is ever on the calendar, the month counts or the stats. The Daily Appointments list has them.
- **Hidden rows.** With `CALENDAR_SHOW_EXTENDED_SLOTS_DEFAULT = false`, early/late appointments are in the payload but have no row. The option's name suggests a user toggle, and the calendar has none. A time deleted in Calendar Times keeps its future `calendar` rows, and the picker keeps offering it (`/available-slots` reads `calendar`, not `times`), but the grid has no row for it.
- **The horizon.** Nothing extends `calendar`: *Regenerate* and `db:setup` are the only writers, and the `numbers` tally covers 0–366 days. `noteCalendarRange` logs a warning nobody reads. After the horizon, the picker shows every day as unbookable with no explanation.

**Fix (owner decision; server-shaped):** drive the grid from `appointments` (bucket each into its slot, or into a nearest-row / "other times" row) and keep `calendar` only for availability. At minimum: stop deleting past rows, render a row for every time that has an appointment, and give the grid the extended toggle the picker has.

**FE-F10-4 — the loading screen is the whole screen.** `calendarRangeQuery` and `calendarMonthQuery` have no `placeholderData`, so a new key (another week, another zoom, another view) starts with `data: undefined`. `AppointmentCalendar` returns its full-page `calendar-loading` block whenever `loading && !calendarData`, and that replaces `CalendarHeader` too. The comment above it says the opposite: "refetches (nav, zoom, mutations) keep the current grid visible". The worst case is the zoom slider: the 250 ms debounce moves the key, the header unmounts, and the `<input type="range">` being dragged disappears. Runtime (`/range` delayed 1.5 s): after Next, `.cal-bar` count 0 and "Loading Calendar..." shown; the slider was present at +150 ms and gone at +550 ms. **Fix:** `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` on both factories (the grid already dims nothing, so an `isFetching` hint in the toolbar is enough), and keep the full-page state for the first load only.

**FE-F10-7 — the per-slot limit is a UI suggestion.** `MaxAppointmentsPerSlot` is read in exactly two places: `transformToCalendarStructure`, which labels a slot `full`, and the month summary. `validateAndCreateAppointment` and `validateAndUpdateAppointment` never read it. The picker disables `full` tiles, from a read up to 30 s old, invalidated only by the booking desk's own writes. The calendar drag and the edit form don't look at capacity at all. So the plan's concurrency question has a short answer: two front-desk PCs taking the last seat both get 200. Runtime: four POSTs into 2026-10-07 16:00 with the default 3 → `[200, 200, 200, 200]`, slot `full` with 4. The scale half is the bigger question for the product. The limit is per **slot**, not per **doctor**, so a 20-doctor center (the CLAUDE.md ceiling: ~28 appointments per 30-minute slot) must set it near 30, and then it no longer limits any one doctor. **Decide:** enforce it on the server (a count under a lock, in the transaction), per doctor or per clinic, or relabel it as a soft warning.

**FE-F10-11 — two literals that make every new center look like this one.** `FIXED_DEFAULT_COLORS` and `DEFAULT_PICKER_HEX` are keyed by `employees.id` 1 and 7, with this clinic's doctors' names in the comments. The fresh-install seed inserts `Clinic` first, so it is id 1 (runtime: `SELECT id … → 1 | Clinic`), and the "neutral Clinic bucket" the legend and grid comments describe is amber on every new deployment. `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` treats `14:00`/`14:30` as "rarely used afternoon" slots regardless of settings, and falls back to this clinic's early/late lists when the options are missing. `db:setup` seeds 10:00–20:30 slots and no options, so a new center's picker hides its own midday. The server's `/range` treats missing options as "hide nothing", so the grid and the picker disagree. **Fix:** delete the id-keyed defaults (a colourless doctor is neutral, or give new doctors a palette colour when created), delete the `14:00`/`14:30` literals, and treat missing options as empty lists on the client too.

**Found along the way, not F10's to fix:**
- **`CalendarTimesSettings` saves the three extended-slot options but invalidates only `qk.settings.option(…)`**, not `qk.calendar.all()`. The grid's rows are computed server-side from the same options, so /calendar keeps the old rows for up to 30 s after a save. File with **F21**.
- **The quick check-in's timestamp choice** (`app_date` = the check-in moment) is F11's surface. FE-F10-2 records only what it does to the calendar.

## F9 · Clinical: visits, diagnosis, imaging links · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: `pages/Diagnosis` 957 · `NewVisitComponent` 555 · `VisitsComponent` 295 · `WebCephModal` 405 · `TeethSelector` 286 · `ThreeShapeScansView` 180 · `XraysComponent` 170 · `DentalChart` 80 — 2,928 LOC — plus their eight CSS modules (2,722 lines). Cross-checks the surface forced: `ContentRenderer`'s `visits`/`new-visit`/`work`/`diagnosis`/`xrays`/`scans` branches and the diagnosis route in `routes.config.tsx`; `diagnosisQuery`, `visitByIdQuery`, `visitsByWorkQuery`, `latestWiresQuery`, `webcephLinkQuery`, the two 3Shape factories and `qk.work.*`/`qk.visit.*`; `visit.contract.ts`, `work.contract.ts#diagnosis`, `media.contract.ts` (WebCeph), `threeshape.contract.ts`; the server halves the client depends on: `diagnosis.routes.ts` + `diagnosis-queries.ts` (the upsert), `visit.routes.ts` + `visit-queries.ts` (the photo-flag roll-up into `works`), `media.routes.ts` + `webceph-service.ts` (guards, timeouts, race), `services/threeshape/{client,dtos}.ts` + the 3Shape API PDF (tooth numbering), `patient-assets.service.ts` (the X-ray list), the `/clinic-assets` static mount, `/api/patients/:id/xray`, `whatsapp-media.routes.ts#sendmedia2` + `SendMessage`'s `?file=` seed; the `works` CHECK constraints and `unq_tblwork_active`; the route-table gates for every F9 endpoint; the `tooth_numbers` table and `public/images/teeth/chart/*.svg`.

**Runtime-verified, but NOT on the owner's dev box.** This session ran in a cloud container with no access to the clinic database (the owner offered mirror credentials; declined, because the mirror is two-way, so any write there reaches the clinic DB through reverse sync, and a read-only role was not needed to audit the code). Instead: a **throwaway local PostgreSQL 16** built from `migrations/pg` (the baseline applied with `psql` and `db:baseline:stamp`, because `db:migrate` cannot install a fresh database, see *Found along the way*), seeded with **fake rows only**: 2 patients, 2 ortho works, 2 visits, 1 diagnosis with ceph values, 3 wires, 2 employees, the work statuses and patient types. Dev ran on Vite `:5173` + Express `:3001`, with the three CDC sinks off, WhatsApp auto-init off and the drift check off. The Playwright probe (`scripts/e2e/.f9-probe.tmp.mjs`, deleted afterwards; no screenshots) launched the preinstalled Chromium through `executablePath`, because the harness's bare `chromium.launch()` finds no browser for the pinned Playwright in that container. **Real writes to the throwaway DB** were made where the write was the point: the first visit update, the final-photo visit, and the two constraint failures. **Aborted and recorded:** the diagnosis POST and the second visit PUT. **Mocked reads:** `GET /api/diagnosis/1` → 500, `GET /api/webceph/patient-link/1` → 500, and the 3Shape cases/media lists. The one `sendmedia2` call used `prog=Telegram` and was refused at the path guard, before any send. **No live-data facts are recorded for F9**: the queries the owner can run are in the F9 clean list.

Cancel, the ← Back button, the sidebar, the header tabs and closing the browser tab all discard the typing silently. Also: Diagnosis navigates away on a 500 ms `setTimeout` that is never cleared and re-enables both buttons during it (FE-F6-9's shape), so leaving the page inside that window still yanks the user back to Works | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

Editing a visit deleted meanwhile shows the 404 banner above a blank, submittable *Edit Visit* form, and its Update toasts success, because `updateVisitByWorkId` updates 0 rows without complaint (by code reading) | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

A retry is harmless (`overwrite: true`), but the message is wrong | ✅ fixed (RB3 + follow-up: race defaults to Caucasian, owner's call) |
