| FE-F9-14 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `Diagnosis.tsx` · `TeethSelector.tsx:42,52,87,90-129` · `NewVisitComponent.tsx:171-247` · `ThreeShapeScansView.tsx:70-76,144-158` | altitude and polish. `Diagnosis` is 957 lines of 45 near-identical field blocks, where a field-config array would be ~150. It fetches the **whole works list** only for the type name in the delete confirm, and that load gates the whole page. `TeethSelector` (`React.memo` + 7 `useMemo` + 3 `useCallback`) and `NewVisitComponent` (6 `useCallback`s) memoize by hand under React Compiler, which CLAUDE.md says not to do. On the 3D Scans tab, a failed **Download** is a plain `<a href>`, so it navigates the SPA tab to the raw JSON error, and the not-connected notice sends non-admins to the admin-only Integrations tab | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F9-15 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `ContentRenderer.tsx:106-112,179-192` | the `case 'work'` diagnosis branch and `workIdFromPath` are unreachable. `/patient/:id/work/:workId/diagnosis` always matches the specific route, which `PatientShell` renders as `page === 'diagnosis'`. Only the bare `/work` → `/works` redirect in that case is live. Also stale: the `visits` case comment (FE-F9-8), and Diagnosis's own header comment says the works read is a "Loose contract … cast" | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

**FE-F9-1 — the diagnosis page cannot tell "none yet" from "couldn't load".** The GET's contract is a raw `200 null` for "no diagnosis" (`diagnosis.routes.ts`, deliberately), and a failure is a 500. `diagnosisQuery` collapses the two: its `catch { return null; }` was carried over from the legacy `.catch(() => null)` read. `Diagnosis` then seeds as a new diagnosis: empty fields, today's date, `diagnosisExists = false`. The user sees exactly what "no diagnosis recorded" looks like, with no error anywhere. Two properties make it destructive rather than just confusing. First, `upsertDiagnosis` is a whole-row `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` over all 45 columns, and every blank field goes out as `''` → NULL. So typing the two required fields and pressing Save replaces a full work-up (facial, intraoral, occlusion, cephalometric) with two sentences, and re-dates it to today. Second, the `null` **is cached**, and the queryFn never rejects, so neither RQ's retry nor `retryOnMount` applies. Runtime: a reopen within `staleTime` made 0 requests and showed the same blank form. After 30 s the reopen **did** refetch and got the real row back (`"Class II div 1, severe crowding"`), and the form **stayed blank**, because the render-phase seed is keyed on `workId` and has already run (FE-F6-8's shape, with the worst possible first value). Triggers are ordinary operations events, not edge cases of the code: a service restart during a deploy (the documented deploy **is** a restart), a 30 s funnel timeout, a dropped Wi-Fi packet. Why 🟠 when the trigger is a failed read: the failure is silent, it destroys a saved clinical record, and nothing on screen lets the user notice before it is gone. **Fix:** let the queryFn throw (keep `null` only for the `200 null` body), give the page an error state with Retry and no form, and seed only from a read fetched after mount (`isFetchedAfterMount`, or `refetchOnMount: 'always'` and wait for `!isFetching`). Optionally, make the server's upsert skip `undefined` fields, so a partial body can't null the rest.

**FE-F9-2 — a visit you just edited opens as it was before the edit.** `visitByIdQuery` is keyed `['visit', id]`, a root of its own, so the save's `invalidateQueries(qk.work.all(workId))` (which does refresh the Visit History list and the latest-wires prefill) never reaches it. The edit form seeds once per visit identity (`initializedVisitKey`), so it takes the cached row. Within 30 s there is no refetch at all; after that, the background refetch arrives after the seed and is ignored. `gcTime` keeps the stale row for 5 minutes after the form closes. The Update is a whole-row PUT (`updateVisitByWorkId` sets every column), so saving the second edit **reverts the first**. Runtime showed every step: the list shows the new elastics, the form shows the old ones, and the PUT carries the old ones. It is FE-F8-2's shape (a render-phase seed guard + React Query handing back the same cached object) with a different outcome. The header's remembered sub-view (`UniversalHeader`'s `lastPatientView`) stores `new-visit?workId=…&visitId=…` too, so the patient tab can reopen a stale edit form directly. **Fix:** invalidate (or `removeQueries`) `qk.visit.byId(visitId)` on save and delete, or nest the key under `qk.work.all(workId)`. Also seed only from a post-mount fetch, as in FE-F9-1.

**FE-F9-3 — Send has nowhere to send from.** `handleSendClick` builds `buildXrayUrl(xray)` (the endpoint that renders the X-ray to an image on demand, via `imaging.processXrayImage`) and opens `/send-message?file=<that URL>`. `SendMessage` posts `file` verbatim to `/api/wa/sendmedia2`. There, `resolveSendablePath` treats a leading `/` as an absolute filesystem path and `isUnderClinicRoot` rejects it (the containment guard added against reading `.env`, and correctly so). Before the guard, the same string would have failed as ENOENT, so this button has not worked since at least `95b599a`. **Fix:** the send has to name the processed image's clinic path. Either the xray endpoint exposes it (or a "prepare for send" call that returns the relative path, the way `/api/convert-path` serves the photo senders), or `sendmedia2` learns to resolve an X-ray reference itself. Both involve the server. Until then, hide the button rather than let it fail at the last step.

**FE-F9-4 — a checkbox that closes a treatment.** The roll-up is the legacy `PhotoInsert`/`MyTrigger`/`PhotoDelete` triggers, now in `visit-queries.ts`: *Final Photo* → `markWorkFinished` (status 2, `f_photo_date` = the visit date, carried wires deleted on the first finish, `recomputePatientType`). Un-ticking it, or deleting the visit, → status 1 + reclassify. The form presents these as five equal checkboxes ("OPG Taken · Initial Photo · Progress Photo · Final Photo · Appliance Removed") beside a delete confirm that says only "cannot be undone". Three consequences, all runtime-confirmed: (a) the Works card keeps **ACTIVE** after the save until a reload, because only `qk.work.all` is invalidated (FE-F7-8's shape, new write sites; the patient-type badge in `qk.patient.info` is stale the same way); (b) *Initial* + *Final* on one visit violates `ck_works` (`f_photo_date > i_photo_date`), and the whole add fails with "Failed to add visit"; (c) re-activating a work while the patient has another active one violates `unq_tblwork_active`, and the whole edit fails with "Failed to update visit", losing whatever else was typed. The same applies to deleting a final-photo visit. The server's messages are generic by design (`internalError`), so the client cannot explain either. **Fix (client):** invalidate `qk.patient.all(personId)` after every visit write and delete. Label the side effects inline ("Final photo — marks this treatment finished"). Confirm before a save that finishes or reactivates a work, the way the Works page's status change does. **Fix (server, belongs with it):** map `23514`/`23505` on these paths to 409s the form can word, e.g. "this patient already has an active treatment".

**FE-F9-5 — why the X-ray grid is all placeholders.** `send`'s default `dotfiles: 'ignore'` rejects any URL with a dot-segment, and CS-Imaging's layout guarantees two of them (`.csi_data`, `.version_4.4`). Runtime on `:3001`: the preview → **404**, the same file under `OPG/plain/` → 200 `image/png`. `onError` then hides the `<img>` and "reveals" a placeholder that was never hidden: the `hidden` utility (`utilities.css`, main bundle) and `.placeholder` (the lazy chunk's module, injected later) have equal specificity, so the later sheet wins. The result looks deliberate (a neat "View X-ray" tile), which is why nobody noticed. **Fix:** scope `dotfiles: 'allow'` to this one path (a dedicated `/api/patients/:id/xray-preview` through `resolveFileForServe` is cleaner than widening `/clinic-assets`), and use a module class, or conditional rendering, for the placeholder instead of the global `hidden`. Add `/clinic-assets` to Vite's proxy so dev can show thumbnails at all.

**FE-F9-6 — one mouth, three numbering systems.** The plan asked whether `TeethSelector`, `DentalChart` and `Diagnosis` share one mapping. Two of them do, and a third system has arrived since. `DentalChart` (hard-coded) and `TeethSelector` (the `tooth_numbers` vocabulary) both use quadrant + number / letter codes (`UR6`, `LLC`), and so do the 32 chart SVGs they share (`/images/teeth/chart/UR6.svg`, one file per permanent tooth). `Diagnosis` has no chart, only free text, and its placeholder teaches `"Crossbite on #24"`, a numeric style used nowhere else in the app. The 3D Scans tab prints 3Shape's UNN integers verbatim. A Palmer- or FDI-trained reader has no way to tell "14" is UNN, and UNN 14 and FDI 14 are different teeth on opposite sides. **Fix:** convert on display, UNN 1–8 → UR8…UR1, 9–16 → UL1…UL8, 17–24 → LL8…LL1, 25–32 → LR1…LR8 (the indication fields are integers, so primary teeth never arrive). Keep the raw value in a `title` for cross-checking against Unite. Change the Diagnosis placeholder to the app's own notation.

**Found along the way, not F9's to fix:**
- **A fresh database cannot be installed with `npm run db:migrate`.** First, `predb:migrate` (`db-migrate-check.mjs`) refuses an unmigrated database outright. Second, the baseline (generated by pg_dump from the live DB) contains `CREATE TABLE public.pgmigrations`, which collides with the ledger node-pg-migrate creates before the first file runs: `relation "pgmigrations" already exists`, rolled back. `db:baseline:verify` applies the Up section through a raw `pg` query, not the migrator, so it never sees this. For a product sold to new centers, the first install is the one that fails. This session installed with `psql` + `db:baseline:stamp` (the documented *repair* path). **✅ Fixed the same day at the owner's request** (see *Deployment fix* below), together with a third blocker the fix uncovered: as a **non-superuser** owner, pg_dump's unwrapped `COMMENT ON EXTENSION pg_stat_statements` failed after the wrapper had skipped the extension.
- **Product constants are not seeded, so a fresh install 500s on first use.** `db-migrations.md` says clinical vocabularies need a separate load. But `work_statuses` (FK of `works.status`; the ids are `WORK_STATUS` constants) and `patient_types` (FK of `patients.patient_type_id`; the ids are `PATIENT_TYPE_IDS`) are **code constants**, not clinic vocabulary. On an empty install, the first work insert fails `fk_work_status`, and the first *Final Photo* visit fails `fk_patients_tblpatienttype` (both hit here). They belong in a migration, like the shade vocabularies. **✅ Fixed the same day** (below).

**Deployment fix · 2026-09-28 (owner's request, after the F9 audit).** Not a frontend change, and not part of the RB count. The baseline no longer creates `pgmigrations` (node-pg-migrate's table) or runs the unwrapped extension comment. `db-baseline-dump.mjs` excludes the ledger and `db-baseline-build.mjs` drops the comment, so a rebuilt baseline can't regress. `db:check` certifies an **empty** database as a fresh install and still refuses a non-empty one without a ledger. `db-baseline-verify.mjs` now creates the ledger first, as the migrator does. New migration `1789460400000_seed-code-constant-lookups.sql` seeds `work_statuses` 1–3 and `patient_types` 1/2/3/4/5/9/10 (EN + AR), `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, moving the `patient_types` identity forward only. The new `services/database/fresh-install.test.ts` (5 tests, DB-free, in the gate) fails if the ledger DDL, an unwrapped `pg_stat_statements` reference, or a missing seed id comes back; it fails 3 of its checks against the pre-fix files. Docs: `db-migrations.md` (*Fresh install*, the `db:check` state table) and CLAUDE.md. **Verified on throwaway PG 16 databases:**
- **Fresh install:** empty DB, non-superuser owner → `npm run db:migrate` applies all 12 files → `db:check` "ledger matches disk".
- **Schema identity:** fresh-installed as a superuser, the schema is **byte-identical** to the old path (old baseline + stamp + the 11 old files), ledger and extension comment included. The non-superuser install differs only by the skipped `pg_stat_statements`.
- **Existing deployment:** on an old-path DB carrying clinic-edited names (`patient_types` 1 renamed, 9 missing, identity at 20), only row 9 was added, names were untouched, and the identity stayed at 20.
- **Refusals:** `db:check` refuses, and `db:migrate` aborts on, both a schema-without-ledger DB (→ *stamp*) and a stray-table DB (→ *investigate*).
- **Down/up:** `db:migrate:down` + `up` on the seed are clean.
- **App on the fresh install:** create patient → add work → add a *Final Photo* visit all return 200, and the patient reclassifies to 2 / the work to *Finished* through the seeded rows.
- **Gate:** green, 27 files / 234 tests, D1 0 / D2 9, only F0's 4 warnings, no route-table diff.
- **Not verifiable here:** `db:baseline:verify` hard-codes Windows paths (`C:/pg18-migration/…`), so its edit was checked by reading, not by running.
- **Server, by code reading only (not runtime-checked):** `applyPhotoUpdate` reacts to **flag** changes, not to a changed `visit_date`. So correcting the date of the visit that set `i_photo_date` / `f_photo_date` / `debond_date` leaves the work's date at the old value, and it can later trip the `works` CHECK constraints from an unrelated edit. File it with FE-F9-4's server half. **✅ Fixed in RB3** (a still-set flag now moves the work's date with the visit).

## F8 · Payments, invoices, expenses · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: `PaymentModal` 1,277 · `PaymentFormDrawer` 264 · `DailyInvoicesModal` 285 · `DoctorPaymentsModal` 181 · `components/expenses/**` 1,023 (`ExpenseModal` 441 · `ExpenseFilters` 258 · `ExpenseTable` 136 · `ExpenseSummary` 104 · `DeleteConfirmModal` 84) · `routes/Expenses` 307 · `hooks/useExpenses` 296 — 3,633 LOC — plus their three CSS modules (1,935 lines). Cross-checks the surface forced: `shared/contracts/payment.contract.ts` + `expense.contract.ts` + `reports.contract.ts#dailyInvoices`, `PaymentService.validateAndCreateInvoice` (the overpayment pre-check, the locked re-check and `validateChangeAmount`), `AlignerPaymentService` + `aligner-payment-queries#createAlignerPayment`, `expense-queries` (list, summary, create/update), the payment/expense/aligner-payment gates in `route-table.txt`, the `payments`/`expenses` catalogs (EN/AR parity + usage), the i18n ratchet, the `qk` keys the surface reads and writes, `PatientSets`' drawer handler, and the live `expenses`, `invoices`, `works`, `aligner_sets` and `sms` rows.

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`** (Playwright via `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs`; no screenshots; three throwaway probes, deleted), the dev server started with the three CDC sinks and WhatsApp auto-init **disabled by env**. Every non-GET was **aborted and recorded**; one read was mocked (`/api/getExchangeRateForDate` → 404, to stand in for a deployment that has never recorded a rate). Probed: the summary cards against a category filter (FE-F8-1), edit → cancel → edit on one expense and the Save that follows (FE-F8-2), the rate editor's display against the POST it would send (FE-F8-3), a fractional USD amount (FE-F8-4), and an IQD-in-IQD payment with no rate on record (FE-F8-5).

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F8-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `ExpenseSummary.tsx:35,43-50` · `expense.contract.ts#expenseSummary` | the three summary cards (count, Total IQD, Total USD) come from `/api/expenses/summary`, which takes **only the dates**, so every other filter is ignored. Runtime, September, category 1 applied: table **33 rows / 151,750 IQD**, cards still **83 / 3,001,750 IQD / 200 USD** — the whole month, on top of a filtered table. The client-side sum it falls back to is exact, because the list is never paginated | ✅ fixed (RB2) |
| FE-F8-2 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `routes/Expenses.tsx:111-115` | editing the **same expense twice in a row** opens a blank **"Add New Expense"** form, and Save **creates a new expense**. Runtime: Edit → *Edit Expense* 60,000 → Cancel → Edit the same row → *Add New Expense*, amount empty; type 60,000 + Save → `POST /api/expenses {amount:60000, currency:"IQD", note:"", isMonthly:false}` (aborted), i.e. an uncategorised duplicate. The seed guard `seededExpense !== editingExpense` is never reset, and React Query hands back the same cached object on the second open | ✅ fixed (RB2) |
| FE-F8-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PaymentModal.tsx:92-98,305-313,896-903` | the Set Rate box **displays one number and saves another**. Its display runs the local `formatNumber`, whose `parseFloat` stops at the first comma, while Save parses with `parseFormattedNumber`. Runtime: type `1560` → `1,560`; one Backspace → the box shows **`1`**; Save → `POST … {exchangeRate: 156}`. Neither side bounds the rate (`moneyInt.positive()`), and a rate for today is saved without a confirm. A wrong rate restates that day's statistics and mis-converts every cross-currency payment made that day | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PaymentModal.tsx:92-98,528-556,709-711` | a fractional amount is **shown rounded but sent truncated**. Runtime, USD work 13105: type `99.5` → Amount `100`, Received `100`, strip *Cash IN $100 · Register 100 USD*; `POST /api/addInvoice {amountPaid:99, usdReceived:99}`. The contract says it rejects a fractional amount at the boundary, but the client `parseInt`s before sending, so the boundary never sees one. The money inputs also accept `-`, `.` and `e` (`1e3` parses as 1,000) | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PaymentModal.tsx:186-190` | the "Auto" cash fill **needs an exchange rate even when nothing is converted**. On a deployment that has never recorded a rate (a new center's first day; mocked 404): IQD work 12975, IQD payment, amount 100,000 → Received stays `0` under an **"Auto"** label, Save is enabled, and submit says *"Please enter at least one currency amount received"*. RA-era work took the rate off the Save gate (`rateRequired`) but not off the fill. Workaround: type the cash by hand; the field is not read-only | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PaymentFormDrawer.tsx:138` · `PatientSets.tsx:1489,541` · `aligner-payment-queries.ts:80-89` | an aligner-set payment is **always booked in USD** (`amount_paid = usd_received = X`) into the work's ledger, **whatever the work's currency**. Live: 11 sets sit on IQD works. Three of those works are really IQD-priced (10419 2,700,000 · 11243 2,250,000 · 12025 200,000). Give one of their sets a cost (sets are USD-only) and *Add Payment* of $500 credits the patient **500 dinars**; the work-level guard compares 500 against the IQD balance and lets it through. 0 such payments yet (those sets have no cost). The other three IQD works (10192/10193/10768) are the mislabelled works already handed to the owner in F7 | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-7 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `PatientSets.tsx:554` | an aligner payment invalidates `qk.work.all(workid)` but not `qk.patient.all(personId)`, so for up to 30 s the patient's Works page (Paid / Remaining / Add Payment) and patient info keep showing the balance **before** the payment. FE-F7-8's shape. A PaymentModal opened from that page in that window checks against the stale balance; the server refuses the overpayment | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-8 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `routes/Expenses.tsx:203,211-214,224,227-228` · `useExpenses.ts:196,208,236,261` | on the translated Expenses page, the hold-tier toast is the English literal **`'Submitted for admin approval'`** (FE-F7-12's shape; the `jsx-only` ratchet does not see call arguments). Both `catch {}` blocks drop the server's message for a generic *"Failed to update expense"*, so a 400's reason never reaches the user. `useExpenseMutations` computes that message into an `error` field that no caller reads | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-9 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `PaymentModal.tsx:1013-1020` · `ExpenseModal.tsx:286-292` · `PaymentFormDrawer.tsx:206-213` | payment and expense dates are **unbounded**: no `min`/`max` on the inputs, and `dateString` takes any real calendar date. A slipped year digit (2062, or 0202 from a half-typed year: the input commits on each year keystroke, FE-F5-4's shape) saves an invoice that counts toward the work's *Paid* but falls outside every daily and statistics period, including today's cash count. 0 future-dated expenses live | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-10 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `DailyInvoicesModal.tsx:125,169,255` · `DoctorPaymentsModal.tsx:35,91,118-119` | the drill-down modals, cosmetically. *Daily Invoices*: the Expenses, Expected Cash and totals block sits inside `invoices.length > 0`, so a day with expenses but no payments shows only "No invoices found" (0 such days since 2025 here, but plausible at another center), and *Amount Paid* renders unformatted (`150000 IQD`) beside formatted columns. *Doctor Payments*: the "Payments" figure is the **capped** row count (≤ 2,000), under a note that says "the totals above cover the whole period"; its doc comment still says `works.currency` is nullable (0 NULLs, enum since RB1) | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-11 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `useExpenses.ts:45-120,204` · `ExpenseSummary.tsx:35` · `ExpenseModal.tsx:21-31,62,65` · `ExpenseFilters.tsx:23-39` · `Expenses.tsx:113-114` · `DailyInvoicesModal.tsx:10-19,70` · `PaymentModal.tsx:23-32,156` | hand-written interfaces and casts over contracted reads (FE-F7-17's shape). `ExpenseSummary` models `totalExpenses / byCategory / byCurrency`, **three fields the endpoint never sends**; the component then re-casts to `{ totals }` to read the real shape. `createExpense` is typed `Promise<Expense>`, but the response is `{ NewID }`. `Category`/`Subcategory` are declared three times. `as Invoice[]` narrows a nullable `currency` string to `'IQD' \| 'USD'`. `as WorkData` sits over the receipt read (FE-F8-14). *(RB2 deleted the phantom `ExpenseSummary` interface together with the read it typed.)* | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-12 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `ExpenseModal.tsx:66-67,353-384` · `ExpenseFilters.tsx:40-41,135-176` | an expense whose lab was later deactivated, or whose employee has since quit, opens for edit with **"Select lab/employee"** showing while the old id is kept and re-sent. The filter cannot select a quit employee at all, so their salary history cannot be pulled. 0 such rows live, but quitting is the normal staff lifecycle. Fix as FE-F7-1 and `WorkDetailItem`'s lab did: append the current entity | ✅ fixed (RB3) |
| FE-F8-13 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `PaymentModal.tsx` | altitude: 18 `useState`s and six keyed render-phase blocks that feed each other. A manual Change override **never resumes auto-calculation**: override 5,000, lower the cash, and the stale 5,000 is what gets submitted (only an entry-mode switch clears it). The file re-declares `formatNumber` and `formatCurrency`; `ExpenseTable`, `ExpenseSummary` and `DeleteConfirmModal` each add another `Intl.NumberFormat('en-US')` copy of what `utils/formatters` already has. The success view hand-rolls a `modalClose` ✕ instead of `<ModalHeader>` | ✅ fixed (RX1) — RB3 fixed the override, formatters and `<ModalHeader>`; RX1 did the reducer |
| FE-F8-14 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `PaymentModal.tsx:34-42,155-156,786-799,814-819,413-488` · `PaymentFormDrawer.tsx:18-21,39,42` | **`GET /api/getworkforreceipt` is fetched on every PaymentModal open, and refetched after every work write, for nothing.** It only enriches `receiptData`, and the success view reads just `amountPaidToday` and `currency`, both already known. Dead with it: `workForReceiptQuery`, `qk.work.forReceipt`, the contract and the route (removing the route diffs `route-table.txt`); `ReceiptData`'s other five fields; `Calculations.isExact`; the `ApiResponse` shim built for an `onSuccess` whose only caller ignores it; the MIXED-mode `suggestedUSD/IQD` that `handleMixed*Change` compute but only the non-MIXED branch renders; `PaymentFormDrawer`'s `workInfo` prop. **Since RB2 also dead:** `GET /api/expenses/summary` with `getExpenseSummary` / `getExpenseTotalsByCurrency` and `expense.contract#expenseSummary`, whose only reader RB2 removed | ✅ fixed (RB3) |

**FE-F8-1 — the Expenses summary answers a different question from the table under it.** `ExpenseSummary` prefers the server rollup whenever both dates are set, which is always, because the page defaults to the current month. `GET /api/expenses/summary` (`expense.contract.ts#expenseSummary`, `expense-queries#getExpenseTotalsByCurrency`) filters by date only. So choosing a category, subcategory, lab, employee, currency or daily/monthly type and pressing Apply narrows the table and leaves all three cards at the whole range. Runtime, September 2026: unfiltered, cards 83 / 3,001,750 IQD / 200 USD and the table agrees. Category 1 applied: the table is **33 rows / 151,750 IQD**, and the cards are still **83 / 3,001,750 IQD / 200 USD**. The page's usual question ("how much did we spend on X this month?") is answered with the month's total. The client has everything it needs: `expensesQuery` never sends `limit`, so the list is every matching row, and `ExpenseSummary`'s own fallback over `expenses` is exact. **Fix:** always sum the filtered list client-side (IQD and USD kept apart, as the fallback already does) and drop the summary read. That also retires FE-F8-11's phantom `ExpenseSummary` interface. The alternative is to thread every filter into the summary endpoint, which is more code for the same numbers.

**FE-F8-2 — Edit, Cancel, Edit opens an Add form, and Save duplicates the expense.** `Expenses.tsx` seeds `currentExpense` during render when `editingExpense` (the `expenseByIdQuery(editingId)` result) differs from `seededExpense`. Close resets `editingId` and `currentExpense` but not `seededExpense`. Reopening the same row gets the **same cached object** back from React Query (structural sharing keeps the reference when a background refetch finds nothing changed), so the guard sees nothing new, `currentExpense` stays `null`, and `ExpenseModal` (`initKey = expense?.id ?? 'new'`) renders **Add** mode with today's date and empty fields. Runtime confirmed both halves: the title reads *Add New Expense* with the amount empty, and a Save from there is `POST /api/expenses` (a new, uncategorised expense), not `PUT /api/expenses/:id`. The title does change, so an attentive user notices; one who retypes the amount they came to fix does not, and the month's expenses grow by a duplicate. Any other row in between resets it, which is why it reads as intermittent. **Fix:** derive rather than seed: `const currentExpense = editingId != null ? editingExpense ?? null : null`, and let the modal show a loading state until it arrives. While there: during that load the modal already renders as *Add* with today's date, and a Save in that window would also create.

**FE-F8-3 — the rate the box shows is not the rate it saves.** The inline editor keeps two strings: `newRateValue`, the raw keystrokes, and `displayValues.newRateValue`, rewritten during render as `formatNumber(newRateValue)`. That `formatNumber` is the module-local one: `parseFloat` with no comma stripping, so `"1,56"` → `1`. Save runs `parseFormattedNumber(newRateValue)`, which strips commas: `"1,56"` → `156`. As soon as the display has grouped a 4-digit rate, any edit desynchronises the two. Runtime: `1560` → `1,560`, Backspace → `1`, Save → `{"date":"2026-09-28","exchangeRate":156}`. Nothing catches a rate an order of magnitude off: the contract is `moneyInt.positive()`, the server keeps no range, and only a **backdated** rate gets a confirm. For today the write is immediate. At 156 IQD/USD, $100 of cash on an IQD work converts to 15,000 IQD, the Statistics page restates the day, and the modal's own "never overwrite a day's rate" rule then protects the wrong value. **Fix:** the digits-only pattern `ExpenseModal`/`PaymentFormDrawer` already use (store the number, format from it). Also confirm any rate more than ~10 % away from the carried-forward one it replaces.

**FE-F8-5 — "Auto" is not automatic without a rate.** The suggested-cash block is gated on `entryMode === 'amount' && formData.amountToRegister && exchangeRate`. Its same-currency arms (`suggestedIQD = amountToRegister` for IQD→IQD, `suggestedUSD = amountToRegister` for USD→USD) need no rate. `exchangeRateForDate` carries the last rate forward, so on this clinic `exchangeRate` is null only before the first rate ever recorded. That is exactly day one of every new deployment: the first payments are IQD-in-IQD, the Received box sits at `0` under an "Auto" badge, and Save refuses with *"enter at least one currency amount"*. Runtime (rate endpoint mocked to 404): as described. **Fix:** move the `exchangeRate` requirement into the two cross-currency arms.

**FE-F8-6 — aligner payments ignore the work's currency.** `aligner-payment-queries#createAlignerPayment` documents the rule it relies on: "Aligner sets are USD-only … so the payment lands wholly in usd_received". The amount goes into `invoices.amount_paid` too, and `works.TotalPaid`, the balance, the commissions and the statistics all read `amount_paid` in the **work's** currency. Nothing checks that the work is USD. Live: `aligner_sets` × `works.currency` = USD/USD 89 · NULL/USD 46 · **USD/IQD 8 · NULL/IQD 3**. Works 10419 (2,700,000), 11243 (2,250,000) and 12025 (200,000) are real IQD aligner cases. Their sets have no `set_cost` yet, so the drawer's button is hidden and `SET_COST_NOT_DEFINED` stops the write. The first time someone prices one of those sets, every payment on it credits dinars for dollars. The client shows the set's currency (`set?.currency || 'USD'`) and never the work's, so nothing on screen hints at it. **Fix (F17 / the aligner contract):** refuse a set cost and a set payment when the set and work currencies differ, or book set payments in the work's currency. Until then, `PatientSets` should not offer *Add Payment* on a set whose work is not USD.

## F7 · Works & treatment items · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: `WorkComponent` 927 · `NewWorkComponent` 958 · `WorkDetailItem` 489 · `WorkCard` 396 · `TransferWorkModal` 323 · `config/workTypeConfig` 208 · `WorkDetailsPanel` 124 — 3,425 LOC — plus their six CSS modules (2,138 lines) and the `ContentRenderer` branches that mount them. Cross-checks the surface forced: `shared/contracts/work.contract.ts` (every work body **parsed with `tsx`** against the payloads the forms actually send), `patientInfoRow`, every work / work-item / transfer / invoice gate in `route-table.txt`, `WorkService.validateAndUpdateWork` + `validateAndTransferWork`, `work-queries#updateWork`/`insertFullPaymentInvoice`, `work-item-queries`, `employee-queries#listEmployees` vs `staff.routes#/doctors`, `PaymentModal`'s balance and currency branches, the FK delete rules on `works`/`work_items`, and the live `works`, `work_items`, `employees` + `positions` tables.

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`** (Playwright via `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs`; no screenshots), the dev server started with the three CDC sinks and WhatsApp auto-init **disabled by env**. Every non-GET was **aborted**, with two in-browser exceptions that never reached the server: one `DELETE /api/deleteInvoice/:id` was **fulfilled locally** and the works refetch after it was rewritten (FE-F7-6b), and `/api/auth/me` was mocked to `clinical` for the role check (FE-F7-7). Probed: the doctor select on a quit doctor's and on the Clinic pseudo-doctor's work (FE-F7-1), the Total-field currency flip (FE-F7-3), the history modal against the card on a discounted work and after a payment delete (FE-F7-6), the stray `0` (FE-F7-9), the transfer confirm in Arabic (FE-F7-11), "Dr. Clinic" and the RTL chevron (FE-F7-12), and an item draft vs. collapse (FE-F7-14).

**FE-F7-1 — the Doctor dropdown cannot show the work's own doctor, and it lists the wrong people.**
`NewWorkComponent` fills the Doctor `<select required>` from `employeesQuery('?percentage=true')`, i.e. `listEmployees({ percentage: true })` → `is_active = true AND percentage = true`. `percentage` is the **commission** flag ([[doctor-commission-feature]]), not "is a doctor", and there is no option for the work's current `dr_id`. On this deployment that list is Shwan Elias, Rojena and **Haliz — whose position is Assistant**. It excludes **Snowbar** (Doctor, quit, not on commission — **1,208 works, 104 still active**), the **Clinic** pseudo-doctor (Doctor, active, not on commission — the 211 intake X-ray/consult works) and Yassen (1). For those 1,420 works (18 %), React finds no matching option and selects the placeholder, so the DOM value is `''`. Runtime on work 8942 (Snowbar, active) and 13103 (Clinic): `validationMessage: "Please select an item in the list."`. Update from the Basic tab → 0 writes and a bubble. From the Dates tab (the select is `display:none`) → 0 writes and only `An invalid form control with name='' is not focusable` in the console, i.e. **nothing happens**. The only way to save a notes edit or a discount is to pick a doctor, and the PUT then carries `dr_id:"1"`. That re-attributes the work, and with it every invoice ever paid on it: the Statistics *Commissions* tab sums `invoices.amount_paid` by `works.dr_id`, so past periods change retroactively, and a Clinic X-ray fee becomes doctor commission. For the product (CLAUDE.md: many independent centers, each its own instance), a center whose doctors are salaried has an **empty** required list and **cannot create any work**. **Fix:** source the list from the Doctor position (`doctorsQuery` → `/api/doctors`, which already includes Clinic) and always append the work's current doctor by its joined `doctor_name`, marked "(inactive)" where it applies. `WorkDetailItem:437-440` already does exactly this for a deactivated lab. Give the select a `name` while there. **Owner decision (2026-09-28): commission-flagged non-doctors stay in the list.** So the list is active employees with position Doctor **or** the commission flag (on this deployment: Shwan Elias, Rojena, Haliz, Clinic), plus the work's own doctor even when it matches neither (Snowbar, Yassen). The server-side filter needs an OR (`listEmployees` ANDs its flags today): either a `doctorsOrCommission` filter or a dedicated `/api/work-doctors` read.

**FE-F7-2 — no implant diameter can be saved.** `workItemFields` uses `optInt` = `z.coerce.number().int()` for `implant_length` and `implant_diameter`. The columns are `numeric(5,2)` (`types/db.d.ts`: `Numeric`), `work-item-queries` binds them through `numericOrNull`, and the inputs advertise half- and tenth-millimetre values with `step="0.5"`/`step="0.1"`. Parsed: `{implant_length:'10', implant_diameter:'3.5'}` → `implant_diameter: expected int`; `{implant_length:'11.5', implant_diameter:'4'}` → `implant_length: expected int`. Standard diameters (3.3, 3.5, 4.1, 4.3, 4.8) are all fractional, so recording an implant with its size fails with a 400 toast and the doctor either rounds (wrong clinical data) or leaves it blank. This clinic has recorded one implant row ever, so nobody has hit it. It is 🟠 because the product ships to dental centers where this is the normal path ([[shared-contract-rollout]] enumerated the body on 2026-06-05 and chose `optInt` by analogy with `canals_no`). **Fix:** a non-negative finite `optNum` capped at 999.99 for the two fields. Cross-scope: the contract is shared, so this is one line with no UI change.

**FE-F7-3 — the Total field changes the currency behind the user's back.** `onChange` computes `newCurrency = numericValue > 10000 ? 'IQD' : formData.currency` on **every keystroke** and never switches back. Editing a number in place passes through larger intermediate values: runtime on work 13105 (2,000 USD, 100 paid), End → type `0` → `20,000` / IQD → Backspace → `2,000` / **IQD**, and the PUT sends `currency:"IQD"`. The server's money checks do not see it: `validateAndUpdateWork` lets an admin change currency on any work, and `TOTAL_BELOW_PAID` compares bare numbers (2000 ≥ 100). So the work is re-denominated and its existing USD payments are now read as IQD. A front-desk user gets the same on a same-day work, or an approval request carrying a currency change nobody intended. The only signal is the adjacent select changing value. The heuristic papers over a wrong default: new works start at `'USD'` while 6,180 of 7,786 works (79 %) are IQD, and 2 live USD works already exceed 10,000. No live fingerprint found: the 35 IQD works with totals under 10,000 all predate the heuristic (`1708769`, 2026-01-16) and were never updated through the app. **Fix:** default the currency from a clinic setting (or the patient's estimate currency); never change it implicitly (a hint at most); and refuse a currency change server-side once a work has invoices, because `TOTAL_BELOW_PAID` cannot catch it. Recommended **first** in RB: it is the one money-wrong path in F7, and the client half is three lines.

**FE-F7-4 — the update contract drops one field and rewrites another.** (a) `updateWork.body` enumerates 21 fields and **omits `estimated_duration`** (the create body has it). Zod strips it, `updateWork()` never sees the key, and the user gets "Work updated". Parsed with the form's real payload: `hasOwnProperty('estimated_duration') === false`. The field only drives the card's duration line and the progress estimate (21 works use it), hence 🟡. Fix: add `estimated_duration: optInt`. (b) `discount: z.union([moneyInt, z.null()]).optional()` is documented as "null = the service's clear signal", but a union tries `moneyInt` first and `z.coerce.number()` turns `null` into `0`. The form sends `discount: null` whenever there is no discount, so every edit of an undiscounted work writes `discount = 0`: 36 live rows, all written since 2026-07-13, against 7,746 NULLs. Every reader does `?? 0` / `> 0` today, so nothing shows. Fix: put `z.null()` first, or preprocess `null` separately.

**FE-F7-5 — EUR is an option on a two-currency ledger.** The work form (and the patient estimate and cost presets, both display-only) offers EUR, and `addWork`/`updateWork` accept any string. The money path is USD/IQD only. `invoices` has `usd_received`/`iqd_received` and nothing else. `insertFullPaymentInvoice` does `currency === 'USD' || currency === 'EUR' ? usd_received`, so "Mark as fully paid" books euros into the dollar cash box. `PaymentModal` sets `accountCurrency = workData.currency || 'IQD'` and branches on `'USD'`/`'IQD'`, so a EUR account falls through to the IQD arms. Statistics and commissions separate IQD from USD only, and there is no EUR exchange rate. 0 EUR works exist, which is why nothing is wrong yet. **Fix:** remove EUR from the work form and make `currency` `z.enum(['USD','IQD'])` on the three work bodies until EUR is a real ledger currency. **Owner decision (2026-09-28): remove EUR from works.** RB removes the option and enums the three work bodies. EUR stays on the patient estimate and cost presets, which never touch the ledger; F8 still checks that no payment path can receive one.

**FE-F7-6 — the payment-history box disagrees with the card behind it.** `selectedWorkForPayment` is the `Work` object captured on click. The modal's *Total Paid* and *Balance Remaining* read it, so they never see a refetch, and the balance is `total_required − TotalPaid` with **no discount**. `WorkCard`, `PaymentModal` (`:177`) and the server (`payment-queries.ts:212`) all subtract it. (a) Work 7755 (2,850,000 − 50,000 discount − 2,800,000 paid): the card says *Remaining 0 IQD* with Add Payment disabled; the history says *Balance Remaining 50,000 IQD* and offers "Add New Payment", which opens a PaymentModal that shows 0 and a server that refuses the overpayment. Front desk quotes that 50,000 to the patient. (b) Delete a payment in the modal: the list refreshes (`qk.work.all`), and the card behind it refreshes (`qk.patient.all` → *Paid 100,000 · Remaining 50,000*), but the box still says *Paid 150,000 · Balance 0 · Fully Paid*, so the Add Payment it now owes is hidden. **Fix:** keep only the work **id** in state and derive the row from `works` each render. Put one `workBalance(work)` helper in `utils/` for all three screens (F8 inherits it). Same area: `colSpan={6}` on a 4-column table.

**FE-F7-7 — the work screens offer `clinical` users writes the server refuses.** Server: `updatework`, `finishwork`, `discontinuework`, `reactivatework`, `deletework`, `addworkdetail`/`updateworkdetail`/`deleteworkdetail`, `addInvoice` and `deleteInvoice` are `FINANCE_ROLES`; only `addwork` is open to `clinical`. Client, with `/api/auth/me` mocked to `clinical`: the card's ⋮ menu offers **Edit Work, Mark Complete, Mark Discontinued, Delete Work**; the payment-history modal renders **30 delete buttons and "Add New Payment"**, although `WorkComponent:95` says "money mutations stay hidden" (only the card's Add Payment honours `writeFinance`); the items panel offers Add/Edit/Delete; `NewWorkComponent`'s "Finish & Add New" calls `/api/finishwork`; and the `new-work?workId=` edit route has no guard. Each ends in "Insufficient permissions" after the work is done. Same shape and same fix as **FE-F6-6**: one capability in `roleCaps`, used for all of these, replacing the two ad-hoc `isAdmin` booleans. **Owner call:** `WorkDetailsPanel` is deliberately the *doctor-facing* panel (English, clinical fields: teeth, canals, implant sizes), yet since `f25bf11` (2026-06-25, no rationale recorded) a doctor cannot write a treatment item. Either open the three item routes to `CLINICAL_ROLES` and keep `item_cost` finance-only, or accept that doctors dictate items to the front desk and hide the panel's buttons. **Owner decision (2026-09-28): doctors and assistants write treatment items.** RB opens `addworkdetail`/`updateworkdetail`/`deleteworkdetail` to `CLINICAL_ROLES` (route table snapshot diff expected). `item_cost` stays finance-only: the server ignores it from a clinical caller and the input hides behind `writeFinance`. The panel then needs no role gate; the rest of this finding (work lifecycle, invoices, Finish & Add New) still does.

**FE-F7-8 — lifecycle writes refresh one patient and no work.** The update path does it right: `qk.patient.all` + `qk.work.all(workId)`. The five lifecycle writes on the Works page invalidate only `qk.patient.all(personId)`. Left stale for up to the 30 s `staleTime`: `workDetailsQuery` (`PatientShell`'s work header on visits / new-visit / diagnosis), the cached `transferPreview`, and on transfer the **target** patient's works/info/type. The natural sequence (look up target B, return to A, transfer, open B) can therefore show B **without** the work, as if the transfer failed. Reopening Transfer on that work within 30 s shows the old "From". Fix: add `qk.work.all(work.work_id)` at the five sites and `qk.patient.all(targetPatientId)` in `handleTransferSuccess`, which already receives it as `_result`. Aligner lists (`qk.aligner.*`) are also untouched by work writes. That is F17/F18's to judge.

**FE-F7-11 — the transfer confirm, in detail.** (a) `.transferArrow` is a flex row, so under `dir="rtl"` FROM lands on the right and TO on the left, while the glyph stays `fa-arrow-right`, pointing *at* FROM. The labels are explicit, which is why this is 🔵, but it is the one diagram on an irreversible action. The whole modal is English inside the RTL Works route, next to `PatientQuickSearch`'s Arabic placeholder; that is staged rollout, not re-filed. (b) The catch reads `(error as HttpError).data.message`; `sendError` puts the text in `error`, so "Target patient not found" / "Work not found" always become the generic fallbacks. Use `httpErrorMessage`. (c) The modal toasts "Work transferred to X" and `WorkComponent` then toasts `toast.transferred`. (d) `getTotalRelatedRecords` adds `wires` (distinct wire-type ids referenced by the visits, i.e. lookups, not records that move) and `alignerBatches` (never listed): runtime "Related Records (3 total)" = 1 visit + 1 payment + **1 wire**.

**FE-F7-14 — the inline item editor has no memory.** `WorkCard` renders `WorkDetailsPanel` only while expanded, and the whole header is the toggle, so one click on it unmounts every `WorkDetailItem`. Runtime: Add Item, type a note, collapse and re-expand → the draft is gone, with no prompt. Separately, `WorkComponent`'s auto-expand re-seed is keyed on the `works` identity, so any works refetch that returns changed data (a payment added and closed, a focus refetch after a colleague's edit) resets the expansion to the active work only and collapses the finished work being inspected. The item-delete confirm says only "delete this work detail?", but `lab_cases.work_item_id` is `ON DELETE CASCADE`, so a started lab case and its stage history go with it (0 lab cases live). **Fix:** a dirty draft makes collapse ask (RA5's `useConfirm` idiom); key the re-seed on `personId`; name the lab case in the confirm.

## F6 · Patient demographics & search · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: `PatientManagement` 945 (+ `routes/PatientManagement` 11) · `AddPatientForm` 800 · `EditPatientComponent` 715 · `ViewPatientInfo` 684 · `PatientFolderPicker` 136 · `PatientTypesReadOnly` 74 — ~3.4k LOC — plus the pieces they lean on: `PhoneInput` + `utils/phoneFormatter`, the `patientManagementLoader`/`patientShellLoader` and the `ContentRenderer` branches that mount the three forms, and their CSS modules' `desktopOnly`/`mobileOnly`/`tableLoadingOverlay` rules. Cross-checks the surface forced: `shared/contracts/patient.contract.ts` (`patientSearch`, `createPatient`, `updatePatient`, `estimatedCost`, `patientInfoRow`), the create/update/delete handlers in `routes/api/patient.routes.ts` and `patient-queries.ts#updatePatient`, every patient-endpoint gate in `route-table.txt`, `shared/auth/roles.ts#roleCaps`, the three reminder builders that read `patients.language`/`country_code` (`whatsapp-batch-plan.ts`, `messaging-queries.ts` ×2, `reminder-format.ts#formatPhone`), the legacy SQL-Server procs in git (`1becf49`), and the live `patients` table (phone lengths, `language`/`country_code` distributions, untrimmed names, `ix_name_id`).

**Runtime-verified** (Playwright via `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs`; no screenshots) on dev `:5273` **and on a production React bundle** (`vite build` into the scratchpad, served by `vite preview` on `:5299` against the same dev API) — the second was needed because **dev's `<StrictMode>` masks FE-F6-3**: its double effect run clears `isRestoring` on the first pass and fires a search on the second. Every non-GET was captured and **aborted**; the one read that needed specific rows (`GET /api/patients/1454`, phone/phone2 lengthened) was fulfilled locally, and FE-F6-3's "edited elsewhere" row was a rewritten search response — nothing touched the live DB. The dev server ran with all three CDC sinks and WhatsApp auto-init **disabled by env** (the `.env` points at the live clinic PostgreSQL).

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F6-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `AddPatientForm.tsx:66,116,660-673` + `createPatient.body` | the Add form's **Alerts** textarea is **silently discarded** — `alerts` is not in the create contract, `z.object` strips it, and nothing else reads it. Runtime: the POST carried `"alerts":"Allergic to latex"`, `createPatient.body.parse` drops it. Dead since `patients.alerts` was retired for the alerts table (2025-11-20) | ✅ fixed (RB1) |
| FE-F6-2 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `AddPatientForm.tsx:574-576` · `EditPatientComponent.tsx:540-542` · `ViewPatientInfo.tsx:180-187` vs `whatsapp-batch-plan.ts:117` · `messaging-queries.ts:697,899` | **cross-scope:** the forms label `language` **0 = Kurdish · 1 = Arabic · 2 = English**; every reminder builder sends **English when `language === 1`, else Arabic** (the legacy procs' `IF @Language = 1 -- English`). Choosing *English* gets a patient Arabic reminders; choosing *Arabic* gets English ones. The UI has never matched (the first React form, 2025-07-02, had 0 English · 1 Arabic · 2 Kurdish) | ✅ fixed (RB1) |
| FE-F6-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientManagement.tsx:221-258,205,354-359,457-461` | the `pm_search_state` snapshot is written from the effect's **cleanup**, so it is always **one commit behind** — a reload restores the *previous* search's rows under the current search text (prod bundle: box `Rand`, **42 `Ahm` rows**, 0 searches) — and a restore is **never revalidated**, so a row edited or deleted meanwhile stays as it was (prod bundle: renamed server-side, old name shown, 0 searches). The deliberate loader exception is hiding exactly the stale-cache bug the plan asked about | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F6-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `EditPatientComponent.tsx:472-488` + `PhoneInput` (`PHONE_MASK` = 10 digits) | a stored number longer than 10 digits is **truncated at load** (IMask fires `onAccept` on mount) and the two phone handlers use `setFormData({...formData, …})`, so the second mount-time accept overwrites the first. Runtime (dev **and** prod bundle): both inputs show `770 123 4567`; Save sends phone **`77012345678`** (not what is shown) and phone2 **`7701234567`** (13 → 10 digits). Any unrelated edit silently cuts phone2. Live: 3 phones of 11 digits, 1 phone2 of 13 | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F6-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `AddPatientForm.tsx:747-789` + `.module.css:372-414` | the desktop tabs **and** the mobile accordion both render every field; the CSS-hidden accordion keeps Basic mounted. On desktop, from any tab but Basic, an empty name **blocks Save with no message** — runtime: 0 POSTs, no alert, only `An invalid form control with name='patientName' is not focusable`. Also: `#add-patient-name` ×2 (mobile labels resolve to the hidden desktop copy) and 6 radios in **one** `intakeKind` group | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F6-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientManagement.tsx:878-879` · `ViewPatientInfo.tsx:332-342,613-619` · `EditPatientComponent` (no guard) | patient **edit, delete and estimated-cost** are `authorize(admin\|front_desk)` server-side, but the client offers them to every role — a `clinical` user fills the whole edit form and gets "Insufficient permissions" at Save. `roleCaps` has no patient-write capability to gate on | ✅ fixed (RB1) |
| FE-F6-7 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `patient.routes.ts:503-511` + `patient-queries.ts:531` · the two forms | **create vs edit divergence**: the name is trimmed on create but **not on update** (`"علي "` passes `ix_name_id` beside `"علي"`; 4 live rows untrimmed); `dir`/`lang` on the name inputs only in Add; `notes` capped at 100 only in Add (column is unbounded `citext`); phone2 free text in Add, masked in Edit (FE-F6-4); both country-code placeholders teach a leading `+` that the SMS builder's `+${formatPhone(…)}` does not strip (`++964…`; 0 live rows today) | ✅ fixed (RB4) |

The shell loader never prefetches `patientByIdQuery`, so every open also shows a spinner first | ✅ fixed (RB4) |

Its two browser-locale date formats are already FE-F3-3's census — not re-filed | ✅ fixed (RB4) |

**FE-F6-1 — the Alerts box on the Add form goes nowhere.**
`AddPatientForm` keeps `alerts` in its form state, renders it on the Additional tab under the label "Alerts" with the placeholder "Important alerts or warnings...", and posts the whole `formData`. `createPatient.body` is a plain `z.object` with no `alerts` key, so `validate()` strips it — the code comment at `:240-243` even says so ("The bare `formData` also carries `alerts`, which the strict create body strips") — and the create handler builds `processedData` from named fields only. Runtime: the POST body was `{…,"notes":"","alerts":"Allergic to latex","intake":{…}}`; `createPatient.body.parse({ patientName:'x', alerts:'Allergic to latex' })` returns `{"patientName":"x"}`. Git: `80b305d` (2025-11-20) removed the `alerts` column from the create path when patient alerts moved to their own table, and the textarea was never re-plumbed. Why 🟠: the field is a clinical-safety input on the intake path — an allergy typed at registration is thrown away, the success toast fires, and the patient's alert list stays empty. **Fix (owner's call between two):** (a) keep the box and persist it — add an optional `initialAlert` (details, and a default severity/type) to `createPatient.body` and insert one `alerts` row inside the create transaction; or (b) remove the textarea and its two `add.alerts*` keys and let staff add alerts from `ViewPatientInfo`, which already works. (a) is the better intake UX; (b) is five deleted lines.

**FE-F6-2 — the language picker and the reminders disagree about what the numbers mean.**
Three reminder builders read `patients.language`: `whatsapp-batch-plan.ts:117` and `messaging-queries.ts:697` (SMS) — `language === 1 ? English body : Arabic body` — and `messaging-queries.ts:899` (the per-appointment message), the same branch. That is the legacy procs' codebook, verbatim (`1becf49`: `IF @Language = 1 -- English`, `ELSE -- Default to Arabic (Language = 0, NULL, or any other value)`). The three demographics screens use a different one: `<option value="0">Kurdish</option><option value="1">Arabic</option><option value="2">English</option>` in both forms, and `ViewPatientInfo.getLanguageDisplay` maps the same way. So today: a front-desk user marks an English-speaking patient **English** → stored `2` → the patient gets **Arabic** reminders; marks a patient **Arabic** → stored `1` → **English** reminders. The first React form (`33d2c76`, 2025-07-02) had a third codebook (`0 English · 1 Arabic · 2 Kurdish`), changed to the current labels in `490605b` (2025-11-14) — neither ever matched the senders. Live data: 6,840 × `0`, **3 × `1`** (1454 Rand, 3444 Joseph, 3447 Rejwan — Latin first names, plausibly set under the legacy app when `1` meant English, i.e. correctly *stored* and wrongly *displayed* as "Arabic" — runtime: patient 1454's Edit form shows **Arabic**), **1 × `2`** (6871, set in the July–November window when `2` was labelled Kurdish). Why 🟠: patient-facing messages go out in the wrong language for anyone whose language the staff set, on a normal path; for a multi-deployment product with English-speaking patients it is every such patient. **Fix — owner call on the codebook, recommended:** keep the stored meaning the senders and the legacy data already share (`1` = English, anything else = Arabic body) and relabel the UI to it — `0` Arabic (default) · `1` English · `2` Kurdish (Arabic body until a Kurdish template exists) — in one `shared/patient-language.ts` both sides import, so the labels and the builders cannot drift again. Then decide row 6871 by hand. Touches three translated screens (the `languages.*` keys stay; only the value↔label mapping moves).

**FE-F6-3 — the "cached view" is one step stale and never refreshed.**
Two defects in the snapshot that makes `PatientManagement` the loader exception. (a) **One commit behind.** The persistence effect has no body and saves from its cleanup; a cleanup runs *before* the next effect, with the *previous* render's closure. So after every change, `sessionStorage` holds the state before it, and only a real unmount writes the current one — a reload never unmounts. Prod bundle, keystroke by keystroke: type `Ahm` (42 rows), then `Rand` (4 rows) → storage holds `searchFirstName:"Rand"` with the **42 `Ahm` rows**; reload → the box reads `Rand`, the table shows the 42 `Ahm` rows, and `isRestoring` suppresses the one search that would correct it (0 searches after reload). Reset-then-reload resurrects the reset results the same way. (b) **Never revalidated.** On return from anywhere, `isRestoring` skips the auto-search and nothing else refetches, so the table is whatever it was when the user left. `EditPatientComponent` returns *here* after Save (via `state.from`) and after Delete (`navigate('/patient-management')`), so the edited row shows its old name/phone/tag and a deleted patient stays listed, one click from a dead record. Prod bundle: open a patient from the list, the search response for that row now says `EDITED ELSEWHERE`, Back → old name, **0 searches**. Dev hides both: `<StrictMode>` runs the auto-search effect twice on mount, the first pass clears `isRestoring`, the second schedules a search (dev fired 1 search after reload and 1 on return). **Fix:** save in the effect **body** (every change, current values), and on restore paint the snapshot and then **revalidate in the background** — one `executeSearch` for the restored criteria with `limit = patients.length` (so Load-More depth and scroll restoration survive), not a reset to page 1. The exception itself stays justified (scroll restoration needs the rows on first paint); it just must not also be the source of truth.

**FE-F6-4 — the Edit form rewrites phone numbers it was only asked to display.**
`PhoneInput` is an `IMaskInput` with `PHONE_MASK = '000 000 0000'` and `unmask`. Given a longer stored value, the mask keeps the first 10 digits and emits `accept` **at mount**, which calls the form's `onChange`. Both phone handlers are `setFormData({...formData, phone: value})` — a closure over the render's `formData` — and both accepts fire in the same tick, so the phone2 write spreads the stale `formData` and **undoes** the phone write. Runtime, GET mocked to `phone:"77012345678"`, `phone2:"7701234567890"`, then Save (PUT aborted), identical on dev and the prod bundle: both inputs **display** `770 123 4567`; the body carries `phone:"77012345678"` (the user saw 10 digits, 11 are saved) and `phone2:"7701234567"` (13 digits cut to 10 — **any** save of that patient, e.g. to fix a typo in notes, corrupts phone2). Live exposure is small today (3 phones with 11 digits, 1 phone2 with 13), and `AddPatientForm`'s phone2 is a free-text `<input type="tel">`, so creation happily stores what Edit later truncates. **Fix:** functional updaters (`setFormData(prev => …)`) for every field in the form (it is the same pattern throughout); stop the mount-time rewrite (ignore an `onAccept` whose value equals the unmasked prop, or seed the mask without firing); and settle the length rule once for both forms — the 10-digit mask cannot hold an international number either (E.164 allows 15), which a `country_code` field implies the product supports.

**FE-F6-5 — two copies of the form, and the hidden one blocks Save.**
The desktop tab view renders only the active tab; the mobile accordion (CSS `display:none` above 768 px) renders every expanded section, and Basic starts expanded. So on desktop the DOM holds a second, hidden `patientName` input with `required`. With the Contact, Medical or Additional tab active the visible copy is unmounted, the only `patientName` left is the hidden one, and on an empty name Chrome's interactive validation cannot focus it: it logs `An invalid form control with name='patientName' is not focusable` and cancels the submit without a bubble — the handler's own "name required" alert never runs. Runtime: 0 POSTs, no alert on screen, that console error. The same duplication gives two `#add-patient-name` (and every other id), so on a phone the labels resolve to the hidden desktop copy and the visible inputs have no accessible name; and the six `intakeKind` radios form **one** group across both copies, so at most one is `:checked` (runtime: 1 of 6 — harmless visually only because the marks are drawn from state). **Fix:** render one layout (a `useMediaQuery` switch, or one field set whose container restyles), not two.

**FE-F6-6 — the patient-write buttons ignore the role.**
Server gates: `PUT /api/patients/:personId` and `PUT …/estimated-cost` are `authorize(admin|front_desk)`; `DELETE` adds `requireRecordAge`. Client: `PatientManagement` shows the Edit and Delete icons on every row for every role; `ViewPatientInfo` shows **Edit Patient** and the cost pencil; the `edit-patient` route has no guard. A `clinical` user (the "Doctor / Assistant" role — `POST /api/patients` *is* open to them, so they are expected on these screens) can open the form, edit, and learn at Save that they get "Insufficient permissions". No such user exists on this deployment today (1 admin, 1 front desk), which is why nobody has hit it; the role is assignable and the product ships to other centers. **Fix:** a `writePatients` flag in `roleCaps` (the registry forbids ad-hoc role checks) and gate the three surfaces on it; alert add/edit/dismiss stay open to all roles, matching the server.

**FE-F6-7 — create and edit validate different things.** (a) The create handler trims every string (`patientName.trim()` etc.); the update handler and `updatePatient` write `patient_name` verbatim, so `"علي "` is stored with its space and passes the `ix_name_id` unique index beside `"علي"` — the duplicate guard both forms rely on. 4 live names are untrimmed. The fix is server-side (trim in the update path), with a client-side trim as belt-and-braces. (b) Add marks the Arabic name `dir="rtl" lang="ar"` and the English names `dir="ltr"`; Edit marks none, so in an Arabic session the English names are typed right-aligned. (c) Add caps notes at 100 characters; Edit has no cap; the column is unbounded `citext`, so Add's cap is the arbitrary one. (d) phone2 — see FE-F6-4. (e) Country code: Add defaults `964` but its placeholder says "e.g., +1, +44"; Edit's placeholder is the literal `+964`. Both are stored verbatim. The WhatsApp path normalizes, but the SMS builder does `+${formatPhone(phone, cc)}`, and `formatPhone` does not strip a leading `+` from the code, so a `+964` entry produces `++9647…`. 0 live rows carry a `+` today; the placeholders are teaching staff to create them.

**FE-F6-8 — the edit form can save an old copy of the patient.** `patientByIdQuery` (`/api/patients/:id`, key `qk.patient.full`) is not prefetched by `patientShellLoader` (which warms `patientInfoQuery`, a different endpoint). The render-phase seed runs when `patientKey` (the `person_id`) changes, so it takes whatever `data` is present first. That is the cached row when one exists (up to 5 min, `gcTime`), and the stale-on-mount refetch that follows lands in `data` but is ignored. Save then PUTs every column from the seeded state. Scenario: the admin opens Edit on a patient and cancels; front desk changes the phone on another PC; within 5 min the admin reopens Edit to fix the notes and saves — the phone reverts, and nothing on screen showed the newer value. Fix: `refetchOnMount: 'always'` for this read and seed only once the mount fetch has settled (`!isFetching`), or show the form after it. Prefetching it in the shell loader for `page === 'edit-patient'` also removes the spinner-first open.

**FE-F6-9 — the patient phone book outlives the patient.** `qk.lookups.patientPhones` (`/api/patients/phones`, name + phones for every patient) feeds `PatientManagement`'s two jump comboboxes, `SendMessage`'s and `TelegramShareModal`'s recipient pickers, and `PatientQuickSearch`. F2 classed `lookups.*` as never-invalidated-and-correct, but this leaf is patient data, not controlled vocabulary. Delete from the list → `executeSearch()` refreshes the table, but the combobox still offers the patient, and Enter/ArrowDown jumps to `/patient/:id/works` for a record that no longer exists. A new or renamed patient is missing or misnamed until a stale refetch happens. Invalidate it on the three writes. Same area: `AddPatientForm`'s success path schedules `onSuccess` 1.5 s out and never clears the timer, so clicking away inside that window still navigates to the new patient.

**FE-F6-10 — four casts over parsed reads.** `ViewPatientInfo` does `(patientInfoData ?? null) as PatientInfo | null` and `(alertsData ?? []) as Alert[]` over reads that `patientInfoQuery`/`patientAlertsQuery` have already typed from their contracts. `PatientInfo` disagrees with `patientInfoRow`: `gender?: string` vs `number | null`, and every nullable becomes optional, so `null` flows where the type says it can't. `PatientFolderPicker` re-asserts `fileExplorer.FileListing` over `patientFilesQuery`'s own result. `PatientManagement` passes a hand-written `Patient` as `fetchJSON`'s generic over `patientSearch.response`, whose row is `looseObject({ person_id, patient_name })` — the six other columns the table renders (`first_name`, `last_name`, `phone`, `date_added`, `last_visit`, `TagName`) are typed only by the interface. Fix per the CLAUDE.md rule: model those columns on the contract and let the data flow in without the cast.

**FE-F6-11 — `ViewPatientInfo` small resilience.** (a) `if (error) return <error card>` checks `error` before `patientInfo`. When a background refetch fails (focus refetch during a server restart, after the retries), RQ keeps `data` but sets `error`, so the page swaps a perfectly good record for "Retry". Render the error only when there is no data, and toast otherwise. (b) The alert ✕ ("archive") dismisses on one click with no confirm. For a *context* alert that is final from the UI, because `TasksHistory` lists push tasks only. Use `useConfirm`, or an undo toast. (c) DOB and date-added go through `toLocaleDateString(undefined, …)`, i.e. the browser's locale, not the app language. They are 2 of FE-F3-3's 53 sites, already filed, and not re-filed here.

**FE-F6-12 — the translated forms' a11y gaps.** `AddPatientForm`'s tab strip is plain buttons: no `role="tablist"`/`tab`/`aria-selected`/`aria-controls`, so a screen reader cannot tell which section is showing. The Add alert `<div>` and the Edit error banner have no `role="alert"`/`aria-live`, so validation, duplicate-name and network messages are silent. Icon-only buttons with no name: `ViewPatientInfo`'s cost ✓ and ✕, and the Edit error banner's ✕ (the alert edit/archive and cost pencil do carry `title`).

**FE-F6-13 — `PatientManagement` is a reducer waiting to happen.** 27 `useState`s: 20 are search criteria that are always read, saved, reset and cleared together. The "is there any criterion" rule is written three times (`hasInputs`, `hadCriteria` — which alone includes `nameStartsWith` — and `activeFilterCount`), and Reset/Show All each clear the same 17 fields by hand. There are two sort UIs (the summary toggle and the sortable `<th>`s) over one `sortConfig`. `urlSearchParam` comes from `window.location.search` rather than the router. Quick check-in toggles the same `loading` flag as search, so a check-in finishing mid-search ends the search's spinner early. Show All fetches without an `AbortController`, unlike every other search, so a slow Show All that lands after a newer filtered search overwrites it. A `useReducer` (or a `usePatientSearch` hook owning criteria + snapshot + FE-F6-3's revalidation) would retire most of this. The file is untranslated and off the ratchet — staged rollout, not a finding.

**FE-F6-14 — dead weight.** `SavedState.lastAppointmentCustomDate`/`hasFinalPhotos` and their two migration reads: `sessionStorage` dies with its tab, so no snapshot from before those fields can still exist. **Two endpoints for one lookup table:** `/api/patients/type-options` (`{id, type}`, used by `PatientManagement`'s filter) and `/api/patient-types` (`{id, name}`, used by the edit form and `PatientTypesReadOnly`) both read `patient_types` into two cache entries — retire `type-options` and map `name`. `EditPatientComponent`'s `validPersonId = personId ?? patientData?.person_id` and the `pid ?? formData.person_id` fallbacks are dead because the record only loads when `personId` is set, and `person_id`/`patient_type_id` ride along in the PUT body only for the contract to strip. `PatientFolderPicker`'s `enabled: personId != null` is always true on a `number`. Two stale comments — `routes/PatientManagement.tsx`'s `// grid.css -> GridComponent.module.css` and `PatientShell.tsx:44-46`'s CSS-migration notes — are leftovers.

## F5 · Header bells, dashboard, statistics · 2026-09-27

Scope read in full: the four header bells (`ApprovalsBell` 364 · `TasksBell` 285 · `PortalActivityBell` 276 · `MyApprovalsBadge` 194), `AlertModal` 380 · `TaskFormModal` 354 · `routes/TasksHistory` 255 · `services/{tasks,approvals,portal-activity}` 135 · `routes/Dashboard` 281 · `StatisticsComponent` 858 · `RevenueBreakdownView` 289 · `DoctorCommissionsView` 134 · `routes/Statistics` 9 · `utils/chartSetup` + `chartTheme` 79 — plus the two statistics children the plan did not list, `PeriodNavigator` 106 and `DoctorPaymentsModal` 181 (the latter is F8's by the plan; read here because both statistics tabs drill into it). ~3.9k LOC. Cross-checks the surface forced: the four contracts behind it (`approvals`, `task`, `portal-activity`, `reports`), every bell endpoint's gate in `app/__snapshots__/route-table.txt`, the server read-time visibility rules (`alert-queries.ts#getHeaderTasks`/`getAlertsByPersonId`/`getAllTasks`), the approval apply/route outcome mapping (`services/approvals/*`, `routes/api/approval.routes.ts`), the portal's insert/update paths in `aligner-portal-external/src/lib/api.ts`, and both databases' `TimeZone`/`LOCALTIMESTAMP`.

**Runtime-verified on dev `:5273`** (Playwright via `scripts/e2e/auth.mjs`; no screenshots). Every write in the probe was **intercepted and aborted** (or fulfilled locally), and the two reads it needed rows for — `/api/tasks`, `/api/approvals?status=pending` — were mocked, so nothing touched the live DB: the snooze keystroke commit (FE-F5-4), the edit body with "None" chosen (FE-F5-5), the GETs after an approve on a patient's works page (FE-F5-2), the confirm-plus-popover Escape and the Tab order out of a bell (FE-F5-12), the two statistics year inputs (FE-F5-9) and the cleared breakdown date (FE-F5-8).

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F5-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | Supabase `LOCALTIMESTAMP` · `set_updated_at_remote()` · `cdc-schema.ts#lwwUpdateClause` | **cross-scope (sync):** the mirror runs in **UTC**, so every portal-originated timestamp is stamped **3 h behind** the clinic's wall clock — the bell shows a fresh event as "3h", and reverse-sync LWW drops a portal UPDATE made within 3 h of a staff edit of the same row | ✅ fixed 2026-09-27 — guard + runbook, then the mirror `ALTER DATABASE` applied and the stale sessions recycled |
| FE-F5-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `ApprovalsBell.tsx:114-125` | approving a hold **applies** a work/invoice/expense/patient write, but the bell invalidates only `qk.approvals.mine()` — the admin's open patient/works screen keeps showing the pre-approval state. Runtime-confirmed | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `ApprovalsBell.tsx:124` + `approval.routes.ts:145-158` | a single Approve the server resolves as **`stale`/`missing`/`failed`** comes back **HTTP 200**; the bell ignores the returned `status`, shows no message, and the row vanishes exactly as a success does | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `TasksBell.tsx:252-256` | the custom-snooze date input commits on **every** `onChange` — typing `10` `15` `2` sent `{"snoozedUntil":"0002-10-15"}` (runtime-confirmed), a valid past date, so the task never leaves the bell | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `TaskFormModal.tsx:151-159,301-312` | the task edit form's two optional selects **cannot clear**: "None" category is dropped from the body (runtime-confirmed) and silently kept; a since-quit assignee renders as "Anyone (unassigned)" while the save preserves them | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-6 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `TasksBell` · `TaskFormModal` · `TasksHistory` → `qk.patient.alerts` | a patient-linked task **is** a row in that patient's alert list (`getAlertsByPersonId` has no `surface_mode` filter), but no task write refreshes it — and the "patient view listens to `tasks:changed`" comments are false | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-7 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `routes/TasksHistory.tsx:49-54` | `displayStatus` ignores `expires_at` — an expired task the bell hides reads **Active** in history, and "Reopen" on one reports success for a task that can never reappear; the "full task log" is the latest 200 | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-8 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `RevenueBreakdownView.tsx:209` · `DoctorCommissionsView.tsx:25` · `PeriodNavigator.tsx:38` | both tabs spread the factory and then **override its `enabled`**, dropping the empty-date guard — clearing From fires `?startDate=&…` → 400 "Invalid request query" (runtime-confirmed); the month chevrons on an empty start produce `NaN-NaN-NaN` | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-9 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `StatisticsComponent.tsx:568,721-750` | the month-nav year input fetches on every keystroke (`year=2`, `20`, `202`, `2025` — runtime-confirmed) and writes `year=NaN` to the URL on clear; the Yearly view's From/To inputs reject every intermediate keystroke and **cannot be typed into at all** | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F5-10 | [BUG] | 🔵 | `StatisticsComponent.tsx:121,163,172,179` | three `as` casts bridge the parsed reports payloads into hand-written view-models (FE-F3-8's shape); the one field the money notice depends on, `exchangeRate`, **isn't on the contract**; plus an ad-hoc `isAdmin` literal the role registry forbids | ✅ fixed (RB4) |

Pairs with FE-F3-9 | ✅ fixed (RC2, via FE-F11-8b; ledger row closed in RB4) |
| FE-F5-12 | [ENH] | 🟡 | the four bell popovers | portaled to the end of `<body>` as `role="dialog"` with **no focus move** — Tab from a bell lands on the next header button (runtime-confirmed), so Approve/Reject/Done/Snooze are mouse-only; one Escape with the Approve-all confirm open closes the confirm **and** the popover | ✅ fixed (RB4) |

Carries the **FE-F1-10 verdict: retire the buses** | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
