**Never invalidated by anything, and that is correct** — do not file these: every `lookups.*` leaf except `labs`/`employees`/`costPresets` (seed/controlled vocabulary, edited only through `adminLookups.table`) — **and except `lookups.patientPhones`, which is patient data, not vocabulary: F6 moved it to the defect list (FE-F6-9)**, every `reports.*` leaf (read-only rollups keyed by period), `auth.me`, `adminLookups.tables`, `settings.{supabase,dolphin,telegram}Status` + `telegram.sendProgress` + `tvDisplay` (own `refetchInterval`), `threeshape.*` (live pull, `retry:false`), `media.photoTypes`, `dailyInvoices`, ~~`visit.byId`~~ (**F9: wrong — it is a defect, FE-F9-2**: the edit form seeds from it and a save never refreshes it), `appointments.daily` + `whatsapp.messages`/`messageCount` (SSE-driven — the server's `broadcastDays()` fans out on every appointment write; **F16: true of `whatsapp.messages`, not of `messageCount`, which no event refreshes: FE-F16-11**), `labCases.*` + ~~`slideshow.list`~~ (**F15: wrong twice.** The slideshow's three writes do invalidate it, but only the current patient's list, and a generic template is in every patient's list: FE-F15-7) + `portalActivity.list` + `approvals.list`/`history` + `announcements.*` (refetched by their own screens' `refetch()`/`refetchInterval`).

**Never invalidated and that is a defect** — the `qk.calendar.*` family: see FE-F2-1.

### Findings

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F2-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | 5 write sites | **no appointment write outside the calendar screen invalidates `qk.calendar.*`** — the booking slot picker can offer a slot that was taken seconds earlier | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F2-2 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `query/loaderQuery.ts:39` + `router/loaders.ts:121` | both 401 branches are **unreachable**: `public/index.html`'s `window.fetch` interceptor strips the status first, so session expiry renders the generic 500 card and `queryClient.clear()` never runs | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `query/client.ts:58` | `retry: 2` retries **every** error including 4xx, though the comment says "transient network/5xx" — six factories already hand-patch `retry: false` | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `PatientAppointments.tsx:56` | deleting an appointment invalidates `patient.appointments` but not its sibling `patient.hasAppointment`, so the Works screen keeps claiming a future appointment | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F2-5 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `router/routes.config.tsx:502` | the `/calendar` route is annotated "100% SSE-driven real-time data" — it has **no SSE at all**; this is the comment that makes FE-F2-1 look like it can't happen | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F2-6 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `router/loaders.ts:415` | `patientManagementLoader` hand-rolls 5 raw `fetchJSON` reads that duplicate 5 existing `queries.ts` factories and land **outside** the RQ cache | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-7 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `router/loaders.ts:231` | the diagnosis deep-link is the one patient route with **no chunk warm-up** — its route has no `:page` segment, so `preloadPatientPage(page)` no-ops | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-8 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `query/keys.ts:96,102` | `qk.appointments` and `qk.whatsapp` are **namespace fictions** — their leaves have different root segments, so no parent key can invalidate the group | ✅ fixed (RC2) |
| FE-F2-9 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `router/loaders.ts:207-278` | `patientShellLoader`'s entire return payload is unread — `PatientShell` has no `useLoaderData`; the loader is purely a prefetcher | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-10 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `query/keys.ts`, `queries.ts`, `loaders.ts` | 10 `qk` keys with zero references, plus the 4 FE-F0-1 items this session was asked to adjudicate (verdict below) | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F2-11 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `config/labStages.ts:31` | the framework-try-in label keys off `MATERIAL_OPTIONS[0]/[1]/[3]` **by index** — inserting a material silently relabels a lab stage | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F2-12 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | `ContentRenderer.tsx:233,244,255` | three "coming soon" placeholder pages (`details`/`history`/`messages`) shipped and reachable by URL, unreachable from the nav | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F2-13 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `query/useApiMutation.ts` + `App.tsx:110` | `useApiMutation` — CLAUDE.md's "standard for new mutations" — is used by **4 files** against ~100 hand-rolled invalidate sites; `App.tsx`'s docblock still advertises the deleted 5-minute cache | ✅ fixed (RX1) |

**FE-F2-1 — the booking picker can hand out a slot that was taken 10 seconds ago.**
Five write paths create, move or delete an appointment from outside `/calendar`, and **not one of them touches `qk.calendar.*`**: `AppointmentForm.tsx:231` (create) and `EditAppointmentForm.tsx:280` (reschedule) invalidate only `qk.patient.all(personId)`; `PatientAppointments.tsx:56` (delete) invalidates only `qk.patient.appointments`; and `WorkComponent.tsx:462` + `PatientManagement.tsx:464` (quick check-in — which **creates** a same-day appointment) invalidate **nothing at all**. The stale reads are `qk.calendar.slots(date,'all')` and `qk.calendar.availability(...)` — which are exactly what `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` renders, i.e. the control inside those same two forms whose job is to stop a double-book — plus `qk.calendar.range/month/stats` behind `AppointmentCalendar`. Nothing self-heals it: the calendar subscribes to **no** SSE (grep: no `EventSource`, no `sse-appointments`, no `refetchInterval` anywhere in `AppointmentCalendar.tsx` or `components/react/calendar/`), and its only refresh is the manual `refetch()` at `AppointmentCalendar.tsx:259`. Failure scenario: front desk books patient A into 2026-09-17 10:00, closes the form, opens `/patient/2/new-appointment` for patient B within the 30 s global `staleTime`, picks 2026-09-17 → `availableSlotsQuery('2026-09-17')` serves the cached pre-booking slot list → 10:00 still shows free → double-booked. Bounded by `staleTime: 30_000`, which is why it is 🟠 and not 🔴, but the window is the exact duration of "next patient, same day". Fix: add `qk.calendar.all()` (and `qk.patient.all(personId)` for the two that invalidate nothing) to all five sites. See also FE-F2-5 — the route comment asserts this is impossible — and FE-F2-8 for why there is no `qk.appointments.all()` to reach for.

**FE-F2-2 — the server-state layer's 401 handling is dead code; `index.html` owns it.**
`loaderQuery` (`:39`) and `withAuth` (`loaders.ts:121`) both branch on `httpErr.status === 401` to clear the cache and redirect. Neither can fire in a browser. `public/index.html:231` installs a classic-script `window.fetch` patch that, on **any** 401, sets `window.location.href='/login.html'` and returns `Promise.reject(new Error('Authentication required'))` — a bare `Error` with no `status` and no `data`. `core/http.ts` calls the bare global `fetch` (`:318`, `:78`), and the patch is installed during parse while the app is a deferred module that additionally waits for `window.onload`, so the patched fetch is always the one in play. The 401 `Response` object therefore never reaches `handleResponse`. Consequences, in order of how much they matter: (1) `loaderQuery` falls through to its final branch and throws `Response(…, 500)`, so an expired session renders `RouteError`'s **generic** "something went wrong" card during the redirect instead of the purpose-built `error.status === 401` → "Unauthorized / You need to log in" card that sits unreachable at `RouteError.tsx:56`; (2) `queryClient.clear()` — commented "session over — don't leave cached data for the next user" — never runs. (2) is **not** a data leak: there is no query persister anywhere in `public/js` (grep: no `persistQueryClient`/`createSyncStoragePersister`), so the cache is in-memory and the hard navigation destroys it regardless. Fix is a decision, not a patch: either delete the two dead branches and document `index.html` as the 401 owner, or move 401 handling into `core/http.ts` and drop the interceptor (which FE-F0-5 already flags as a second client layer).

**FE-F2-3 — `retry: 2` is not the policy the comment describes.**
`query/client.ts:58` sets a bare numeric `retry: 2`, and TanStack Query retries *every* rejected query that many times — 400s and 404s included, with a 1 s → 2 s backoff. The file's own header says "retry 2 — transient network/5xx retry for idempotent reads", which is the policy `core/http.ts` implements for its own opt-in retry layer (`isRetriable`, `:265`), not this one. The two layers do not stack today only because `fetchJSON` defaults `retries` to 0 and no factory overrides it. Observable cost: a read that legitimately 404s — `labCaseQuery` on a deleted case, `expenseByIdQuery`, `transferPreviewQuery` on a removed work — costs 3 requests and ~3 s before the screen can say "not found". The tell is in the file itself: `threeShapeCasesQuery`/`threeShapeMediaQuery` (`:1502,1514`) carry `retry: false` with the comment "a not-connected / unreachable error is actionable, not transient", and `optionQuery` (`:1344`), `diagnosisQuery` (`:1101`) and both exchange-rate factories (`:722,735`) carry it too — six of 127. Fix: make the default a predicate that skips 4xx, and delete the local opt-outs it subsumes.

**FE-F2-4 — a sibling key the parent would have covered.**
`handleDelete` invalidates `qk.patient.appointments(personId)` = `['patient', id, 'appointments']`. `qk.patient.hasAppointment(personId)` = `['patient', id, 'has-appointment']` is its **sibling**, not its child, so it is untouched. `hasAppointmentQuery` backs the works screen's future-appointment flag. Scenario: delete the patient's only future appointment from the Appointments tab, click back to Works within 30 s → the screen still says the patient has an upcoming appointment. One-character class of fix: invalidate `qk.patient.all(personId)`, which is what the other 16 patient write sites do.

**FE-F2-5 — the comment that makes FE-F2-1 invisible.** `routes.config.tsx` labels `/calendar` "Monthly Calendar (100% SSE-driven)" with "// No loader - 100% SSE-driven real-time data". The calendar is 100% React Query: three `useQuery`s (`calendarRangeQuery`/`calendarMonthQuery`/`calendarStatsQuery`) and one manual `invalidateQueries(qk.calendar.all())`. The sibling comments on `/send`, `/send-message` and `/auth` are accurate (those really are SSE), which is what makes this one costly: an author adding an appointment write reads "SSE-driven" and correctly concludes they need no invalidation.

**FE-F2-6 — the one loader that does not use the cache.**
Every other loader goes through `loaderQuery` → `ensureQueryData`. `patientManagementLoader` instead issues five raw `fetchJSON` calls (`/api/patients/phones`, `/api/getworktypes`, `/api/getworkkeywords`, `/api/patients/tag-options`, `/api/patients/type-options`) and returns them as loader data — duplicating `patientPhonesQuery`, `workTypesQuery`, `workKeywordsQuery`, `tagOptionsQuery` and `typeOptionsQuery`, which already exist in `queries.ts` and already carry the same schemas. Three consequences: (a) **this is why `typeOptionsQuery` is dead** (FE-F0-1) — its only would-be consumer inlines the fetch; (b) the five results never enter the RQ cache, so a work type or patient tag edited through Settings → Lookups (which invalidates `qk.adminLookups.table`) cannot reach the patient-management dropdowns until a full route re-navigation; (c) `emptyOnHttpError` (`:35`) converts **any** HTTP status into an empty array, so a 403 or a 500 on one lookup renders a silently empty dropdown with no error anywhere. The screen's loader exception is legitimate (URL-param search + scroll restoration) — the exception is about *search*, not about bypassing the cache for five lookups.

**FE-F2-7 — the one patient route with the waterfall the design removes everywhere else.**
`patientShellLoader` opens with `preloadPatientPage(page)` to warm the tab's chunk in parallel with the data fetch. `page` comes from `params.page`. The diagnosis route is `':personId/work/:workId/diagnosis'` — it has **no `:page` segment**, so `page` is `undefined`, `preloadPatientPage` returns at its first guard, and `pages/Diagnosis.tsx` (957 LOC, the largest patient sub-page) only begins downloading after `PatientShell` mounts and renders it. `PatientShell` gets this right for rendering — it derives `effectivePage = isDiagnosisRoute ? 'diagnosis' : page` at `:121` — the loader just never got the same treatment. Fix: mirror that derivation in the loader.

**FE-F2-8 — two `qk` groups whose members share no prefix.** `qk.appointments.daily(d)` → `['daily-appointments', d]` but `qk.appointments.byId(id)` → `['appointments','by-id',id]`; `qk.whatsapp.messages(d)` → `['whatsapp-messages', d]` but `qk.whatsapp.messageCount(d)` → `['whatsapp-message-count', d]`. Both are annotated "keeps the legacy key shape", and `keys.test.ts:40` pins the two legacy shapes deliberately — so this is known, not accidental. The cost is that the JS object shape promises a hierarchy the cache does not have: there is no key that invalidates "this day's appointments" across both leaves, and no `appointments.all()` for a write site to reach for (FE-F2-1). Either give each group a real shared root and migrate the two SSE hooks' keys in one commit, or drop the grouping objects so the shape stops implying a prefix.

**FE-F2-9 — a 90-line loader result nobody reads.** `PatientShellLoaderResult` declares six fields; `patientShellLoader` builds them with three `as` casts (`as PatientData | null`, `as WorkData | null`, `as TimepointData[]`). `PatientShell.tsx` contains **no `useLoaderData`** — it reads `useParams` for routing and the RQ cache for data (3 `useQuery`s), which is precisely the documented design ("thin prefetchers into the React Query cache"). So the whole payload, the result interface, and the three casts are dead weight, and `currentPage` in particular is a decoy: `Navigation`'s `currentPage` prop comes from `PatientShell`'s own `effectivePage`, not from here. `labTrackingLoader` shows the shape this should be — `Promise<null>`. (Deleting the casts also removes three of the tree's remaining unchecked bridges out of a parsed contract payload.)

**FE-F2-10 — the dead inventory, and the FE-F0-1 verdicts this session owed.**
- **10 `qk` keys with zero references anywhere**: `aligner.work`, `adminLookups.all`, `stand.items.all`, `stand.sales.all`, `users.all`, `portalActivity.all`, `approvals.all`, `slideshow.all`, `settings.all`, `threeshape.all`. Nine are documented parent/prefix keys that no write ever needed (`stand.all()` is used instead of `stand.items.all()`, etc.) — harmless as documentation of the hierarchy, and `keys.test.ts` does not cover them. `aligner.work(workId)` is the odd one: it is not a parent of anything, its comment claims "Parent for a work's aligner data (sets/batches)", and the aligner sets screen keys off `qk.work.all` instead (`PatientSets:554`). That one is genuinely dead.
- **`patientInfoLoader` + `workDetailsLoader` (FE-F0-1) — verdict: superseded, not a lost-loader regression.** Repo-wide grep finds each exactly once, at its own declaration; `routes.config.tsx` imports eight loaders and neither is among them. `patientShellLoader` does strictly more than both (it prefetches patient info **and** work details **and** timepoints, and warms the page chunk) and is wired to every `/patient/*` route including `:personId/work/:workId/diagnosis`. No route flashes empty. Delete both, with `PatientInfoLoaderResult`/`WorkDetailsLoaderResult`.
- **`typeOptionsQuery` (FE-F0-1) — verdict: dead because of FE-F2-6**, not because a screen stopped needing it. It comes back to life the moment `patientManagementLoader` uses the factory.
- **`approvalsHistoryQuery` (FE-F0-1) — verdict: the screen was never built.** `GET /api/approvals/history` is a live, contracted server route with a client factory and **no consumer anywhere** — the maker-checker audit trail is unreachable in the UI. Not a client-side cleanup: **F5 owns the call** (build the screen or retire the endpoint).

**FE-F2-11 — a label that depends on array order.** `labStages.ts:31` reads `MATERIAL_OPTIONS[0]`, `[1]` and `[3]` positionally to decide between "Zirconia Core Try-In", "Metal Try-In" and the generic label. `MATERIAL_OPTIONS` is typed `readonly string[]` (the `as const` is widened away by the annotation), so TypeScript cannot check the indices and the inline `// 'Zirconia'` / `// PFM / Full Metal` comments are the only thing holding it together. Inserting one material before index 3 relabels a stage on the `/lab-tracking` board and on the work card. Fix: drop the `readonly string[]` annotation so `as const` gives literals, then compare against the literals by name.

**FE-F2-12 — three placeholder pages in the shipped switch.** `ContentRenderer`'s `renderContent()` has 22 `case` labels; 19 map to a `lazyPage` chunk and three (`details`, `history`, `messages`) render a hardcoded "…view coming soon…" card. None appears in `Navigation`'s item list, so they are reachable only by typing `/patient/7/details`. They are also the only three page strings with no preloader entry, which is why a mechanical preloader-vs-switch diff flags them — worth recording so F4 does not re-derive it as drift.

**FE-F2-13 — the standard and the practice.** `useApiMutation` is imported by exactly four files (`TvDisplaySettings`, `PatientSlideshow`, `useLabCases`, `Announcements`); raw `useMutation` is imported by **zero**. The other ~100 invalidation sites are hand-written `await postJSON(...)` + `queryClient.invalidateQueries(...)` inside a `try/catch`, which is exactly the shape in which FE-F2-1 and FE-F2-4 happen (the invalidation is a separate statement a reviewer can forget, rather than a required field on the mutation). Not something to convert wholesale, but worth stating so later sessions do not each rediscover the gap. Related and free: `App.tsx`'s docblock still promises "5-minute cache for static data (patient info, work details)" and "Appointments, messaging use component-level fetching" — both from the pre-`query/client.ts` era (the real defaults are `staleTime` 30 s / `gcTime` 5 min).

## F1 · HTTP funnel + client core · 2026-09-16

Scope read in full: `core/` (10 modules + the 2 test files' coverage), `services/` (6), `utils/` (9) — 4,267 LOC. Two findings were verified against a running dev server (`:3101`), not just read.

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FE-F1-1 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `core/http.ts:204` | a `{ schema }` read silently **skips validation** when the 200 response isn't JSON — and an unmatched `/api/*` path returns the SPA shell as 200 `text/html` | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F1-2 | [BUG] | 🟠 | `core/http.ts:454` + `SetFormDrawer.tsx:246`, `Videos.tsx:322` | uploads inherit the 30 s API timeout; the same endpoint's other call site already had to override it to 120 s | ✅ fixed (RA1) |
| FE-F1-3 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `core/http.ts:77` | `fetchCsrfToken()` is the one fetch in the funnel with **no timeout**, and it is awaited *before* the timeout is armed | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F1-4 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `utils/whatsapp-api-client.ts:94` | the bespoke client **retries POSTs** (the funnel never does) and has no timeout; one retried POST has no server-side in-progress guard | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F1-5 | [BUG] | 🟡 | `core/fileSystemAccess.ts:99` | every IndexedDB helper opens a connection it never closes, and `openDatabase()` has no `onblocked` → a future `DB_VERSION` bump hangs forever | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F1-6 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `core/iniParser.ts:49` | the INI round-trip **destroys** comments and out-of-section keys — Settings → Protocol Handlers rewrites the whole file | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F1-7 | [ENH] | 🟡 | `services/sse-whatsapp.ts` | byte-for-byte copy of `sse-appointments.ts` (~150 identical lines) — every fix must be made twice | ✅ fixed (RC1) |
| FE-F1-8 | [ENH] | 🟡 | ~20 files | ~20 hand-rolled local `YYYY-MM-DD` builders while `utils/calendarDate.ts#toLocalDateString` is the SSoT | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F1-9 | [DEAD] | 🔵 | 4 files | 15 more dead exports that the F0 scanner **could not see** (they are referenced by their own module's default-export object) | ✅ fixed (RA4) |
| FE-F1-10 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `services/{tasks,approvals,portal-activity}.ts` | three hand-rolled window-`CustomEvent` invalidation buses running parallel to React Query | ✅ fixed (RB4) |
| FE-F1-11 | [ENH] | 🔵 | 6 spots | small ones, bundled (see below) | ✅ fixed (RA4) |

**FE-F1-1 — the fail-loud guard has a hole, and the server hands it exactly the payload that opens it.**
`handleResponse` only validates inside the `contentType?.includes('application/json')` branch; the else branch is `return response.text() as unknown as T`, schema ignored. Verified live on `:3101` with an authenticated session: `GET /api/does-not-exist` → **200, `text/html`, 11,167 bytes** — the SPA shell. There is no `/api` 404 terminator anywhere (`grep`ed `app/mount-routes.ts`, `routes/api/index.ts`, `middleware/`), so every unmatched `/api/*` GET falls through `express.static('./dist')` into `routes/web.ts`'s `'/*splat'` catch-all, which only 404s paths that *look like assets*. Failure scenario: an endpoint is renamed or removed server-side (ordinary deploy drift) while a client build still calls it → `fetchJSON<T>(url, { schema })` **resolves** with an 11 KB HTML string, React Query caches it as the payload, and the screen renders empty or dies later in a child with an unrelated `TypeError`. CLAUDE.md calls the client schema "the only runtime guard in prod"; this is the one path where it doesn't run. Fix in `core/http.ts`: when `schema` is supplied and the body is not JSON, throw the same fail-loud `HttpError` `validateResponse` throws. (A JSON 404 terminator for `/api` is the deeper fix but is backend scope.)

**FE-F1-2 — the 30 s timeout is an API timeout applied to uploads.**
`postFormData` inherits `DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` (30 s). The server gives `POST /api/aligner/sets/:setId/upload-pdf` **120 s** (`timeouts.long`, `routes/api/aligner-file.routes.ts:54`), and `PatientSets.tsx:1061` matches it with `timeoutMs: 120000` plus a comment saying the 30 s default "would abort large PDFs client-side while the upload was still completing server-side". `SetFormDrawer.tsx:246` posts to **the same endpoint** with no override — and accepts files up to **100 MB** (its own size check at line 235). So the same upload succeeds from the sets list and fails from the set drawer: the client aborts at 30 s, the user gets "Failed to upload PDF", and the server finishes the upload (and the Drive copy) anyway — so a retry duplicates it. Related, and worth the owner's call: `POST /api/videos` (`Videos.tsx:322`, educational-video upload) and `POST /api/patients/:personId/files/upload` (`SequenceSidebar.tsx` ×2, `FileExplorer.tsx:183`) run on 30 s on **both** sides — no `timeouts.long` on either route — so a video larger than ~30 s of LAN transfer cannot be uploaded at all.

**FE-F1-3 — the CSRF pre-flight sits outside the timeout it is supposed to be covered by.**
In `attempt()`, `headers['x-csrf-token'] = await ensureCsrfToken()` (line 313) runs *before* `withTimeout(...)` (line 316), and `fetchCsrfToken()` (line 78) issues a bare `fetch` with no `signal`. A server that accepts the connection but never answers `/api/csrf-token` (proxy holding it open, event loop wedged) hangs **every mutation in the tab** with no abort, no rejection and no toast — the one failure mode the funnel's 30 s rule exists to prevent. Single-flight means one stuck fetch stalls all of them. Fix: give that fetch its own `AbortSignal.timeout`, and let the failure surface as an ordinary mutation error.

**FE-F1-4 — a second HTTP client with the opposite retry policy.**
`APIClient.request()` wraps **every** request — GET and POST alike — in `RetryManager.withRetry` (3 attempts, 1 s → 2 s backoff), whereas `core/http.ts` never retries a mutation by design. Three POSTs go through it (`WhatsAppSend.tsx`): `WA_SEND`, `MESSAGE_RESET`, and `SEND_EMAIL`. `WA_SEND` is safe — the server has an `alreadyInProgress` guard — and `MESSAGE_RESET` is idempotent, but **`POST /api/email/send-appointments` has no in-progress guard** (`routes/api/email.routes.ts:49`): it regenerates the appointment PDF and re-sends the staff email on each attempt, so one failed-but-delivered send (502 from the tunnel, a restart mid-send, any 5xx after SMTP handoff) becomes 2–3 identical emails to the whole recipient list. Staff-facing, not patient-facing — hence 🟡, not 🟠. Two more defects in the same file: (a) **no timeout at all** — only an explicit `AbortController`, so a stuck WhatsApp request leaves the button disabled forever; (b) `finally { this.abortControllers.delete(requestKey) }` deletes **by key**, so when a second request reuses a `cancelPrevious` key, the first one's teardown removes the *second's* controller and a later `cancelRequest(key)` silently does nothing.

**FE-F1-5 — IndexedDB connections are never closed.**
All seven handle helpers (`saveHandle`, `getHandle`, `removeHandle`, `listHandles`, `clearHandles`, …) call `openDatabase()` and never `db.close()`. Today: one live `IDBDatabase` per call on a long-lived tab (the chair PCs and the aligner screens call these repeatedly). The latent failure is the upgrade path — bump `DB_VERSION` to 2 and every open connection blocks it; `indexedDB.open` then fires only `blocked`, which nothing handles, so `openDatabase()`'s promise **never settles** and "remember this folder" spins with no error and no timeout. Fix: close after each op (or keep one cached connection) and reject on `onblocked`.

**FE-F1-6 — saving the INI deletes what the parser didn't understand.**
`ProtocolHandlersSettings` reads the file, `mergeConfigs`, `formatIniContent`, then `writeTextFile` — a **full overwrite** (lines 130 → 303-307). `parseIniContent` keeps only `[section]` headers and `key=value` lines inside a section: comments are skipped (line 62), keys before the first section are dropped (`equalIndex > 0 && currentSection`, line 77), an inline `; comment` stays glued to the value, and duplicate keys collapse. So the first save from Settings strips every hand-written note from `C:\ShwanOrtho\ProtocolHandlers.ini` — on machines where that file is edited by hand during setup. Either round-trip unknown lines or state the overwrite in the UI.

**FE-F1-7 — the two SSE singletons are one file typed twice.** Normalize the channel name (`sed 's/appointments/CH/'` vs `s/whatsapp/CH/'`) and the diff is 81 lines, **all of them comments plus the event-registration block**. The emitter surface, `getFreshness`/`markStale`, the refcount `ensureConnected`/`release`, `connect`/`disconnect`, and the visibility/`pageshow` handlers are identical. Extract `createSseChannel({ url, events, logName })`; `sse-appointments` can then use the `WIRE_EVENTS` array form its twin already uses.

**FE-F1-8 — ~20 copies of one date formatter.** SSoT is `utils/calendarDate.ts#toLocalDateString`. Re-implemented in: `core/utils.ts#formatISODate`, `hooks/useDateManager.ts#getLocalDateString`, `services/tasks.ts#dateFromTodayYmd`, `utils/formatters.ts:196`, and inline in `ExpiringItemsPanel`, `SalesHistoryTable`, `SaleDetailModal`, `PhotoSessionDialog`, `UniversalHeader`, `DailyInvoicesModal`, `EditAppointmentForm` (×2), `AppointmentForm`, `SimplifiedCalendarPicker` (×2), `Expenses`, `DailyAppointments`, `AppointmentsHeader`, `StandReports`, `Dashboard`. They agree today (all use local getters, none uses `toISOString()`), so this is upkeep, not a live bug — but it is 20 places to get the UTC-shift wrong in.

**FE-F1-9 — dead exports the F0 scan structurally could not find.** Each is referenced by its own module's `export default { … }` object, which makes a textual scan see a second use:
- `core/utils.ts` — `deepClone` and `formatPhoneNumber`: **0 callers**. (`formatPhoneNumber`'s `digits.startsWith('+' + countryCode)` branch at line 176 is unreachable regardless — `digits` has already been `\D`-stripped at line 170, so it can never contain `+`.)
- `core/storage.ts` — `clear()`: 0 callers (it would also wipe `shwan_theme`/`shwan_language`/`shwan_arabic_font`/`chairId`, so nothing *should* call it as-is).
- `core/iniParser.ts` — **8 of 12 exports unused** (~110 lines): `validateIniConfig`, `getValue`, `setValue`, `removeKey`, `getSectionKeys`, `getSections`, `hasKey`, `countKeys`. Its only consumer imports four names.
- `core/fileSystemAccess.ts` — `listHandles`, `clearHandles`, `getFileFromDirectory`, `isPermissionError` unused; `getHandle` and `pickFile` are internal-only and need not be exported.

**FE-F1-10 — three CustomEvent buses beside the server-state layer.** `TASKS_CHANGED_EVENT`, `APPROVALS_CHANGED_EVENT`, `PORTAL_ACTIVITY_CHANGED_EVENT` are hand-rolled `window.dispatchEvent` fan-outs telling bells to refetch, where `queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.<domain>() })` is the documented mechanism (CLAUDE.md: React Query "owns all staff-app server state"). The three modules are otherwise identical in shape — reads already moved to `query/queries.ts`, only mutations + the signal left. F2 owns the call.

**FE-F1-11 — bundled small ones:**
- `utils/calendarDate.ts:249` — `parseLocalDate(date instanceof Date ? date : date)`: both branches of the ternary are the same expression.
- `core/storage.ts:32` — `isLocalStorageAvailable()` does a `setItem`+`removeItem` probe on **every** get/set/remove, so one read costs three localStorage ops. Memoize the probe per page load.
- `core/fileSystemAccess.ts:410` — `readTextFile(handle, _encoding)` ignores `_encoding` and always decodes UTF-8; a Windows-1252/UTF-16 INI comes back with replacement characters. Drop the parameter or honour it.
- `core/http.ts:454` — `postFormData`'s `headers: {}` is vestigial (the real Content-Type removal is the `body instanceof FormData` branch at line 307), and its `...options` spread sits **after** `method`/`body`, so a caller-supplied `method` would silently win.
- `public/index.html` FOUC RTL check vs `core/language.ts#isRtlRoute` — the inline script prefix-matches `/expenses/` but exact-matches `/dashboard` and `/appointments`, while `isRtlRoute` prefix-matches all three. No route has children today, so it is latent; the two lists must move together (CLAUDE.md's 3-place ratchet).
- `services/photo-render-watch.ts:85` — `timer: 0 as unknown as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>`; make the field optional instead of faking a handle.

## F0 · 2026-09-16 (mechanical — all five are **leads**, confirmed by the owning session)

F0 makes no judgement calls by design, so nothing here is a confirmed defect: each row is a mechanical observation with the session that owns the surface. None is 🔴 or 🟠 — the tree is gate-green and has no orphan files or orphan stylesheets at all.

| ID | Type | Sev | File | One-line | Owner | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| FE-F0-3 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `components/react/LookupEditorModal.tsx:109` | `useQueries` without `combine` → new array identity every render → the `referenceOptions` memo (line 115) recomputes every render. **Not the freeze class** (`[[usequeries-combine-referential-stability]]`): the memo is read only in render at line 294, no effect depends on it — so this is wasted work, not a loop. Add `combine` anyway | F21 | ✅ fixed (RX1) |
| FE-F0-4 | [ENH] | 🔵 | `pages/aligner/PatientSets.tsx:2275,2299` | the only 4 lint warnings in the whole tree (a11y: click handler on a non-interactive element, no keyboard listener) | F17 / F25 | ✅ fixed (RX1) |

**FE-F0-1 — the 14 confirmed-dead exports** (verified with a repo-wide grep including `shared/`, server code and the entry HTMLs; each appears exactly once, at its own declaration):

|---|---|---|

The two unused **query factories** and the two unused **loaders** are the interesting pair: F2 should establish whether a route lost its loader (a prefetch regression — the screen now flashes empty) or the loader was deliberately replaced by a component-level `useQuery`. **Answered by F2 (FE-F2-10): no route lost a loader.**

**FE-F0-5 — what is actually in `public/index.html`:**
- **A global `window.fetch` monkey-patch** (line ~231) that redirects to `/login.html` on **any** 401 and rejects with `new Error('Authentication required')`. It is **load-bearing, not dead**: `core/http.ts` has no 401 handling of its own. F1 owns the consequences — every 401 becomes a hard navigation, so a component `catch` can never show the server's message for one, and the rejection reaching the funnel is a generic `Error` rather than the funnel's own shape.
- **11 unguarded `console.*`** including a `[Auth] Global fetch interceptor installed` line on every load (`public/js` itself is clean — the CLAUDE.md "Winston, not console.log" rule stops at the bundle).
- **`window.addEventListener('error'` / `'unhandledrejection'`** that only `console.error` — additive with `core/error-reporter.ts`'s own listeners, so both run. Not a conflict; F23 decides whether the inline pair earns its place.
- **The React mount waits for `window.addEventListener('load')`** — i.e. for every image and stylesheet, not just DOM ready. F26 measures what that costs on a cold load.
- `window.performance.timing` (deprecated Navigation Timing Level 1) in the same block.

# Read and found clean (do not re-derive)

Per session, the files read closely that produced nothing. This list is what keeps later sessions cheap — fill it in even when it feels like filler.

## F21 · 2026-10-05

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F21 ledger (4,994 LOC + CSS). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's five questions, answered.**
  - *Clinic identity is configuration.* The branding form keeps the header name (`CLINIC_NAME`) and the message names (`CLINIC_MESSAGE_NAME`/`_AR`) as three separate fields, compares each against its own saved value, and sends them in one `PUT /api/branding`; nothing on these screens folds them together. The only identity problem is the placeholders (FE-F21-13).
  - *Employee quit semantics.* Settings reads `?includeInactive=true` (the only screen that does); unticking *Currently employed* clears the email and appointment flags on the client and again on the server, and the commission flag and rate are kept. Every other employee feed is active-only: `employeesQuery()` (task assignee, Telegram share, expenses' new-row picker — the expense list reads `includeInactive` to show history), and `/api/doctors`, `/api/work-doctors`, `/api/operators` filter `is_active` server-side. New-task assignment to a quit employee is refused server-side (`employeeIsActive`). The gaps are cache freshness (FE-F21-11) and delete (FE-F21-6).
  - *Exchange rates.* Settings edits **today's** rate only (`POST /api/updateExchangeRateForDate` with today's date, rounded) and shows history read-only; a 404 for today is the "not set" empty state, not a toast. One `qk.exchangeRates.all()` invalidation refreshes today's rate, the history and the payment modal. The tab is `financeOnly`, matching the server's `admin\|front_desk`.
  - *Lookup editor.* The right-click entry is on all three lab selects (work item, lab case, expense), each invalidating `qk.lookups.labs()`. Deleting a row that an FK protects (addresses, alert types, document types, expense categories/subcategories, keywords, labs, referrals, tags) answers 409 "still referenced" and the toast says so. Escape layering is right: the popover and the context menu capture + `stopImmediatePropagation`, so only the top layer closes. `LookupEditorModal`'s reference selects use `useQueries` with `combine`. The holes are the tables with no FK (FE-F21-2) and roles (FE-F21-3).
  - *`CalendarTimesSettings` altitude.* The `useState` pile is mostly the edit buffer + its baseline + three render-phase seed keys; it reads clearly and the remediation keeps it. The success banner's timer is cleaned up on unmount and on a second message.
- **Server state:** every read carries `{ schema }`; General's bulk save refreshes `qk.settings.options()` **and** each touched `qk.settings.option(name)` (the work form's default currency); Calendar Times invalidates its three option keys and the `tbltimes` table; holiday writes invalidate `qk.calendar.all()`; cost-preset writes invalidate `qk.lookups.costPresets()`.
- **Per-device settings** (chair ID 1–10 validated by `storage.setChairId`, theme, language, Arabic font) are local and correct for every role; `DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY` is a closed select over `WORK_CURRENCIES`.
- **Calendar:** a time hidden by the early/late setting still renders when booked (server, `getSlotSettings`); *Regenerate* is `admin\|front_desk` on the server. Times have no FK references, so deleting one only stops future generation.
- **Cost presets:** EUR is a real estimate currency (the patient estimate offers it), so the three currency tabs are right; amounts are whole numbers by design.
- **CSS:** no dead classes in the nine modules (the scan's hits were `:global(...)` selectors and comments).
- **ESLint:** 0 errors, 0 warnings on the 13 files.
- **Live-data facts** (read-only; names and lengths only): 30 `options` rows, among them `gram_session` (369 chars) and `EMAIL_SMTP_PASSWORD` (16); 9 legacy rows nothing reads; works by type: Filling 2,442, Ortho (Braces) 1,913, Other 659, Scaling 591, Exo 445, Endo 436, Retainer 342, Consult 225. FKs onto `employees`: `works.dr_id`, `appointments.dr_id`, `expenses.employee_id` (NO ACTION), `alerts.assigned_to` (SET NULL). The Supabase mirror's `options` has RLS on, no policies, and no `anon`/`authenticated` grants.

## F20 · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F20 ledger (3,402 LOC + CSS). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.**
  - *`LabelPreviewModal` raw-fetch blob POST.* The CSRF token is attached by hand (`prefetchCsrfToken()`), the blob URL is revoked after 60 s, and the `X-Total-*` headers feed the toast. What is wrong is the success path when the window is blocked (FE-F20-4), the sheet position (FE-F20-5) and the error text. The 18-`useState` pile is real but split cleanly by mode; the remediation keeps it.
  - *GrapesJS.* It is lazy twice over: the designer is a lazy route, and `grapesjs` is a dynamic `import()` inside it, with its CSS riding the same chunk. The editor is destroyed on unmount, and the StrictMode double-mount is guarded by a per-run `cancelled` flag. Template HTML is never rendered into the app document: the designer loads it into GrapesJS's canvas iframe, the preview goes to a separate window, and receipts are opened as their own document. `renderTemplate` escapes every substituted value. GrapesJS's parser drops `<script>` by default. The file-path handling was the hole (FE-F20-1).
  - *Lab board.* Transitions are guarded server-side under row locks (`WHERE status = $from`, forward-only advance, backward-only remake — `remake-guard.ts`), so a stale card can't skip or reverse a stage; the client's skips are deliberate (the quick picker jumps a checkpoint). There is no drag and drop. The gaps are a one-click delivery with no way back (FE-F20-6) and keyboard access (FE-F20-8c).
  - *Print path.* Labels are a server PDF (PDFKit), so print CSS doesn't apply; receipts carry their own `@page` size and `@media print` rules. Dark tokens are `@media screen`, and the label dialog renders dark surfaces correctly in dark mode (runtime: dialog `rgb(17, 21, 28)`, inputs dark).
- **Server state:** every lab-case write is a `useApiMutation` invalidating `qk.labCases.all()` and the work's `qk.work.detailsList(workId)` (the work-card badge); reads carry `{ schema }`; the board has a loader. The modal's unsaved guard resets on `dataUpdatedAt`, so saving and staying open doesn't arm a false prompt. Template writes invalidate `qk.templates.all()`.
- **Roles:** every lab-case and template write is `CLINICAL_ROLES` (all three staff roles) and so is every control on these screens; the lab-case hard delete is admin-only and has no UI. Labels are `CLINICAL_ROLES`.
- **Print queue:** persisted in `sessionStorage`, grouped by patient with one shared `groupByPatient`; a batch can't be queued twice; the indicator's *Clear All* asks twice; the chevron class bug was fixed earlier (RX1).
- **Lab stage config:** the stage order and location live in the contract (`LAB_STAGE_META`), the client only maps labels; the framework try-in label is keyed by material name (FE-F2-11's fix holds); `defaultRemakeTarget` mirrors the server's walk-back.
- **i18n/RTL:** none of these files is on the ratchet; they stay LTR English. The lab tracker's dashboard tile is the only bilingual piece.
- **CSS:** the template modules are prop-drilled (`styles={styles}`), so a per-file scan misreads them; scanned across the folder, three classes have no reader (FE-F20-11). The label dialog keeps a local `--lpm-*` palette of literal colours; F24 can re-check.
- **ESLint:** 0 errors, 0 warnings on the 17 files.
- **Live-data facts** (read-only): `lab_cases` 0 rows (the tracker is not in use yet); `document_templates` 3 (ids 2–4, distinct files, created by `migration_script`/`system`); options `LatestLabelPos` = 4 and `LabelTemplatePath` = `C:\Aligner_Sets\Labels\OL291-07.docx` (both legacy, unread); no `CLINIC_LOGO` row; 82 aligner batches manufactured in the last 90 days.

## F19 · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F19 ledger (4,659 LOC + 22 CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's five questions, answered.**
  - *POS arithmetic.* Line totals, the cart total and the change are integer IQD; the server recomputes every line from the item's current price (`validateAndCreateSale`) and the completion dialog shows the server's `change`, not the client's. There are no discounts. The stand is IQD-only by schema (integer price columns, no currency column), the same base currency the rest of the app uses, so nothing is mixed or converted. What is wrong is the cart's stale price snapshot (FE-F19-14a) and the report's "Net Profit" (FE-F19-1).
  - *Checkout atomicity.* The sale is one transaction: header, lines, a guarded stock decrement per line (`current_stock >= qty`, 0 rows aborts) and the ledger rows. A refused sale (400 `INSUFFICIENT_STOCK`/`UNDERPAID`/`ITEM_INACTIVE`, or a 5xx) leaves the cart untouched and toasts the server's message. The double sale is FE-F19-5.
  - *Stock movements.* Restock, adjust, void, sale and an item's opening stock each write their ledger row in the same transaction as the stock change; adjust refuses a negative result on both sides plus a concurrent-change guard; a void is idempotent at the row level and restocks the lines. Every stand write invalidates `qk.stand.all()`, which covers the item list, low-stock, expiring, movements, dashboard, sales and reports, so no screen keeps an old number after a write.
  - *Scanner handling.* The barcode box submits on Enter, trims, clears and distinguishes a 404 ("No item found for barcode") from a failed lookup. Focus is the defect (FE-F19-6).
  - *Charts.* Both destroy their Chart.js instance on unmount and on every data/theme change (`resolvedTheme` dependency); ticks, grid and legend come from `getChartThemeColors()`, so they re-colour on a theme switch. The Stand is not on the i18n ratchet, so it stays LTR English and RTL doesn't apply. Only the canvas title colour is a finding (FE-F19-15).
- **Server state:** every read is a `queryOptions` factory with `{ schema }` (the contract's response); every write is a `useApiMutation` that awaits the stand invalidation before resolving, so the modals close on fresh data. The item search and the sale list key on their filters (RQ discards out-of-order results); search debounces at 300 ms, starts at 2 characters and clears its timer on unmount.
- **Roles:** every `/api/stand/*` read is `FINANCE_ROLES`, and the Dashboard hides the Stand card from `clinical`, so no clinical user lands on a page of 403s. Only the admin-only writes are offered too widely (FE-F19-7).
- **Modals:** all on `<Modal>` + `<ModalHeader>` except the void dialog (FE-F19-15). The item form has `unsavedGuard` and a `submitting` guard; restock, adjust and delete have their own; the void dialog blocks backdrop and Escape while voiding and requires a reason (also enforced by the contract). The category deactivate goes through `useConfirm`.
- **The AI scan:** at most three images, compressed to 1024 px JPEG before upload (the server also caps each at ~5 MB); the browser's `BarcodeDetector` result wins over the model's reading; a 429/503 from Gemini reaches the toast with its own message. Only the date it returns is a finding (FE-F19-10).
- **Sales history:** defaults to this month with an explicit *Apply*; the server's range is half-open and index-driven; voided rows are styled and lose the *Void* button; the item summary comes from one correlated subquery, not a per-row read.
- **Product ceiling:** a stand holds hundreds of items, and the sales list is bounded by its date range (a month at 100 sales a day is 3,000 rows). Nothing on these screens grows without bound.
- **CSS:** one dead class (`.noteCell`, FE-F19-16); no `!important`. The KPI tiles use five literal gradients with white text, the same treatment the CSS audit left literal on other gradient cards; F24 can re-check.
- **ESLint:** 0 errors, 0 warnings on the 25 files. `frontend-dead-scan.mjs`: no unimported file and no orphan module among them.
- **Live-data facts** (read-only):
  - `stand_items`: 17 (16 active), 15 with a barcode, 10 with an expiry, none expired. One deactivated row (`ZZZ_TEST_ITEM`, barcode `ZZZBAR1`, 140 in stock).
  - `stand_sales`: 60 since 2026-05-20, 7 voided, all `cash`, **none linked to a patient**. Monthly revenue / cost / profit: May 26,000/21,000/5,000 · Jun 42,000/28,595/13,405 · Jul 136,000/96,645/39,355 · Aug 199,000/140,575/58,425 · Sep 64,000/44,370/19,630.
  - `stand_stock_movements`: initial 14, restock 11 (347,545 IQD), sale 60, void 7, waste 15. Restocks in Jun/Jul/Aug: 139,670 / 93,275 / 114,600. Four restocks at a unit cost different from the item's `cost_price`.
  - Unique indexes: `ux_standitems_barcode` and `ux_standitems_sku` (partial on NOT NULL, not on `is_active`), `stand_categories_categoryname_key`.

## F18 · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F18 ledger (2,644 LOC + eight CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's three questions, answered.**
  - *Archform: stale or missing file, and matching that silently goes wrong.* A missing or unreadable file is handled. Only `/archform/patients` reads SQLite (`/archform/matches` reads PG), and it answers 503 `{ unavailable, path }` when `isReadable()` fails. The page shows its own "Archform Database Unavailable" screen with the resolved path and a *Retry* that refetches both reads (runtime: this box resolves the share to `/mnt/work_pc/Archform/__ARCHFORMDB`). On the server, a stale handle is reopened (`journal_mode` probe), and writes retry once on the network-volume codes. A write's 503 reaches the toast, because `httpErrorMessage` reads `message`. Matching is not a heuristic: the user picks the set by hand, so nothing is auto-matched or fuzzy-matched. What does go wrong is the link's integrity (FE-F18-3) and the configuration (FE-F18-4).
  - *Announcements over the mirror: "sent" means recorded locally.* See FE-F18-12. When the mirror is behind, the page shows exactly what the local DB has, with no sync state. Read times come back through the reverse sink and display through `formatLocaleDateTime`. The three live receipts predate the 2026-09-27 mirror clock fix, and the owner chose to leave those rows (FE-F5-1).
  - *List paging at the product ceiling.* FE-F18-13. The all-sets query was already rewritten for this ceiling (one grouped pass instead of per-row subqueries), so the cost that grows is the payload and the browser's work, not the SQL.
- **Hand-downs, answered.** F17's `AlignerDoctorsSettings.tsx:214` placeholder → FE-F18-10. F13's `PatientsList.tsx:173` → RD moved the avatar onto the working-files thumbnail route (no year-long cache any more, so the stale-photo half is closed); the guessed lower-case, tp-0-only name is still open → FE-F18-8(c). FE-F5-1's portal-authored times on `doctor_announcement_reads` → display is right (above). F2's map rows `announcements.all` / `aligner.doctorsAdmin` / `aligner.archform*` → the announcement writes (`useApiMutation`, `invalidate: [qk.announcements.all()]`) cover both list flags and every receipts panel; the Archform writes invalidate both Archform keys; the doctor writes miss `qk.aligner.doctors`, which is FE-F18-10.

The delete prompt is `isDangerous`.

Dark theme: the inline react-select colours lose to the global `.react-select__*` sheet (runtime: options `rgb(34, 41, 55)` on `rgb(34, 41, 55)` menus), so only the light-theme literals are a finding (FE-F18-14).

The badge state machine itself is right; only the *No Next* toggle and the *Final* legend count step outside it (FE-F18-2).

Search debounces at 300 ms and clears its timer on unmount; the 2-character minimum holds on both sides; the query factory is gated by `enabled`.
- **Patient list:** the filter covers name, phone and id; avatars load lazily and fall back to a placeholder on error. The working-files route answers `private, no-cache` without a `?v`, so a re-cropped smile shows on the next visit.
- **Roles:** every write on these screens is `CLINICAL_ROLES`, all three roles, and the Settings *Aligner Doctors* tab is open to all of them, so no control is offered to a role the server refuses.
- **Layout / mode toggle:** FE-F4-14's nav semantics hold (`<nav aria-label>`, `aria-current`, `type="button"`); All Sets locks the shell to the viewport so only the table scrolls. Only the wrong `aria-current` on the non-list routes is a finding (FE-F18-14).
- **i18n/RTL:** none of these files is on the ratchet or in `RTL_ROUTES`/`RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS`; they stay LTR English by design.
- **CSS:** every class in the eight modules has a reader; no `!important`; no hex literals. The only literal colours are rgba glows and shadows (DoctorsList, PatientsList, SearchPatient) and white-on-gradient text on the *All Doctors* card, which is what the CSS audit left literal elsewhere. F24 can re-check.

## F17 · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F17 ledger (4,266 LOC + two CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.**
  - *`remaining_*` semantics:* no client code derives "delivered" from `total − remaining`. The progress bar is the server's `DeliveredAligners` over `upper + lower` (net of templates, as the server computes it). The batch drawer checks a new batch's real consumption (count minus a template slot) against `remaining_*` plus the edited batch's own previous consumption, and `lastFlagPrompt` compares remaining before and after the save (9 tests). The card shows `remaining_*` as "Remaining Upper/Lower", which is the not-yet-batched number the server keeps.
  - *Can `loadBatches` serve stale data after a sibling write?* Not after a write made on this page: every write reloads what it changed. Batch create, edit, delete, the status changes and the undos reload the set's batches and the sets; set create, edit and delete, payments and PDF uploads reload the sets; note writes reload that set's notes. It does serve stale data after a write made anywhere else (another tab, a colleague, the doctor's portal): FE-F17-2. The altitude ✨ is FE-F17-14.
  - *Money:* `set_cost` keeps its sign and ceiling rule on both sides (`moneyDecimal`: non-negative, at most `NUMERIC_10_2_MAX`), and the box can't type a minus. *Add Payment* is offered only to `writeFinance`, only while the balance is above 0, and only on a USD work (RB3); the payment invalidates `qk.work.all` + `qk.patient.all` (FE-F8-7's fix holds). Paid, Balance and the status badge come from the server's sums (`TotalPaid`, `set_cost − paid`, `PaymentStatus`). A cost of 0, a cost below what's paid, and the non-USD and role questions are FE-F17-8/-9.
  - *The doctor-portal round trip on deletes:* the server side holds. Read from the live catalogue: `aligner_batches`, `aligner_notes` and `aligner_activity_flags` cascade from the set, `doctor_announcements` cascades from the batch, and `invoices.aligner_set_id` is NO ACTION, which the route turns into a clear 409. `deleteBatch` locks the set, refuses to renumber a made batch, gives the aligners back and resequences. What the prompts say is FE-F17-11.
- **Earlier sessions' hand-downs, answered.** F16's "Shwan Lab" → FE-F17-7. F12's aligner PDF upload name (`AlignerPdfService.ts:207`) → fixed by RD: `uploadSinglePdf` comes from `createUpload` (utf8 names). F0/RA4's three dead exports in `aligner.types.ts` (`AlignerSetForLabel`, `AlignerSetFormData`, `AlignerBatchForLabel`) → already gone. FE-F7-8's "aligner lists untouched by work writes" → FE-F17-6. RB3's "set cost on a non-USD work" → FE-F17-9 (owner call). FE-F5-1's portal timestamps on `aligner_notes` → note times go through `formatLocaleDateTime`, and the mirror's clock was fixed on 2026-09-27. FE-F1-5's handle store → the page's folder access (`getDirectoryHandle`/`saveHandle`/`checkPermission` under `base_aligner_sets`) follows the same path as `useImportFolder`; a real directory grant can't be given headless, and FE-F1-5 itself was closed in RX1. RB4's "the work edit form can overwrite a concurrent change" is F7's file, not F17's; FE-F17-1 is the same fix and links to it.
- **PDF uploads:** both sites (the card and the set drawer) pass `timeoutMs: 120000`, which matches the route's `timeout(120000ms)`, and check type and the 100 MB cap before posting. The set drawer uploads after the save and reports an upload failure on its own.
- **Batch drawer derivations:** the start/end preview mirrors `createBatch` (per arch, the highest end over all batches + 1; a template starts at 0 and only on the first batch), and none of it is sent (`batch_sequence` and the four sequences are left out of the body and derived server-side). `is_active` asks for a delivered batch on both sides. The *Last batch* prompts follow `lastFlagPrompt`.
- **Status buttons:** each is offered only in a state where it is valid (manufacture until made; deliver once made and not delivered; the undos in the drawer, undo-manufacture refused on a delivered batch both client- and server-side). Date order is checked three times: the input's `min`/`max`, the handler, and the server's `INVALID_DATE_ORDER`. Today's date comes from `formatISODate()` (local getters).
- **Errors:** the batch writes' business rules arrive as 400s with the server's message (one error map for every batch path), and the drawer shows `error`; the set delete's 409 shows its own wording; the page's toasts use `httpErrorMessage`.
- **Labels:** `buildLabelsFromRanges` keys labels by the real sequence, keeps a template's 0, and treats a null range as "no aligners on that arch". The label modal and the queue's PDF are F20's.
- **`AlignerPhotoViewer`:** on `<Modal>` (stack-aware Escape, focus trap, labelled by the file name); the arrow-key listener is removed on cleanup; prev/next are disabled at the ends.
- **`<Modal>` census for F17:** `SetFormDrawer` and `BatchFormDrawer` (both `unsavedGuard`, `ariaLabelledBy`, `ModalHeader`, dismissal blocked while saving), `AlignerPhotoViewer`, and the page's two `ConfirmDialog`s. `PaymentFormDrawer` and `LabelPreviewModal` belong to F8 and F20. No hand-rolled dialog; the "Uploading PDF…" overlay is a progress shield, not a dialog.

- **CSS:** every class in `PatientSets.module.css` has a reader; no `!important`; the only literal colours are three rgba shadows and the overlay's backdrop. The global classes the three screens use all resolve to rules in `aligner-common.css`, except the five named in FE-F17-13 and FE-F17-15 (and the Font Awesome classes, which are vendor).

## F16 · 2026-10-04

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F16 ledger (2,381 LOC + two CSS modules, plus RX1's rewritten hooks). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.**
  - *QR lifecycle:* F3's analysis still holds after RX1. `useWhatsAppAuth` (6 listeners) and `useWhatsAppSync` (10) each `off()` exactly what they `on()` and `release()` in the same cleanup; `GlobalStateContext`'s provider holds the app-wide refcount (so `activeQRViewers > 0` whenever a staff tab is open), and `WHATSAPP_AUTO_INIT=false` blocks the on-demand path. The QR itself is withheld from non-finance roles on both the SSE frame and `initial-state` (`mayPair`). What the hooks do with transport events is FE-F16-12.
  - *`whatsapp-api-client` as a sanctioned raw consumer:* annotated (the `no-restricted-syntax` disable gives the reason; the `whatsapp`, `email-api` and `messaging` contracts say which responses stay raw and why). After RX1 it attaches the CSRF token on mutations, never replays a POST, retries a GET only on network/timeout/5xx, has a per-attempt 30 s timeout, surfaces the server's own message (`APIHttpError`), and deletes abort controllers by identity. Whether it should exist at all is FE-F16-8.
  - *Bulk send:* the batch is server-side. `POST /api/wa/send` calls `whatsapp.send()` without awaiting it and answers at once, so navigating away can't stop it. `batchSendActive` is set synchronously before the first await, so a double click gets `alreadyInProgress` (toasted as a warning). The zero-ack watchdog's `whatsapp_send_unconfirmed` reaches the page as a 30 s error toast plus a refresh. Progress accuracy is FE-F16-1/-2/-11; the client-side re-send loop is FE-F16-7.
  - *Recipients and the clinic's name:* the client composes no message body. Batch reminders are built server-side with `getClinicNames()` (`CLINIC_MESSAGE_NAME`/`_AR`, `messaging-queries.ts:410`); "Copy message text" fetches the server-built text from `message-text`; `sendmedia2` sends files, not text. Recipients are picked server-side (`getWhatsAppMessages`), and phones are normalized server-side (`PhoneFormatter.forWhatsApp`/`forTelegram`), so the client's only phone handling is the send-message page's `'964' + phone`, which matches the live data (5,480 of 6,855 patients store `7xxxxxxxxx`; 1,349 have none). The one literal clinic name in the WhatsApp path is a backend task notice (found along the way).
- **`email/send-appointments`:** the per-date 409 guard (RX1) holds; the page's client never retries it.
- **The auth state machine** (`whatsappAuthMachine.ts`, 9 tests): `enforce()` runs after every event; CHECKING_SESSION's 3 s settle is an effect keyed on the state; the 30 s QR re-poll interval is cleared on a state change and on unmount; the post-pairing redirect fires only after the page saw QR_REQUIRED, and its timer is cleared. Refresh QR and Re-link dispatch `reset` before their POST, so their buttons disappear on the first click (no double POST). Restart's ordering is FE-F16-3.
- **`GroupSettings`:** seeds the form on the payload's identity (no setState-in-effect), writes the saved payload back into `qk.settings.whatsappGroupSettings()` so a remount can't reseed stale values, trims the name and requires one while enabled, and disables the fieldset while loading or saving. The role gap is FE-F16-4.
- **`useMessageStatus`:** React Query owns the read, with `{ schema }`; status ticks invalidate the day's key through a 400 ms debounce with cleanup; `isLoading` (not `isFetching`) keeps the rows on screen during refetches; the heading's date goes through `formatLocaleDate` (a local day, no UTC shift).
- **Time Sent:** the server sends `toISOString()` of a `timestamp` parsed as local time, and `formatLocaleTime` converts it back, so the wall-clock time is right in the clinic's zone; an unsent row shows "Not sent".
- **Row actions:** re-send and copy go through the funnel with the contract schemas (`resendAppointment`, `messageText`); the clipboard helper has a non-secure-context fallback for plain-http LAN use; "Copy phone number" prefers the server's country-coded phone. The re-send guards are FE-F16-7.
- **Date window:** `getDateSendability` allows 1–2 calendar days ahead; the default is tomorrow, Saturday from Thursday or Friday. That matches the Sat–Thu week (a Wednesday can't send for Saturday, which is three days out: the rule as written).
- **`sendmedia2`:** the server's containment guard (`isUnderClinicRoot`) and its 120 s timeout are in place (backend-audited); the client half is FE-F16-6.
- **Dev-only init:** `WhatsAppAuth`'s `POST /api/wa/initialize` is behind `import.meta.env.DEV` and compiles out of the prod bundle.
- **`<Modal>` census for F16:** none. The two screens are pages and the row menu is `LookupContextMenu`; no hand-rolled overlay.
- **i18n/RTL:** neither page is on the ratchet or in `RTL_ROUTES`, so both stay LTR English by design; the one translated surface touched, the booking confirmation, is FE-F16-9.
- **CSS modules:** apart from the rows in FE-F16-15, every class in `WhatsAppSend.module.css` (768 lines) and `WhatsAppAuth.module.css` (383) has a reader. The only `!important`s are the three in the auth page's `prefers-reduced-motion` block (the sanctioned a11y exception). The literal colours are WhatsApp's tick colours and soft row tints (deliberate) plus shadow rgba; `background: white` appears only in the `prefers-contrast: high` block, with stylelint disables that give the reason. For **F24**: the tick colours aren't tokens.
- **Gate:** not run. F16 changed only this document; the probes stayed in the scratchpad.
- **Live-data facts** (read-only; SQL inside `BEGIN READ ONLY … ROLLBACK`, prod logs grepped):
  - Recent batches: 2026-09-29 (22 reminders) and 2026-10-03 (22), each after a service restart that same day, with 25 and 33 one-off WhatsApp sends in between (FE-F16-1's evidence). There was no batch request for Saturday 2026-10-03 (39 appointments, 0 reminders sent).
  - `/api/wa/initial-state` keys: `success, htmltext, finished, clientReady, initializing, needsRelink, restoring, clientStatus, persons, qr, stats, sentMessages, failedMessages, timestamp`.

## F15 · 2026-10-01

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F15 ledger (2,256 LOC + four CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.**
  - *`configResolver` precedence, and a missing photo:* there is no automatic precedence; nothing is applied when the page opens. *Apply* lists the patient's own presentations ("This patient": the server filters by `person_id`, and the client filters again), then the generic templates, and applying over a non-empty timeline asks first ("Replace current timeline?"). Templates count the photos a patient lacks and say so ("n photos not available", or "no matching photos" when none match). The gaps are FE-F15-3 (which session *first*/*latest* mean) and FE-F15-4 (presentations check nothing).
  - *Wake lock and fullscreen on unmount, navigation and tab switch:* the hook releases its lock on unmount, and removing the fullscreen element ends fullscreen (runtime: closing the player left fullscreen). Leaving fullscreen by Esc or an OS gesture closes the player through `fullscreenchange`, gated by `wasFullscreenRef` so a denied request doesn't close it at once. When fullscreen is unavailable, the fixed overlay covers the whole viewport, above the app header (runtime, 412×915 with `requestFullscreen` removed: stage 412×915, a tap in the right third advanced, Escape closed). The tab-switch gap is FE-F15-2.
  - *Annotation canvas:* the backing store follows `devicePixelRatio`, a ResizeObserver resizes it and repaints every stroke, `touch-action: none` plus pointer capture make a finger draw instead of scroll, and the canvas is keyed by slide, so changing slide clears it. The gaps (screen-pixel coordinates, one stroke shared by two pointers) are FE-F15-6.
  - *Timers in the player:* the transition timer and the controls' auto-hide timer are cleared before each re-arm and on unmount, the window `keydown` listener is removed on unmount, and the builder's long-press timer is cleared on `pointerup`/`pointercancel`. The neighbour preloads are plain `new Image()`s, which are fine to abandon.
- **Builder gestures.** One `AbortController` per drag removes all four window listeners at once; moves are filtered by `pointerId`; `pointercancel` ends a drag without dropping; touch scrolling is blocked only while a drag is live; a touch that moves before the 220 ms hold scrolls instead of dragging, and the click that follows a drag or a scroll doesn't add. The drop index is corrected for the removed source. `computeDrop` refuses every pair the reducers would reject (a full slide, a slide onto itself, a pair as an extra), so the `toast.info` calls inside `pairPhotoOnto`/`pairSlides`' state updaters can't be reached from the UI. They would toast twice in dev StrictMode if they could. Runtime: a mouse drag paired *Smile* onto *Profile*, and a grip drag moved slide 2 to the front.
- **Player navigation.** `indexRef` keeps the keyboard handler off stale state; the ends are bounded; reduced motion skips the animations in both JS and CSS; Space and Backspace are `preventDefault`ed; a swipe needs 50 px; the tap zones are thirds of the stage; paired slides caption each photo; the dots appear only for 2–15 slides.
- **Saved presentations: the plumbing.** The payload is a closed discriminated union (1–3 photos per slide, at least one slide), and the server validates it on the way in. Writes need `CLINICAL_ROLES`, which is all three roles; reads are open to any signed-in user. Save is disabled while busy, Enter submits, and an ineligible template checkbox is disabled with the reason shown. Every write toasts its outcome through `httpErrorMessage`. Deleting a patient cascades to their presentations (`patient-queries.ts`). The invalidation's scope is FE-F15-7.
- **The stored timeline** is per patient (`slideshow_seq_{id}`), wrapped in try/catch both ways, and re-read when the patient changes. What it stores is FE-F15-4; how it is read is FE-F15-11.
- **`<Modal>` census for F15:** four render sites: the *Replace* confirm in `PatientSlideshow`, `SaveConfigModal`, `ManageConfigsModal` and `FolderPickerModal`. All four use `ModalHeader` and pass `ariaLabelledBy` to a real id. No hand-rolled overlay. The player is a fullscreen stage (`role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`), not a `Modal`, and that is right, because the modal root would not render inside the fullscreen element. None needs `unsavedGuard`: *Save* has one field, and *Manage* edits in place.
- **Dates** are `tp_date_time` `date` strings formatted by slicing, with no `new Date`, so there is no UTC shift (FE-F13-11's shape doesn't apply) and no browser-locale formatting (FE-F3-3's).
- **Identity:** no clinic name or logo anywhere in the slideshow. The placeholder is a neutral camera icon.
- **Phone width:** the builder at 412 px has no horizontal overflow (runtime; the config bar is 284 px wide and the tray 332 px). The player's fallback is above.
- **CSS modules:** 0 `!important` across the four (1,133 lines), and no unreferenced class (the one hit, `.btn`, is in a comment). The literal colours are the player's always-dark stage, the scrims over photos and the annotation palette, all deliberate (like the editor's guide lines). One undefined token: `SlideshowModals.module.css:63` uses `--primary-color-translucent`, which nothing defines, so the name field's focus ring is the fallback, 8 % black (the border still turns primary). That is a token candidate for F24. The physical `left`/`right` are fine, since the screen isn't on an RTL route.
- **Hand-downs, recorded.** F13's `configResolver.ts:120` is FE-F15-4(b). FE-F12-7's identity leak has no slideshow sibling. FE-F13-12's one-line key fix also covers FE-F15-8.
- **Gate:** not re-run. F15 changed only this document, and `git status` showed no other modified file since F13's green run the same day.
- **Live-data facts** (read-only; SQL inside `BEGIN READ ONLY … ROLLBACK`):
  - `slideshow_configs`: 2 rows, the two seeded templates (both *first* + *latest*, one slide each); 0 saved presentations; 0 templates made by a user.
  - Sessions, by view rows: 1,881 photographed patients; 802 with one session (776 of them with a *Smile*, 767 with a *Profile*); 1,079 with more than one, of whom 279 have no *Smile* in their newest session (29 of those have one in a middle session) and 14 have no view rows at all in it.
  - Files under `C:\clinic1` (depth 4, `working` excluded): 476 `.tif` in 265 patients' folders (284 at the root), no `.heic`/`.heif`.

## F14 · 2026-10-01

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F14 ledger (2,885 LOC + eleven CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.**
  - *Editor state vs disk state:* the gaps are FE-F14-2 (remove), FE-F14-3 (a session renamed or re-dated under the editor), FE-F14-4 (an original moved away under it) and FE-F14-5 (*Rename folder*). The rest holds. `HYDRATE` never overwrites a slot with a live edit, so a late render or a refetch can't wipe framing in progress. The page is keyed by `tpCode`, so no slot state leaks between sessions. A background render landing while the editor is open re-hydrates it (gallery + `filesAll` invalidated, sidebar re-listed). EXIF is handled the same on both sides: the 2048-px proxy comes from `thumbnail.service` (`.rotate()`), the render uses `.autoOrient()`, and the browser's `naturalWidth/Height` are post-EXIF. The proxy/original toggle keeps `(croppedAreaPixels, mediaSize)` in one pixel space, because both are recorded on every populated slot, active or not. The view delete removes both case variants (`workingFileNameVariants`). A cross-folder original is rendered but not tagged, by design (`tagOriginalForView`).
  - *Raw `fetch` and CSRF:* F14 has **no** raw `fetch`. Every write goes through the funnel (`postJSON`, `deleteJSON`, `postFormData`), so it carries the CSRF token. Every read carries `{ schema }`.
  - *Telegram's background job:* the poll stops on `done`; per-file failures are listed in the result view with *Try again*; a clean full send toasts and closes, once (`finalizedRef`). Closing the tab mid-job leaves the server job running to the end, which is right, but nobody hears the outcome (FE-F14-6). The gaps are FE-F14-6.
  - *LocalSend timeouts:* every outbound call has one: probe 8 s, prepare-upload 90 s (the receiver's Accept prompt), upload 5 min, protocol cancel 10 s, all through `timedFetch`'s `AbortController`. The messages are FE-F14-7.
- **The card move is safe against data loss.** Card files are removed only after the server has answered 200 for the whole batch. multer refuses a whole request (51st file, size limit) rather than skipping a file. A failed `finalizeUpload` part-way also fails the request, so nothing is deleted: a retry can duplicate the first files as `name (1).jpg`, but loses none. A file picked outside the granted card folder is removed only through its own handle, which a picker grant can't do without write permission, so it is reported as "left on the card". A forgotten or ejected card folder (`NotFoundError`) clears the remembered handle. Whether users know it is a move is FE-F14-10.
- **The render watchdog's plumbing** (its timing gap is FE-F14-4): one `ensureConnected`/`release` pair per job, an idempotent `settle()` (event and timeout can't both toast), the listener detached when the last job settles, and a deadline that scales with the slot count (90 s + 15 s per slot).
- **The save path itself.** *Save* is disabled while saving and while nothing is placed; the 202 answers in well under a second; `justSavedRef` lets the post-save navigation through the blocker, and a save or an emptied grid resets a pending block. The server renders slot by slot, tolerates partial failure, always announces completion (`finally`), and emits `tpCode` in camelCase, which the grid, the editor and the watcher all match.
- **The slot grid and cropper.** One non-passive wheel listener zooms only the selected, populated slot, with the cropper's own clamp and step; inactive slots render the same controlled cropper with `pointer-events: none`, so focusing a slot never moves its framing. Flip previews are canvas blobs that are revoked on replacement and on unmount, and a cancelled load revokes its own blob. Rotation is normalised to 0–359 in the reducer, and the slider presents it as −180…180.
- **The share chooser.** `ShareSheet` resets to the menu on every open, and only one target modal is mounted at a time. The three entry points hand it the same `ShareSource[]`; `share-ref.ts` resolves each kind on the server behind a containment check (working-folder basenames are pattern-checked, then path-checked). The Telegram recipient sources fetch only while their option is selected, and quit employees are excluded by `employeesQuery`'s default.
- **`<Modal>` census for F14:** six render sites: `PhotoEditor` ×2 (remove, leave), `RenameFolderModal`, `ShareSheet`, `TelegramShareModal`, `LocalSendShareModal`. All six pass `ariaLabelledBy` to a real id; four use `ModalHeader`, and the editor's two have their own drag-handle `<h2>` (FE-F14-8). No hand-rolled overlay. None needs `unsavedGuard`: none is a data-entry form, and the editor guards at the route level.
- **Role gates.** The editor's routes and every share write (`/share/stage`, `/telegram/send`, `/localsend/{probe,send,cancel}`) allow `CLINICAL_ROLES`; the reads are open to any signed-in user. The sidebar's file writes are `admin|front_desk` (FE-F14-9, FE-F12-6).
- **Phone width:** the share chooser, LocalSend and Telegram dialogs are 379 px wide at 412 px, with 16 px gutters and no horizontal overflow (runtime). The editor itself is a desktop drag-and-drop tool; its layout belongs to F25.
- **CSS modules:** 0 `!important` across the eleven, and no unreferenced class (the scan's four hits are comments and a `:global(.reactEasyCrop_Image)`). Literal colours: `SlotCanvas`'s `#111` crop well and the cyan guide lines drawn over photos (fixed on purpose, like the Compare stage), and `RenameFolderModal`'s selected-row tint `rgba(0, 123, 255, 0.08)` and box shadow (token candidates for F24).
- **Hand-downs, recorded.** FE-F12-7's editor siblings (`SlotGrid.tsx:108`, `SlotCanvas.tsx:151`) are FE-F14-11. F13's reader-side note (no editor write refreshes `timepointImages`) is in FE-F14-2. FE-F12-6 now also covers the editor sidebar (FE-F14-9).
- **Gate:** not re-run. F14 changed only this document, and `git status` showed no other modified file since F13's green run the same day (ESLint 0 errors + F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings; vitest 36 files / 292 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 8).

  - Patient folders under `C:\clinic1`: 3,427 patients have folders; 2,509 folders are a session's originals folder (1,484 patients have at least one); 3,315 patients have `OPG`, 100 `OPGIMG`; 124 folders are shaped like a session folder but match no session.
