## F13 · 2026-10-01

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F13 ledger (2,806 LOC + five CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.**
  - *`useQueries` + `combine`:* still there and still correct. `combine` folds the results into one record, and React Query's `replaceEqualDeep` keeps that record's identity while its content is unchanged, so the load effect that depends on it settles. Runtime: some 40 pair loads across the probes, with no loop and no freeze. The inline `combine` closes over `selectedTimepoints`, and its identity changing when the selection changes is correct.
  - *Canvas and geometry:* Compare has **no flip or mirror**. The mirror-rect + θ conjugation math belongs to the editor's server render (`photo-render.service.ts`, F14). Compare composes translate · rotate · scale, with an axis-aligned clip set before the rotate, so a crop stays straight on a rotated photo. `geometry.ts` mirrors the engine's three drawing branches exactly (`getContainerRect` ↔ `render*`, `getDrawSize` ↔ the aspect fit, `applyTransform` ↔ the canvas transform). The identity-transform branch draws the same rectangle as the transform branch, so the first adjustment doesn't jump.
  - *`devicePixelRatio`:* the canvas is sized in source pixels, never by DPR. In the *Auto* modes it is always far larger than the box it is shown in, so it never looks soft. The overlay's `dpi` is really the canvas-to-CSS ratio, which is the right scale for its handles.
  - *Resize:* the canvas keeps its aspect ratio (`max-width`/`max-height` with `auto` sizes), so it scales and never stretches. The SVG overlay has the same box and viewBox with `preserveAspectRatio="none"`, `clientToCanvas` reads the live `getBoundingClientRect`, and a `ResizeObserver` rescales the handles. Runtime at 412 px: a touch a quarter of the way down the canvas selected Image 1, the top photo.
  - *Memory:* when a load completes, the engine drops its references to the previous images and they are collected. The gaps are that superseded and timed-out downloads keep running (FE-F13-9), and how much gets decoded (FE-F13-6).
  - *Touch at 412 px:* dragging works and doesn't scroll the page. The gaps are FE-F13-13.
- **The engine as a store.** Every mutation ends in `commit()`, snapshots are immutable and rebuilt lazily, and `subscribe`/`getSnapshot` are stable arrow properties, so `useSyncExternalStore` neither tears nor loops. The engine is created in a callback ref, because the canvas mounts behind a spinner, and it is dropped when the canvas unmounts. Dev StrictMode's detach and re-attach just build a new engine. `loadSeq` makes a superseded load give up before it swaps anything in, and `logoSeq` does the same for the logo swap.
- **Crop.** Each inset is clamped to 0–0.95, the two sides of an axis are scaled back so their sum stays at or below 0.95, the handle drag holds the opposite side back by 5 %, and a double-click resets one side. *Expand* is disabled at zero, and crop mode switches itself off when the target leaves Image 1 or 2.
- **Pointer drags.** Each drag registers `pointermove`, `pointerup` and `pointercancel` on `window` under one `AbortController` and filters by `pointerId`, so a second finger can't take it over, and `pointercancel` ends it. Scale is clamped to 0.1–5 on both paths (drag and buttons). Wheel zoom is registered `passive: false`, only on the canvas shell, and acts only when something is selected.
- **Slideshow.** It steps only through types present in **both** sessions, and resets the alignment at each step. Leaving fullscreen by Esc or an OS gesture ends it (`fullscreenchange`). Runtime: fullscreen was entered in headless Chromium, and the counter read *Occlusal Lower 5 / 8* after four fast steps. The arrow buttons are `/*rtl:ignore*/` on purpose, so they match the ←/→ keys.
- **Share and save plumbing.** The native branch is gated on a real phone (the user agent, or touch plus `pointer: coarse`), unlike the grid in FE-F12-2, so Windows Chrome gets the in-app ShareSheet. The staged upload goes through the funnel and so carries the CSRF token. `isSharingRef` stops a double native share, and `staging` disables both Share buttons. *Save* downloads through a data-URL anchor, which worked at 45 MB in Chrome (runtime). The stage route allows `CLINICAL_ROLES`, so every role that can open Compare can share from it (unlike FE-F12-6). The two failures are FE-F13-6's size and timeout.
- **Role gates.** Apart from the stage upload, Compare only makes GETs, and every read is open to any signed-in user, which is right for a staff screen.
- **`<Modal>` census for F13:** no `Modal` render site of its own. The only overlay is the shared `ShareSheet` (F14's), there is no hand-rolled overlay, and fullscreen is the browser's own.
- **CSS modules:** 0 `!important` across the five, and no unreferenced classes (the two hits, `w3` and `org`, are inside the rotate cursor's SVG data URL). The literal colours are the stage's pinned night palette, dark on purpose like a lightbox, and the overlay's selection and crop strokes, which match the canvas constants in `CompareOverlay.tsx`. `left`/`right` appear only on the `/*rtl:ignore*/` slideshow arrows and in one `left: 50%` centring. The rail cards use only semantic tokens (0 `--palette-` in `ControlsPanel`/`SelectionPanel`), so they follow dark mode.
- **Hand-downs, recorded.** FE-F12-7's Compare sibling (`compare/types.ts:128`) is FE-F13-7; `photo-editor/SlotGrid.tsx:108` and `SlotCanvas.tsx:151` stay with F14. FE-F3-3 lists `SelectionPanel` as one of its sites, and FE-F13-11 adds the UTC half. FE-F12-3(c) is recorded in the ledger from the reader's side. F0 noted `CompareComponent` as the one `useQueries` that has a `combine`; confirmed above.
- **Gate baseline re-measured (2026-10-01):** `npm run gate` green. ESLint shows 0 errors and F0's same 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (2285/2309); vitest 36 files / 292 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 8, at baseline; both typechecks clean; build green. F13 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts** (read-only, with the owner's go-ahead; every query ran inside `BEGIN READ ONLY … ROLLBACK`):
  - Sessions: 1,910 photographed patients, all with a tp 0 (1,886 named *Initial*). 346 have three or more sessions, and at least 85 of their default pairs share no view. 25 patients have a code order that differs from their date order.
  - Rows vs files: 22,743 view rows, 20 of which point at missing files; 35 view files with no row; 21,658 view files that exist only in upper case.
  - Renders since June: 1,058 of 1,068 are lower case; median 2.2 MB (p90 4.4 MB, max 7.2 MB); facial views a median of ~18 MP, intraoral ~11 MP.
  - Production log, 9 Jun–21 Sep: 438 views rendered, 32 of them more than once.

## F12 · 2026-10-01

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F12 ledger (3,245 LOC + seven CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's four questions, answered.** *URLs and `encodeRelPath`:* correct. Folders named `صور قديمة`, `a#b`, `50% off`, `q?x` and `a+b c` were created, then opened both by click and by URL, with the right breadcrumb (runtime). Both content-URL builders use `URLSearchParams`, and `/DolImgs/{name}` only ever gets a name the server built. The only name defect is in **uploaded file** names (FE-F12-1). *Delete scopes:* each scope removes exactly what its dialog lists, checked on disk. *Cropped* removes the `working/` renders; *entry* also removes the row (cascading to `time_point_images`) and keeps the originals; *all* also removes the originals folder. The gaps are which folder *all* resolves to after a collided rename (FE-F12-5), that it is permanent, and the private flags left behind (FE-F12-11). *A large session:* grid cells are 480 px WebP thumbnails from the working-files thumbnail endpoint (runtime `naturalWidth` 416/480), versioned by `?v=mtime`, and the full-resolution image loads only in the lightbox. The explorer virtualizes rows (TanStack Virtual, overscan 8), with 240 px lazy thumbnails and a truncation notice. *Invalidation:* FE-F12-3 and FE-F12-4. Otherwise, the explorer's own writes invalidate `qk.patient.filesAll` (every path, flat or not), which matches F2's map.
- **Path safety on the server** (runtime, all refused): `files?path=..%2F3` → 403, `path=%2Fetc` → 404, `files/content?path=..%2Fworking%2F…` → 403, `working-files/content?name=..%2F1%2Fx` → 400, another patient's render by `name` → 400, `path=.trash` → 404.
- **The lightbox lifecycle.** There is one PhotoSwipe instance per gallery identity, destroyed in the effect's own cleanup. `appendToEl` follows native full screen, so the lightbox stays visible in the top layer. The cached share blob is cleared on destroy and on unmount. `trapFocus: false` is a documented, accepted trade-off (the LocalSend modal needs the keyboard). The anonymize bar is an SVG whose `viewBox` is the photo's natural size with `xMidYMid meet`, which is exactly `object-fit: contain`. In the lightbox it is repositioned on every `imageSizeChange`. The full-screen overlay's Escape handler steps aside while the lightbox is open.
- **The live-render hook-up.** The grid holds the shared appointments channel by refcount (acquire on mount, release on unmount). `photos_rendered` matches both `tpCode` and `tp_code` and refetches only its own patient and session.
- **The session menu.** The originals-folder check runs only when the menu opens, a late answer for another tab's menu is dropped (`menuTpRef`), and the popover is clamped to the viewport. Its keyboard gaps are FE-F12-13(b).
- **The explorer's writes.** Upload never overwrites (it names duplicates `name (1).ext`), gets 120 s on both sides (FE-F1-2's fix holds), and is staged on the same volume. Batch delete sends only selected paths that are still in the listing. The selection clears when the folder or the flat toggle changes. Single and batch delete confirm through `useConfirm` and go to `.trash`.
- **Double-click on a folder is harmless** (runtime, with no gap and with a 180 ms gap): it lands in the folder and opens nothing inside it. Double-clicking a file at a human pace leaves the preview open.
- **`<Modal>` census for F12:** five render sites (`EditTimepointModal`, `DeleteTimepointModal`, `PhotoSessionDialog`, the explorer's prompt, `FilePreviewModal`), and all five pass `ariaLabelledBy` to a real id. Three use `ModalHeader`; the prompt and the preview have their own headers, and the preview's is the drag handle. None has `unsavedGuard`; RA5's candidate list already names the two data-entry ones.
- **Role gates.** Reads (timepoints, gallery, folder check, files, working files, photo dates) are open to any signed-in user, which is right for staff screens. The writes are `admin|front_desk`, and whether `clinical` should have them is FE-F12-6.
- **`useImportFolder`:** F3 read it (clean apart from FE-F1-5), and it is unchanged. FE-F1-5 still can't be exercised here, because headless Chromium can't grant a real directory handle. It stays with F17 or a manual check.
- **CSS modules:** 0 `!important` across the seven modules. The only unreferenced classes are FE-F12-16's two. The literal colours are overlay `rgba()`s and the anonymize bar's `#000`, which is black on purpose.
- **Hand-downs, recorded.** FE-F0-2 is confirmed and folded into FE-F12-16. F0's `TODO(phase3)` is FE-F12-12. FE-F4-17's concrete case is FE-F12-8(c). FE-F3-3: `fileHelpers.formatDate` (`toLocaleDateString(undefined, …)`) is one of its sites; the explorer isn't translated, so it matters only on an Arabic-locale Windows box.
- **Gate baseline re-measured (2026-10-01):** `npm run gate` green. ESLint shows 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (still at 2285/2309); vitest 36 files / 292 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 8 (at baseline); both typechecks clean; build green. F12 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (throwaway install). For the owner to run, read-only:
  - FE-F12-1, garbled upload names on the clinic volume (PowerShell on the server): `Get-ChildItem C:\clinic1 -Recurse -File | Where-Object Name -match '[ØÙ]' | Measure-Object`.
  - FE-F12-11, hidden flags a future session would inherit: `SELECT p.person_id, p.timepoint_code, p.image_name FROM private_photos p LEFT JOIN time_points t ON t.person_id = p.person_id AND t.tp_code::text = p.timepoint_code WHERE t.time_point_id IS NULL;`

## F11 · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F11 ledger (3,374 LOC + eleven CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The plan's five questions, answered.** *Event storm:* one day-query per SSE frame per open board, with at most one in flight per client (FE-F11-12). *"Live" after a laptop sleep:* not trustworthy (FE-F11-7), and after a proxied restart the board never reconnects at all (FE-F11-3). *`getCurrentStatus()`:* not filed as i18n, as the plan says; it is filed as **dead** (FE-F11-9), because it reads fields the payload never has. *Chair CLOSED-stream self-reload:* correct in code (delayed by `CLOSED_STREAM_RELOAD_DELAY_MS`, latched, cleared in cleanup, gated by `cancelled`); its single-shot limit is FE-F11-15(d). *Kiosk pinned light + LTR:* verified at runtime (`data-theme="light"`, `dir="ltr"`, `lang="en"`, no app header), and the unmount restores the stored theme and language. *Videos:* the first request is `Range: bytes=0-` → 206, `streamFile` handles suffix, multi and unsatisfiable ranges and destroys the read stream on abort; the global 30 s timeout leaves a streaming response alone (`headersSent`). Memory is FE-F11-13.
- **The check-in state machine is sound on the server.** `updatePresent` takes `FOR UPDATE` inside a transaction, the forward guards match the card's enabled buttons one for one (undo check-in disabled once seated, undo seat disabled once dismissed, dismiss disabled until seated), and a stale click comes back as 400 `INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION`, which the client recovers from silently through the shared `isInvalidStateTransition`. Every state write broadcasts the appointment's **own** day. A double click cannot double-submit: the list is `pointer-events: none` from the click until the refetch lands.
- **Role gates.** The daily read, both state endpoints and quick check-in are admin / front_desk / clinical, and the client offers them to all three: correct. `/api/tv-display` is `USE [authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical)]`, matching `SettingsComponent`'s "deliberately available to every staff role" comment. Video writes are `CLINICAL_ROLES`; reads are open to any signed-in user; the share page `/v/:id` is public by design (educational content).
- **The doctor filter scales.** It is a client-side filter over one day's payload (≤ ~400 rows at the CLAUDE.md ceiling). The dropdown and the card badges share `useAppointmentDoctors` with the calendar, and each colour is the employee's own `appointment_color`, so adding a doctor never recolours another. `DoctorFilterSelect` portals to `<body>`, clamps to the viewport and removes its three listeners on close. The a11y gaps are FE-F11-11(e).
- **Stats.** The chips are derived from the two **filtered** lists, so they follow the doctor and search filters and equal the server's whole-day numbers when unfiltered. The count-up animation is StrictMode-safe: it resumes from `displayed` rather than guarding on previous props, and clears its intervals in cleanup.
- **The kiosk's visit summary is safe HTML.** Everything is escaped, then only attribute-less `<br>`, `<font color=blue>` and `</font>` are re-enabled, and `buildVisitSummary` emits exactly those. A user who types them gets a line break or a blue span, nothing more. The chair server channel checks `chairId` against `1–10` on both sides, guards stale loads with `chairEpoch`, expires the replay after 12 h and sits behind the auth gate. Runtime: the three intraoral images loaded through the session cookie.
- **The TV tab's settings draft is correct.** The render-phase seed is keyed on the serialized server payload (the per-image map sorted, so key order never reads as dirty), a remote change re-seeds only when nothing is edited locally, *Discard* moves the seed with the draft, and the 20 s status refetch never clobbers an edit. A failed upload leaves nothing behind (runtime: the staging folder was empty after the timed-out upload). The playlist half is FE-F11-14.
- **Videos.** The upload carries 120 s on both sides (FE-F1-2's fix holds). The thumbnail has a fallback image and a keyboard path (`role="button"`, Enter/Space), the print window escapes the title and URL, and every write invalidates `qk.videos.all()`, matching F2's map.
- **SSE transport hygiene on the server** matches CLAUDE.md: `setTimeout(0)` on both halves, the four headers, a 2.5–3.5 s jittered `retry:`, one shared 25 s keep-alive interval (`unref()`'d), and teardown ends every stream and clears the chair maps.
- **The quick check-in's timestamp** (F10's hand-down) is right for the board: a walk-in's time is the minute it arrived, and it sorts correctly. Nothing more to file.
- **CSS modules:** 0 dead classes across the eleven modules (the scan's two hits in `Videos.module.css` are the `w3.org` inside a data URI) and 0 `!important`. The physical `left`/`right` properties are flipped by postcss-rtlcss. Literal colours are limited to box-shadow `rgba()`s and the kiosk's own palette (22 in `ChairDisplay.module.css`), which is deliberately outside the theme because the kiosk is pinned light.
- **Hand-downs, recorded.** FE-F3-9: decided (FE-F11-8a). FE-F3-8: the card's reader list is under the F11 table. FE-F1-7: pair the channel extraction with FE-F11-3 and FE-F11-7 in RC, since both fixes belong in it. FE-F2-8 (the `qk.appointments` namespace fiction): unchanged; RC can fold it into FE-F11-10/-17. FE-F4-10 (the chair beacon order): unchanged, still a server change.
- **Gate baseline re-measured (2026-09-29):** `npm run gate` green. ESLint shows 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (still at 2285/2309); vitest 34 files / 279 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 8 (at baseline); both typechecks clean; build green. F11 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (no clinic DB in this session). For the owner to run on the clinic DB, read-only, to size the findings:
  - FE-F11-1, afternoon stamps shown as AM (last 30 days): `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE present >= '13:00') AS pm, count(*) AS total FROM appointments WHERE present IS NOT NULL AND app_day >= CURRENT_DATE - 30;`
  - FE-F11-4, check-ins on days that have not happened yet (certainly wrong): `SELECT appointment_id, app_day, present FROM appointments WHERE app_day > CURRENT_DATE AND (present IS NOT NULL OR seated IS NOT NULL OR dismissed IS NOT NULL);`
  - FE-F11-16: `SELECT option_value FROM options WHERE option_name = 'VideosPath';` (set on this clinic; the point is every new install). FE-F11-6: whether the service's environment sets `PUBLIC_URL`.

## F10 · 2026-09-29

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F10 ledger (4,490 LOC + five CSS modules + `appointment-calendar.css`). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The clinic week is right everywhere on this surface.** The grid pages by `addWorkingDays` and anchors on `getWeekStartSaturday`; the month grid has six Sat–Thu columns and the server skips `getDay() === 5`; the picker omits the Friday cell and offsets the 1st correctly when it falls on a Friday; the server's `getCalendarGridEnd` never lands on a Friday. FE-F3-1's weekend fix holds, and no calendar file re-derives a weekend.
- **Wall-clock discipline holds on the write path.** Every appointment write sends a local `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS` built from date/time strings (form, edit form, drag), never `toISOString()`. The server casts it `::timestamp`. `APPOINTMENT_SUMMARY_SELECT` returns `to_char(… 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS')`, so the list and the edit form parse a local time. The picker's `formatLocalDate` and the forms' date extraction use local getters. The only UTC parses are the date-only ones in FE-F10-16. The `toISOString()` in `/available-slots`' `dateTime` field is never read by the client.
- **The translated booking workflow is in the ratchet and renders correctly.** The four files are in `eslint.config.js`'s `i18next/no-literal-string` list; `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS` and the `index.html` FOUC regexes match `appointments`, `new-appointment` and `edit-appointment(/.*)?` exactly. `formatAppointmentDateTime`, `formatAppointmentListDateTime`, `formatScheduleDate`, `formatMonthName` and `calendarWeekdayHeaders` all take `language` as a parameter. The Arabic weekday headers are the short forms without "ال", in Sat–Thu order, and digits stay Western. The calendar screen itself (`/calendar`) is untranslated and LTR, which is correct.
- **FE-F2-1's fix holds.** Create, edit and delete from the patient screens invalidate `qk.calendar.all()` as well as `qk.patient.all()`; `qk.calendar.all()` is a true prefix of every calendar leaf (range, month, stats, availability, slots). The gap is the reverse direction (FE-F10-10).
- **Server-side rules match what the forms assume.** Create and update share `createAppointmentBody` (enumerated, strict) and run the same holiday / doctor / one-per-patient-per-day checks. The PUT and DELETE broadcast both affected days to the Daily Appointments SSE, so that board is not stale after a calendar write. A second DELETE of the same id returns 200, so the calendar's missing in-flight guard (FE-F10-20) cannot surface an error. The create form maps the two conflict codes onto translated messages (the edit form's gap is FE-F10-17).
- **Double-submit is guarded** on both forms (`loading` disables Cancel and Create/Update) and in the holiday modal (`saving`).
- **Doctor colours are stable** when doctors are added or leave: a colour is the employee's own `appointment_color`, not a position in a list, so adding, reordering or deactivating one doctor never recolours another. `useAppointmentDoctors` builds the legend and the tints from one memoized read, so they cannot disagree. The literal defaults are FE-F10-11. At a 20-doctor center, doctors without a picked colour render neutral. That is a design choice, not a defect.
- **Per-doctor filtering scales.** The filter goes into the query key and into the SQL (`ta.dr_id = $doctorId` inside the JOIN), not into a client-side filter over the whole payload. `hideEmptySlots` then collapses empty rows. A 30-day window at the CLAUDE.md ceiling (~400/day) is ~12k appointments in one `/range` payload; the zoom maximum (`N_MAX = 30`) is the thing to revisit if a center ever reaches it, and F26 can measure it.
- **Holiday handling on writes is right.** The server refuses a booking or an edit into a holiday (`HOLIDAY_CONFLICT`, worded on create). The picker makes holiday days non-clickable. The calendar's drop target refuses a holiday column (`dropEffect: 'none'`, no `onDrop`). `fillCalendar` skips holiday dates. The visibility of appointments already on a holiday is FE-F10-3.
- **Cleanup and listeners.** The "+N more" popover, both context menus and the month panel each remove the document listeners they add. The menus arm their outside-click listener on the next frame and cancel that frame in cleanup. `HolidayQuickModal` clears its focus timer. The forms cancel their Web Animations flash on unmount. `AnalogClock` pauses its 1 s tick on a hidden tab and re-syncs on return. The zoom debounce timer is cleared per change.
- **Render-phase seeds are keyed correctly.** `HolidayQuickModal` (open + holiday id, reset while closed), `EditAppointmentForm` (appointment identity), the picker's three option seeds and its auto-expand key each re-run exactly when their input changes, and none writes state it reads in the same guard. The seed *source* is FE-F10-1, not the seeding.
- **CSS modules: only `.dayTooltip` is dead** (FE-F10-19). 0 `!important` in the five modules (`AppointmentForm.module.css`'s only hit is a comment explaining why it avoids one). `appointment-calendar.css`'s dead and unstyled classes are in FE-F10-19; its dynamic classes (`count-*`, `tint-zebra`, `cal-legend-swatch neutral`) are live through template strings.
- **Gate baseline re-measured in this environment (2026-09-29):** `npm run gate` green. 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (now at 2285/2309 — the same two elements, moved by later edits); 34 test files / 279 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 **8** (the script's baseline is now 8, one under CLAUDE.md's "baseline 9"); both typechecks clean; build green. F10 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (no clinic DB in this session). For the owner to run on the clinic DB, read-only, to size the findings:
  - FE-F10-2, the slot table's reach: `SELECT min(app_date), max(app_date), count(*) FROM calendar;`. Appointments the grid can never show inside that window: `SELECT count(*) FROM appointments a WHERE a.app_date >= (SELECT min(app_date) FROM calendar) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM calendar c WHERE c.app_date = a.app_date);`. And the hidden rows: `SELECT option_name, option_value FROM options WHERE option_name LIKE 'CALENDAR_%';`
  - FE-F10-3: `SELECT count(*) FROM appointments a JOIN holidays h ON a.app_date::date = h.holiday_date WHERE a.app_date >= CURRENT_DATE;`
  - FE-F10-7, slots already over the limit: `SELECT app_date, count(*) FROM appointments GROUP BY app_date HAVING count(*) > COALESCE((SELECT option_value::int FROM options WHERE option_name = 'MaxAppointmentsPerSlot'), 3) ORDER BY 2 DESC LIMIT 20;`
  - FE-F10-11: `SELECT id, employee_name, appointment_color FROM employees WHERE id IN (1, 7);` (on this clinic the literals match; the point is every other install).
  - FE-F10-13: `SELECT e.id, e.employee_name, p.position_name FROM employees e JOIN positions p ON p.id = e.position WHERE e.get_appointments AND p.position_name <> 'Doctor';`

## F9 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F9 ledger (2,928 LOC + eight CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The tooth-numbering answer the plan asked for.** `DentalChart` and `TeethSelector` use one notation: quadrant + `1–8` for permanent teeth, `A–E` for primary teeth. They share the 32 chart SVGs, which are named by that code, so `tooth_numbers.tooth_code` **must** keep those exact strings or the selector's images break. `DentalChart` emits `UR6` / `Between UR1 and UL1` as free text into the notes. `TeethSelector` stores `tooth_numbers.id`s and sorts right quadrants 8→1 and left 1→8 by `tooth_number`, which is correct Palmer display order. `Diagnosis` has no tooth mapping at all (free text). The only divergence is the 3Shape tab (FE-F9-6) and the Diagnosis placeholder. `tooth_numbers` is not seeded by any migration; that is the documented clinical-vocabulary data load, not a frontend defect.
- **Role gates match the server.** Every visit endpoint, both diagnosis writes and every WebCeph endpoint is `authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical)` = `CLINICAL_ROLES` = all three roles. The WorkCard buttons (New Visit / Visits / Diagnosis) are ungated, and that is correct. `GET /api/diagnosis/:workId` carries only `validate(params)` in its own chain, and is authenticated by the `USE /api [authenticate]` layer above it. `/clinic-assets` sits behind `USE / [authenticateWeb]`.
- **Double-submit is guarded.** `NewVisitComponent`'s `loading` disables both submit buttons, and implicit Enter-submission is suppressed while the default button is disabled (F7's rule). Diagnosis Save and Reset share `saving || deleting`. The diagnosis upsert is idempotent on `UNIQUE (work_id)`, so even the timer window in FE-F9-7 cannot create a second row. The WebCeph create is guarded server-side: a local-row check before the external call, and "already in WebCeph" refused. Both upload paths resolve the WebCeph id from the DB and never from the client.
- **Dates are handled tz-safely where they matter.** `formatISODate`/`formatDate` pass bare `YYYY-MM-DD` through without `new Date()`. The diagnosis GET returns `to_char(dx_date,'YYYY-MM-DD')`, and the POST builds local midnight (`parseLocalDate`). `visit_date` is passed through as a string (`toDateOnly`). The Visit History sort's `new Date(visit_date)` parses both sides as UTC midnight, so the comparison is consistent. `visit_date` is `NOT NULL`, so no `NaN` sort. The only browser-locale formats (WebCeph `createdAt`, X-ray dates) are FE-F3-3's census.
- **Visit contract round-trip.** The form's `''` for "no wire/operator" is coerced by `optionalCoercedInt` (`'' → undefined`) and stored as NULL. The five flags are `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`, and the form always sends booleans. The edit seed maps every NULL text column to `''`, so the `z.string().optional()` fields never receive `null`. The latest-wires quick-pick sets numeric ids that the `<select>` shows as selected.
- **Invalidation that is right.** Visit add, update and delete all refresh `qk.work.all(workId)`, which covers `visits`, `latestWires` and the shell's `details`. Diagnosis save and delete invalidate `qk.work.diagnosis` while the page is still mounted, so the refetch lands before the navigation. The WebCeph create writes the new link with `setQueryData` (no refetch flash). The gaps are FE-F9-2 (`visit.byId`) and FE-F9-4 (`patient.all`).
- **3Shape client assumptions, recorded for when it is first connected.** The client reads only the normalized contract (`cases[].{id, workflowStatus, creationDate, indications[].{from,to,type,material}}` and `media[].{id, mediaType, captureDate, uniteCloudLink, files[]}`). The server's `dtos.ts` matches the PDF's camelCase and parses leniently (`safeParse` + skip). Both reads are `retry: false`, deliberately, with a documented reason. The thumbnail and download proxies take the 3Shape id verbatim in the path (the service says it arrives already percent-encoded; the one thing to check on the first live response). `uniteCloudLink` opens with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`. The not-connected / unreachable error text comes from the server via `httpErrorMessage`, which is the "is the workstation on?" path CLAUDE.md asks for.
- **Legacy `tshape:` leftovers: none on the client.** The only trace in `public/js` is `Navigation.tsx:112`'s comment, which accurately records the replacement. The protocol handler itself (`protocol-handlers/`, the `TShapePath` INI key) is **intentionally kept** as a fallback per `3shape-integration.md`. Not dead.
- **CSS modules: nothing to file.** Eight modules, 2,722 lines: 0 `!important`, 0 dead classes (every class in each module has a `styles.x` reader in its component), and one literal colour: `NewVisitComponent.module.css:880`, a `-webkit-tap-highlight-color` rgba (cosmetic, not a themeable surface). The X-ray placeholder's cascade loss is FE-F9-5 and is not re-filed.
- **i18n posture is as designed.** Diagnosis, the visit screens, X-rays and 3D Scans are doctor-facing English, on LTR routes (none are in `RTL_ROUTES`/`RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS`), and are not findings. Only `WebCephModal` renders inside a translated RTL route (FE-F9-9).
- **Gate baseline re-measured in this environment (2026-09-28):** `npm run gate` green. 0 errors and exactly F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings; 26 test files / 229 tests; contracts D1 0 / D2 9 at baseline; both typechecks clean; build green. F9 changed only this document.
- **Live-data facts: not recorded** (no clinic DB in this session). For the owner to run on the clinic DB, read-only, to size the findings:
  - FE-F9-1's blast radius: `SELECT count(*) AS diagnoses, count(*) FILTER (WHERE coalesce(c_sna,c_snb,c_anb,f_vertical,o_overjet,i_upper_crowding) IS NOT NULL) AS with_workup FROM diagnoses;`
  - FE-F9-2's frequency: `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE updated_at IS NOT NULL) AS edited, count(*) AS visits FROM visits;`
  - FE-F9-4's reach: `SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE f_photo) AS final_photo_visits, count(*) FILTER (WHERE i_photo AND f_photo) AS both_same_visit FROM visits;` and `SELECT count(*) FROM works w WHERE w.status = 2 AND w.f_photo_date IS NOT NULL;`
  - FE-F9-9: `SELECT count(*) FROM patients WHERE web_ceph_patient_id IS NOT NULL;`
  - FE-F9-10: open one `OPG/.csi_data/.version_4.4/*/meta` file and note the format of the value two lines under `'seriesDate'`.

## F8 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F8 ledger (3,633 LOC + three CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **FE-F7-5's hand-down is answered: no UI path can put EUR on a ledger.** `PaymentModal` takes its account currency from the work, which is `USD`/`IQD` only since RB1 (enum on the three work bodies and on `addWorkWithInvoice`; `insertFullPaymentInvoice` throws on anything else). Its payment-currency select is USD / IQD / MIXED, and the invoice body carries only `usdReceived`/`iqdReceived`. Aligner-set payments are USD-only by contract (FE-F8-6 is about the *work's* currency, not EUR). The expense form offers IQD / USD only. One server-side looseness remains: `expense.contract.ts` types `currency` as `z.string().optional()`, so only a non-UI caller could write another code (live: IQD 4,331 · USD 19, nothing else). Worth the same one-line `z.enum` RB1 gave the work bodies when FE-F8-1's slice runs. It is not filed, because no UI can produce it.
- **Currency separation holds on every F8 screen.** The payment modal converts only the foreign leg, and change is always IQD. The expense summary, the expense table and both statistics drill-downs keep IQD and USD in separate figures and never add them. `DailyInvoicesModal`'s cash totals sum `iqd_received`/`usd_received` (cash actually taken), not `amount_paid` (the ledger currency), which is right for a cash count.
- **The payment math matches the server's rules.** The client's overpayment check is the server's rule: remaining = `total_required − discount − TotalPaid`, with the discount subtracted (unlike FE-F7-6's history box). The server re-checks under a row lock (`addInvoiceWithBalanceGuard`), so the client check is a fast fail, not the guarantee. `change` is submitted exactly when `isChangeDisabled` says it is tracked, and the receipt shows what was recorded (the RA-era fix holds). A negative amount typed into the modal reaches the server and 400s through `moneyInt`, and the toast shows the server's text through `httpErrorMessage`. The rounding directions are deliberate and consistent: what the patient owes rounds **up**, what they hand over rounds **down**, and change rounds down to 1,000 IQD. Registering a whole-thousand IQD amount therefore can never come out short.
- **The set-state-in-effect cascade has not regressed.** `PaymentModal` has **0** `useEffect`s. The six keyed render-phase blocks each write only state outside their own key and converge (runtime: typing, mode switches and the rate editor all settle, and ESLint is clean on every F8 file). The rate editor closes when the payment date changes, so it can never write to a date other than the one it opened for, and it refuses to overwrite a day's own rate. `unsavedGuard` + `resetKey` are wired as RA5 recorded.
- **The rate path is the right one.** The modal reads `exchangeRateForDate`, which carries the last rate forward and says so in the banner ("carried forward from …"). The server validates change against `getExchangeRateAsOf(paymentDate)`, the same as-of semantics. A rate save invalidates `qk.exchangeRates.all()`, which prefixes the modal's `forDate` key and the Settings history.
- **Cache after a payment.** Save invalidates `qk.work.all(workId)`, which covers the payment-history list and the work header. Closing the modal (after success or not) invalidates `qk.patient.all`, which covers the works list with its Paid/Remaining. `dailyInvoices` and `reports.*` are keyed by date/period and not invalidated, as F2 recorded. The only gap is the aligner path (FE-F8-7).
- **Approval tiers say so.** A front-desk edit or delete of an expense created before today returns `outcome: 'pending'`. The page then toasts it (in English, FE-F8-8), calls `notifyApprovalsChanged()` (FE-F3-2's fix holds at `useExpenses:230,255`) and leaves the row untouched instead of looking applied. `addInvoice` is not a hold-tier write, and an invoice **delete** is F7's (`WorkComponent`).
- **Role gates match the server.** `addInvoice`, `updateExchangeRateForDate` and `POST /api/aligner/payments` are `admin|front_desk`, and the modal is only reachable through the card's `writeFinance`-gated button (RB1). The aligner drawer's button is `caps.writeFinance`. Expense reads are any-authenticated, and expense writes are `admin|front_desk` (+ `requireRecordAge`): the page hides Add / Edit / Delete behind `caps.writeFinance` and shows the table read-only to clinical. The statistics drill-downs are `FINANCE_ROLES` / `ADMIN_ROLES` and are only opened from views gated the same way (F5).
- **The expense form basics.** The amount is digits-only (`[^\d]` stripped), so it is integer and non-negative like the contract's `moneyInt`. Only the sub-level matching the chosen category is sent (subcategory, lab or employee). The category change resets all three. Submit is guarded by `submitting`. The Lab dropdown's right-click editor invalidates `qk.lookups.labs()`, which is exactly `labsQuery`'s key, so the live dropdown refreshes (checked by code). The hardcoded category ids 5 (Employees) and 7 (Lab) are seed ids, documented as fixed across deployments in `config/expenseCategories.ts`, the same posture as the backend constant.
- **`PaymentFormDrawer`.** Digits-only amount, the balance check mirrors the server's set-balance rule (the authoritative check runs locked in `createAlignerPayment`), dismiss is blocked while saving, and the server's message reaches the drawer (`PatientSets` rethrows it via `httpErrorMessage`). The `Balance || set_cost` fallback would misread a zero balance, but the only opener requires `Balance > 0`, so it is unreachable. Its missing `unsavedGuard` is RA5's documented deferral, not re-filed. Its English strings are fine: `PatientSets` is untranslated (staged rollout).
- **The i18n ratchet is in sync for both F8 translations.** The six expense files and `PaymentModal` are in `eslint.config.js`'s list. `/expenses` is in `RTL_ROUTES` and in `index.html`'s FOUC list. `PaymentModal` inherits the Works route's RTL. `payments.json` (73 keys) and `expenses.json` (86) have EN/AR parity, and **every key is used**. Money renders with Western digits everywhere (`toLocaleString('en-US')` / `Intl.NumberFormat('en-US')`). The browser-locale dates in `ExpenseTable`, `DeleteConfirmModal` and `DailyInvoicesModal` are already counted in F3's census (FE-F3-3), and `useExpenses`' shared `loading` flag is FE-F3-12. Neither is re-filed.
- **CSS modules: nothing to file.** `Expenses.module.css` (859), `PaymentModal.module.css` (821), `DoctorPaymentsModal.module.css` (255): 0 `!important`. The literal `rgba(255,255,255,.95)` field backgrounds on the expense filter card are deliberate and annotated: theme-independent fields on a fixed gradient card, with a stylelint disable and a note on why a flipping token turned them white-on-white. The remaining literals are shadows.
- **Live-data facts recorded so nobody re-queries them** (2026-09-28). `expenses`: 4,350 rows (2020-07-09 → 2026-09-28), currency IQD 4,331 · USD 19, 0 future-dated, 0 on a quit employee, 0 on an inactive lab, at most 104 in any month. Categories: 1 → 741, 2 → 333, 3 → 274, 4 → 2,706, 5 → 233, 6 → 18, 7 → 21, 8 → 1, none → 23. September 2026: 83 expenses, 3,001,750 IQD + 200 USD. No day since 2025-01-01 has expenses without an invoice. `sms` rates: 1,556 (09-27) · 1,575 (09-21) · 1,586 (09-16); 2026-09-28 has none of its own, so the modal shows the carried-forward banner. `aligner_sets` × `works.currency`: USD/USD 89 · NULL/USD 46 · USD/IQD 8 · NULL/IQD 3 (FE-F8-6); none of the 11 on IQD works has a payment.

## F7 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F7 ledger (3,425 LOC + six CSS modules). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The i18n ratchet is in sync for the Works page.** `WorkComponent` and `WorkCard` are in `eslint.config.js`'s list. `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS` has exactly `/^\/patient\/[^/]+\/works\/?$/`, and `index.html`'s FOUC check carries the same regex. `works.json` has 135 keys with EN/AR parity, every literal `t('…')` key exists, and the four `filter.*` keys are live (read through `labelKey`). `WorkDetailsPanel`/`WorkDetailItem` are English by design (doctor-facing, CLAUDE.md). `NewWorkComponent` and `TransferWorkModal` are English and off the list: staged rollout. The only i18n findings are FE-F7-11a and FE-F7-12.
- **Double-submit is guarded on every create/update path.** `loading` disables both submit buttons and is set synchronously inside the submit handler. Implicit Enter-submission is suppressed while the form's default button is disabled. Both confirm dialogs' buttons share `loading`. The "Mark as fully paid" path cannot fall into the duplicate-active dialog, so it can never be re-sent through `/api/addwork` without its invoice: `unq_tblwork_active` is partial on `status = 1`, and `addWorkWithInvoice` inserts status 2.
- **Work-creation invalidation is complete.** `qk.patient.all(personId)` covers works, `info` (the works-derived patient type, recomputed server-side in the create transaction) and `hasAppointment`. The fully-paid invoice arrives as the works row's `TotalPaid`. Update invalidates `patient.all` + `work.all(workId)`. Finish-existing-then-add invalidates before and after the add, correctly. The gap is the lifecycle writes only (FE-F7-8).
- **Item writes refresh exactly what they change.** `qk.work.detailsList(workId)` is the key the panel reads, and it is awaited before edit mode closes. The item routes touch no `works` row, so no works-list invalidation is owed. Lab-case writes go through `useLabCases` (F3's reference implementation). The draft is rebuilt from the row on every `enterEdit`, so a refetch while reading never leaks into the next edit. Item cost is digits-only and integer.
- **Shade pairing is correct client-side.** Changing the system clears the shade. The shade select is disabled until a system is chosen and lists only that system's values. The stored `shade_system` is the hardcoded label from `shade-queries.ts#getShades`, so the label is a stored key and renaming it orphans rows. That is the documented design (`[[shade-selection-feature]]`), and 1 live row uses it. The contract does not check the pair server-side; the client is the only pairing guard. The lab dropdown keeps a deactivated lab selectable by its joined name, which is the pattern FE-F7-1 needs.
- **Transfer semantics are right on the server.** Only `works.person_id` moves, and everything keyed by `work_id` goes with it: invoices, visits, items, diagnoses, aligner sets/batches, implants, screws. Time points, appointments and patient files are per-person and stay, which is correct. Both patients' types are recomputed in the same transaction. Same patient → 400; an active-work conflict at the target → 409. It is admin-only on both sides (`isAdmin` / `authorize(admin)` on preview and transfer), `PatientQuickSearch` excludes the source, and the confirm is two-step with a "cannot be undone" warning. The preview is keyed on the work id only, which is correct because it does not depend on the target. The step advance is a render-phase transition guard, and Back clears the selection.
- **Discount editing matches the server.** The client hint is the server's `validateDiscount` rule (discount ≤ total − paid), and the server validates before the role gate, so an over-limit front-desk discount 400s instead of queuing. Non-admin changes go to the approval queue with `notifyApprovalsChanged()` (FE-F3-2 holds at `NewWorkComponent:317`, `WorkComponent:324,754`). All 4 live discounted works carry a `discount_date`, so the "default to today" branch cannot turn an unrelated edit into a phantom discount-date change.
- **Work delete** reads the 409's `details.dependencies` in the server's `WorkDependencies` shape and handles the hold-tier `pending` outcome. Its dialog is `<Modal>` + `<ModalHeader variant="danger">` with a matching `titleId`/`ariaLabelledBy`. The status-change and duplicate-work dialogs are wired the same way. `NewWorkComponent`'s 409 reads both server conflict shapes: `{workId,typeName,…}` for a duplicate, `{work_id,type,doctor}` for a status change.
- **CSS modules: nothing to file.** Six modules, 2,138 lines: 0 literal colours, 0 `!important`, 0 dead classes (the three "unused" `btn*` names are a comment and a `:global(.btn)`). Physical properties are left for postcss-rtlcss to flip, as designed. The unflipped chevron glyph is FE-F7-12.
- **`WorkComponent` is not keyed by `personId`, and it doesn't need to be.** Its per-patient state (`checkedIn`, `patientPhotoError`, `expandedWorks`, search) would carry over on a direct `/patient/A/works` → `/patient/B/works` navigation. No in-app path does that: nothing inside the shell links patient to patient, and the sticky tab only ever points at the current patient. Latent only; recorded so nobody files it.
- **Auto-expand** uses the repo's render-phase keyed guard and is idempotent. Only one work per patient can be active (the unique index), so "first active work" means "the active work". The re-seed trigger is FE-F7-14's.
- **Live-data facts recorded so nobody re-queries them** (2026-09-28). `works.currency`: IQD 6,180 · USD 1,606 · 0 NULL · 0 EUR; 2 USD works above 10,000. 35 IQD works have totals under 10,000, all legacy (2018–2025, `updated_at` NULL, so not FE-F7-3's fingerprint). Five of them are still **active** and look like mislabelled USD amounts: 592 (Ortho, 1,500, 2018), 10029 (Filling, 50, paid 50), 10192 / 10193 (Aligner Lab, 570 / 690), 10768 (Ortho Aligners, 525). These were handed to the owner to check on 2026-09-28 — a data check, not a frontend finding. `estimated_duration`: 552 × 0, 21 × >0. `discount`: 7,746 NULL, 36 × 0 (FE-F7-4b), 4 × >0. `work_items`: 1,139 rows, `item_cost` all NULL, 1 implant row, 1 shade row. `lab_cases`: 0. Works by doctor: Shwan Elias 5,933 · Snowbar (Doctor, quit, no commission) 1,208 (104 active) · Rojena 347 · Clinic (Doctor, pseudo) 211 · Haliz (**Assistant**, commission) 86 · Yassen (Receptionist) 1. FK delete rules: `invoices`, `visits`, `diagnoses`, `implants`, `aligner_sets` → `works` are CASCADE, while `work_items` and `screws` are NO ACTION. `deleteWork` counts dependencies before deleting, so none of the cascades can fire from the UI.

## F6 · 2026-09-28

Scope read in full: the files listed at the top of the F6 ledger (~3.4k LOC, plus `PhoneInput`, `phoneFormatter` and the two loaders). Everything below was checked and needs no further work. **Do not re-derive it.**

- **The i18n ratchet is in sync for all three translated screens.** `ViewPatientInfo`, `EditPatientComponent` and `AddPatientForm` are in `eslint.config.js`'s `no-literal-string` list. `RTL_ROUTE_PATTERNS` matches exactly `/patient/:id/{patient-info,edit-patient,add}`, not the subtree, and `index.html`'s FOUC check carries the same three regexes. Phone and email values carry `dir="ltr"`, and `PhoneInput` pins its own. The lookup-row values staying untranslated is the documented staged state (CLAUDE.md), not a finding.
- **The works-derived patient type is displayed as the server stores it, and refreshed when it changes.** `ViewPatientInfo` shows the materialized `patient_type_name`. Edit shows it read-only and `disabled`, and `updatePatient.body` strips any `patient_type_id` a client sends. Add has no picker, so the intake selector alone seeds the first work. Every works write invalidates `qk.patient.all`, which covers both `info` and `full`. The client never re-derives the type.
- **Intake validation matches `intakeSchema` exactly.** X-ray fee > 0, consult ≥ 0, and the fee input admits digits only, so it is integer and non-negative like `moneyInt`. The x-ray type is limited to OPG/CBCT/Cephalo on both sides. The currency resets to IQD, the currency the presets are quoted in. The 409 `DUPLICATE_PATIENT_NAME` path reads `details.code`. The 422 `IntakeConfigError` surfaces the server's message. On success the button stays disabled through the redirect, so a second click cannot create a duplicate.
- **Search mechanics in `PatientManagement` are sound apart from FE-F6-3/-13.** The debounced auto-search calls the latest `executeSearch` through a ref, so a completed search can't echo a second one. Each search aborts the previous one. Reset kills the debounce and the in-flight request. A sort change refetches from offset 0. Load More pages with the current sort and offset. `hasMore`/`totalCount` come from the server. RA4's FE-F2-6 fix holds: the five dropdowns read the RQ cache, which the loader filled through `ensureQueryData` + `emptyOnHttpError`. FE-F4-4's shared matcher gets `nameStartsWith`.
- **Row actions are guarded.** While `loading`, `.tableLoadingOverlay` sets `pointer-events: none`, so a double-clicked check-in cannot fire twice. Check-in invalidates `qk.patient.all` + `qk.calendar.all` (RA1's FE-F2-1 fix holds). Both delete flows (list and Edit) guard with `deleting`, send the hold-tier `pending` outcome to `notifyApprovalsChanged()` (FE-F3-2 holds), and warn on `folderRemoved === false`. Both delete dialogs are `<Modal>` + `<ModalHeader variant="danger">` with a matching `titleId`/`ariaLabelledBy`.
- **Edit form basics.** The TDZ fix holds: `patientTypeLabel` is declared below `formData` (`[[closure-tdz-not-caught-by-tsc]]`). `formatISODate` passes a `'YYYY-MM-DD'` DOB through untouched, with no UTC round-trip. Estimated cost is digits-only with display commas and goes out as a string that the server's `toInt` parses. Translate-name is bounded and never silently blanks the names. Save/Cancel return to `state.from`, falling back to works.
- **`ViewPatientInfo` writes.** Estimated cost: `moneyInt` coerces `''` to `0`, which clears the value (shown as `-`). `currency` is `.min(1)`. The save invalidates `qk.patient.all`. Presets are filtered to the selected currency. Alert dismiss refreshes `qk.patient.alerts` and calls `notifyTasksChanged()`. `AlertModal` edits keep `surface_mode` through `showInHeader`, so editing a push task here does not demote it to a context alert. The WebCeph open state lives in `?webceph=1`, set through a functional updater that preserves the other params. Money and phone display use Western digits (`toLocaleString('en-US')`, `formatPhoneForDisplay`), and the phone display tolerates a stored `+` in `country_code`.
- **`PatientTypesReadOnly`**: read-only by design and excluded from `LOOKUP_TABLE_CONFIG` so the classifier's rows cannot be edited. It has loading, error and empty states. Nothing to report.
- **`PatientFolderPicker`**: lists folders and images only, falls back to an icon per `relPath` when a thumbnail fails, uses the file-explorer query and helpers, and has a retry. Its one cast is in FE-F6-10.
- **Server gates for everything these screens call** were checked against `route-table.txt`. `/api/patients/search`, `phones`, `type-options`, `:id`, `:id/info` and `:id/alerts` GET are open to any authenticated user. Create and `transliterate-name` are all three roles. Alert add/edit/status/snooze and quick check-in are all three roles, so the client's all-roles rendering of those is correct. The only mismatch is FE-F6-6.
- **Live-data facts recorded so nobody re-queries them**, from `patients` on 2026-09-28: `phone` is 10 digits (no leading 0) in 5,472 of ~5,490 non-empty rows; `country_code` is `964` × 6,824 or NULL × 20, with no `+`; `language` is 0 × 6,840, 1 × 3, 2 × 1; 4 `patient_name`s are untrimmed. The unique index is `ix_name_id (patient_name) INCLUDE (person_id)`, on `citext`.

## F5 · 2026-09-27

- **Client role gates match the server's, endpoint for endpoint** (checked against `route-table.txt`). Approvals list / history / approve / reject / acknowledge / approve-all / acknowledge-all are `authorize(admin)` and only `ApprovalsBell` (admin-only render) calls them; `/api/approvals/mine` is any-authenticated and backs the front-desk `MyApprovalsBadge`; `/api/tasks*` + `/api/alerts/*` are `CLINICAL_ROLES` = all three roles, as `TasksBell`'s all-roles render assumes; `/api/portal-activity` reads are any-authenticated and its two PATCHes all three roles. Statistics: `/api/statistics` is `FINANCE_ROLES`; yearly / multi-year / commissions / revenue-breakdown / doctor-payments are `ADMIN_ROLES`, which is exactly `isAdminOnlyView()` (Monthly → yearly endpoint, Yearly → multi-year). No F5 request can 403 on a normal path: every query is `enabled` on `effectiveViewMode`, and a clinical user gets the lock card with the queries disabled.
- **Polling is three endpoints per admin/front-desk session, two for clinical — not four on one timer.** Tasks, portal activity and approvals (or `mine`) each run their own `refetchInterval: 5 min`; RQ's `refetchIntervalInBackground` defaults to false, so all of them **pause in a hidden tab**. The only waste is the unconditional visibility refetch (FE-F5-13).
- **Badge counts agree with the lists they open.** `TasksBell`'s badge and list are the same array, and the read-time visibility rules (active · not snoozed past `CURRENT_DATE` · not expired · push, or context with `escalate_at <= CURRENT_DATE`) are applied once, server-side, in `getHeaderTasks`. Portal activity: badge = day-groups with an unread row among the 200-row feed, list = all those groups. Approvals: badge = holds + notices, both listed (the live DB's 57 pending notices are why the admin badge reads high — by design, they are FYIs). `MyApprovalsBadge`: badge = pending holds only, as its docblock says.
- **FE-F3-2's fix holds on every hold-tier path.** Each `outcome === 'pending'` branch now calls `notifyApprovalsChanged()`: `NewWorkComponent:317`, `WorkComponent:324,754`, `EditPatientComponent:316`, `PatientManagement:504`, `useExpenses:230,255`.
- **The portal-activity `is_read` NULL mismatch is unreachable.** The client counts `!r.is_read` (NULL = unread) while `markActivityRead` updates only `is_read = false`, so a NULL row could never be cleared — but the portal has no INSERT grant on `is_read` (DB default `false`, `phase3-announcements.sql`), and the live table holds 0 NULLs.
- **Currency separation holds on all three statistics tabs.** Commissions and Breakdown never add IQD to USD; the USD-equivalent is null-propagating (no house rate — `totals.usdEq` stays `null` unless a row converted), the ranking chart is omitted rather than drawn empty when there is no rate, and the Daily view's converted figures render `—` via `formatConverted`. The breakdown rate is the server-echoed latest real rate; the monthly view's is per-day on the server. `DoctorPaymentsModal`'s headline totals come from the clicked aggregate row, never from summing a possibly-truncated list (and it says so).
- **Charts are theme-correct except the title (FE-F5-15).** `chartSetup.ts` registers an explicit subset (not `chart.js/auto`); both chart effects list `resolvedTheme` in their deps and rebuild; the `${hex}cc` alpha trick is safe because `--success-green` and `--primary-color` resolve to 6-digit hex in both themes (`#22c55e`/`#40c057`, `#007bff`/`#4dabf7`); each effect destroys its instance on cleanup.
- **Popover geometry is fine down to 360 px.** All four set `max-width: calc(100vw - 24px)` and `placePopover` clamps `left` to ≥ 8, so nothing overflows horizontally.
- **`<Modal>` census, case-insensitive this time: still 0 hand-rolled overlays.** `grep -rni "modaloverlay\|modal-overlay"` finds one hit, `DailyInvoicesModal`'s `overlayClassName={styles.statisticsModalOverlay}` — a prop on `<Modal>`, sanctioned. (F4's case-sensitive grep would not have matched `…ModalOverlay`; this confirms it missed nothing.)
- **`AlertModal`** — create/edit bodies match `alertBody` exactly (numbers `parseInt(…, 10)`, `''` clears the two dates, `assignedTo` omitted so context alerts stay unassigned); render-phase init keyed on open + id; `notifyTasksChanged()` + `onSave` refresh both surfaces. Its missing `unsavedGuard` is RA5's documented deferral, not re-filed; its English strings are FE-F5-14.
- **`TaskFormModal`** — render-phase populate/reset keyed on open + `alert_id`; the patient typeahead debounces 250 ms and aborts the previous request; create posts exactly `createTask.body`; `unsavedGuard` is wired (RA5).
- **`services/{tasks,approvals,portal-activity}.ts`** — re-confirmed F1's verdict: thin mutation wrappers through the funnel (CSRF via `postJSON`/`putJSON`/`patchJSON`/`deleteJSON`). Mutation responses carry no `{ schema }`, correctly (the rule is for reads).
- **`DoctorCommissionsView`** — per-currency totals, quit doctors included for their periods, drill-down is a real `<button>` (keyboard-reachable). `commission_percentage` is the doctor's *current* rate applied to past periods — a server-side design choice (`[[doctor-commission-feature]]`), not a client defect.
- **Dashboard** — the saved order merges forward (unknown keys dropped, new cards appended), the role filter is applied at render so a hidden card keeps its slot, the RTL arrow mapping is symmetric, and the card-footer arrow is flipped by hand in CSS because postcss-rtlcss cannot reach a Font Awesome glyph. On the i18n ratchet with nothing to report.
- **CSS modules** — the literal fallbacks in `MyApprovalsBadge`/`ApprovalsBell` (`var(--warning-100, #fef9c3)` etc.) are all behind **defined** tokens with dark overrides, i.e. dead fallbacks for F24's strip, not dark-theme holes. `StatisticsComponent.module.css`'s ~23 classes with no reader in its own component are all used by `DailyInvoicesModal`, which imports the same sheet. `!important`: StatisticsComponent's is `@media print`; Dashboard's two are FE-F5-17.

## F4 · 2026-09-17

Scope read in full: the 17 shell/primitive components listed in the plan (2,975 LOC). Everything below was checked and needs no further work — **do not re-derive it.**

- **The hand-rolled-overlay census is still 0.** `grep -rln "modalOverlay\|modal-overlay" public/js --include='*.tsx'` → **0 files**, matching the 2026-09-15 baseline the plan asked this session to confirm. Every overlay in the staff app goes through `<Modal>`; 67 `<Modal>` render sites across 59 files. Any later session that finds one files it against the owning feature.
- **The header at 412 px is clean** (runtime-measured, Galaxy-S23-Ultra portrait, `/dashboard`): `document.documentElement.scrollWidth === 412` with **zero** overflow offenders, header height 92 px, and all five right-region controls at full size — three 42×42 bells, the 46×24 theme pill, the 60×34 user pill — with the three nav tabs (94/109/71 px) wrapped onto their own row. `universal-header.css` carries four breakpoints (1024 / 768 / 640 / 480) and the 768 one pins `max-width:100vw; overflow-x:hidden` on the container.
- **Bell role-gating is correct and reads from the shared enum.** `UniversalHeader:331-332` renders `<ApprovalsBell/>` only for `ROLES.ADMIN` and `<MyApprovalsBadge/>` only for `ROLES.FRONT_DESK`, imported from `@shared/auth/roles` (not a string literal). `TasksBell` and `PortalActivityBell` are deliberately all-roles. Whether the *endpoints* behind them enforce the same split is **F5**'s, not a hole here.
- **The body-scroll lock is genuinely nest-safe.** `openCount` is module-scoped and refcounted, the scrollbar-width compensation only applies at count 1, and the saved `overflow`/`paddingRight` are restored only at 0 — so the live Expense→Lookup nesting (and any future stack) cannot leak `overflow:hidden` onto `<body>`. `aria-hidden` is set on `#app-root` **and** `#single-spa-application`; both exist in `public/index.html` (216/270) and `#modal-root` (225) is their **sibling**, so the dialog is never inside the hidden subtree. (The `single-spa-*` id is dead branding from a retired framework — cosmetic, and it belongs to **F23**'s HTML-entry-point pass, not here.)
- **Focus handling in `Modal` is right apart from the naming bug (FE-F4-2).** Initial focus prefers `initialFocusRef`, falls back to the first focusable, then to the dialog itself with `preventScroll`; restore is guarded by `document.contains(prev)` so focus can't be thrown at `<body>` when the trigger unmounted; the Tab trap wraps both directions and handles the zero-focusable case. `initialFocusRef` is passed by exactly two consumers (`PaymentFormDrawer:146`, `files/FileExplorer:548`) and **both pass a stable `useRef`**, so the `[isOpen, initialFocusRef]` deps can't churn.
- **The backdrop-dismiss gesture is implemented correctly** (separate from FE-F4-6, which is about the *default*): `onMouseDown` records whether the press started on the backdrop and `onClick` requires both ends to be there, so releasing a drag over the backdrop does not dismiss. The drag itself is transform-on-a-ref (no re-render), clamped to keep 60 px on screen on both axes, re-centred on every open, auto-disabled for `/drawer/i` overlays and `≤768 px`, and cleans up `user-select` + pointer capture on unmount.
- **`ContentRenderer`'s lazy map cannot drift**, re-confirmed: 19 `lazyPage()` registrations cover 20 page strings (`'work'` and `'diagnosis'` share the `Diagnosis` chunk by construction), `preloaders` is populated *by* `lazyPage`, and `preloadPatientPage` swallows rejections so a speculative warm-up never files a spurious `[client-error]`. The only switch cases with no preloader are the three dead stubs (**FE-F2-12**). Unknown `page` renders a "Page Not Found" card rather than throwing.
- **A lazy patient-tab chunk failure lands in a class boundary**, as it must: both `PatientShell` routes (`routes.config.tsx:397`, `:411`) are wrapped in `<RouteErrorBoundary>`, which F3 established is class-backed. (That the boundary is *wider* than the Suspense is FE-F4-17 — a scoping question, not a coverage hole.)
- **`ThemeToggle`, `NavigationProgress`, `ModalHeader`, `PhoneInput` — nothing to report.** `ThemeToggle` is a real `<button role="switch" aria-checked>` driven by the *resolved* theme (so it shows the right side under `auto`); `NavigationProgress` is stateless and unmounts itself; `ModalHeader` wires `titleId`/`aria-label`/`data-modal-drag-handle` correctly and is the only place the close button's markup lives; `PhoneInput` pins `dir="ltr"` so digit groups and the caret stay LTR inside an Arabic form, and `PhoneDisplay` does the same for display + `tel:` links.
- **`UserMenu` logout is correct.** It is one of CLAUDE.md's sanctioned `window.location` exceptions, and it calls `queryClient.clear()` **before** the reload, so the next user in that tab cannot read the previous user's cached patient data. Popover close-on-outside-click/Escape/resize all unregister on cleanup.
- **The protocol-handler contract still holds.** `explorer:` (Navigation `:220`) and `csimaging:`/`dolphin:` are the sanctioned full-navigation exceptions; `PatientsFolder` is still UNC in the DB (`\\CLINIC\Clinic1\`, trailing separator present) and `VideosPath` still local (`C:\clinic1\ovideos\`), matching CLAUDE.md's client-facing-vs-server-local split. The staleness of the *client cache* is FE-F4-3; the values themselves are right.
- **Why there are two patient searches — answered** (see FE-F4-13): `PatientSearchCombobox` is the successor and owns `PatientManagement`'s two fields; `PatientQuickSearch` survives for `TransferWorkModal` alone. Both use the same matching rules, and the combobox's keyboard contract (ArrowUp/Down + Enter to jump, plain Enter to submit, Escape to dismiss with `stopPropagation` so a host modal stays open) is implemented correctly, with `aria-activedescendant` and `onMouseDown` preventDefault so a click beats the blur.
- **`UniversalHeader`'s sticky patient tab is sound.** The render-phase keyed guard (`patientCode !== stickyCode`) is the repo idiom and is idempotent; the `/patient/(\d+)` anchor correctly excludes `/aligner/patient/:workId`; `sessionStorage` (not `localStorage`) keeps it per-tab; and the tab reads `patientInfoQuery` on the **same** key `PatientShell` uses, so it resolves from cache and refreshes on any patient invalidation.
- **`PatientShell`'s scroll memory is correct**: keyed by `pathname + search` (so a timepoint slot gets its own slot), saved rAF-throttled with cleanup, restored across up to 8 frames for late-growing photo grids.
- **`SettingsTabNavigation`'s mobile auto-scroll is horizontal-only** (`container.scrollBy`), so it cannot move the page; the active-button ref is conditional and re-attaches before the effect reads it.

## F3 · 2026-09-16

Read closely, nothing found (or the only findings are the ones filed above):

- **Error-boundary coverage is complete, and the class/function split is right.** All 41 `path:` entries in `routes.config.tsx` were walked: every route that renders a component is wrapped in `<RouteErrorBoundary routeName="…">`; the only unwrapped entries are the eleven `<Navigate>` redirects and `/chair-display`, which carries its own `errorElement: <RouteError />`. `RouteErrorBoundary` → `ErrorBoundary`'s **default export** (`ErrorBoundaryWithNavigate`, which supplies `resetKey={location.pathname}`) → the **class** `ErrorBoundary`, so a `React.lazy` chunk rejection — which React re-throws during render and function boundaries cannot see — lands in a class as it must. `GlobalErrorBoundary` correctly imports the **named** class instead, because it wraps `<RouterProvider>` in `App.tsx` where `useNavigate`/`useLocation` would throw. This answers the question F2 left open.
- **`ErrorBoundary.componentDidCatch` is correct** — `selfHealChunkError(…, 'render-boundary')` runs first and returns early while a reload is in flight (so a stale tab heals silently), and only a chunk error that survived its reload is reported; everything else goes to `reportClientError` with `componentStack`. `componentDidUpdate` clears a caught error when `resetKey` changes, so navigating away from a crashed screen is not a dead end. `RouteError` runs the same self-heal from an effect (out of render) and reports only `reload-exhausted`.
- **`ThemeContext`, `LanguageContext`, `FontContext` — clean, and structurally identical on purpose.** Lazy `useState` initializers read the same stored value the `index.html` FOUC script already applied (so the mount reconcile is a no-op on the happy path), `value` is memoized, every `storage`/`matchMedia` listener is removed in its cleanup, and `setLanguage` writes storage → DOM → i18next → state in that order so there is no frame of stale direction. `applyLanguageAttributes(lang, path?)` resolves `dir` through `resolveDirection(lang, currentPath)`, i.e. **route-scoped**, and the cross-tab `storage` handler calls `i18n.changeLanguage` as well as the attribute apply — the two halves that would otherwise flip layout without flipping strings.
- **`ToastContext`'s memo is deliberate and correct** (it carries the comment explaining the refetch-storm it prevents), the auto-dismiss timer is cleared on unmount, manual close and auto-dismiss are idempotent against each other, and `window.toast` is installed and `delete`d symmetrically. The only gap is the missing live region (FE-F3-4).
- **`ConfirmContext` cannot leak a promise** — replacing a pending confirm resolves the old one `false` first; the only note is where that resolve happens (FE-F3-10).
- **The SSE hooks' subscribe/unsubscribe symmetry is total.** `useAppointmentsSync` (5 transport listeners + `appointments_updated` + `window online`), `useWhatsAppSync` (10 listeners) and `useWhatsAppAuth` (6) each `off()` exactly what they `on()` and call `sseAppointments.release()` / `sseWhatsapp.release()` in the same cleanup, and every `setTimeout`/`setInterval` in the three files is cleared except the one noted in FE-F3-5. Under StrictMode's mount→cleanup→mount, the refcount returns to 0, `disconnect()` nulls the handle, and the second mount's `connect()` re-opens — the pending first `ensureConnected()` promise resolves off the emitter's `connected` event rather than a specific `EventSource`, so it cannot hang.
- **`useAppointmentsSync`'s recovery pipeline is sound** — one debounce window shared by the reconnect, `online` and 5-minute-safety-net triggers; the retry recursion routed through `runRecoveryRef` to dodge the TDZ/immutability trap; `cancelledRef` set in the effect body and cleared in cleanup so a late retry after unmount is a no-op; `markStale()` on failure so the "Live | Stale" indicator reflects the gap instead of lying. The broadcast handler double-checks `receivedDate === expectedDate` even though the server no longer filters. Only the midnight derivation (FE-F3-9) is open.
- **The QR-viewer gate survives the app-wide SSE subscription.** `GlobalStateProvider` holds a permanent refcount on the WhatsApp stream for the whole session, and `services/messaging/sse-whatsapp.ts` registers a QR viewer for every stream — so `activeQRViewers > 0` is true whenever any staff tab is open, which would defeat the documented on-demand-init gate. It does not, because `whatsapp.ts:2313` blocks the on-demand path outright under `WHATSAPP_AUTO_INIT=false` and says why in a comment naming this exact subscription. Register/unregister is latched on both sides (`cleanedUp`, `closedEarly`) so the count cannot drift. Nothing to file — recorded so **F16** does not re-derive it.
- **`GlobalStateContext`'s WhatsApp reconcile is the right design.** `whatsapp_client_ready` and `whatsapp_qr_updated` are one-shot events that never replay to a stream that opens later; reconciling from `/api/wa/initial-state` on every `connected` (plus once immediately when the transport was already fresh) closes the whole missed-event class for every consumer at once. The QR handler reads the frame's own `clientReady` rather than inferring it from a blanked `qr`, which is what keeps non-finance tabs from believing WhatsApp is unlinked. Both behaviours are commented with the bug they fixed; leave them alone.
- **`useAppointments`' mutation path** — POST → `invalidateQueries` → render with no optimistic state, `loadAppointments` returning the post-invalidation query status so the sync hook can retry, and silent recovery on the server's 400 conflict. The loader seed is gated on `initialData.loadedDate === date && !initialData.error` and carries `initialDataUpdatedAt`, so a stale loader payload cannot masquerade as fresh, and a date change correctly drops to a normal fetch. Only the casts (FE-F3-8) and the duplicated predicate (FE-F3-18) are filed.
- **`useLabCases` is the reference implementation** — seven mutations, all through `useApiMutation`, all invalidating both `qk.labCases.all()` **and** `qk.work.detailsList(workId)` via one shared helper, with `workId` passed in the variables precisely because the raw row does not carry it. This is the shape FE-F2-13 wants everywhere.
- **`useImportFolder`** — `loadSaved` is written as a `.then`-chain (not `async`) specifically so no `setState` runs synchronously in the mount effect; `requestPermission` is never called outside a user gesture; `loading` starts from `supported` so the unsupported case needs no effect. Its only defect is inherited from FE-F1-5 (the IndexedDB handles it opens are never closed).
- **`useLocalizedName`, `useAppointmentDoctors`, `useLabelModal`, `useSetDrawer`, `useBatchDrawer`, `useMessageCount`** — clean. The four drawer/modal hooks are single-caller extractions out of `PatientSets`, which is deliberate decomposition of a 2,340-line screen, **not** a one-caller-should-be-inlined finding. `useAppointmentDoctors` memoizes `buildDoctorColors` on the query data so the legend and the card tints cannot disagree.
- **No hook in `hooks/` has zero importers** (counted: `useStand` 22, `useExpenses` 7, `useLocalizedName` 5, `useWhatsAppAuth` 4, `useDateManager`/`useLabCases`/`useLookupManager` 3, `useImportFolder`/`useAppointmentDoctors` 2, the rest 1).
- **`useMessageStatus`'s SSE→invalidate loop** — a 400 ms debounce coalesces the per-message status burst during a live send, `query.isLoading` (not `isFetching`) drives the placeholder so the table never blanks mid-send, and the debounce timer is cleared on every dep change. `EMPTY_MESSAGES` is a module constant, so the empty case keeps a stable identity.
- **`PrintQueueContext`'s persistence survives navigation as designed** — `sessionStorage` seeded through a lazy initializer (no mount effect), written on every queue change, and the provider sits in `RootLayout` above `RootLayoutInner`, so neither the queue nor the open print modal unmounts on a route change. `buildDefaultLabels` goes through the shared `buildLabelsFromRanges`, so a template batch's sequence 0 is preserved rather than `|| 0`-ed away.
- **The browser-locale date census (FE-F3-3), recorded so no session re-derives it** — 53 sites in 36 files: `SyncStatusPanel` (4) · `MessageStatusTable`, `IntegrationsSettings`, `AppointmentCalendar` (3 each) · `AppointmentTab`, `PatientSets`, `WorkComponent`, `ViewPatientInfo`, `NewWorkComponent`, `HolidayEditor`, `DailyInvoicesModal`, `AdminUserManagement` (2 each) · `TasksHistory`, `ChairDisplay`, `VisitsTab`, `PhotosTab`, `PaymentsTab`, `Announcements`, `useMessageStatus`, `TemplateCard`, `StockMovementsModal`, `ItemTable`, `LabCaseModal`, `fileHelpers`, `SelectionPanel`, `XraysComponent`, `WorkDetailItem`, `WebCephModal`, `ProtocolHandlersSettings`, `PortalAccessCard`, `MonthlyCalendarGrid`, `HolidayQuickModal`, `CalendarDayContextMenu`, `AnalogClock`, `ExpenseTable`, `DeleteConfirmModal` (1 each).
- **The weekend definition, settled once and now authoritative** (FE-F3-1, confirmed by the owner 2026-09-17): the clinic week is **Sat–Thu, FRIDAY the only day off**. Implemented in `CalendarViewService.ts:441` (`if (d.getDay() === 5) continue`), `utils/calendarDate.ts#addWorkingDays`/`getWeekStartSaturday`, `SimplifiedCalendarPicker`, `useDateManager.getDefaultDate()` (Thursday → +2 to reach Saturday) and `utils/arabic-day.ts` (index 5 → `''`). `MonthlyCalendarGrid.isWeekend` was the single dissenter and is gone. **CLAUDE.md's "Fri+Sat weekends" prose was wrong and has been corrected**, including the ~2M → ~2.4M ceiling arithmetic derived from it; the week shape is now its own top-level note there. No later session needs to re-derive this.

No factory hand-writes a response `interface` (all are `z.infer<typeof …>`); the one place an interface is declared (`SyncSinkStatus`/`SyncDriftReport`/`SyncSinkStatusResponse`, `:1540-1597`) is the deliberately contract-less sync-status pair and is documented as such.

''`) pairs it with an `enabled` that is false in exactly that case, so the placeholder key is never fetched into.

The `retry` value is the one finding (FE-F2-3).

- **`query/loaderQuery.ts` error mapping** — AbortError rethrows for React Router, an already-mapped `Response` propagates untouched (so a nested loader's 401 isn't double-handled), any numeric status becomes `Response(status)` for the `errorElement`, everything else becomes 500. Correct, except that the 401 arm is unreachable (FE-F2-2).
- **`router/loaders.ts` — the other seven loaders.** `patientShellLoader` parallelises patient/work/timepoints with `Promise.all` and gates the timepoints leg on the page actually needing it; `alignerPatientWorkLoader` validates `workId` with a radix-10 `parseInt` and 404s a work with no `person_id` before the second read; `templateDesignerLoader` correctly no-ops into create mode; `labTrackingLoader`'s `{}` prefetch **does** hit the component's key (RQ's `hashKey` drops `undefined`-valued properties, so `LabTracking`'s `{status:undefined,…}` filters hash identically to `{}`); `dailyAppointmentsLoader` is a deliberate raw fetch that is bridged into RQ by `useAppointments`'s `initialData` + `initialDataUpdatedAt` — **not** a double fetch.

All 41 `path:` entries reviewed: each nested group ends in a `path:'*'` → `<Navigate>` so there is no dead-end route, and `/chair-display` sits outside `RootLayout` with no context consumers at all (grep: `ChairDisplay.tsx` uses none of `useToast`/`useTheme`/`useLanguage`/`useConfirm`/`useGlobalState`/`usePrintQueue`), so the kiosk's provider-free mount is safe.

(Which boundary catches what, and whether `RouteErrorBoundary` is class-backed, is **F3**'s to settle.)

- **`layouts/RootLayout.tsx`** — provider nesting is deliberate and documented (Theme outermost so `#modal-root` inherits the vars); the header's `dir` is pinned to the *language* while `<html dir>` follows the *route*, which is the documented RTL split; the `applyDirectionForPath` navigation watcher covers the axis `LanguageContext` doesn't. `ScrollRestoration`'s `getKey` includes `location.search`, so the patient-management deep-link keeps its own scroll slot.
- **`layouts/AlignerLayout.tsx`, `config/workTypeConfig.ts`, `config/expenseCategories.ts`, `constants/sse-liveness.ts`, `query/keys.test.ts`, `query/useApiMutation.test.ts`** — clean. (`workTypeConfig` correctly re-exports the ids from the cross-boundary `shared/treatment-taxonomy` rather than re-declaring them.)

The sibling relationship between `settings.options` and `settings.option(name)` is harmless here — no reader crosses it.
