Checked against the server: `middleware/csrf.ts:125` sends `{ success:false, error:'…', code:'EBADCSRFTOKEN' }`, which is exactly what the client matches on. Concurrent 403s cost an extra token fetch but stay correct.
- **`httpErrorMessage`** — `utils/error-response.ts` types `error` as `string` on every path, so the `data?.error || data?.message || err.message || fallback` chain can never print `[object Object]`.
- **Envelope unwrap** — deliberately conservative (`success === true` AND a `data` key); bare arrays and top-level `{success, user}` shapes pass through. Matches its tests.
- **The M8 retry policy** — mutations are never retried (`!mutation` in the retriable check), `AbortError` never retries, `TimeoutError`/`TypeError`/502/503/504 do. `withTimeout` cleans up both the timer and the caller-signal listener, and the CSRF retry path re-arms its own timeout.
- **`core/error-reporter.ts`** — throttle (30 s per signature), dedupe, the 50/page cap, and the never-throws guarantee all hold; `isChunkFetchMessage` skips the self-healing chunk noise; a contract-drift `HttpError` (which carries `validation` and **no** `status`) correctly passes the `unhandledrejection` filter and gets reported.
- **`core/chunk-reload.ts`** — the `vite:preloadError` contract is right: `preventDefault()` happens **only** when a reload is actually starting, so the `undefined → reading 'default'` manufactured error from `[[admin-403-root-router-incident]]` can't come back.
- **`core/language.ts` / `core/theme.ts` / `core/font.ts`** — registry + raw-string storage round-trip agrees with the inline FOUC scripts; `applyDirectionForPath` keeps route-scoped RTL honest. (One latent list drift filed in FE-F1-11.)
- **`utils/formatters.ts`** — the Arabic weekday hand-map is index-correct for `getDay()`, digits stay Western in both languages (`numberLocale: 'en-US'`), and `language` is an explicit parameter everywhere, never module state — the React Compiler staleness trap is avoided as documented.
- **`services/sse-appointments.ts`** — refcount lifecycle is symmetric; `connect()` always closes the previous handle; the `visibilitychange`/`pageshow` handlers attach once and are guarded by `refcount > 0`; `disconnect()` only fires at refcount 0.
- **`services/photo-render-watch.ts`** — one `ensureConnected`/`release` pair per job, `settle()` is idempotent, and the per-job timeout covers a server restart mid-render.
- **`services/tasks.ts` / `approvals.ts` / `portal-activity.ts`** — thin, contract-typed mutation wrappers; correct funnel use (CSRF via `postJSON`/`putJSON`/`patchJSON`).
- **`utils/phoneFormatter.ts`, `utils/chartTheme.ts`, `utils/chartSetup.ts` (explicit Chart.js registration, not `/auto`), `utils/aligner-labels.ts`, `constants/sse-liveness.ts`** — clean.
- **`core/http.test.ts` (242 ln) / `core/chunk-reload.test.ts`** — 21 cases covering unwrap, schema fail-loud, CSRF attach/cache/retry/give-up, timeout, retry policy and `httpErrorMessage`. The gaps are the two paths filed above: a non-JSON body **with** a schema, and the CSRF fetch's own timeout.

Six are one `MouseEvent` cast repeated in `CalendarGrid`/`CalendarDayContextMenu` (F10 may still want to remove the repetition); the rest are generic-passthrough casts in `core/`.

# Remediation log

Per remediation session: what was fixed, the commit, whether `npm run gate` passed, and anything deliberately **not** fixed (with the reason — a deliberate non-fix must end up annotated in the code too, the way `docs/services-audit.md` does it).

## RF1 · 2026-10-05 — F21, Settings I (done)

F21 was audited and fixed in one session (the owner's audit→fix-per-phase rule). All 15 rows are closed, none partial: the 🔴 on the spot, the rest after three owner decisions. **`npm run gate` green** (394 tests; D1 0 / **D2 6**, ratcheted from 7 by modelling the lookup table-config feed; build OK). **Route table: 3 lines** — `GET /api/options [authorize(admin)]`, `GET /api/options/:optionName [validate(params)]`, `+PUT /api/calendar/slot-settings [authorize(admin|front_desk) -> validate(body)]`. No migration. **Uncommitted.** Runtime: 111 checks on the throwaway install (37 + 22 for the 🔴, 21 API + 31 UI after the rest), all passing — two UI probe lines failed on the probe, not the app (the browser logs the expected 409 as a console error; the "no Shwan on the page" check matched `index.html`'s static `<title>`, filed for F23).

### Owner decisions taken during RF1 (2026-10-05)

| ID | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F21-2 | Which work types may be deleted? | **None that works use, and not the four the app creates itself (OPG, CBCT, Cephalo, Consult).** Any other unused type can be deleted; renaming stays allowed everywhere. |
| FE-F21-3 | Who runs Calendar Times? | **Admin + front desk.** Front desk can now save the early/late categories too; clinical doesn't see the tab. |
| FE-F21-9 | What does System Options show? | **The known settings, labelled and described, plus a collapsed read-only list of every other stored row.** Rows other screens own stay out of it; nothing is deleted from the table. |

### What changed, per finding

- **FE-F21-1 (🔴)** — `services/settings/option-access.ts` (pure, unit-tested): `isSecretOption` (the three credential rows + any name that reads like a credential: password/secret/token/api key/session), `canReadOption(name, isAdmin)`, `withoutSecretOptions`. All case-insensitive (`option_name` is citext). `GET /api/options` is admin-only and drops credentials; `GET /api/options/:name` gives non-admins only the five rows the working screens read (the three calendar options, `DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY`, `AlignerSetsFolder`) and nobody a credential; the single and bulk PUTs refuse a credential name (403, nothing written). General Settings doesn't fetch the list for non-admins and hides Branding + System Options from them (`roleCaps().manageSettings`, new). The Telegram, SMTP and Gemini screens read their own rows server-side and are unaffected.
- **FE-F21-2** — `LOOKUP_TABLE_CONFIG` entries can carry `protectedRows` (ids the code names, with the reason) and `softReferences` (columns that store the id with no FK). `deleteLookupItem` runs in a transaction: lock the row (404 if missing — it used to answer "deleted"), refuse a protected id, count each soft reference, then delete; the 409 says why ("Cannot delete: 2,442 works use this item."). Work types: `works.type_of_work` + the intake types 14/18/22/23; implant manufacturers: `work_items.implant_manufacturer_id`. The table-config feed now carries `protectedIds`, and the editor disables Delete on those rows with a tooltip.
- **FE-F21-3** — Settings tabs declare the capability they need (`requires: keyof RoleCapabilities`) instead of `adminOnly`/`financeOnly`: Database, Database Backup, Email, Employees, Integrations → `manageSettings`; Users → `manageUsers`; Exchange Rates → `writeFinance`; Lookups and Calendar Times → `manageLookups`. A role sent to a tab it can't have lands on General. Cost presets are read-only for non-admins (no form, no action column). `useLookupManager` does nothing for a role without `manageLookups` (the right-click stays the browser's own), and `LookupManagerModal` shows the error instead of a permanent spinner when the config read fails or names no such table.
- **FE-F21-4** — The Settings shell keeps one flag, "the active tab has unsaved edits", reported through a callback bound to that tab's id (the ref + effect race is gone), and puts it through `useUnsavedRouteGuard`: switching tabs, a header link or the browser's Back asks; a reload gets the browser prompt. General reports its branding and chair-ID edits too, and *Refresh Settings* asks before discarding.
- **FE-F21-5** — `PUT /api/calendar/slot-settings` (contract `updateSlotSettings`): the early list, the late list and the toggle in one `INSERT … ON CONFLICT` (`options-queries#upsertOptions`), sorted and de-duplicated; either list may be empty; a time in both lists or a non-`HH:MM` value is a 400. Calendar Times saves through it and also refreshes `qk.calendar.all()`. A missing or empty option now seeds an empty list (what the calendar itself does) instead of four stand-in times. Add/delete slot errors show the server's message.
- **FE-F21-6** — `DELETE /api/employees/:id` counts the employee's works, appointments and expenses first and answers 409: "Sara Ahmed can't be deleted: they have 5 works, 43 appointments. To take them off every list, edit them and untick "Currently employed"." A missing employee is 404; an FK violation from a race is a 409, not a 500.
- **FE-F21-7** — `HolidayEditor` is now a thin wrapper over `LookupEditor` with a `beforeSave` hook: the day's appointments are checked for a new holiday **and** for an edit that moves the date; a failed check asks "Save the holiday anyway?" instead of saving silently. The warning lists who is booked (the contract now models `patient_name`/`app_detail`).
- **FE-F21-8** — The cost-preset form has no currency of its own: a preset is saved in the tab being viewed, editing one switches to its tab, and leaving that tab ends the edit.
- **FE-F21-9** — `config/systemOptions.ts`: six known settings (Patients folder, Aligner sets folder, Educational videos folder, Archform database file, Appointments per time slot, Default currency for new works), each with a label and a description; rows other screens own (`CLINIC_*`, `EMAIL_*`, `CALENDAR_*`, `gemini_*`, `whatsapp_*`, `AlignerLabelNextPosition`, `DEMO_SEED_MANIFEST`) are not shown; anything else is listed under a collapsed, read-only "Other stored values (N)".
- **FE-F21-10** — One write at a time on *Add/Update Employee*, *Add Preset* / preset delete and the lookup/holiday delete. The employee modal has `unsavedGuard={{ watchInput: true }}` with the children-as-function `dismiss`, and checks both tabs' required fields itself, switching to the tab that's missing one.
- **FE-F21-11** — An employee write refreshes `qk.lookups.employeesAll()` (new prefix), `doctors()`, `workDoctors()`, `operators()` and `qk.reports.commissionsAll()` (new prefix). Saving `PatientsFolder` refreshes `qk.lookups.patientsFolder()`. `query/lookupFeeds.ts` maps each lookup table to the feeds that show it (work types, keywords, shades, labs, appointment types, tags, referrals, addresses, alert types, implant manufacturers, document types, the calendar for holidays and times, expense categories and subcategories — `qk.expenses.subcategoriesAll()` is new); every lookup-editor write refreshes them.
- **FE-F21-12** — The `Clinic` pseudo-doctor can't be renamed, marked as quit or deleted (server 409; the form makes the name read-only with an explanation, disables *Currently employed* and offers no Delete). Document type 1 (`RECEIPT_DOCUMENT_TYPE_ID`, now a constant in `template-files.ts` that `receipt-service` uses) and expense categories 5/7 (now in `shared/expense-categories.ts`, re-exported by the client config) are protected rows.
- **FE-F21-13** — The branding placeholders are neutral ("The clinic's name", "The name patients see in reminders", "اسم العيادة في الرسائل"). General answers saves, refreshes and chair-ID changes with toasts; its info dialog and its CSS are gone.
- **FE-F21-14** — The add/edit form renders through `<Modal>` + `<ModalHeader>` (focus moves in and returns, Tab is trapped, the close button is named, Escape closes only the top layer, and typed input is guarded). Both delete popovers are gone: deletes ask through `useConfirm`. The popover CSS (backdrop, anchor positioning, delete popover) was removed from `lookup-editor.css`; the form styles moved to `.lookup-editor-dialog`.
- **FE-F21-15** — `lookupAdmin.tables.response` is modelled (`LookupColumn`, `LookupTableInfo`), so the four hand-copied interfaces, the `as TableConfig[]` casts and all four `any` are gone; `LookupEditorModal` exports `LookupItem`/`LookupFormData`. `DeleteConfirmPopover`/`calculatePosition` (both copies), `addButtonRef`, `OptionResponse` + its casts, the `CostPreset` interface (now the contract row) and `getPositionName` are gone; the two dead `.formGroup select` rules went with the currency select.

### Runtime verification: 111 checks on a throwaway install

As in RE3, on the copied throwaway PG (`:55432`), served on `:3201`/`:5373` this time (another session's dev server held `:3101`). Logins `Admin`, `desk1` (front desk), `doc1` (clinical).
- **The 🔴 (59):** 37 API checks with planted fake credentials — the admin list carries no credential and keeps `EMAIL_SMTP_USER`/`PatientsFolder`; both roles get 403 on the list and on every credential by name in any case, 403 on rows the screens don't need, and 200 on the five allowed rows; admin gets 403 on every credential by name; a PUT or a bulk save naming a credential is refused and writes nothing. 22 UI checks — for `desk1`/`doc1` the list is never requested, Branding and System Options are hidden, the per-device settings show, no error dialog, no secret anywhere in the page; for `Admin` all of it shows.
- **The rest, API (21):** an in-use work type → 409 "1 works use" (wording since made singular) and the row stays; Consult → 409; an unused, unprotected type deletes; a missing id → 404; the config feed carries `protectedIds [14,18,22,23]`; document type 1 and expense category 5 → 409; renaming, quitting and deleting `Clinic` → 409 and the row is unchanged; a doctor with history → 409 naming 5 works and 43 appointments; an employee without history deletes; a missing one → 404; `desk1` saves an empty early list (stored `''`, toggle `true`, late list sorted and de-duplicated); a time in both lists and `25:00` → 400; `doc1` → 403.
- **The rest, UI (31):** the tab strips for `desk1` and `doc1` are exactly the allowed tabs, `/settings/database` lands on General with no error dialog, `desk1`'s cost presets are read-only; System Options shows labels and descriptions, hides runtime rows and lists a planted legacy row collapsed; a tab switch and a header click with an unsaved edit both ask, *Keep editing* keeps it; *Save* toasts "Settings saved", and a clean tab then switches freely; Calendar Times saves an all-Main layout in one request; a double-clicked *Add Preset* on the USD tab sends one `"currency":"USD"`; Consult's Delete is disabled; deleting an in-use type toasts the reason; the add dialog takes focus and Escape with typed input asks; moving the demo holiday onto a booked day warns and *Cancel* leaves it unsaved; a missing commission rate on the hidden tab takes the user there without a request; a double-clicked *Add Employee* creates one; the `Clinic` row has no Delete; no page errors.

- **F22** — the Email settings mask `'********'` round trip (see the F21 ledger).
- `alerts.assigned_to` is `ON DELETE SET NULL`, so deleting an employee who only has tasks unassigns those tasks silently. Owner call whether that delete should also be refused.
- The nine legacy option rows (`Dailysms`, `Welcomesms`, `Thursdaysms`, `Insta`, `Source`, `OldOPG`, `LatestVersion`, `LatestLabelPos`, `LabelTemplatePath`) are left in the live table; deleting them is a data change for the owner.

## RE3 · 2026-10-04 → 2026-10-05 — F20, lab tracking / templates / labels (done)

All 11 F20 rows are closed, none partial, over two sessions: the first fixed the 🔴 on the spot (with the 🟠) and stopped at a clean point on the owner's request; the second finished the list. **`npm run gate` green** (387 tests; D1 0 / D2 7; build OK). **Route table: +1 line** (`GET /api/aligner/labels/settings`, a read under the authenticated `/api` mount). No migration (the new `options` row is created on the first print). **Uncommitted.** Runtime: 85 checks on the throwaway install, all passing (11 in the first session, 74 in the second).

### Owner decision taken at the start of RE3 (2026-10-04)

| ID | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F20-3 | Which logo do aligner labels carry? | **The clinic logo uploaded in Settings → General; none uploaded = no logo.** This clinic has no `CLINIC_LOGO` yet, so its labels print without a logo until one is uploaded (which also puts it in the header). |

### What changed, per finding

- **FE-F20-1 (🔴) and FE-F20-2 (🟠)** — first session. `services/templates/template-files.ts` owns the template directory, the one containment rule (`resolveTemplateFile`, moved out of `receipt-service.ts`) and per-template file names (`templateFilePathFor` → `<slug>-<id>.html`, `template-<id>.html` for a name with no Latin letters). `updateTemplate.body` no longer takes `template_file_path` or `modified_by`, `createTemplate.body` no longer takes `created_by`; the routes record the session user. `GET /:id/html` and `POST /:id/save-html` resolve through the containment rule; save-html reassigns a path that escapes the directory or is shared with another template (`template-queries#isTemplateFileShared`) and writes via a same-directory temp file + rename.
- **FE-F20-3** — server (first session): `pdf-assets.ts` has no bundled fallback; `aligner-label.routes.ts#configuredLabelLogo` passes the configured `CLINIC_LOGO` (PNG/JPEG — PDFKit can't draw WebP). Client (second): the dialog asks the server whether a logo can print (`labelSettings.logo`, which also checks the file is readable) and, when not, disables *Include Logo* in both modes with "Upload a clinic logo (PNG or JPEG) in Settings → General to print it on labels"; what is sent is `includeLogo && logo`. `public/shawan logon.png` deleted (nothing referenced it).
- **FE-F20-4** — `LabelPreviewModal#handleGenerate` opens the PDF's window synchronously in the click (a "Preparing labels…" placeholder), then navigates it to the blob. Blocked anyway (or closed by the user) → the PDF is downloaded under the server's file name, with an info toast. Any failure closes the placeholder, keeps the dialog and the queue, and toasts the server's `error` field (not the raw body). 30 s timeout (the server's own); a timeout says so. An in-flight ref stops a double click.
- **FE-F20-5** — `GET /api/aligner/labels/settings` (contract `labelSettings`: `{ nextPosition 1–12, logo }`) and a new option `AlignerLabelNextPosition`, written by the generate route after each PDF (a failed write logs and doesn't fail the print). Not the legacy `LatestLabelPos`, whose meaning nothing records. The dialog's position is `null` = "the stored one" until the user clicks a cell, so a read that lands after opening still applies; the query is always stale and enabled only while open, so every opening re-reads it (another workstation may have printed), and a print invalidates it. The option shows in General Settings' raw option list — see *Found along the way*.
- **FE-F20-6** — *Deliver* before *Ready to Cement* asks first ("This case is at …, before Ready to Cement. Mark it delivered now?"). *Send back* is offered on delivered cases. Server: a remake now clears `delivered_at`/`delivered_by` (a remake always lands before `delivered`, so the case is no longer delivered; it left the previous delivery on a live case, as the live fixture showed).
- **FE-F20-7** — (a) *Send back to* lists earlier stages only, and the button is hidden when there is none (the first stage). (b) The note is sent as typed, so `''` clears it. (c) The new due date rides on the advance (one transaction; the modal and the card both dropped the second PATCH). (d) One write at a time: the modal's handlers share a `runWrite(failure, fn)` (ref guard + server-message toast); the card's *Confirm* has its own ref. (e) A failed case read shows *Retry*; due dates go through `formatLocaleDate` on the card and in the modal.
- **FE-F20-8** — (a) The search debounce reads the latest filters through a ref, so a stage/lab change during the 300 ms is kept. (b) `LabTracking` drops a `status` outside `LAB_CASE_STATUSES` and a non-positive-integer `labId` instead of forwarding them to a 400. (c) The card body is `role="button"`, `tabIndex={0}`, Enter/Space opens the case, with a `:focus-visible` ring.
- **FE-F20-9** — (a) The designer uses `useUnsavedRouteGuard`; the toolbar's *Back* just navigates, so every way out (Back, a header link, the browser's Back; a reload through the hook's `beforeunload`, not runtime-tested here) asks, and only when dirty. **Dirty is a content comparison, not GrapesJS's change counter:** the counter also moves on its own after a design loads, so an untouched template counted as changed (caught at runtime). `GrapesJSEditor` snapshots HTML + CSS after a load and after a save (`DESIGN_SAVED_EVENT`, triggered by the designer), and `update` compares against it; GrapesJS's own `noticeOnUnload` is off so there is one prompt. (b) The *Clinic Header* block inserts `{{clinic.Name}}` only (receipts already render it from the configured name, first session). (c) The toolbar reads "Template Designer", and a saved document's `<title>` is the template's name (escaped), not "Receipt Preview". (d) *Create* has an in-flight ref + a busy button; the create modal also got `unsavedGuard={{ watchInput: true }}`. (e) No `created_by`/`modified_by` from the client.
- **FE-F20-10** — Both dialog modes use `<ModalHeader>` (named close button; the queue's counts are its subtitle) and the children-as-function form, with `unsavedGuard={{ isDirty }}`: edits here are mostly clicks, which `watchInput` can't see, so `isDirty` compares the state with what the dialog was opened with (labels, logo choice, patient/doctor names, removed batches, a typed-but-unadded label). `doctorLabel()` in the single dialog and in `PrintQueueContext#addToQueue`; the doctor is optional (no doctor = no doctor line). Found while testing: Escape inside a label's edit box also closed the whole dialog — the edit inputs now `preventDefault()` their Escape. The label dialog's own header CSS is gone.
- **FE-F20-11** — `interface Set`/`Batch`/`Patient` → `LabelSet`/`LabelBatch`/`LabelPatient`; `onGenerate`/`isGenerating` dropped. `TemplateCard` uses the contract's `DocumentTemplateRow`; `TemplateManagement` uses the contract rows uncast (`docType.icon ?? 'fa-file-alt'`); `CreateTemplateModal` types its output as `CreateTemplateBody` and takes `DocumentTypeRow[]`; the create and save-html calls carry their response schemas; `GrapesJSEditor`'s template type is a `Pick` of the row. `useLabelModal` is plain functions. The three dead CSS classes are gone.

### Runtime verification: 85 checks on a throwaway install, all passing

First session (11): the template containment/sharing checks and labels without a logo (see FE-F20-1/-2/-3 above). Second session (74), as `Admin` unless noted:
- **Labels (33):** the settings read (no row → 1, garbage → 1, readable by `clinical`); a generate stores the next position (11 + 2 → 1); the dialog opens at the stored position (7), with the named close button, a disabled *Include Logo* and the hint; `Dr. Sara Ahmed` via `doctorLabel`; Escape in a label edit cancels only the edit; removing a label then Escape asks, and *Keep editing* keeps the edit; an empty doctor still prints; a 500 toasts "Boom from server", keeps the dialog, closes the placeholder and leaves the stored position alone; `window.open` is called before the request starts; success navigates the window to the PDF blob (headless Chromium has no PDF viewer, so it surfaces as a download from that window), closes the dialog and stores 7 + 7 labels → 2; reopening picks up 2; a blocked popup downloads `Labels_….pdf` and says so; queue mode has the shared header and disabled logo toggles, and a failed print keeps the dialog and the queue; after a PNG logo upload the settings say `logo: true`, the box is enabled and checked, and a PDF with the logo generates; no page errors.
- **Lab tracking (23):** due dates formatted on the cards and in the modal; a card body is a keyboard button and Enter opens the case; no *Send back* at the first stage; *Send back* lists only `Sent to Lab` from Wax-Up Try-In; *Deliver* asks before Ready to Cement and a cancel sends nothing; a double-clicked *Advance* sends one request carrying `dueDate` (no PATCH) and stores `pattern_fab 2026-11-02`; a confirmed delivery, then *Send back* (11 options) to Glaze clears `delivered_at`/`delivered_by`; an emptied note is cleared; a failing case read offers *Retry*, which loads it; the card's double-clicked *Confirm* sends one request; a stage picked during the search debounce is kept (`?status=glaze&q=zz`); `?status=bogus&labId=abc` loads the board; no page errors. (One probe defect corrected: a read that failed only once was absorbed by React Query's own retry, so the Retry check re-ran with a read that keeps failing.)
- **Templates (18):** the list renders from the contract rows; *Set Default* sends `{"is_default":true}`; a double-submitted create sends one POST with no `created_by` and records `Admin`; the designer's title; an untouched design leaves by *Back* without a prompt; after deleting an element, the header's Home control, the browser's Back and the toolbar's *Back* all ask, and *Keep editing* stays; *Save* writes the change with `<title>` = the template's name; after the save *Back* leaves without asking; no page errors. Every probe file in `data/templates/` was deleted afterwards.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)

- **F21 — start here. Any staff login can read the clinic's secrets in `options`.** `GET /api/options` and `GET /api/options/:optionName` are gated only by the `/api` `authenticate` layer (route table lines `GET /api/options`, `GET /api/options/:optionName` — no `authorize`), and return every row unfiltered. The live `options` table holds `gram_session` (the Telegram account's login session, 369 chars — whoever has it is logged in as the clinic's Telegram account) and `EMAIL_SMTP_PASSWORD`. Verified on the throwaway with planted fake values: as `doc1` (clinical) and `desk1` (front desk), both endpoints return both. General Settings also renders every row as an editable text box (admin-only writes), so it displays the session string and the password to whoever opens it, and runtime rows like `AlignerLabelNextPosition`, `whatsapp_*` and the demo manifest sit beside real settings. Likely 🔴 by the house rule; the owner asked to stop before F21, so it is filed, not fixed.
- Deleting a template leaves its HTML file in `data/templates/` (the delete route removes the row only). Backend, 🔵.
- The print queue's default *Include Logo* is still keyed on the aligner doctor's own `logo_path`, while the logo printed is the clinic's (owner decision above). Harmless today (no clinic logo); worth an owner look when one is uploaded: should a doctor's labels carry that doctor's logo instead?
- In the template designer the app header sits under the designer's full-height layout, so it can't be clicked (Playwright: the designer's `<h1>` intercepts the pointer); keyboard and the browser's Back still leave. F25.

## RE2 · 2026-10-04 — F19, Stand inventory & POS (done)

Audited and fixed in the same session, at the owner's request ("after auditing a single phase, start fixing it immediately"). Every F19 row: **16 closed** (2 🟠 · 7 🟡 · 7 🔵), plus one defect found while fixing (folded into FE-F19-15). **`npm run gate` is green** (387 tests in 48 files, was 375: `utils/standCart.test.ts` and `utils/expiryDate.test.ts`; 0 lint warnings; contracts D1 0 / D2 7; build OK). The route table changed by three lines (below). **Runtime: 61 checks on a throwaway install, all passing.** **Uncommitted.** No migration.

### Owner decisions taken at the start of RE2 (2026-10-04)

| ID | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F19-1 | What should the Reports page's fourth card show? | **"Net Cash" = Revenue − Purchases**, labelled as cash, not profit. Gross Profit stays the profit figure. |
| FE-F19-2 | An expired item at the till: refuse or warn? | **Warn and let the cashier confirm.** The server accepts the sale; the dashboard lists expired stock. |
| FE-F19-9 | Which cost does a restock leave on the item? | **Weighted average** of the stock on hand and the delivery. |

### Route table (3 lines)

- `POST /api/stand/items/:id/reactivate` (new): `[authenticate -> authorize(admin) -> validate(params)]`, undoes the soft delete.
- `GET /api/stand/reports/summary` and `/top-items` now `-> validate(query)` (real `YYYY-MM-DD` days; `limit` a digit string).

### What changed, per finding

- **FE-F19-1**: the fourth card is *Net Cash* (revenue − purchases) with a one-line note on each of the three money cards saying what it counts.
- **FE-F19-2**: `getExpiringItems` and the dashboard also count active items past their expiry that still have stock (an expired item with none stays out); the dashboard contract gains `expiredCount`. The panel is "Expired & Expiring Soon" and shows "Expired Nd ago" first; the KPI row has a red "N expired items still in stock" badge. The POS search shows an expired item's date in red; adding one (search or scan) asks "… expired on DATE. Sell it anyway?" (danger confirm, once per line) and the cart line is tagged *Expired*.
- **FE-F19-3 / -4**: the checkout uses `PatientSearchCombobox` over `patientPhonesQuery` (name, or phone/ID when the term starts with a digit — keyboard-navigable), as the Transfer dialog does. The till remounts the checkout after every sale (`key`), so no patient, note or payment method carries over.
- **FE-F19-5**: `submittingRef` in the confirm handler; one sale per double click.
- **FE-F19-6**: `hooks/useScannerCapture.ts` — a capture-phase window listener recognises a scanner burst (≥ 4 keys, each within 50 ms, ended by Enter) outside the barcode box, swallows the Enter, restores whatever the burst typed into the focused field (native value setter + `input` event, so React state follows) and runs the lookup. The barcode box is refocused after an add and after the success dialog closes. *Amount Paid* counts as "edited" only when it differs from the total, so a backed-out scan leaves it following the cart.
- **FE-F19-7**: `roleCaps().adminWrites` gates *Delete*, *Adjust*, *Reactivate*, *Manage Categories* (and its modal) and *Void* (table and sale dialog).
- **FE-F19-8**: create/edit answer a barcode or SKU collision with 409 naming the owning item and saying when it is deleted (`DUPLICATE_BARCODE`/`DUPLICATE_SKU`); a deleted row offers *Reactivate* (admin) instead of Restock/Adjust/Delete; re-adding a deactivated category's name reactivates it (`reactivated: true`), a duplicate or a rename onto an existing name is 409 `DUPLICATE_CATEGORY`.
- **FE-F19-9**: `restockItem` sets `cost_price` to the rounded weighted average in the same `UPDATE` (pre-update values), returns it, and the ledger row keeps the delivery's own cost. The dialog labels the field "Unit Cost of this delivery" and previews the new average; the toast reports it.
- **FE-F19-10**: `expiryDate` is `dateString` in the create/update contracts; `utils/expiryDate.ts#normalizeScannedExpiry` turns a scanned "05/2027" / "2027-05" into the month's last day, a printed day-first date into that day, and anything else into a warning toast with the field left as it was.
- **FE-F19-11**: the sale dialog shows the error with *Retry*; a failed KPI read toasts like the two panels.
- **FE-F19-12**: expiry badges compare local days (`daysUntil`/`isExpired`, judged on the day the list was read: the query's `dataUpdatedAt`); the trend chart parses its days locally; one `standFormat.ts#formatStandDateTime` (named month) for sales and movements; the reports ask for a range when a date is cleared or reversed; report queries validated.
- **FE-F19-13**: the page's persisted "form open" flag is cleared on unmount (a reload still restores); *Update Item* sends only the changed fields (runtime: `{"notes":"only notes"}`).
- **FE-F19-14**: the cart is `utils/standCart.ts` (pure, tested) held in state + a ref, with notices shown after the change instead of inside an updater; a re-add refreshes the line's row. Sales carry `expectedTotal`; a mismatch with the current prices is 409 `PRICE_CHANGED` with those prices, and the till reprices the cart and asks again. `quantity` is `int().positive()` in the contract. The quantity box is a text draft committed on blur/Enter.
- **FE-F19-15**: the item search is an `aria-activedescendant` combobox (arrows + Enter, options not tab stops); the void dialog uses `<ModalHeader variant="danger">`; the charts lose their duplicate canvas titles; movement types read as words (*Opening stock, Restock, Sale, Sale voided, Added, Removed*). Found while fixing: the Stand page's panels were wrapped in a second card repeating their headings — the wrapper is gone.
- **FE-F19-16**: request bodies typed from the contract (`CreateItemBody`, `UpdateItemBody`, `CreateSaleBody`); `POSCart`'s own `StandItem` and the patient-row interface and casts are gone; `useStandItemByBarcode` is a plain `lookupStandItemByBarcode()`; every `React.memo` and the manual `useCallback`s on these screens removed; `.noteCell` and the old dropdown/spin-button CSS deleted; `loadModalState` runs once (lazy `useState`).

### Runtime verification: 61 checks on a throwaway install, all passing

The RE1 PostgreSQL 18 data dir (copied into this session's scratchpad), every `.env` key blanked, sinks / WhatsApp / LocalSend off; stand fixtures written through the API (three categories, six items: expired, expiring in 10 days, low, out, deleted). As `Admin` and `desk1`.

- **API (23):** 409s naming the owner for a deleted item's barcode, an active barcode, a SKU and an edit; 400 for "05/2027"; category duplicate/rename 409 and reactivation; reactivate 403 for front desk, 200 for admin (barcode scans again), 404 unknown; expired-in-stock listed first, expired-without-stock left out, `expiredCount`; weighted average (9 @ 3,000 + 5 @ 4,000 → 3,357; from empty → the delivery cost) with the ledger keeping 4,000; `PRICE_CHANGED` with the current prices, 1.5 → 400, a matching total sells; report date/limit 400s.
- **Till (17):** a name finds the patient; the sale carries `personId` and `expectedTotal`; one POST per double click; focus back in the scan box; the next sale clean; a scan with nothing focused and one typed into *Amount Paid* both add the item and leave the amount at the total; human-speed typing + Enter untouched; the expired item shown, refused, then sold tagged; arrow-key pick; a retyped quantity; a price changed mid-sale repriced and then sold at the shown total.
- **Screens (21):** front desk sees no Delete/Adjust/Manage Categories/Void (table and dialog); a failed sale read shows *Retry*; one heading per Stand panel; the expired row and badge; the restock preview; no form reopening after leaving; an edit sends one field; the scanned expiry normalised and an unreadable one reported; *Reactivate* on a deleted row; movement labels; Net Cash = Revenue − Purchases (74,000 − 41,400 = 32,600); range prompts.

**Not fixed here:** nothing from F19. The live clinic's deactivated `ZZZ_TEST_ITEM` (barcode `ZZZBAR1`) can now be reactivated or left; it is test data for the owner to clean up.

## RE1 · 2026-10-04 — Phase E's audited half: F16 + F17 + F18 (done)

Every F16, F17 and F18 row: **46 closed** (1 🟠 · 18 🟡 · 27 🔵), plus the three sessions' *found along the way* items that were small enough to fix here, and four defects the runtime checks found. **`npm run gate` is green** (375 tests in 46 files, was 341; 0 lint warnings; contracts D1 0 / **D2 7**, ratcheted from 8; build OK). The route table changed by three lines (below). **Runtime: 168 checks on a throwaway install, all passing** once the probes' own mistakes were fixed. **Uncommitted.**

### Owner decisions taken at the start of RE1 (2026-10-04)

| ID | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F16-4 | Doctors and assistants open /send and /auth, but pairing and group settings are front-desk/admin. | **Hide those parts from them.** A `roleCaps().manageWhatsApp` flag; /auth says who pairs. No route change. |
| FE-F17-9 | Set cost on a work not billed in USD; may `clinical` price sets? | **USD-only and finance-only.** The server refuses a *changed* price from a clinical user (403) and on a non-USD work (400); re-sending the stored price passes, so the three legacy priced sets on works 10192/10193/10768 stay editable. Their works' currency is still the owner's to correct. |
| FE-F17-12 | Does opening a set count as reading the doctor's notes? | **No — read only when viewed.** A note is marked read once the message list has been on screen for 1.5 s, with an idempotent set-read endpoint. |
| FE-F18-4 / -12 | Show optional integrations on installs that don't use them? | **Hide both.** The Archform tab shows only with an `ARCHFORM_DB_PATH`; Announcements, the *Portal Sign-in* column, the uploads section and the auto announcements exist only with a doctor-portal mirror (`SUPABASE_FAILOVER_DB_URL`). |

### Route table (3 lines)

- `GET /api/aligner/features` (new, read): `{ archform, portal }` — which optional integrations this install uses.
- `GET /api/aligner/all-sets` now `[validate(query)]` (the `inactive` / `finished` opt-ins).
- `PATCH /api/aligner/notes/:noteId/toggle-read` → `PATCH /api/aligner/notes/read` (same gate; sets the state for a list of notes instead of flipping one).

One migration: `1789460800000_aligner-sets-folder-option.sql` — row data only (no mirror DDL), a no-op except on this clinic (guarded on its `\\WORK_PC\…` Archform path), where it pins the old aligner share root as the new `AlignerSetsFolder` option. `npm run db:setup` now also creates `AlignerSetsFolder` and `ARCHFORM_DB_PATH` empty on a new install.

### What changed, per finding

**WhatsApp send (F16)**
- **FE-F16-1 / -2 / -11**: the reminder batch owns its progress. `messageState` keeps a batch-scoped `BatchProgress` (`startBatch` zeroes it, only the batch loop counts via `recordBatchResult`, `finishBatch` closes it with an `error` when it stopped early or never started). One-off sends no longer touch it; `report()` no longer emits a "finished" frame. All three SSE frames carry the whole progress object (parsed client-side with the contract's `batchProgress`), and `/api/wa/initial-state` reports it with `active`, so a late tab joins a running batch. The panel ranks a dropped stream first, counts failures as attempted, says "handed to WhatsApp", reports an empty day and a stopped batch, refreshes the count banner when a batch ends, and disables Start at a zero count. Unit test `services/messaging/messageState.test.ts` replays F16's probe (receipts before a batch, a second batch in the same process).
- **FE-F16-3**: `/api/wa/restart` is fire-and-forget like `/refresh-qr`; the page resets before the POST.
- **FE-F16-4**: see the owner decision; the dev-only auto-init POST also requires `canPair`.
- **FE-F16-5 / -6 / -13 / -14**: `SendMessage` rewritten on its own CSS module — one send at a time, 120 s timeout, `/sendmedia2` reports `total` + every file's error so "2 of 3 sent" is said, contact sources from `GOOGLE_CONTACT_ACCOUNTS`, contact rows typed from contracts (the Google response is now modeled: D2 −1), patients without a phone left out, a live auth-required banner (no "Check Again"), and `/auth?popup=1` closes the popup after pairing. `SuccessDisplay` says what happens next. The group placeholder is neutral.
- **FE-F16-7**: `hooks/useReminderResend.ts` — module-scoped in-flight set and bulk run, so a re-send can't be doubled and a running "Re-send all failed" survives leaving /send; it reports through `window.toast`.
- **FE-F16-8 / -15**: the bespoke `APIClient` and `whatsapp-validation.ts` are deleted; count, reset, send and email go through the funnel (count/reset with their contracts). Dead `CONFIG` keys, `UI_STATES` and `MESSAGE_STATUS_CLASS` removed; `/send?date=` validated as a real date with `dateString`.
- **FE-F16-9**: `/api/wa/send-appointment` answers a `code` per refusal; `bookingError.confirmationFailureMessage` maps them to `form.wa*` keys (EN + AR); the calendar uses the same codes in English.
- **FE-F16-10**: the status table's rows are the contract's `StatusRow`; the always-empty Message column and the `escapeHtml`/`dangerouslySetInnerHTML` round trip are gone; row tints and the status panel's states are module classes.
- **FE-F16-12**: both hooks listen for `reconnecting`; the auth machine keeps a shown QR on a reopen, has a `streamDown` flag, and its footer covers RESTORING and NEEDS_RELINK (CONNECTED state removed). 3 machine tests added.
- **FE-F16-16**: one `whatsappInitialStateQuery` (`qk.whatsapp.initialState`) read by the status provider and both hooks. Every stream (re)open cancels a read that began before it and re-reads, so the snapshot always post-dates the open (two reads per page load).

**Aligner sets page (F17)**
- **FE-F17-2 / -3 / -6 / -12 / -14**: `PatientSets` is on React Query (`qk.aligner.sets/batches/notes/photos`, CLAUDE.md's loader exception retired) and split into `pages/aligner/sets/{SetCard,BatchList,SetAttachments,SetCommunication,setHelpers}`. The route renders it keyed by `workId`. `query/aligner.ts#invalidateAligner()` (the `['aligner']` prefix) follows every aligner write, the Works page's lifecycle writes and a Portal-activity bell click. A failed read shows an error with Retry. Doctor notes: see the owner decision; `PATCH /api/aligner/notes/read` sets, never toggles. Breadcrumb "Back" goes where the user came from; each set has its own note draft; `React.memo` and the drawer hooks' manual `useCallback`s are gone.
- **FE-F17-1**: `updateBatch` is partial on the server (contract, service, query); the batch drawer and the set drawer send only what changed; the inline link edits send one field. Activating a batch now reports the batch it deactivated.
- **FE-F17-4**: the set header is a plain container; the set number is a heading holding a disclosure button; photo and scan cards hold separate view and delete controls.
- **FE-F17-5 / -13**: setting, editing or clearing a batch date refreshes in place with the drawer open (the undo flows moved into the drawer). The drawer reads its dates from the live batch list, formats them as local days, and its date rows have CSS. A stored set type outside the list stays selected; a duplicate set number is caught client-side and answers 409 (was a 500); deleting the PDF in the drawer refreshes the card.
- **FE-F17-7**: the share root is the `AlignerSetsFolder` option (`shared/clinic-options.ts`); Open Folder says so when it isn't set; the folder-access check expects the configured folder's own name; lab notes are labelled with the clinic name, else "Lab".
- **FE-F17-8 / -9**: an emptied cost saves NULL; two decimals; `set_cost != null` everywhere (no stray "0"); a 0 cost with nothing paid is "Paid". Pricing rules: see the owner decision.
- **FE-F17-10 / -11**: `ConfirmDialog` awaits an async `onConfirm` and holds its buttons meanwhile (one fix for every call site); Send Note has an in-flight guard. Delete prompts (set, batch, note, upload) say what goes with them and use the danger confirm.
- **FE-F17-15**: contract types for the manufacture/deliver/batch responses; the queue builds its doctor from contract fields; the loader is typed from `WorkDetails`/`PatientInfo` (the work-details contract now models `person_id`/`currency`/`type_name`); stale comments, the inline style, the unused `LastFlagPrompt` export and the dead wrapper classes are gone.

**Aligner lists, Archform, announcements (F18)**
- **FE-F18-1 / -11**: the form edits the *last day shown* and stores the following midnight, so "today" shows today and an unchanged edit keeps its expiry; `min` = today; doctors without email are listed but disabled; link URLs must be `/…` or `http(s)://…` (contract + form).
- **FE-F18-2**: "No Next" is the badge's "Not Created" state exactly; the legend's Final counts the badge's final state.
- **FE-F18-3 / -4**: dangling Archform links get their own section with Unmatch; `updateArchformId` refuses (409) to overwrite a set's link or to link one Archform patient twice; renaming a vanished Archform patient answers 404; no default Archform path (`ArchformNotConfiguredError`, a "set it up" screen linking Settings); the row is seeded by `db:setup`; the tab follows `GET /api/aligner/features`.
- **FE-F18-5 / -6 / -7 / -8**: no stray "0" on doctor or patient cards; failed reads show `AlignerLoadError` (doctors, patients, search; a hard-load doctors failure lands on the route error page); the header reads "Patients" until the doctor is known. Quick Search matches a patient ID, escapes `%`/`_`, and stops at 50 with a note. Patient cards are keyed by work, All Doctors shows unread notes, phones use `PhoneDisplay`, and the thumbnail uses the server-found real file name (`findFirstSmiles`, one directory read), so a patient without a Smile costs no request.
- **FE-F18-9 / -10**: doctor add/edit has an in-flight guard and an unsaved guard (so has the announcement modal); doctor writes `invalidateAligner()`; the delete prompt says sets block it and announcements go; the dead Logo Path field (never saved — the contract strips it) is gone.
- **FE-F18-12 / -13**: Announcements shows a banner when the failover or reverse sink isn't keeping up (from `/api/sync/supabase-status`), says "saved — appears in the portal once synced", and hides with the portal (see the owner decision). The history list is capped at 300; All Sets filters server-side with `hidden` counts for its toggles; All Doctors draws 120 cards at a time; the matcher mounts one set picker (open works first) and uses `Map`s.
- **FE-F18-14 / -15**: one `doctorLabel()` helper (also the matcher's `Dr_Name_N` auto-rename, `Admin_N` for Admin); the matcher's headers are keyboard-sortable with `aria-sort`, its inline rename takes Enter/Escape, icon buttons are labelled, react-select styles are layout-only; All Sets rows carry a real patient link; `aria-current` only on the four list routes. No-op casts, the empty types section and the stale contract text are gone; `docs/archform-matcher.md` corrected.

**Found along the way (the earlier sessions' and this one's), fixed here**
- The WhatsApp booking confirmation, the staff task notice and the staff schedule/test emails opened with this clinic's name as a literal: they read `CLINIC_NAME` now (`getClinicDisplayName()`, cached with the message names, wording unchanged for this clinic).
- `docs/archform-matcher.md` no longer carries the share's password (it stays in git history — **the owner should change it if still in use**).
- `PUT /api/announcements/:id` refuses an auto announcement (409); its 404s read "Announcement not found" (was "… not found not found").
- **Doctor edits wiped `logo_path`**: `updateDoctor` wrote it unconditionally while the contract strips it. It writes it only when sent now (live check: doctor 1's logo is still intact).
- **Escape that closes a react-select menu inside a modal also closed the modal** (or raised the unsaved prompt). `Modal`'s Escape stack ignores a `defaultPrevented` Escape now — every modal with a dropdown.
- **A same-day booking conflict showed English on the Arabic forms**: the 409 omitted its `code`. It carries `APPOINTMENT_CONFLICT` now.
- An install without the portal's R2 storage answered 500 on every set's uploads read: it is an empty list now (`isR2Configured`).
- Auto announcements are not written on an install without a portal mirror.

**Not fixed here (still for their sessions):** the print queue's logo choice (F20); `CanvasControlButtons.module.css` orphan (F24); the Settings/portal clinic literals (F21–F23); the three legacy priced sets on non-USD works (owner data call).

### Runtime verification: 168 checks on a throwaway install, all passing

PostgreSQL 18 from the scratchpad (`:55432`), migrated, `db:setup` + `db:seed:demo` ("Bright Smile Orthodontics"), a scratch `MACHINE_PATH`, every `.env` key blanked, sinks and WhatsApp auto-init off, plus fixtures (four aligner doctors, an IQD aligner work, an inactive `Correction` set, a finished aligner work, unread doctor notes, a test Archform SQLite file with four patients, a dangling link). The dev box holds a stored WhatsApp session, so every probe intercepted `initialize`/`restart`/`refresh-qr`/`unlink`; nothing paired. Three orphaned dev servers from the F16–F18 sessions (live DB, sync off) were stopped by pid first. No screenshots.

- **F16** (42): initial-state shape; role gating on /send and /auth; restart reset-first; a late tab joining a running batch; a fake always-open SSE stream playing two batches (the second not "finished" after one message, counts per batch, banner refresh); zero-count Start; confirm timers; re-send guards across in-app navigation; the send-message page (styled, one POST, "2 of 3", registry sources); the send-appointment code, and an **Arabic booking** toasting «واتساب غير متصل…».
- **F17** (53): notes not read off-screen, read after being viewed, banner follows; keyboard on the set header; inline URL edit sends one field; partial batch edit over a simulated portal days change (no revert, no bogus flag); date apply and undo inside the drawer; cost null/decimals/0 = Paid; pricing 403/400; duplicate number client-side and 409; one note POST per double click; delete prompts; re-reads after the cache's 30 s and after writes; a failed read on a patient switch.
- **F18** (62): features endpoint; nav (Archform tab, `aria-current`); stray zeros; error states; deep-link header; All Doctors unread banner and no guessed thumbnails; search by ID and literal `%`; All Sets server filters, hidden counts, No Next = Not Created, row link by keyboard; the matcher (dangling section, one picker, match, 409s, 404 rename, keyboard sort, Enter/Escape rename, one DELETE per double click); announcements (sync banner, today's expiry stored as tomorrow 00:00, unchanged edit, link rule client + server, disabled audience row, unsaved guard, one DELETE, auto-row 409); doctor settings (one add per double click, logo kept, lists refreshed, prompt).
- **No portal, no Archform** (11): tabs and links hidden, direct links explain, no uploads section or R2 request, no Portal Sign-in column, no auto announcement on manufacture, an empty R2 listing; the two option rows listed in Settings → General.

Probe notes worth keeping:
- **Never call `/api/wa/{initialize,restart,refresh-qr,unlink}` on the WSL box**: `.wwebjs_auth/session-client` holds a stored WhatsApp session. Intercept them; `WHATSAPP_AUTO_INIT=false` blocks the on-demand init.
- A `route.fulfill` SSE body ends the stream (EventSource then fires `reconnecting`); to play frames on an open stream, replace `EventSource` for `/api/sse/whatsapp` in an init script (`fake-sse.mjs` pattern).
- `page.goto` reloads the app and drops module state; for in-app navigation click the header's `.logo-section` and use `page.goBack()`.
- Playwright `fill('')` on an unfocused input whose display changes on focus races the re-render; click first, then select-all + Backspace.
- `psql -At` prints the `INSERT 0 1` tag after `RETURNING` rows — take the first line.
- A `waitForFunction` on text must name the final state, or it catches the first frame.

## RX1 · 2026-10-03 — the cross-phase leftovers (done)

Every row the phase sessions had left open: Phase A's deferred and partial rows (FE-F0 through FE-F4), plus the two partial Phase B rows (FE-F8-13, FE-F9-12). Several had been deferred to sessions that have not run yet (F16, F17, F20, F21). They are fixed here rather than waiting, because each one was self-contained and none needed the later audit's reading. **22 rows closed, plus one defect found and fixed along the way (FE-F4-21). The board is empty: 0 🔴 · 0 🟠 · 0 🟡 · 0 🔵 open. 248 closed** since the start. **`npm run gate` is green** (341 tests in 42 files, was 293; contracts D1 0 / D2 8; **0 lint warnings**, FE-F0-4's four are gone; build OK). The route table did not change. **Runtime: 125 checks on a throwaway install, all passing** once the probes' own mistakes were fixed. **Uncommitted.**

### Owner decisions taken at the start of RX1 (2026-10-03)

| ID | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F1-6 | Settings → Protocol Handlers rewrote the whole INI on Save. Preserve the file, or keep overwriting and warn? | **Edit in place.** Save re-reads the file and rewrites only the changed keys' lines. Backup copies the file as it is on disk; Restore writes the backup back unchanged. |
| FE-F9-12 | Should the visit chart get primary (deciduous) teeth? | **Yes, behind a toggle**, hidden by default: an A–E row under each arch, same letters as the work-item `TeethSelector`. |

### What changed, per finding

**Dates and locale (FE-F3-3, FE-F1-8)**
- **FE-F3-3**: `utils/formatters.ts` gained `formatLocaleDate` / `formatLocaleDateTime` / `formatLocaleTime`.
  - They always pass an app locale from `LANGUAGES`, read a `'YYYY-MM-DD'` as a local day, and return `''` for bad input, so each caller keeps its own placeholder.
  - `lang` defaults to English (an untranslated screen's chrome is English); a translated screen passes `useLanguage().language`.
  - All 46 remaining sites in 31 files now use them; the expense table and delete dialog pass the live language. Three `SyncStatusPanel` counts that were numbers now use `formatNumber`. The four patient-portal tabs pin English too, like the rest of the portal's text, and its amount formatter pins `en-US`.
  - **Lock-in:** an ESLint `no-restricted-syntax` rule bans `toLocale(Date|Time)?String()` with no locale or an `undefined` one. CLAUDE.md's i18n section states the rule.
  - Side effect worth knowing: several of those sites also did `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')`, which is UTC midnight, a day early west of UTC (expense dates, the WhatsApp page's date heading, aligner batch dates, the invoices modal title). The helper reads them as local days.
- **FE-F1-8**: the hand-rolled `YYYY-MM-DD` builders now call `utils/calendarDate.ts#toLocalDateString`: `core/utils#formatISODate`'s Date branch, `useDateManager` (its exported `getLocalDateString` is gone), `useAppointmentsSync`, `Expenses`, `StandSalesHistory`, `StandReports`, `AppointmentsHeader`'s day stepper, `DailyInvoicesModal`, `PortalActivityBell`'s day key.
  - **Bug found in the sweep:** `LabCaseCard`'s overdue check compared against `new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)`, the UTC date. From 00:00 to 03:00 Baghdad time that is still yesterday, so a case due yesterday did not read as overdue. Now local.
  - `AllSetsList` and `ArchformMatcher` had their own copies of `core/utils#formatDate`; `ArchformMatcher`'s also parsed date-only strings as UTC. Both now import it. `ExpiringItemsPanel` parses the stand `expiry_date` (a PG `date`) with `parseLocalDate`, for both the label and the days-left count.
  - The remaining `DD/MM/YYYY` display builders in the stand sales screens are explicit numeric formats with no locale or UTC problem, and were left alone.

**Shared state and server-state layer (FE-F3-6, FE-F3-12, FE-F2-13, FE-F3-11)**
- **FE-F3-6**: `GlobalStateContext` is now two memoized contexts behind the same `GlobalStateProvider`: `useAuthUser()` and `useWhatsAppStatus()` (`{ clientReady, qrCode }`). All 16 call sites moved over and `useGlobalState` is gone. A WhatsApp QR rotation (about every 20 s while unpaired) now re-renders only the three WhatsApp readers, not the 13 screens that only read the user. That re-render saving was not measured; the harness has no profiler.
- **FE-F3-12**: `useStandItemMutations`, `useStandSaleMutations`, `useStandCategoryMutations` and `useExpenseMutations` are now built from one `useApiMutation` per operation, with their public signatures unchanged.
  - The invalidation is awaited before the caller's `await` resolves.
  - `loading` ORs the per-operation pending flags, so two overlapping writes can't clear each other's spinner.
  - A 5xx is reported through the `MutationCache` like every other mutation's.
  - The category hook now rejects the same way as its siblings. The unread `error` fields on the item and sale hooks are gone (callers already toast through `httpErrorMessage`).
  - A held expense edit or delete (`outcome: 'pending'`) refreshes `qk.approvals.all()` instead of the expense list, exactly as before.
- **FE-F2-13**: `App.tsx`'s docblock now describes the real cache (30 s stale / 5 min gc, loaders as prefetchers, `useApiMutation` as the standard). The four hooks above are the adoption point the finding asked for. A wholesale conversion of the ~100 hand-rolled invalidation sites was **not** done, deliberately: that stays the standing advice for new code, not a sweep.
- **FE-F3-11**: `useWhatsAppSync`'s initial-state read was deduplicated by date, but the endpoint takes no date, so an explicit refresh on the same date did nothing. It is now single-flight: a call made while one is in flight is dropped, and every later call refetches. The hook lost its unused `currentDate` parameter.

**WhatsApp (FE-F3-5, FE-F1-4)**
- **FE-F3-5**: new `hooks/whatsappAuthMachine.ts`, a pure reducer that owns `authState` and `error` and mirrors `clientReady`/`qrCode`.
  - Every former writer is now an event: the status context, the initial-state read, the SSE transport events, the client-ready frame, the actions and their failures.
  - `enforce()` applies, after every event, the invariants the two render-phase blocks had kept between them: a ready client is AUTHENTICATED; a QR in hand turns AUTHENTICATED or CHECKING_SESSION into QR_REQUIRED.
  - The 3-second CHECKING_SESSION settle is an effect keyed on the state, so it is cleared when the state changes or the page unmounts.
  - `useWhatsAppAuth` keeps its return shape and re-exports `AUTH_STATES`. 9 unit tests, including the two cases each old block covered alone.
  - One intended difference: a page that mounts with the client already ready starts AUTHENTICATED instead of flashing INITIALIZING.
- **FE-F1-4**, client: `utils/whatsapp-api-client.ts` changed in four ways.
  - Only an idempotent GET is retried, and only on a network error, a timeout or a 5xx. A POST is never replayed, and a 4xx or a cancel is never retried.
  - Each attempt has a timeout (`CONFIG.OPERATION_TIMEOUT_MS`, 30 s, the server's own).
  - A non-2xx carries the server's message (a new `APIHttpError` class) instead of "HTTP 409: Conflict".
  - The abort controller is deleted by identity, not by key.
- **FE-F1-4**, server half: `POST /api/email/send-appointments` refuses a second send for a date already being sent, with **409** (an in-memory per-date set; one process serves the clinic). No middleware changed, so the route table is the same.

**Print queue, chair display, lookups (FE-F3-14, FE-F4-10, FE-F3-13, FE-F0-3, FE-F4-21)**
- **FE-F3-14**: `PrintQueueContext` exports a pure, generic `groupByPatient()`. The context's `getGroupedQueue` and `LabelPreviewModal`'s grouping both use it; the modal groups its own edited copies.
  - The modal takes the context's `PrintQueueItem` as `queuedItems`. Its private `QueuedItem`/`PatientGroup` copies are gone, and so is a dead `doctorLogoPath` fallback.
  - `RootLayout`'s `usePrintQueue() as {…}` cast and its own copy of the interface are gone.
- **FE-F4-10**: each tab tags its chair-display beacons with a random `src` and an increasing `seq` (`PatientShell`).
  - The contract accepts both as optional. The route drops a beacon older than one already accepted from the same tab (`services/messaging/chair-beacon-order.ts`, which remembers 32 tabs per chair).
  - Between tabs, arrival order still decides: comparing their counters would need synchronized clocks. A cached older client that sends neither field is ordered by arrival as before.
- **FE-F3-13**: `useLookupManager`'s menu label and `LookupManagerModal`'s title and loading text default to `common:lookups.*` (EN + AR), not English literals. Both files are on the i18n ratchet now; the hook builds its menu items outside JSX for that. The `LookupEditor` body stays English until Settings is translated.
- **FE-F0-3**: `LookupEditorModal`'s `useQueries` has a `combine`, so the reference-options memo recomputes only when a table's rows change.
- **FE-F4-21** (found here): one Escape in the lookup editor's add/edit popover closed the `LookupManagerModal` under it as well. The popover now captures Escape and stops it, the same way `LookupContextMenu` does. The key is also swallowed while a save is in flight, so the manager can't close mid-write.

**Small and dead (FE-F0-1, FE-F2-10, FE-F2-11, FE-F4-14, FE-F0-4, FE-F0-5, FE-F1-5)**
- **FE-F0-1 / FE-F2-10**: the last dead exports are deleted.
  - `aligner.types.ts`: `AlignerSetForLabel`, `AlignerSetFormData`, `AlignerBatchForLabel`.
  - `whatsapp-send-constants.ts`: `MESSAGE_TYPES`/`MessageType`, `BUTTON_STATES`/`ButtonState`, `ConfigType`, `ApiEndpointsType`.
  - `whatsapp-validation.ts`: `ApiSuccessResponse`. `formatters.ts`: `formatMoneyInput`. `portal.schemas.ts`: `PortalPatientData`. `iniParser.ts`: `setValue`, `mergeConfigs`, `formatIniContent`, `getProtocolHandlerFormatOptions` and its option types, all replaced by the FE-F1-6 rewrite.
  - `cleanPhone` was not dead any more (RB4's `PhoneInput` uses it). `approvalsHistoryQuery` gained its consumer in RB4's `/approvals/history`. The nine parent `qk` keys stay as documentation of the hierarchy, as RA4 decided.
- **FE-F2-11**: `MATERIAL_OPTIONS` is a literal tuple with a `Material` type. The framework try-in labels are a `Partial<Record<Material, string>>` keyed by name, so renaming a material is a compile error and inserting one relabels nothing. 4 tests.
- **FE-F4-14**: `AlignerModeToggle` owns `AlignerModeToggle.module.css`. The layout's one rule for it (no sticky in the viewport-locked all-sets shell) is now a `sticky` prop. The four buttons are a mapped list, and the toggle exports the `AlignerMode` type the layout used to repeat.
- **FE-F0-4**: the aligner photo viewer was a hand-rolled fixed overlay inside `PatientSets`.
  - It had a click handler on a `role="dialog"` div (the tree's last four lint warnings), no focus trap, and a window-level Escape outside the modal stack.
  - It is now `pages/aligner/AlignerPhotoViewer.tsx` on `<Modal>`, with its own CSS module; the global `.aligner-photo-viewer-*` rules (about 125 lines) are gone from `aligner-common.css`. ←/→ still step through the photos.
- **FE-F0-5**: `public/index.html`'s bootstrap changed in four ways.
  - It mounts as soon as the module runs; a deferred module script already waits for the head's stylesheets. It used to wait for window `load`, every image and font included.
  - The two console-only `error`/`unhandledrejection` listeners are gone; `core/error-reporter.ts` already reports both.
  - The `performance.timing` block is gone. It read `loadEventEnd` inside a `load` handler, where it is still 0, so it logged a negative "page load time".
  - The informational `console.log`s and the commented-out service-worker block are gone. The 401 interceptor stays (it is load-bearing); only its "installed" log went. The mount-failure message is now set with `textContent`.
- **FE-F1-5**: `core/fileSystemAccess.ts` changed in two ways.
  - Every handle-store operation goes through `withStore()`, which opens the database, runs one request, resolves on the transaction's `complete` (a write is committed, not just queued) and always closes the connection.
  - `openDatabase()` rejects on `onblocked` with a readable message, and each connection closes itself on `versionchange`.

**INI and money forms (FE-F1-6, FE-F8-13, FE-F9-12)**
- **FE-F1-6**: `core/iniParser.ts` is rewritten around `applyIniChanges(text, changes)`, following the Windows profile API the protocol handlers read the file with.
  - It replaces only a changed key's value, on its first occurrence in its section, and keeps the indent and `=` spacing.
  - It appends a missing key after its section's last line and a missing section at the end. Every other line is left byte-for-byte, and so is the file's CRLF or LF.
  - It refreshes an existing `# Last updated:` comment, and a value can't inject a line.
  - `parseIniContent` now keeps the first occurrence of a duplicate key, like Windows.
  - Save re-reads the file at save time, so an edit made on disk while the page was open survives. Backup writes the file as it is on disk; Restore writes the backup back unchanged. 10 tests.
- **FE-F8-13**: the payment form is `utils/paymentForm.ts`, one pure reducer, and `PaymentModal` went from 1,203 to 823 lines.
  - Every input is an event. The three auto-fills (suggested cash in amount mode, amount from cash in cash mode, change) run once per event, in dependency order, each only when one of its own inputs changed: the same triggers the six render blocks were keyed on.
  - The suggestions, the totals and the display strings are now derived, not stored. A stale suggestion hint after clearing the amount is gone with that.
  - The payment-date handler closes the rate editor itself, so that render block is gone too.
  - 16 tests pin the money rules: rounding up when asking, down when converting and giving change, the no-rate cases, mode detection and toggles, and the override rule from RB3.
- **FE-F9-12**: `DentalChart` has a "Primary teeth" switch, off by default. On, it adds an upper and a lower A–E row (URE…URA | ULA…ULE) between the arches, and a click inserts e.g. "URC".
  - The chart stays one tab stop. ↑/↓ move between the four rows at the matching position, and switching the rows off falls back to UR8.

### Runtime verification: 125 checks on a throwaway install, all passing

The install: RB4's PostgreSQL 18 data directory and photo volume, copied into this session's scratchpad (`:55432`), and the env-blanking wrapper regenerated from the current `.env` keys (`PORT=3101`, `VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:3101`, the four sync/WhatsApp flags off). An orphaned dev server from an earlier session, still pointing at a stopped database, was stopped by pid first. Every write went to that database only. No Playwright screenshots were taken.

- **Locale and bootstrap**, 18: the browser ran as `ar-IQ`, where a bare `toLocaleDateString()` really does print `٣‏/١٠‏/٢٠٢٦`.
  - Eleven screens show no Arabic-Indic digit: expenses, calendar, users, all sets, works, templates, lab board, stand, WhatsApp send, task history, patient info.
  - The calendar title is in English. In Arabic, the expense dates read `23‏/9‏/2026` (Latin digits, d/m/y).
  - The page mounts with no bootstrap logs.
- **Payments**, 22, all real payments.
  - IQD amount mode: the cash auto-fills, and the payment is saved as `250000/0/250000/NULL`.
  - USD with a $100 bill for $90: the change is 14,000; a 5,000 override holds until the bill changes to $95, which recomputes it to 7,000; saved as `90/95/0/7000`.
  - MIXED with cash typed first: cash mode locks, $100 makes the amount 145,000, plus 20,000 IQD makes 165,000, and the form sends `change: null`.
  - Pay-full-balance; the rate editor closes on a date change; the unsaved guard still asks.
- **Mutations and lookups**, 18.
  - Stand: category add, and an injected 500 that toasts the server message and re-enables Add; an item create listed the moment the form closed (365 ms); restock to 15; a POS sale to 14; a void.
  - An Arabic expense create; the Arabic lab context menu and lookup-manager title; no unhandled rejections.
- **Aligner and visit chart**, 25.
  - The toggle is sticky on `/aligner` and static on all-sets, with the current view marked.
  - The photo viewer (photos mocked onto real static images) is a named dialog, steps with ←/→, keeps focus inside, and closes on Escape (only itself), on the backdrop and on ✕.
  - Three queued batches group under one patient in the label modal.
  - The primary-teeth switch, the row order, a URC click into the notes, one tab stop, and ↑/↓ across the rows.
- **WhatsApp, email and chair display**, 22.
  - The auth page in five server states (QR, ready with no redirect, parked, restoring, nothing → QR after 3 s).
  - On `/send`: a 503 on the count GET is retried, a 502 on reset is sent once with its message in the toast, and a 409 on the email send is sent once with its reason in the toast.
  - Four simultaneous email sends for one date: the overlapping ones are refused 409.
  - A stale CLEAR from the same tab is dropped, a newer one clears, untagged beacons keep arrival order, and the browser tags every beacon with a growing `seq`.
- **INI and IndexedDB**, 7: an INI file planted in the browser's private file system, with its handle stored the way the app does.
  - The Settings screen loads it, and an edit is made on disk while the page is open.
  - After Save, only the edited value changed: comments, the orphan key, spacing, CRLF and the on-disk edit all survive.
  - A `DB_VERSION` bump is not blocked afterwards.
- **Misc**, 10: the lookup editor's category options; the default date ranges on stand reports, stand sales and expenses; the daily board's day stepper across a month end; the header user and statistics through `useAuthUser`; the lab board.
- **FE-F4-21**, 3: one Escape closes only the popover, a second closes the manager and leaves the expense form, and the standalone popover still closes.

Probe notes worth keeping:
- The chair-display payload names the patient `pid`.
- Playwright does not expose `sendBeacon` blob bodies, so wrap `navigator.sendBeacon` in an init script.
- The beacon guard remembers a probe's `src` across runs, so use a fresh one each run.
- The lookup editor is a popover, not a `role="dialog"`.
- With no SMTP on a throwaway, an email send fails fast, so only overlapping sends hit the 409. Fire several at once.
- Intl puts U+200F marks around `/` in Arabic dates.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)
- **The patient portal still uses the retired language codebook** (`portal/PortalApp.tsx`: `LANG_RTL = new Set([1, 2])`). Under RB1's codebook (0 Arabic · 1 English · 2 Kurdish), a patient marked English gets a mirrored portal and an Arabic one gets LTR. The portal's text is English only, so the real question is whether it should flip at all before it is translated. For F23 (owner call).
- **The staff schedule email hardcodes this clinic's name** (`routes/api/email.routes.ts`: the header `<h1>`, the footer line and the plain-text title say "Shwan Orthodontics"). CLAUDE.md's identity corollary says it should come from `CLINIC_NAME`. Backend scope, the same family as the identity leaks reported by the demo-seed work.
- **`LookupEditorModal` is a hand-rolled portal popover** with a backdrop, but no `role="dialog"`, no focus trap and no `aria-modal`. Its Escape is fixed (FE-F4-21); the semantics are for F21 or F25.
- Noted, not a defect: a cash-mode or USD payment sends `change: null` and the server stores **0** (`payment.routes.ts` does `change ?? 0`; only an IQD-in-IQD payment keeps NULL). In cash mode nothing is handed back, so 0 is true.

## RB4 · 2026-10-03 — Phase B's remainder (done)

The slice RB had not taken yet: every open F5, F6 and F7 row, plus the Phase A rows that live in those files. It ran over two sessions; the owner stopped the first after F5 and F6, and the second ran its runtime checks, then did F7 and CLAUDE.md. **43 rows closed:** every open F5, F6 and F7 row, FE-F1-10 and FE-F4-13, plus 3 rows earlier sessions had fixed without closing. **`npm run gate` is green** (293 tests, contracts D1 0 / D2 8, build OK; the 4 lint warnings are FE-F0-4's). **Runtime: 150 checks on a throwaway install; 149 pass** once the probes' own mistakes were fixed, and the other one found a pre-existing defect, filed below (see *Runtime verification*). **Uncommitted.**

**Board after RB4:** 0 🔴 · 0 🟠 · 7 🟡 · 15 🔵 (22 open, 5 partial). **225 closed** since the start. Of RB4's 43, 40 were fixed in code here, and 3 had already been fixed by earlier sessions without closing the ledger row:
- FE-F5-11 and FE-F3-9: RC2's FE-F11-8 (a board following today rolls at midnight and pins no date).
- FE-F5-18: RB3's FE-F8-10 (the count reads as a floor, e.g. "2,000+").

### What changed, per finding

**Header bells (F5 + FE-F1-10)**
- **FE-F5-13 / FE-F5-12**: new `components/react/HeaderPopover.tsx` (+ `.module.css`) is the one shell for all four bells:
  - It owns the bell button, the badge slot, the portaled card, positioning, outside click and the focus handling.
  - Opening moves focus into the card, Tab cycles inside it, and Escape closes it and returns focus to the bell.
  - Escape is a React handler on the card, not a document listener, so with the Approve-all confirm open one Escape closes only the confirm.
  - A click inside `#modal-root` is not an outside click.
  - Its `RelativeAge` replaces the four `relAge` copies; the shared helper is `utils/formatters.ts#relativeAge`, worded through `common:age.*`.
  - The four bells' stylesheets lost the shell rules.
- **FE-F5-13**: one `HEADER_BELL_POLL` in `query/queries.ts` (5-min poll + `refetchOnWindowFocus`, so the 30 s staleTime now gates the alt-tab refetch). It replaces three `REFRESH_MS` copies and the unconditional visibility listeners. `ACTION_LABELS` ×2 became `services/approvals.ts#ACTION_LABEL_KEY` + the `approvals:action.*` strings. The `as Row[]` casts are gone.
- **FE-F1-10**: the three window `CustomEvent` buses are retired, and the `notify*Changed` / `*_CHANGED_EVENT` exports are deleted. In their place, three React Query helpers:
  - `services/tasks.ts#invalidateTasks(personId)`: `qk.tasks.all()` plus that patient's `qk.patient.alerts`.
  - `services/approvals.ts#invalidateApprovals()`: `qk.approvals.all()`.
  - `services/portal-activity.ts#invalidatePortalActivity()`.

  Every former `notify…` call site uses these: WorkComponent ×2, NewWorkComponent, EditPatientComponent, PatientManagement, useExpenses ×2, AlertModal, ViewPatientInfo, TaskFormModal, TasksHistory and the bells.
- **FE-F5-2**: an approved hold now runs `invalidateApprovalTarget(row)`, which refreshes what the replayed write changed:
  - work actions: the patient and `qk.work.all(target)`;
  - an invoice delete: the patient, `qk.work.root()` and the new `qk.dailyInvoicesAll()` (the invoice row names no work);
  - expense actions: `qk.expenses.all()`;
  - a patient delete: the patient and `qk.lookups.patientPhones()`.

  Approve-all refreshes the targets of every hold it was shown.
- **FE-F5-3**: `approveRequest` now parses its response with the contract, and the bell reads `row.status`: `approved` toasts success, `stale` warns, and `failed` shows the server's `review_note`.
- **FE-F5-4**: the custom snooze date is a draft (`SnoozeDate` in `TasksBell.tsx`). It commits on *Set* or Enter only, with `min` = tomorrow and a "pick a date after today" check.
- **FE-F5-5**:
  - `patient.contract#alertBody.alertTypeId` is now `.nullable()`, and the edit form sends `null` for "None". The server's `updateAlert` already wrote a provided null; POST quick-add still maps a missing id to 1.
  - A since-quit assignee shows by name ("… (no longer on staff)") instead of "Anyone".
- **FE-F5-6**: every task or alert write calls `invalidateTasks(person_id)`, so a patient-linked task refreshes that patient's alert list, and a header-shown alert refreshes the bell. The false "patient view listens to `tasks:changed`" comments are gone.
- **FE-F5-7**: `TasksHistory` has an **Expired** state (active and `expires_at < today`, the server's own rule). Reopen is hidden for a task past its expiry, and the page notes when it shows the server's 200-row cap.
- **FE-F5-14**: the bell cluster is translated. Two new namespaces:
  - **`tasks`**: TasksBell, TaskFormModal and AlertModal (the patient alert form, which opens from the translated `ViewPatientInfo`);
  - **`approvals`**: ApprovalsBell, MyApprovalsBadge and the new history page.

  Interpolations use `n`, not `count`, so i18next never looks for Arabic plural forms that don't exist. All seven files are on the ratchet. The ratchet's `jsx-attributes.exclude` gained `icon`, `contentClassName` and `overlayClassName` (class-name siblings), and its punctuation pattern gained `— – × · …`.
- **FE-F5-16** (owner's verdict: build): **new route `/approvals/history`** (`routes/ApprovalsHistory.tsx`, admin, linked from the bell header). It shows every decided hold and notice: requested when and by whom, the action, the summary, the patient link, the outcome badge, who reviewed it and when, and the note. It filters by status and kind and has a search. It is translated: `/approvals` was added to `RTL_ROUTES` and to the `index.html` FOUC list.

**Statistics + Dashboard (F5)**
- **FE-F5-8**: both date-range tabs compose `enabled` with the factory's empty-date guard. `PeriodNavigator`'s fields are drafts (`DateField`) that commit only a complete date from year 2000 on, so no `0002-…` and no `''` reaches the query. `stepMonth` falls back to this month instead of producing `NaN-NaN-NaN`.
- **FE-F5-9**: a `YearInput` (in `StatisticsComponent.tsx`) commits at the 4th digit, on a spinner step, or on Enter/blur, and only for a year in range. An invalid draft snaps back on blur. It is used by the month-nav year, the Monthly start year and the Yearly From/To.
- **FE-F5-10**: the three hand-written payload interfaces and their `as` casts are gone. The types come from `z.infer` of the contract. `reports.statistics.response` now **models `exchangeRate: z.number().nullable()`**. The ad-hoc `isAdmin` is replaced by a new **`roleCaps().viewReports`** capability (admin) in `shared/auth/roles.ts`.
- **FE-F5-15**:
  - (a) Chart titles take `getChartThemeColors().title` (new field, `--text-heading`).
  - (b) Day labels, `formatDate` and the monthly aggregation parse through `parseLocalDate`.
  - (c) Refresh also refetches the rollup behind the Monthly/Yearly chart.
  - (d) `?view=` is validated and written back (replace).
- **FE-F5-17**: `isRtl` comes from the language (the Dashboard is a translated route), not from `<html dir>`. The `as any` is gone (`Map<string, …>`). The finance cards stay hidden until the role is known. The two `!important`s became a doubled-class selector.

**Patient demographics (F6)**
- **FE-F6-3 / FE-F6-13**: `PatientManagement` was restructured around one `Criteria` object, with `EMPTY_CRITERIA`, `hasCriteria` and `filterCount` (the "any criterion" rule is written once).
  - The snapshot is saved in the effect **body**.
  - A restore paints the snapshot and then **revalidates in the background** to the depth it was left at, in ≤500-row pages, so Load More's depth and the scroll position survive.
  - Older-shape snapshots are dropped.
  - The auto-search keys on the criteria's values.
  - Show All goes through the same abortable `runSearch`.
  - Quick check-in has its own `checkingInId` flag.
  - `?search=` is read through `useLocation`.
  - `currentOffset` is gone (it always equalled `patients.length`).
- **FE-F6-4**: three changes, so a stored number survives the edit form:
  - `PHONE_MASK` = `'000 000 0000[ 00000]'`: 10-digit numbers display as before, and up to E.164's 15 digits fit. Tested with IMask: 11 and 13 digits survive, and more than 15 is cut at 15.
  - `PhoneInput` ignores an `accept` whose value equals the prop's digits, so the mount no longer writes.
  - The Edit form updates every field through `setField` (a functional updater).

  `formatPhoneForDisplay` also stopped dropping digits past the 10th. The Add form's phone2 is now `PhoneInput` too.
- **FE-F6-5**: new **`hooks/useMediaQuery.ts`** (`useSyncExternalStore`). `AddPatientForm` renders the tabs **or** the accordion, never both. The header and bottom action bars render by layout too, and the `.desktopOnly` / `.mobileOnly` / `.headerActions` CSS is gone.
- **FE-F6-7**:
  - (a) **Server**: `PUT /api/patients/:id` now trims the names, phones, email, notes and country code as create does.
  - (b) Edit marks the Arabic name `dir="rtl" lang="ar"` and the English names `dir="ltr"`.
