  - (c) Add's arbitrary 100-character notes cap is removed.
  - (e) Country code is digits-only in both forms (≤4), and both placeholders read "e.g., 964 (digits only)".
- **FE-F6-8**: the edit read uses `refetchOnMount: 'always'`, and the form seeds only once that fetch has settled. It shows the spinner until then.
- **FE-F6-9**: create, edit and delete (both delete paths) invalidate `qk.lookups.patientPhones()`. Add's 1.5 s redirect timer is cleared on unmount.
- **FE-F6-10**: the `patientSearch` row contract models `first_name`, `last_name`, `phone`, `date_added`, `last_visit` and `TagName`. `PatientManagement`, `ViewPatientInfo` and `PatientFolderPicker` read the parsed types with no cast. `AlertModal` and `WebCephModal` widened their prop types to the contract's nullability.
- **FE-F6-11**:
  - (a) A failed background refetch keeps the record on screen and toasts.
  - (b) Archiving an alert asks first (`useConfirm`).
  - (c) DOB and date-added format in the app's locale (Western digits) via `parseLocalDate`. This takes 2 of FE-F3-3's 53 sites.
- **FE-F6-12**:
  - Add's tab strip has `tablist` / `tab` / `aria-selected` / `tabpanel`, and the accordion headers have `aria-expanded`.
  - The Add alert has `role=alert`/`status`, and Edit's error banner has `role=alert` plus a named ✕.
  - ViewPatientInfo's cost ✓/✕ and the alert pencil/✕ are named.
- **FE-F6-14**: **retired `GET /api/patients/type-options`**, along with `typeOptions` (contract), `getPatientTypeOptions` and `PatientTypeOption` (queries), `typeOptionsQuery` and `qk.lookups.typeOptions`. The filter and the loader read `patientTypesQuery` (`/api/patient-types`). Also removed:
  - the legacy `SavedState` keys;
  - Edit's dead `validPersonId` / `person_id` / `patient_type_id` fallbacks (and those two from the PUT body);
  - `PatientFolderPicker`'s dead `enabled`;
  - the stale CSS-migration comments in `routes/PatientManagement.tsx` and `PatientShell.tsx`.

**Route table:** one line, `-GET /api/patients/type-options` (accepted with `vitest -u`).

**Works & treatment items (F7) + FE-F4-13** (second session)
- **FE-F7-6**: new **`utils/workBalance.ts`** (`workBalance(row)` → total, discount, net, paid, remaining, fullyPaid) is the one balance computation. The work card, the payment-history box and `PaymentModal` all use it.
  - The history box reads the work **live** from the works list by id (`historyWorkId`), not a click-time snapshot. It shows the discount, so its balance and its *Add New Payment* agree with the card, and a payment delete updates it in place.
  - `PaymentModal` keeps the row it opened with (it is a form seeded once). It now takes the contract's `WorkRow` (`Pick<…>`) and `parseWorkCurrency` instead of its own `WorkData` interface.
- **FE-F7-8**: complete / discontinue / reactivate refresh `qk.work.all(workId)` as well as the patient. A delete **removes** the work's queries instead of refetching them into a 404. A transfer refreshes the source, the work and the **target** patient; the modal now reports `{ workId, targetPatientId }`.
- **FE-F7-9**: a duration of 0 and an estimate of 0 render nothing (they used to render a bare `0`).
- **FE-F7-10**: the patient card reads the contract's `phone` (formatted, `dir="ltr"`) and `AlertCount`. The alert badge counts the active, unexpired alerts and opens the patient's info page. `invalidateTasks` now also refreshes `qk.patient.info`, so the badge follows a task write.
- **FE-F7-11 + FE-F4-13**: `TransferWorkModal` is rebuilt on **`PatientSearchCombobox`** (a name field and a phone/ID field), and `PatientQuickSearch` is **deleted**, with its stylesheet and its `react-select/async` import.
  - The combobox exports `PatientOption` and gained `hint`, `groupLabels` and `inputRef` props. The dialog passes the name input as `<Modal initialFocusRef>`, because `Modal` focuses its first focusable after a child's `autoFocus`.
  - The source patient is excluded from the list.
  - Under RTL the From → To arrow (and the Back arrow) are mirrored; stacked on a phone the arrow still points down.
  - Errors show the server's own text through `httpErrorMessage` (409: "Target patient already has an active work").
  - Success toasts once (the modal's own toast is gone).
  - **Server**: `wires` is dropped from the preview counts. A visit's wire is a lookup reference, not a record that moves. The total now sums the rows it lists, and aligner batches are listed.
  - The modal is **translated** (`works:transfer.*`) and on the ratchet.
- **FE-F7-12**: the two "Submitted for admin approval" toasts on the Works page are `works:toast.submittedForApproval`.
  - The Clinic pseudo-doctor reads **"Clinic" / "العيادة"**, never "Dr. Clinic". The dead `=== 'Admin'` exception is gone. New **`shared/clinic-doctor.ts`** (`CLINIC_DOCTOR_NAME`, `isClinicDoctorName`, case-insensitive like the citext column) also serves `PatientService.resolveClinicDoctorId` and `BookingForm`'s "Clinic first" sort, which was case-sensitive.
  - The collapsed chevron mirrors under RTL.
  - The estimate and every date on the page format with the app's locale (Western digits, date-only strings on their own day). That takes WorkComponent's sites off FE-F3-3's census.
- **FE-F7-13**: `.toast-message` and `.newWorkError` keep line breaks (`white-space: pre-line`). The work-delete dependency toast now reads as its 12 lines.
- **FE-F7-14**:
  - Each treatment-item editor reports whether its draft differs from what it opened with (`onDirtyChange`, through `WorkDetailsPanel`). A card with a dirty item asks through `common:unsaved.*` before a collapse discards it.
  - The first-active-work auto-expand runs **once per patient**, not on every refetch that changed data, so a refetch no longer collapses cards or unmounts a draft.
  - The item delete confirm says that the item's **lab case** and its history are deleted with it (ON DELETE CASCADE).
  - A cleared item cost stays empty, not `0`.
- **FE-F7-15** (`NewWorkComponent`):
  - Every field write goes through `setField` (functional updater).
  - The form is **seeded once per work**. A refetch, e.g. after a colleague's payment, no longer wipes the edits in progress. The paid total (currency lock, discount cap) and the current doctor are read from the live row instead.
  - A `workId` that is not among this patient's works (moved by a transfer) shows "This work no longer exists" instead of a blank form. An id that does not exist at all was already a 404 from the route loader.
  - `useUnsavedRouteGuard(dirty)`, against a baseline that includes the clinic's default currency, so an untouched new form leaves without asking.
  - The tabs are a `tablist` with `tab` / `tabpanel` wiring.
- **FE-F7-16**:
  - The ⋮ menu has `aria-label`, `aria-haspopup="menu"`, `aria-expanded`, `role="menu"` / `menuitem`. It closes on an outside click, on Escape (focus back on ⋮) and with its card.
  - The search box also matches the work type.
  - The progress bar shows on ortho works only.
- **FE-F7-17**: **`getWorkDetailsList.response`** is fully modeled (`WorkItemRow`: the 23 columns, `lab_status` as `LAB_CASE_STATUSES`). So is **`transferPreview.response`** (`TransferPreview`).
  - `WorkDetail` is `WorkItemRow`; the server's `WorkItem` type is the same type.
  - The lookup props take the lookup contracts' types.
  - All eleven casts are gone: three in WorkComponent, five in WorkDetailsPanel, one in TransferWorkModal, two `postJSON<WorkResponse>` in NewWorkComponent. So is the `as LabStage`.
  - The card's `Work` **is** the contract's `WorkRow`.
- **FE-F7-18**: removed the payment-history *Edit* stub (and its two strings), `WorkResponse` and its `{} as WorkResponse` ×2, the `onSave(result)` payload, the hardcoded `[19, 20, 21]` (→ `ALIGNER_SET_WORK_TYPE_IDS`), the threaded `WORK_STATUS` prop (→ imported from `shared/treatment-taxonomy`), the `getProgressPercentage` / `isAlignerWork` props and the `console.error`. The two ad-hoc `isAdmin`s had already gone with FE-F7-7.

**CLAUDE.md** (the last handover step): the i18n paragraph now lists the catalogs (`navigation`, `patients`, `tasks`, `approvals`), the bells, the approvals history page and the Transfer dialog as translated, `/approvals` in `RTL_ROUTES`, the ratchet's new files, and the exclude list's new entries (`icon`, `contentClassName`, `overlayClassName`, and `mode`, the combobox's enum).

**Route table:** unchanged by F7 (one line in the first session: `-GET /api/patients/type-options`).

### Runtime verification: 150 checks on a throwaway install, 149 passing

The install: the first session's scratchpad (PostgreSQL 18 on :55432, `db:migrate` + `db:setup` + `db:seed:demo`, the env-blanking wrapper with `PORT=3101` and `VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:3101`). Probe data was written through the API and with SQL into that database only: a front-desk user, held edits, a notice, tasks with a since-quit assignee, a discount, a zero duration and a lab case. The PatientManagement checks ran on a production React bundle (`vite build --mode development` + `vite preview`), because dev StrictMode masked FE-F6-3.

- **Bells (F5, FE-F1-10)**: 53.
  - Approvals bell, 11: keyboard open, Tab cycle, Escape back to the bell; Escape with the Approve-all confirm closes only the confirm; approving on a works page refetches `getworks`; a stale approve warns.
  - Tasks bell, 16: typing a snooze date fires nothing, *Set*/Enter commit one request, today is refused; category "None" sends `alertTypeId: null`; the quit assignee shows by name; *Done* refetches the patient's alerts and the task leaves the list.
  - History page and Arabic, 18: the page lists, filters, searches and is RTL; the bells' chrome is Arabic. The 18th check is the one that did not pass: the approvals bell's rows read English in Arabic. That is the server's own summary text, filed below, not an RB4 regression.
  - Front-desk badge and the task history's *Expired* state, 8.
- **Statistics (F5)**, 13: typing a year fires one read; Yearly From/To can be typed into; clearing From fires nothing; `?view=` round-trips and an invalid one falls back; the chart title takes `--text-heading` in dark mode.
- **Demographics (F6)**, 22.
  - Forms, 12: an 11- and a 13-digit phone survive Edit → Save untouched; names are saved trimmed; the Arabic name is `dir="rtl"`; a colleague's change shows on reopening Edit; "+964" becomes "964" in both forms; Add from the Contact tab with an empty name shows the message; no duplicate ids at desktop or 412 px.
  - PatientManagement on the production bundle, 10: a reload shows the current search; Back after an edit elsewhere shows the new row; Show All clears the filters; a deleted patient leaves the combobox in the same session.
- **Works (F7)**, 62.
  - Works page, 34: card phone and alert badge; ⋮ semantics and closing; search by type; progress on ortho only; the history box live and with the discount; PaymentModal's balance; the lab-case delete wording; the cleared cost; the collapse guard (asks, Keep editing, Discard, clean collapse); a forced refetch with changed data keeps the card open and the draft; completing refetches the item list; the 12-line toast.
  - Transfer in Arabic, 15: RTL From → To positions and the mirrored arrow; "العيادة"; the mirrored chevron; focus in the name search; the source patient excluded; the server's 409 text; exactly one toast. An **in-app** return to the target within the 30 s staleTime refetches it and shows the moved work.
  - Work form, 12: a moved `workId` shows "no longer exists"; tab semantics; a works refetch caused by approving a hold of that very work keeps both the typed notes and the old total (seed-once); Cancel and an in-app link are guarded; Save leaves with no prompt; an untouched or defaults-only form leaves with no prompt.
  - A front-desk held delete toasts in Arabic, 1.

Probe mistakes, fixed and re-run, not code defects:
- A click into a date input lands on whichever segment it hits; focus it and type.
- An i18n label regex was wrong.
- The page's first `h1` is the header's clinic name.
- A `visibilitychange` event must bubble to reach React Query's listener, and focus refetch is off app-wide anyway.
- A hand-rolled `pushState` + `popstate` bypasses React Router's blocker (there is no history index); use a real link.
- `page.goto` is a full load, so it proves nothing about cache invalidation; navigate in-app.
- A background dev server dies at the tool's 30-minute limit; start it with the 2-hour one.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)
- `routes/api/alert.routes.ts` quick-add maps a missing `alertTypeId` to the literal **1** ("General"). Whether a fresh install seeds `alert_types` row 1 is unchecked. For F21, or the fresh-install test.
- **Approval texts are English server strings.** In Arabic, the bells, the front-desk badge and `/approvals/history` show `approval_requests.summary` ("Edit work #1", "Delete work #19") and the server's `review_note` ("Target changed since request was submitted") untranslated, beside translated chrome and a translated action label. The fix is to compose the summary on the client from `action_type` + `target_id`, and to key the system notes. 🔵, for a later i18n pass.
- **The work edit form can overwrite a concurrent change.** It sends every field, so a colleague's change made while the form was open (runtime: an approved held total) is overwritten by the value the form opened with. Before RB4 the refetch wiped the user's own edits instead. The fix is to send only the fields that differ from the baseline. ✨, for F17/RE or the next pass over F7's files.

## RD · 2026-10-01 — Phase D: all of F12, F13, F14 and F15

**61 rows closed, none partial**: every F12–F15 row (59: 2 🟠, 23 🟡, 34 🔵), plus **FE-F4-17** (the inner error boundary) and **FE-F0-2** (the reasonless disable, with FE-F12-16). **Phase D is fully remediated.** The work ran over two sessions, split by a usage limit.

**`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, only F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings, **36 files / 293 tests** (+1 test: `galleryAll` and `work.root` are prefixes), contracts **D1 0 / D2 8**, build green. **The route-table snapshot diffed by 13 lines**, all intended, and was accepted:

```
- GET     /api/patients/:personId/timepoints/:tp/images                  (Compare's last reader moved to the gallery)
~ PUT/DELETE /api/patients/:personId/timepoints/:tpCode                  admin|front_desk → +clinical
~ GET/POST   /api/patients/:personId/photos/visibility                   admin|front_desk → +clinical
~ POST upload|folder|rename|delete-batch, DELETE /api/patients/:personId/files   admin|front_desk → +clinical
~ POST    /api/share/stage                                               + timeout(120000ms)
```

**No migration.**

### Owner decisions taken during RD (2026-10-01)

| Finding | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F12-6 | Should `clinical` manage photo sessions and patient files? | **Yes.** All eleven routes above move to `CLINICAL_ROLES`, which also makes the editor sidebar's Upload / Move / Rename work for doctors (FE-F14-9's half). |
| FE-F12-1 | Repair the garbled names already on the clinic volume? | **Dry-run, then apply.** `scripts/repair-upload-names.mjs` was dry-run read-only over the live `C:\clinic1` (20,386 folders) and the TV media folder. It found **0** garbled names: the only two Latin-1 names on the volume (`Brackets estéticos.mp4`, `Invisalign®.mp4`) are genuine. So there was nothing to apply. The script stays for any install that has them. |

### What changed, per finding

**Shared pieces (serve several rows)**
- `query/photos.ts#invalidatePatientPhotos(personId, { works? })` refreshes the session tabs, every gallery (new prefix key `qk.patient.galleryAll`), visibility, working files and the Files tree. With `works` it also refreshes the patient's works (new `qk.work.root()`). Every photo write calls it: session edit and delete, prepare (in the dialog, for all three call sites), editor Remove, render completion (watcher + grid + editor), a session-folder rename in Files, and the *Rename folder* dialog. **FE-F12-3, FE-F14-2**, and F13's reader-side note.
- `shared/photo-session-folder.ts`: `sessionFolderName`, `RESERVED_PATIENT_FOLDERS` (`OPG`, `OPGIMG`, `CBCT`) and `folderOwner`. The server's `timepointFolderName` delegates to it, and the rename guard and the editor's *Rename folder* both classify folders with it.
- `shared/photo-views.ts#VIEW_LABELS` / `viewLabel` is the one label table. The slideshow's `PHOTO_TYPE_LABELS` and the editor's copy now read it (FE-F15-12).
- `middleware/upload.ts#createUpload()` pins `defParamCharset: 'utf8'`. All seven multer instances use it, and an ESLint rule bans a bare `multer()` in `routes/`.
- `key={personId}` on Compare and the slideshow (**FE-F13-12, FE-F15-8**).
- `useFloatingMenu` is now keyed on the anchor's coordinates, so an inline `{ x, y }` cannot loop. It now also serves the session kebab, the editor's slot menu and the slideshow's *Apply* menu.
- `slideshow/useFullscreen` gained a fixed-overlay fallback (`isOverlay`, Escape leaves it). The slideshow player opts out with `{ fallback: false }`.
- CLAUDE.md, *Conventions*: two new lines, on `createUpload()` and on gallery-sourced photo URLs plus `invalidatePatientPhotos`. The LocalSend line about `describeFetchError` is now true.

**F12 — grid, files**
- **FE-F12-1**: the shared multer factory, plus the repair script (above). TV, WebCeph and aligner PDFs get UTF-8 names too. `video.routes.ts`'s deliberate `_` replacement is unchanged.
- **FE-F12-2**: the lightbox decides by `(pointer: coarse)`, not by "has `navigator.share`". A desktop gets *Send Message*; a touch-first device gets the OS share sheet and the blob prefetch. The LocalSend button is now titled "Send to a device or Telegram".
- **FE-F12-4**: the hidden-from-patient marks are derived from the visibility query on every render. A toggle writes the query cache (`setQueryData`), and the lightbox reads the cache synchronously. Clinical can read and toggle them (decision above).
- **FE-F12-5**:
  - The session edit checks the target folder BEFORE writing. If the session's own folder exists and the target name does too, the edit answers 409 "A folder named … already exists", with no row change.
  - A rename that fails afterwards reverts the row and answers 409.
  - *Delete everything* moves the originals to `clinic1/.trash/{personId}/{stamp}/` (`softDelete`). `hardDelete` had no other caller and is deleted. The delete dialog's wording now says so.
- **FE-F12-7, FE-F14-11, FE-F13-7**: the grid's centre cell shows the branding logo (not a lightbox slide any more), or the clinic name. The editor's centre cell does the same, and its empty slots show a neutral camera mark.
- **FE-F12-8**: the tab strip stays during loads (`placeholderData: keepPreviousData`) and errors, and an error has Retry. (c) is FE-F4-17's fix: `ContentRenderer` wraps the page body in a `RouteErrorBoundary`, so a failed tab chunk keeps the sidebar. The outer boundary is now labelled "patient record", not "Patient Portal".
- **FE-F12-9**: a synchronous `busyRef` guards rename, new folder, upload, drop and both deletes.
- **FE-F12-10**: the date shortcuts use the date string as-is (no `new Date()`). Appointments are limited to today and earlier, five unique days, newest first. Visits are now newest first too (they were the five oldest). The client-side "Patient name is required" check is gone; the server's `needsName` covers it, and the `patientInfo` prop with it.
- **FE-F12-11**: `deleteNativeTimePoint` also deletes the session's `private_photos` rows, in one transaction.
- **FE-F12-12**: the edit and delete toasts use `httpErrorMessage`. The edit causes one refetch (invalidate only), and `loadTimepoints`/`TODO(phase3)` are gone.
- **FE-F12-13**:
  - (a) A file tile is no longer a `role="button"` around buttons: its NAME is the keyboard/AT button (a checkbox in select mode).
  - (b) The session menu uses `useFloatingMenu`.
  - (c) The preview's ←/→ ignore focused media and inputs.
- **FE-F12-14**: the text preview sends `Range: bytes=0-199999` with an `AbortController`, and stops reading at the cap. Video and audio go through a `MediaPreview` that empties its `src` on close.
- **FE-F12-15**:
  - `photoVisibilityList` is a closed `{ tp, name }` array.
  - The modals take the contract's `TimepointRow` (the `LegacyTimepoint` adapter is gone).
  - The `FileListing` casts are gone.
  - `PhotoSessionDialog` reads the typed `photoDates` (`description` was always `''` and is dropped).
- **FE-F12-16**: the dead checkbox CSS, the `console.error`, `fileHelpers.errorMessage` and the reasonless disable are gone (FE-F0-2 closed).

**F13 — Compare**
- **FE-F13-1, -5, -8**: each selected session's gallery is read through `useQueries` + `combine` (kept for referential stability). URLs are `/DolImgs/{name}?v={mtime}` from it, and a view is available only when both galleries have its file. `timepointImagesQuery`, `qk.patient.timepointImages`, the contract, the route, `PatientService.getPatientTimePointImages` and `getTimePointImgs` are deleted. Their one other reader, the chair kiosk's payload builder, now uses `getImageSizes` too.
- **FE-F13-2**: auto-select takes the first and last session by date, with no `tp_code > 0` filter.
- **FE-F13-3**: the pair is the selection in the list's date order, so Before/After and the drawing order both follow dates.
- **FE-F13-4**:
  - The engine has `clear()`, called whenever the selection is not a complete, loadable pair.
  - A failed load clears the canvas.
  - The snapshot carries `sourceKey`, and Save/Share are enabled only while it equals the selection's key.
- **FE-F13-6**: `exportBlob()` is a JPEG at 0.9 via `toBlob`. *Auto* caps the long edge at 4,096 px, with *Full resolution* as an explicit option. The stage upload gets 120 s on both sides. `dataURItoBlob` is gone.
- **FE-F13-7**:
  - The watermark is the branding logo, recoloured locally into white or black silhouettes (or kept in its own colours; an opaque logo starts in its own colours).
  - With no logo set there is no watermark, no toggles, and no *Logo* target.
  - The old two-asset toggle and its unhandled rejection are gone (FE-F13-15b).
- **FE-F13-9**: both photos load in parallel. A newer load releases the older images, which cancels their downloads, and there is no 10 s cap.
- **FE-F13-10**: a change of view resets position, zoom, rotation and crop.
- **FE-F13-11**: dates are parsed locally (`parseLocalDate`) and formatted `en-GB`.
- **FE-F13-13**:
  - (a) The stepper wraps.
  - (b) On a coarse pointer the handles are 22 px, and two fingers on the selected image pinch-zoom.
  - (c) There is a fullscreen overlay fallback.
- **FE-F13-14**:
  - (a) The chips show a focus ring.
  - (b) The stepper is an `<ol>` with `aria-current="step"`.
  - (c) The canvas is `role="img"` with a label naming the view and both sessions.
  - (d) Escape is ignored while a dialog is open.
- **FE-F13-15**: (a) the load toast names the session and the view; (c) the panel shows the session-list error with Retry.
- **FE-F13-16**: `dragRef`, `DragState.abort`, `EngineSnapshot.version`, the cast, the duplicate predicate and five exports are gone.

**F14 — editor, share dialogs**
- **FE-F14-1**: LocalSend devices are a React Query read (`localsendDevicesQuery`, `qk.localsend.devices`) made on mount. *Rescan* reads `?rescan=1`, then once more after 1.5 s.
- **FE-F14-2**:
  - *Remove* writes `null` into the cached gallery before invalidating, so the hydration cannot re-seed the slot.
  - HYDRATE is authoritative: a view absent from the gallery is cleared, unless it holds a live edit.
- **FE-F14-3**:
  - The editor takes the session's name and date from `timepointsQuery` by code, and shows a notice for an unknown code. The URL carries the code only.
  - `/render` accepts `tpCode`, which is authoritative: the row supplies the name, date and folder, and an unknown code is a 404. The legacy name+date shape is still accepted.
  - The view delete resolves the folder from the row; `tpName`/`tpDate` left the contract.
- **FE-F14-4**:
  - The watcher is registered BEFORE the POST, keyed by a client `jobId` that the server echoes in `photos_rendered`. It waits up to 3 s for the SSE stream, and is cancelled if the POST fails.
  - The event carries `problems: [{ view, reason }]`. A reason is curated text, and a 404 reads "Its original photo was moved or renamed".
  - The event is a contract, `renderedEvent`, parsed with `safeParse` everywhere.
- **FE-F14-5**: owned folders (another session's, `OPG`/`OPGIMG`/`CBCT`) are listed apart, under a warning, and renaming one asks first. The server refuses those renames with 409 `SESSION_FOLDER` / `RESERVED_FOLDER` unless `force` is sent. Files asks and resends with `force` too.
- **FE-F14-6**:
  - Telegram's poll keeps running in a hidden tab (`refetchIntervalInBackground`).
  - A 404 is final ("Lost track of the send…") for both transports.
  - Finished Telegram jobs are kept 15 min, and running ones are only swept after 6 h.
  - Closing either dialog mid-send hands the share to `services/share-watch.ts`, which toasts how it ended. The dialogs say that closing does not stop the send.
- **FE-F14-7**: `localsend.service.ts#describeUnreachable` maps a timeout, a refused or unreachable connection, and other codes onto copy that names the device. A prepare-upload timeout reads as "didn't accept the files — nobody tapped Accept…".
- **FE-F14-8**: the editor uses `useUnsavedRouteGuard` (+ `allowNextNavigation`) and `useConfirm`. Its two bespoke modals and their CSS are gone.
- **FE-F14-9**: `httpErrorMessage` throughout the sidebar and the rename dialog.
- **FE-F14-10**:
  - *Move from card* (Chromium) asks once before its first move.
  - *Upload (copy)* is always offered.
  - The session dialog says that moving deletes from the card.
- **FE-F14-11**: the logo/name rows above. Telegram's source list now comes from `GOOGLE_CONTACT_ACCOUNTS`, and the phone is sent as stored (no client `964` prefix). The registry's labels are still this clinic's; that is for F16/F22.
- **FE-F14-12**: saved slots show the 480 px thumbnail.
- **FE-F14-13**:
  - (a) Click a sidebar photo (`aria-pressed`), then a slot, to place it; Escape un-picks.
  - (b) *Restore original* and *Remove* are toolbar buttons too. The slot menu opens by keyboard (`anchorFrom`) and takes focus.
  - (c) The divider is a focusable separator with a value, and ←/→ resize it. `pointercancel` now ends a drag.
  - (d) The progress bars are `role="progressbar"`, and the status is a live region.
  - (e) Every share field has a label.
- **FE-F14-14**: the editor's hydration and the sidebar read the typed gallery and listing; `FileEntryLite` is gone.
- **FE-F14-15**: the flag docblock is fixed, and `defaultFlipV` / `OutputDims` / `SlotGridLines` are no longer exported. The owner's `.env` still sets the dead `NATIVE_PHOTO_EDITOR_ENABLED`, which is harmless.

**F15 — slideshow**
- **FE-F15-1**: hidden controls get `visibility: hidden`, switched after the fade, so they take no taps and no tab stops. Any key brings them back.
- **FE-F15-2**: `useWakeLock` clears its ref on the sentinel's `release` event, and releases a lock that resolves after teardown.
- **FE-F15-3**:
  - (a) `first`/`latest` resolve to the earliest/newest session that HAS the view.
  - (b) A slide whose refs resolve to the same photo is skipped and reported ("compares two sessions, and this patient has only one…").
  - (c) A one-session template becomes `first` only when that session is the patient's earliest; otherwise `latest`. The save dialog spells the mapping out.
- **FE-F15-4**:
  - Galleries are React Query reads (`useQueries` + `combine`).
  - The timeline stores identity. URLs, versions and captions are resolved every render, and a gone photo is marked, skipped on Play and reported.
  - Saved presentations rebuild against the current sessions and fresh galleries, and count what is gone. `Apply` reads the galleries with `staleTime: 0`.
- **FE-F15-5**: TIFF/HEIC folder photos play through `thumb=2048`; their tiles use `thumb=240`.
- **FE-F15-6**: annotation points are fractions of the displayed photo rectangle (the union of the slide's images' contain boxes), repainted on frame resizes. Each pointer draws its own stroke.
- **FE-F15-7**: template writes invalidate `qk.slideshow.all()`.
- **FE-F15-9**:
  - (a) PageDown/PageUp.
  - (b) Only the primary mouse button navigates.
  - (c) The chip grip is a button, and ←/→ move the slide.
  - (d) The *Apply* menu uses `useFloatingMenu`.
  - (e) Slide changes are announced in a live region. Focus moves into the player and back to *Play*. The swatches are named.
- **FE-F15-10**: tiles, chips and the ghost use thumbnails.
- **FE-F15-11**: the slideshow's `Timepoint` is the contract row, and the stored timeline is parsed with zod (`storedSlide`); bad entries are dropped.
- **FE-F15-12**: `isLogoImage`, `labelForImageName`, the legacy `readSession` fold, the stale docblocks (and the two backend LOCAL-ONLY ones from *Found along the way*) and the hand-written `useMemo` are gone. Labels are shared.

**Also fixed along the way (F13's *Found along the way*)**: the Works page's smile photo now uses the gallery entry (any case, a 240 px thumbnail, versioned). The chair kiosk's images carry `?v=`.

### Runtime verification: 80/80 on a throwaway install

PostgreSQL 18 was copied from F14's scratchpad (port 55432, `Asia/Baghdad`) and built with `db:migrate` + `db:setup` ("Bright Smile Orthodontics") + `db:seed:demo`. A wrapper blanked all 100 `.env` keys and pointed `MACHINE_PATH` at an empty scratch folder. Express ran on `:3101` and Vite on `:5273`. LocalSend and Telegram were exercised through `page.route` mocks (both are off on the throwaway). The live volume was read only, by the repair dry-run.

| Area | Checks | Covers |
|---|---:|---|
| Grid + Files | 19/19 | FE-F12-1, -2, -3a, -4, -5 (409 + row unchanged + trash), -7, -8a/b, -9, -11, -12, -13a, -14; FE-F14-5 in Files |
| Editor | 18/18 | FE-F14-2 (slot + grid), -3 (title from row, body has `tpCode`/`jobId`, no duplicate session, unknown code), -4 (instant failure toast in 0.3 s, by name), -5 (lists + confirm), -8 (shared guard, post-save navigation), -11, -12, -13a/b/c |
| Share dialogs | 10/10 | FE-F14-1, -6a/b/c, -11 (registry labels), -13d/e |
| Compare | 14/14 | FE-F13-1 (new `?v` after an in-place re-render), -2, -3, -4 (incomplete + failed load), -5, -6 (a JPEG, 47 KB on demo photos), -7 (no logo, then branding logo + tone), -8, -13a (412 px), -14b/c, -15a |
| Slideshow | 17/17 | FE-F15-1 (top-right is the gesture layer), -2 (wake-lock mock), -3a/b, -4a/b, -5, -6 (ink follows the photo after a Reel toggle), -7, -9a/d/e, -10 |
| Patient change | 2/2 | FE-F13-12, FE-F15-8 (a delayed gallery read never lands under another patient) |

Probe mistakes corrected along the way:
- The envelope's `timestamp` was compared; the DB confirmed the row.
- The app header was read instead of the editor's top bar.
- An accessible name was matched exactly, although it includes the icon glyph.
- A fresh context has an empty sessionStorage timeline.

**One real defect was found by runtime and fixed before the gate**: *Apply* read the cached galleries, so a photo removed in another tab within the 30 s staleTime was not reported; it now reads fresh. A gate failure was also fixed: the LocalSend rethrows lacked `cause`.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)

- **`db:setup` warns that `CLINIC_MESSAGE_NAME_AR` and `whatsapp_group_name` still hold this clinic's values on a fresh install**, and that no default work currency is set. This came from setup's own output; for **F21**/**F22**.
- **The Google contact-account labels are still this clinic's** ("Dr. Shwan Phone"); the Telegram dialog now reads the registry. For **F16**/**F22**.
- **`clinic1/.trash` is still write-only**, and *Delete everything* now feeds it too. A restore/purge screen is for **F22** (F12's note).

## RC2 · 2026-09-30 — the rest of Phase C: F10's other 🟡, the 🔵 of F10 + F11, and RC1's partial rows

The session RC1 queued. **25 rows closed, none left partial**: F10's five remaining 🟡 (FE-F10-3, -4, -8, -9, -11), RC1's ten partial rows (FE-F10-5, -6, -10, -16, -17, -18, -19, FE-F11-9, -11, FE-F2-8), and every other 🔵 of F10 and F11 (FE-F10-12, -13, -14, -15, -20, FE-F11-8, -12, -13, -14, -15). **Phase C is now fully remediated**: no F10 or F11 row is open.

**`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, only F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings, **36 files / 292 tests**, contracts **D1 0 / D2 8**, build green. **The route-table snapshot diffed by exactly one line**, and the diff was accepted:

```
- GET     /api/calendar/stats  [validate(query)]
```

**One migration, data only**: `1789460600000_pin-legacy-doctor-colours.sql`, see FE-F10-11. No DDL, so no Supabase half; `cdc_capture` replicates its two UPDATEs like any Employee Settings save. It ran clean on a fresh install, where it is a no-op.

### Owner decisions taken during RC2 (2026-09-29)

| Finding | Question | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| FE-F10-13 | Who counts as a "calendar doctor"? The booking lists, legend and daily board used the `get_appointments` flag; the calendar filter and the server used position = Doctor. | **Position = Doctor** (active employees). Every list reads `/api/doctors`; the server keeps its rule. The edit form still shows an appointment's departed doctor as "(current)". |
| FE-F10-11 | `doctorColors.ts` coloured employee ids 1 and 7. How does this clinic keep its two colours once the literals go? | **Guarded data migration**: it writes the two hexes only where the id **and** the name match **and** no colour is set yet. |

### What changed, per finding

**F10's 🟡**
- **FE-F10-3**: a holiday no longer hides its appointments. The week grid renders a holiday column's bookings (no covering sash) and its header reads "Holiday · 1 appt"; the month cell keeps its count.
- **FE-F10-4**: the range and month factories carry `placeholderData: keepPreviousData`. The toolbar and zoom slider stay mounted and enabled during a load, and a spinner in the toolbar (`aria-busy`) replaces the full-page "Loading Calendar...".
- **FE-F10-5** (completes RC1): the client pages months from the 1st (`monthStep`), so Jan 31 → Next is February. Switching to Month opens the visible week's month.
- **FE-F10-6** (completes RC1): the `/month` read computes the month's own stats, doctor-filtered, and the header rounds the percentage. `GET /api/calendar/stats` (always one week), `getCalendarStats`, `calendarStatsQuery` and `getWeekEnd` are gone. The shared slot-settings read lives in `CalendarViewService`.
- **FE-F10-8**: a drop on a past slot is refused on the client, and the server now refuses one too (`PAST_SLOT`, mapped by `bookingError`). A move to another day sends the WhatsApp confirmation, like the edit form. The drag payload is the appointment id, not a position.
- **FE-F10-9**: Update navigates once. Back and Cancel fall back to the patient's appointment list when there is no in-app history (`ContentRenderer#goBackOr`), so a pasted link never leaves the app.
- **FE-F10-10** (completes RC1): the calendar's holiday writes refresh `qk.adminLookups.table('tblHolidays')`, and Settings → Holidays' writes refresh `qk.calendar.all()`.
- **FE-F10-11**: no staff or schedule literals are left.
  - `doctorColors.ts` has no id table. A doctor without `appointment_color` is neutral everywhere, including the Employee Settings picker ("None (neutral)").
  - The picker's `14:00`/`14:30` extras and its 12:00–13:30 / 21:00–22:30 fallbacks are gone. "Extended" means the `EarlySlots`/`LateSlots` options, exactly as on the grid, and none when they are unset.

**F10's 🔵**
- **FE-F10-12**: the extended slots render once, in time order. An all-booked extended set shows without a toggle.
- **FE-F10-13**: one definition, per the owner's decision above.
  - `/api/doctors` returns active Doctors in the Settings sort order, each with its `appointment_color` (`staff.contract#doctors`). It feeds the booking forms, the calendar filter and legend, and the daily board (`useAppointmentDoctors`).
  - `AppointmentService.verifyDoctor` requires an active Doctor for a new booking or a doctor change, while an edit that keeps a since-departed doctor stays valid (`INVALID_DOCTOR`, translated).
  - The edit form lists that doctor as "<name> (current)", using the `DrName` the by-id read now carries.
  - Employee Settings shows a swatch only for calendar doctors.
- **FE-F10-14**: new `roleCaps().manageLookups` (admin + front desk, the mount's own tier; pinned in `role-registry.test.ts`). The day menu offers the holiday actions only to those roles. A failed appointments-on-date check now shows in the holiday modal instead of vanishing.
- **FE-F10-15**: new `hooks/useFloatingMenu.ts`, shared by both menus.
  - `anchorFrom()` places a keyboard-opened menu under its element, and the six `KeyboardEvent as unknown as MouseEvent` casts are gone.
  - Menus are clamped to the viewport, take focus, move with the arrow keys, and return focus on Escape.
  - The day header opens its menu by click or Enter, and the menu gains "Open day view". The month grid's click panel has the same button, so day view is no longer double-click-only.
  - The stray "0" is gone.
- **FE-F10-16** (completes RC1): the four client sites parse dates with `parseLocalDate`.
- **FE-F10-17** (completes RC1): no English literal reaches the translated booking files.
  - `bookingError` maps `PAST_SLOT` and `INVALID_DOCTOR` too, and the WhatsApp fallbacks are catalog strings.
  - The picker's month buttons are named and mirror in RTL. Its tiles use the new `formatters#formatClockTime` (12-hour plus the language's marker).
- **FE-F10-18** (completes RC1): no hand-written types or casts over contracted reads are left.
  - New `BookingForm.tsx` holds the ~180 lines the two forms duplicated. `AppointmentForm` and `EditAppointmentForm` are thin wrappers, and `BookingForm.tsx` is in the i18n ratchet.
  - The void PUT is a plain `putJSON`. The picker's types derive from the contract, `CalendarHeader` imports its types, and the four `console.error` calls are gone.
  - `qk.calendar.availability(startDate, endDate)` / `slots(date)` take the real parameters.
- **FE-F10-19** (completes RC1): dead code removed.
  - Selection mode (`initialDate`/`initialViewMode`/`mode`/`onSlotSelect`/`selectedSlot`, `CalendarMode`, the month grid's `mode`), `TINT_MODE`, the slot's "＋" mark and its `role="button"`.
  - The dead CSS. `.calendar-error` and its actions are now styled.
- **FE-F10-20**: all three deletes (calendar appointment, calendar holiday, patient list) go through `useConfirm`, toast success and carry an in-flight guard; the bespoke modals and their CSS are gone. Narrowing the window forces one day without persisting it. `AnalogClock` lost its `exhaustive-deps` memo, so React Compiler handles it.

**F11's 🔵**
- **FE-F11-8**: new `utils/appointmentsDate.ts` (`rememberAppointmentsDate`/`appointmentsPath`).
  - A board following today is not pinned in `sessionStorage`, so the header and the Dashboard tile open today. A picked date still reopens.
  - A board showing today rolls to the new day at midnight, while a picked date stays put.
  - An unknown `?dr=` reads "All doctors" and is dropped from the URL.
- **FE-F11-9** (completes RC1): `StatsCards`' `cards` variant and its CSS, the `/appointments` route's calendar-CSS import, Vite's `/sse` proxy and the stale comments are gone.
- **FE-F11-11** (completes RC1): the accessibility gaps are closed.
  - The header's weekday uses the shared explicit-language formatter (سبت, not السبت), and the day chevrons mirror in RTL.
  - The search box has a label, and the mobile toggle is a labelled group with `aria-pressed`.
  - The doctor listbox opens by key, moves with the arrows, and returns focus on Escape.
- **FE-F11-12**: `useAppointments` splits `initialLoading` (skeletons) from `busy` (a click in flight, or another day's placeholder). A background refetch neither dims nor blanks the lists.
  - `useAppointmentsSync` coalesces a burst per day: three writes from another desk cause one read.
  - Refresh from another date reads today once.
  - The flash timer is single and cleared.
- **FE-F11-13**: Videos.
  - The player uses `ModalHeader`, and closing it cancels the stream: the source is set in an effect and emptied on cleanup, not a `src` prop that StrictMode's double setup would leave empty.
  - Delete uses `useConfirm` with a guard.
  - The upload form has `unsavedGuard`, and closing it mid-upload aborts the request (no toast, no video).
- **FE-F11-14**: an upload appends to a pending playlist and a delete removes only that file from it. Neither discards the draft any more.
  - The tab's unsaved flag covers the playlist.
  - *Seconds per picture* can be cleared while typing; it keeps the last value on blur.
- **FE-F11-15**: the kiosk.
  - (a) The visit date is parsed locally.
  - (b) The server keeps a `Set` of streams per chair, so two kiosks on one chair both stay connected and both get the patient.
  - (c) The idle clock follows a resize.
  - (d) On a CLOSED stream the kiosk reloads only once `/health/basic` answers. It probes 15 s after the error, then at growing intervals (15 s, 30 s, then every 60 s), so a server that is still down never strands it on a proxy's error page.
- **FE-F2-8** (completes RC1): `qk.whatsapp` has a real root (`['whatsapp']`), and `keys.test.ts` pins it.

### Runtime verification: 65/65 on a throwaway demo database

A throwaway PostgreSQL 16 cluster was set to `Asia/Baghdad` and built with `db:migrate`. `db:setup` set it up as "Bright Smile Orthodontics", and `db:seed:demo` added the demo data. It used scratch `MACHINE_PATH`/TV directories, with every sink and WhatsApp off. Fixtures:
- a booking on the demo holiday;
- a flagged Assistant, and a departed doctor with a future booking;
- early/late slot options.

Express and Vite ran as separate processes, and Playwright used `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium`. Page clocks were pinned wherever "today" matters. The database, role, `.env`, probes and recordings were deleted afterwards.

| Area | Checks | Covers |
|---|---:|---|
| Calendar + booking | 36/36 | FE-F10-3, -4, -5, -6, -8 (server `PAST_SLOT`, id payload, past drop sends nothing, day move sends WhatsApp), -9, -11, -12, -13 (Assistant 400, `/api/doctors` shape, "(current)", booking list), -14, -15a/c/d, -17, -20; `/api/calendar/stats` gone |
| Daily board | 15/15 | FE-F11-12 (3 writes → 1 read, no dim/skeletons, Refresh reads once, filter reads nothing), -8a/b/c, -11a/b/e, the Arabic ص/م marker |
| Videos, TV tab, kiosk | 14/14 | FE-F11-13 (ModalHeader, stream cancelled on close with 0 bytes after, unsaved guard, upload abort, delete), -14 (upload and delete keep the pending order, seconds box), -15a/b/c/d (server down: probes at 15/30 s and stays; server back: one reload, reconnected) |

Probe mistakes corrected along the way:
- Headless Chromium runs no native drag session, so the drags were dispatched as synthetic HTML5 events.
- `/calendar` had been opened by `goto` with no history, which correctly takes the pasted-link fallback.
- The playlist-row delete button had been mis-selected.
- A kiosk wait was too short.

**One real defect was found by runtime and fixed before commit**: the first version of the FE-F11-13 player fix emptied a prop-driven `src` on cleanup, and StrictMode's setup → cleanup → setup left the dev player blank.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)

- **An unknown `/api/*` path answers 200 with the SPA shell**, not a JSON 404 (seen when probing the removed `/api/calendar/stats`). This is pre-existing, from the web router's catch-all. File with a backend/routing pass.
- **Vite's dev proxy keeps a proxied SSE response open when Express dies**, so in dev the board and kiosk sit on "Live" until the silence watchdog fires. This is dev-only: Caddy/the tunnel answer with a 502, which is the path verified above.
- **The WhatsApp confirmation's partial-failure warning shows the server's `message` verbatim** (English on the Arabic form). File with **F16**.

## RC1 · 2026-09-29 — Phase C's three 🟠, the owner rules, and F11's 🟡

The first Phase C slice: all three 🟠 (FE-F10-1, FE-F10-2, FE-F11-1), the four findings that needed an owner decision, and every F11 🟡. Along the way it took three older findings the work passed through (FE-F1-7, FE-F3-8, FE-F2-8's appointments half). **15 fixed, 10 partial**; each partial row names what is left. **Gate green**: 0 errors and F0's 4 warnings; vitest **36 files / 291 tests** (+2 files, +12 tests); contracts D1 0 / D2 8; both typechecks and the build clean. **38 of 38 runtime checks** passed on a throwaway database (below).

### Owner decisions taken at the start of RC1 (2026-09-29)

| Finding | Decision |
|---|---|
| FE-F10-2 | **Build the grid from the appointments.** Days come from the date range (Sat–Thu, holidays marked). Rows are the configured times, plus any time that has an appointment. The `calendar` table stays only for the booking picker's availability, and Regenerate stops deleting past rows. |
| FE-F10-7 | **Enforce `MaxAppointmentsPerSlot` per clinic slot**, on the server, under a lock in the booking's transaction. |
| FE-F11-4 | **Refuse future days, allow past days.** A future day's check-in, seat and dismiss are refused by the server and not offered by the board. Past days stay open for corrections, and undo is never blocked. |
| FE-F11-16 | **Default `VideosPath` under `MACHINE_PATH`** (`clinic1/ovideos/`) when the row is missing, and make it editable in Settings. |

### What changed, per finding

- **FE-F11-1 🟠 — the stamps.** `getDailyAppointmentsOptimized` now sends the three stamps as 24-hour `HH:MM:SS` (trimmed; the columns are plain `time`, which legacy rows may carry with fractions). `apptime` is sent as 24-hour `HH:MM`. `AppointmentCard.formatTime` turns both into `h:mm` plus the active language's marker. The contract now declares every field the card reads, with a strict 24-hour `clockTime`, so a regression to 12-hour fails loud. The same bug had already been fixed once for the appointment PDF (the comment in `appointment-queries.ts#getAppointmentsWithPhones`).
- **FE-F10-1 🟠 — Edit from the calendar.** `EditAppointmentForm` no longer reads router state. It always reads `appointmentByIdQuery` (`refetchOnMount: 'always'`) and seeds from the contract type, so both the Back/Forward re-seed and the `ExistingAppointment` cast are gone. `CalendarContextMenu` and `PatientAppointments` stop passing state. `appointmentById.response` now declares the fields the form seeds from, and the server's `AppointmentSummaryRow` is made honestly nullable (`app_detail`, `dr_id`). The dead `app_date`/`person_id` aliases went from `CalendarViewService`, `calendar.contract` and `calendar.types`. A save also invalidates `qk.appointments.all()`.
- **FE-F10-2 🟠 — the grid source.** New `getAppointmentsInRange` (one range scan of `ix_tblappointments_appdate_optimized`), plus `buildGridDays` for `/range` and `buildMonthDays` for `/month` in `CalendarViewService`:
  - **Columns.** Every working day of the span is a column, past ones included.
  - **Rows.** The visible configured times plus every time that has an appointment, so a walk-in at 15:07, a booking in a hidden early/late row, and one at a deleted time all render.
  - **`full`.** A slot is `full` against the clinic-wide count, even when the grid is filtered to one doctor.
  - **Stats.** The week stats come from the configured rows of the non-holiday days and follow the doctor filter.
  - **Regenerate.** `fillCalendar` no longer deletes past rows.
  - **The horizon.** The hourly `noteCalendarRange` check now **extends** the slot table when it runs short, instead of only logging. The fill is additive and idempotent.
  - **Removed.** `/api/calendar/week` and `calendar.contract#week` had no caller (FE-F10-19's server half); the route table diff is below.
  - **Tests.** Seven new unit tests (`CalendarViewService.test.ts`) pin the four ways an appointment used to drop out, the clinic-wide `full` rule, holidays in the stats, and month ends.
- **FE-F10-7 — the slot limit.** `assertSlotHasRoom` takes `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('appointment-slot:' || ts::text))`, counts the slot and refuses with `SLOT_FULL`, inside the same transaction as the insert. `createAppointment` now accepts the caller's transaction. On update, only a move into **another** slot is checked, so an appointment in a slot that is already over the limit stays editable. Walk-ins (arrival minute) are exempt. On the client, the new shared `bookingError()` maps `HOLIDAY_CONFLICT` / `APPOINTMENT_CONFLICT` / `SLOT_FULL` for both booking forms. The edit form had no mapping before (FE-F10-17's first item). A `SLOT_FULL` refusal also refreshes the picker's cached slots. The calendar drag shows the server's English message on the English calendar.
- **FE-F11-4 — other days.** `updatePresent` refuses a forward step when `app_day` is after today (`FUTURE_APPOINTMENT`, 400). `undoAppointmentState` is untouched. The board withholds check-in, seat and dismiss on a future day, and the hook toasts the translated refusal if one gets through.
- **FE-F11-16 — the videos folder.** `defaultVideosPath()` (`clinic-paths.ts`), and `getVideosPath()` returns the option or that default. The two video reads bind the path instead of `CROSS JOIN`ing an options row that might not exist; that join made the list empty on a fresh install. The upload creates the folder, and it no longer caches the path for the life of the process. `db:setup` gains a **Videos folder** step that writes the row when it is absent, so it appears in Settings → General.
- **FE-F11-2 — action vs. read errors.** A failed state change is now a translated toast and never becomes the page's `error`. A failed read keeps the header and shows a translated message with **Retry**. The four `console.error` catches and the English fallbacks are gone (FE-F11-11(d)).
- **FE-F11-3 + FE-F11-7 + FE-F1-7 — the SSE channel.** Both singletons are now instances of one `SseChannel` (`services/sse-channel.ts`). The channel retries a CLOSED stream with backoff (`CLOSED_STREAM_RETRY_DELAYS_MS`) while anyone holds it. It also reopens an OPEN stream that has delivered nothing for `SILENT_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS` (60 s). Both server broadcasters now send a named `event: ping` every 25 s in place of the invisible `:\n\n` comment. The chair kiosk runs the same silence check on its own EventSource. Four unit tests (`sse-channel.test.ts`) use a fake EventSource and fake timers. `docs/whatsapp-integration.md` and CLAUDE.md now describe the ping and the channel.
- **FE-F11-5 — TV uploads.** New `timeouts.upload` (10 min, `TIMEOUTS.UPLOAD`) on `POST /api/tv-display/media`, and the client's `postFormData` uses the same 10-minute budget.
- **FE-F11-6 — identity.**
  - **Kiosk.** The welcome line and the printed QR footer read `CLINIC_NAME` through `brandingQuery`. With no name set, the kiosk says just "Welcome".
  - **TV page.** The public TV page's `<title>` and placeholder are filled per request from `CLINIC_NAME`, HTML-escaped.
  - **TV settings.** The TV tab drops the daemon path and the source-file list, and points IT to `docs/tv-display.md`.
  - **QR address.** `config.urls.publicUrlIsDefault` flows through the QR endpoint (`usesDefaultAddress`, contracted), and the share modal warns when the link is the built-in domain.
- **FE-F11-17 + FE-F10-10 + FE-F2-8 — keys and invalidation.**
  - **Keys.** `qk.appointments` has a real root: `all()`, with `daily` = `['appointments','daily',d]`. `keys.test.ts` pins the prefix.
  - **Loader.** `dailyAppointmentsLoader` now `fetchQuery`s into the cache (`staleTime: 0`) instead of returning `initialData`, which React Query ignored for a cached key.
  - **Writes.** The two quick check-ins, both booking forms, the patient-list delete and the calendar's reschedule/delete all invalidate `qk.appointments.all()`. The calendar's two also invalidate the patient.
  - **Read factory.** There is a new `dailyAppointmentsQuery` factory, so `useAppointments` no longer defines a key outside `queries.ts`.
- **FE-F3-8 + FE-F11-10 — casts.** The daily board, its hook and its loader use the contract's `DailyAppointmentRow` / `DailyAppointmentStats`. Three hand-written row types, two `AppointmentStats`, `useLoaderData() as LoaderData`, four array casts and two status casts are gone. `Videos` uses `VideoRow` and the contracted QR type.
- **FE-F11-9 (partial).** `getCurrentStatus`, `isWaiting`, `data-status`, `.waiting`, `isCheckedIn`, `getStats`, the English `emptyMessage` default, the `actions.checkedIn` and `errors.failedToLoad` strings and two stale comments are gone. CLAUDE.md's appointments note now describes the 24-hour times instead of selectors that never existed.

The route table diff, reviewed before `vitest -u`:

```
~ POST    /api/tv-display/media  [timeout(600000ms) -> uploadMediaFiles]
- GET     /api/calendar/week  [validate(query)]
```

No migration and no Supabase DDL. The new `options` row is written by `db:setup` only, and only when it is absent.

### Runtime verification — 38 of 38 checks passed

Same recipe as F11: a throwaway PostgreSQL 16 database (`db:migrate` → `db:setup` as "Bright Smile Orthodontics" → `db:seed:demo`), scratch `MACHINE_PATH` and TV folders, sinks and WhatsApp off, and Express and Vite as separate processes. The fixtures were the evening appointments and the 19:52 waiting patient again, a walk-in at 15:07:23, and bookings on Sunday 4 Oct at a hidden late slot (20:30) and at a non-configured time (18:15). The late slots were configured with extended rows hidden. The probes, `.env`, the database, the role and the scratch data were removed afterwards, and no screenshots were taken. Five first-run failures were probe mistakes, rerun and passed:
- the Edit probe clicked a past appointment, which the calendar never offers to edit;
- the four "concurrent" bookings fetched four CSRF tokens that invalidated each other;
- `/tv-display` was fetched through Vite, which does not proxy it.

| Area | Checks |
|---|---|
| Stamps (FE-F11-1) | 19:52 → "7:52 PM", booking "7:30 PM"; the walk-in reads "3:07 PM" twice; no afternoon stamp reads AM across the demo day (10:25–19:52); Arabic "7:52 م" / "7:30 م"; 0 `data-status` attributes |
| Errors (FE-F11-2) | an aborted check-in → toast, header and 10 cards stay; a 500 on the next day's read → header + "Could not load the appointments." + Retry, and Retry recovers |
| Other days (FE-F11-4) | Thu 1 Oct shows no check-in buttons; the API refuses 400 `FUTURE_APPOINTMENT`; undo on it → 200; a past-day check-in (Sep 28, appointment 249) → 200, stamped |
| Walk-in cache (FE-F11-17) | board → Search → quick check-in → Appointments: the walk-in is on the board |
| SSE (FE-F11-3/-7) | Express killed under an open board → 502 → "Connection Error"; after the restart the board went back to **Live on its own** (responses `200, 502, 502, 200`) and received another desk's check-in. The streams were frozen by a TCP proxy (no FIN/RST) after the check-in and a kiosk patient load: **board and kiosk both caught up at ~75 s** |
| Grid (FE-F10-2) | 26 Sep – 1 Oct renders 6 columns, past days with their appointments; the 15:07 row holds the walk-in (API and DOM); 4 Oct renders the booked 20:30 row but not the empty 20:00, and the 18:15 booking; the month view counts the walk-in; `month?date=2026-10-31` ends Oct 31 and its grid ends Thu Nov 5 |
| Edit (FE-F10-1) | calendar → Edit Appointment: the form's Doctor and Type equal the opened appointment's own (#71: 3 / Follow Up) |
| Slot limit (FE-F10-7) | 4 concurrent bookings into a max-3 slot → `200, 200, 200, 400 SLOT_FULL`; moving another appointment in → 400 `SLOT_FULL {booked: 3}`; a doctor change inside the full slot → 200; the edit form shows "This time slot is already full. Please pick another time." |
| Videos (FE-F11-16/-6) | with the `VideosPath` row deleted, an upload lands in `machine/clinic1/ovideos/`; the QR modal warns about the built-in address; the printed footer is "Bright Smile Orthodontics" |
| TV (FE-F11-6/-5) | the public page's title and placeholder are the clinic name with no "Shwan"; the tab no longer shows `lgtv-scheduler`; a 6 MB clip at 160 KB/s (~41 s) completes |
| Kiosk (FE-F11-6) | "Welcome to Bright Smile Orthodontics" |

## RB3 · 2026-09-29 — F9's three 🟠, then the rest of F8 + F9

The session the board queued after RB2: F9's three 🟠 first, then every other F8 and F9 finding that did not need an owner decision. **24 fixed, 3 partial** (FE-F9-11, FE-F9-12, FE-F8-13 — each names what is left; FE-F9-11 was completed by the same-day follow-up at the end of this section, so the ledger reads **25 fixed, 2 partial**), plus RB2's *found along the way* (a failed background refetch closed an open expense edit) and F9's server-side *found along the way* (the photo roll-up ignored a corrected visit date). Runtime testing found **one more defect in FE-F9-4's class**, fixed here too (see FE-F9-4 below). **`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, only F0's 4 `PatientSets.tsx` warnings (now at 2285/2309: RB3 added 10 lines above them), **33 files / 276 tests** (7 files and 47 tests up on RB2), contracts **D1 0 / D2 8**. The D2 baseline was ratcheted from 9 to 8 because `media.patientLink` is now modelled. Build green. **The route-table snapshot diffed by exactly these lines**, and the diff was accepted:

```
- USE     /clinic-assets  [serveStatic]
+ GET     /api/patients/:personId/xray/preview  [validate(params,query)]
+ GET     /api/patients/:personId/xray/send-path  [validate(params,query)]
- GET     /api/getworkforreceipt/:workId  [authorize(admin|front_desk|clinical) -> validate(params)]
- GET     /api/expenses/summary  [validate(query)]
~ POST    /api/webceph/upload-image       [timeout(255000ms) -> uploadImage -> validate(body)]
~ POST    /api/webceph/upload-from-file   [timeout(255000ms) -> validate(body)]
```

No migration, no schema change, no Supabase DDL. The three new server paths are two GETs and one request timeout. The `/clinic-assets` removal takes away a static mount of the **whole** clinic volume that any logged-in user could read. Its only consumer, the X-ray card, never rendered through it.

### What changed, per finding

**F9's three 🟠**
- **FE-F9-1**: failed diagnosis read.
  - `diagnosisQuery` no longer catches: only the route's `200 null` means "none yet", and a failure throws (RQ's transient retry applies).
  - `Diagnosis` is split into a thin route component and a `DiagnosisEditor` keyed by `workId`. The editor reads with `refetchOnMount: 'always'` and seeds **once, from the read this mount made** (`isFetchedAfterMount && isSuccess && !isFetching`). Until then there is no form, so there is nothing to Save.
  - A failed read shows an error panel with the server's message, **Retry**, and *Back to works*, and says why the form stays closed.
  - The works list no longer gates the page (it only names the work in the delete confirm).
- **FE-F9-2**: stale visit edit.
  - The visit form reads `visitByIdQuery` with `refetchOnMount: 'always'` and seeds only from the post-mount read. `ContentRenderer` keys it by `workId:visitId`.
  - A save marks `qk.visit.byId(visitId)` stale (`refetchType: 'none'`, since the form is leaving). A delete removes it.
- **FE-F9-3**: X-ray *Send*.
  - New `GET /api/patients/:personId/xray/send-path` (contract `patient.xraySendPath`, charset-validated `file`/`detailsDir`). It runs the viewer's own `processXrayImage` and returns the image's server path, the way `/api/convert-path` serves the photo senders.
  - The card opens a blank tab **inside the click** (so a slow render can't trip the popup blocker), fetches the path, then points the tab at `/send-message?file=<path>`. On failure it closes the tab and shows a toast.

**The rest of F9**
- **FE-F9-4**: the photo flags' roll-up into the work.
  - *Client:* every visit save and delete also invalidates `qk.patient.all`. The three lifecycle boxes carry inline hints ("marks the treatment Finished", "sets the treatment's initial-photo / debond date").
  - *Client:* a save that finishes the work (Final Photo newly ticked) or reopens it (un-ticked) asks first: *Finish treatment?* / *Reopen treatment?*. Deleting the Final Photo visit says it reopens the treatment.
  - *Server* (`visit.routes.ts#visitRollupConflict`): these failures now return worded **409**s instead of a generic 500:
    - `ck_works` / `ck_works_deb` / `ck_works_debiph` → the photo-date ordering rule;
    - `unq_tblwork_active` → "another active treatment";
    - **new in RB3**: `photof_index` / `photo_index`, the partial unique indexes allowing **one** Final Photo and **one** Initial Photo visit per work. Runtime found this state reachable from the UI: finish through a Final Photo visit, *Reactivate* on the Works card, then tick Final Photo on a later visit. That returned "Failed to add visit" (500).
  - `utils/pg-errors.ts` exports `isCheckViolation`.
  - F9's server note is fixed too: `applyPhotoUpdate` now moves the work's `i_photo_date` / `f_photo_date` / `debond_date` when a **still-set** flag's visit date is corrected.
- **FE-F9-5**: X-ray thumbnails.
  - New `GET /api/patients/:personId/xray/preview?detailsDir=` sends `…/<detailsDir>/t.png` with `dotfiles: 'allow'`, scoped to that one fixed, charset-validated path (`patient-assets.service#xrayPreviewPath`).
  - The placeholder is conditional rendering (a `brokenPreviews` set), not the global `hidden` class that lost the cascade.
  - Dev now shows thumbnails without a Vite proxy change, because `/api` is already proxied.
- **FE-F9-6**: new `utils/toothNotation.ts#unnToPalmer` (tested). The 3D Scans tab prints 3Shape's UNN numbers in the app's Palmer notation ("Bridge UL6–UL8 · Crown UR6") with the raw UNN in the tooltip. The Diagnosis placeholder `#24` became `UL4`.
- **FE-F9-7**: new `hooks/useUnsavedRouteGuard.ts`, the page-sized sibling of `<Modal unsavedGuard>`.
  - `useBlocker` plus the shared `useConfirm` with the `common:unsaved.*` strings, and `beforeunload` for reload and close. A same-path/query navigation is never blocked.
  - `allowNextNavigation()` lets the page's own post-save redirect through.
  - It is wired into `Diagnosis` and `NewVisitComponent`. Cancel, Back, the sidebar and the header tabs now ask.
  - The 500 ms post-save timer is gone: the page leaves immediately, and its buttons stay disabled until it unmounts.
- **FE-F9-8**: the visit screens' edges.
  - `/visits` without `workId` explains that visits are per treatment and links to Works (no empty list, no `workId=null` Add).
  - Add and Edit opened from Visit History carry `from=visits` and return there.
  - A failed list read has a Retry instead of a × that cleared nothing.
  - An edit of a missing visit shows the error and no form. **Server:** `updateVisitByWorkId` returns `success: false` for a missing row, and the route answers **404** instead of a success over 0 rows.
- **FE-F9-9**: the WebCeph modal.
  - It is pinned `dir="ltr"` through a `display: contents` wrapper, so it stays LTR inside the Arabic patient-info route.
  - A pending link read shows *Checking…*, and a failed one shows the error with Retry. Only a real 404 shows the Create card.

- **FE-F9-10**: `formatXrayDate` reads DICOM `YYYYMMDD` and anything else through `formatDate`. It never parses the file name, and an unreadable value is "Unknown Date". This also removes the card from FE-F3-3's browser-locale census. **The `YYYYMMDD` format is still the DICOM assumption.** F9's query for the owner stands: check one real `meta` file.
- **FE-F9-11 (partial)**: the upload timeout budget.
  - `media.contract#WEBCEPH_UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 255 s` is derived in its comment from the service's worst case (2 calls × 3 attempts × 20 s / 60 s caps, plus back-off, = 246 s).
  - It is applied as the two upload routes' `requestTimeout` and as the client's `timeoutMs`, and `webceph-service.ts` points at it.
  - **Not done in the first pass:** `race: 'Asian'` remained hard-coded. **Done in the follow-up below.**
- **FE-F9-12 (partial)**: a11y.
  - `DentalChart` is **one** tab stop with a roving tabindex (←/→ along an arch, ↑/↓ between arches, Home/End, Enter/Space). The 30 slots are named ("Between UR1 and UL1"). Teeth are named by `aria-label`, and their images are decorative.
  - Both notes labels are associated with their textareas. The error ×s have `aria-label`s.
  - Both forms' tab strips have `tablist` / `tab` / `aria-selected` / `tabpanel`.
  - **Not done:** primary teeth on the visit chart (`TeethSelector` has them). There are no primary-tooth chart images, so this needs a UX decision, not a bug fix.
- **FE-F9-13**: hand-written types over contracts.
  - Diagnosis types are derived from `workContract.DiagnosisBody`, and `diagnosis-fields.test.ts` fails if a contract field has no row.
  - `VisitFormData` is `Required<Omit<AddVisitBody, …>>` plus the three select ids.

  - `media.createPatient` / `uploadImage` / `uploadFromFile` / `patientLink` responses model what the modal reads (`patientLink` was a D2 `z.unknown()`). The create route falls back to the submitted id when WebCeph omits it, so no link row is stored without one.
- **FE-F9-14**: altitude and polish.
  - `Diagnosis` went from 957 lines to 343 plus `diagnosis-fields.ts`, a config of 44 fields across 5 tabs.

Checks, grouped:

### Follow-up · 2026-09-29 — the WebCeph race (owner decision, completes FE-F9-11)

The owner's call: **"we are in Iraq — change the race accordingly, and make it clear that this race is chosen by default."**
- **The value.** WebCeph offers four norm sets: african / asian / caucasian / hispanic. Cephalometric norm sets group Middle Eastern populations (Arab and Kurdish) with the **Caucasian** norms. WebCeph's "Asian" norms are East Asian. So every patient created until now was analysed against the wrong norms.
- **One definition.** New `shared/webceph-race.ts` holds `WEBCEPH_RACES`, `WEBCEPH_DEFAULT_RACE = 'caucasian'` (with the reasoning in its comment) and the labels. The media contract's `race` is now `z.enum(WEBCEPH_RACES)` instead of any string. `webceph-service` falls back to the shared default (it was `'asian'`) and validates against the shared list. `docs/WEBCEPH_SETUP.md` says the same.
- **Visible, not assumed.** The WebCeph dialog's *Create* card has a **Race (cephalometric norms)** select. It is pre-selected to *Caucasian (default)*, and a hint under it reads "**Caucasian is chosen by default** — the norm set used for Middle Eastern (Iraqi) patients. Change it only for a patient of another background." If staff pick another value, the hint changes to "Changed from the default (Caucasian) for this patient". Either way it adds that the race is sent once, at creation.
- **Test.** `shared/webceph-race.test.ts` (3 tests) pins the default, the four values and the contract's refusal of anything else. The gate is green: 0 errors, F0's 4 warnings, 34 files / 279 tests, D1 0 / D2 8, no route-table diff.
- **Runtime, 8/8**, on the same throwaway demo database, with both create requests aborted so nothing reached WebCeph:
  - The select opens at `caucasian` / "Caucasian (default)", the hint shows, and all four options are offered.
  - *Create* sends `race: "caucasian"`.
  - Picking Asian shows the "changed" hint and sends `asian`.
  - `POST /api/webceph/create-patient` with `race: "Asian"` → 400 at the contract.
  - No page errors.
  - The probe, the temporary `.env`, dev and the database were removed or stopped afterwards.
- **Per-deployment note.** For a center outside the region, the default is the one constant in `shared/webceph-race.ts`, and the per-patient select already overrides it. If the product is sold outside the Middle East, the default should become a Settings option the way `DEFAULT_WORK_CURRENCY` is. That was not built here.
- **Patients already in WebCeph keep the race they were created with.** The app does not send race again after creation. Correcting existing ones would be done in WebCeph itself.

### Found along the way, not fixed (file for the owning session)
- **Font Awesome icons are in accessible names** wherever the `<i>` lacks `aria-hidden` (e.g. the visit form's *Update* reads as " Update"). RB3 added `aria-hidden` on the screens it rewrote. The rest is **F25**'s sweep.
- **`WorkComponent` status changes (Reactivate) do not check for a Final Photo visit.** Reactivating a work finished by a visit leaves that visit's `f_photo` set, so the work reads Active with a final-photo visit on file, and a later Final Photo tick hits `photof_index` (now worded). Whether Reactivate should also clear the flag, or warn, is an owner call. **F7's surface.**
- **The set cost of an aligner set on a non-USD work** can still be entered (only the payment is refused now). **F17**, with FE-F8-6's note.

## RB2 · 2026-09-28 — F8's two 🟠

At the owner's request, the two 🟠 F8 filed were fixed straight after the audit, and the session then stopped. Nothing else in F8 was touched. **2 fixed.** **`npm run gate` green**: 0 errors, and the only warnings are F0's 4 pre-existing `PatientSets.tsx` a11y ones. 229/229 tests in 26 files. Contracts D1 0 / D2 9, both at baseline. Build green. **No route-table diff** (client-only change). Not committed.

### What changed
- **FE-F8-1** — `ExpenseSummary` now totals **the rows the table shows**: `expenses` + `loading` come from the page's own `useExpenses(filters)`, count = rows, IQD and USD summed separately. The currency match is trimmed and case-insensitive, like the `citext` column (live values are exactly `IQD`/`USD`). The date-only read it used to prefer is gone from the client, along with everything that existed only for it: `useExpenseSummary`, the phantom `ExpenseSummary` interface (it modelled `totalExpenses / byCategory / byCurrency`, fields the endpoint never sent), `expenseSummaryQuery` and `qk.expenses.summary`. The list is never paginated (`expensesQuery` sends no `limit`), so the sum is exact. The server route stays for now (see below).
- **FE-F8-2** — `routes/Expenses.tsx` **derives** the expense being edited instead of seeding it into state: `currentExpense = editingId != null ? editingExpense ?? null : null`. `currentExpense`/`seededExpense` and their setters are deleted. Update vs create is decided by `editingId`, not by whether a row happens to be loaded. The modal opens for an edit only once its row is present (`expenseModalOpen`); a cached row is instant. That closes the second half of the finding too: before, the form rendered as an empty *Add* form while the row loaded, and a Save in that window would have created.

### Runtime verification — 11 of 11 checks passed
Dev `:5273` was started with the three CDC sinks and WhatsApp auto-init **disabled by env**. Every non-GET was **aborted**. One read was **delayed** 1.2 s (`GET /api/expenses/:id`) to exercise the cold open. No screenshots. The probe (`scripts/e2e/.rb2-probe.tmp.mjs`) was deleted afterwards.
- **FE-F8-1:** the cards equal the table's own count and per-currency sums **unfiltered (83 rows)**, with **category 1 (33 rows / 151,750 IQD — the cards read 3,001,750 before the fix)**, with **currency USD (2 / 200 USD)** and with **category 5 (12 rows)**.
- **FE-F8-2:**
  - A cold edit renders **no modal** while its row loads, then opens as *Edit Expense* with 60,000.
  - Edit → Cancel → Edit the same row opens *Edit Expense* with 60,000. Its Save sends **`PUT /api/expenses/4759`** and no POST.
  - A → B → A each opens its own row (60,000 / 430,000 / 60,000).
  - *Add New Expense* still opens empty, and its Save sends `POST /api/expenses`.
- One probe check first reported a failure that was the probe's own: Playwright's `innerText()` waits for the element, so the "400 ms" read had silently waited out the delayed fetch. An instrumented rerun showed the title appears only after the response (≈ 4.6 s into the run, 1.5 s after the click). The check now uses `count()`, which does not wait.

It is recorded on **FE-F8-14** (DEAD). Removing it is a route-table diff, so it belongs to that cleanup, not to a 🟠 fix. **✅ Removed in RB3.**

So if the by-id query refetches (window focus, stale) while the modal is open, and that refetch fails, the modal closes and the typing is lost. This predates RB2, and it takes a network or 5xx failure mid-edit. Gate it on `!editingExpense` when F8's remaining findings get their RB slice. **✅ Fixed in RB3.**
